# Regia Engine v0.5.0

Builds on v0.2.17. The v0.2.14 to v0.2.17 tags shipped without their own notes, so this note covers every change
since v0.2.13, the last tag with a note: a game pinned to any tag from v0.2.13 to v0.2.17 reads this one note. The
version line skips v0.3 and v0.4. Their work, the lightmap line (v0.3.0) and the Tier 2 renderer and general systems
(v0.4.0), ships in this release.

Read **Upgrade: host changes since v0.2.13** first if you build a game or a shell against the engine crates. New
features are off, or leave existing projects unchanged, unless **Changed** says otherwise. **Known issues** lists what
is not yet accepted, from the v0.5.0 evidence ledger; a feature is described as working on a platform only where a
cited run shows it (see **Platform evidence**).

## What v0.5 delivers

- **Renderer line.** Every built-in pass now enters its node in the compiled frame graph and records in the graph's node
  order, and `render.graph` reports the dispatch. This is the minimal graph slice; full resource ownership and transient
  aliasing move to v0.5.x (ADR-4945). Shader permutations are bounded and checked at cook and package time (`cargo xtask
  check-shaders --matrix`). The visibility buffer gains cluster residency with per-tier budgets, masked and swaying
  foliage clusters, and LOD hysteresis; the dense default stays off. A temporal quality chart scores TSR, TAAU and FSR
  3.1, and opt-in TSR at half render scale meets every chart target on lavapipe and an RTX 5090 (on Metal the thin-orbit
  row reads 0.889; see Known issues). Vendor upscalers are opt-in: an XeSS adapter on Vulkan and DLSS packaging. New
  opt-in shading includes an energy-conserving BRDF, many-light sampling, ReSTIR DI and GI, IES profiles and area
  lights, a tangent-plane subsurface kernel and water caustics. Post volumes v3 add convex shapes and LUTs,
  `PhysicalCamera` saves with the level, and the path tracer gains presets, a denoiser and a calibration suite.
- **Lightmaps (the v0.3.0 line).** The baker adds a denoiser, crease-aware texels, seam stitching, automatic density,
  checkpoint and resume, and per-phase timings. The cook adds multi-page atlases, BC6H and ASTC HDR texels, and one bake
  that derives every package tier. At runtime, levels name their lightmap, receivers get indirect specular, and capsule
  occluders and lightmap debug views are available. A Regia-built interior plate and a phone memory sample come with it.
  The base bake and the derived package tiers meet the seam bar with no dark points (ADR-5125), and the Mobile Low
  golden is re-blessed after the owner's approval.
- **Global illumination and sky.** DDGI gains probe seeding and a visibility blend on the low and medium tiers, the
  radiance cache gains gather controls and attribution, and baked GI volumes split past one binding. Local reflection
  captures become level content, interiors lose their sky haze, and fog sun shadow and cirrus clouds can be calibrated.
  Dynamic GI on the High tier meets every acceptance bar; the Low tier keeps its 2 m probes and misses the bar in
  three rooms, and the radiance cache's golden check has not run (see Known issues).
- **General systems.**
  - Physics: continuous collision, physics materials, force fields and contact sounds.
  - Cloth and destruction: cloth layers, presets and a runaway guard; structural load and impact damage; explosions.
  - Characters and vehicles: active ragdolls; geared vehicles with slip tires, trailers, AI drivers, audio and wheel
    effects; aircraft; swim, ladder and mantle traversal.
  - Animation and AI: motion-matching cooking; AI perception, utility AI, GOAP and squads with Behavior and State Tree
    editors.
  - Scripting and templates: Lua script components, and a playable Third Person template and combat sample.
  - World: terrain editing tied to levels, HLOD proxy atlases, voxel far fields, parallel and WFC procedural generation,
    nav jump links, an optional fluid crate and `.vfx.ron` v7.
  - Media and UI: sequencer v3, music on the audio clock, boot movies and built-in AV1 encoding, UI collections and
    virtual lists, SVG, culture formatting, translator tools and player accessibility settings.
  - Determinism: simulation math moves to software kernels and fixed-step clocks, so results can match across platforms
    and thread counts; some sample digests still differ between Linux and Windows (see Known issues).
- **Tools.**
  - Agent and editor: MCP over HTTP, a headless editor, one job, review and golden flow for long work, an agent
    benchmark and a protocol snapshot.
  - Editor: preferences, keymap and layouts, viewport tools, multi-select editing, a frame profiler and prefab variants.
  - Import and export: EXR/HDR, Aseprite, LDtk and MaterialX importers, glTF collision proxies and scene export to
    `.glb`.
  - Cook and content: a shared cook cache, pak update diffs, a provenance policy, project generators, and entity kinds
    with a project map.
- **Networking.** The v0.5 scope is client, listen and dedicated servers over raw UDP and Steam sockets, lobbies and a
  content handshake.
  - Security: clients pin a server's key on first use, sessions rekey, IPv6 sources group by /64, and floods are shed
    before proof of address.
  - Replication: `#[derive(NetMessage)]`, asset handles on the wire and opt-in entropy coding.
  - Gameplay: networked bodies and vehicles, lag-compensated poses, server bots and persistent server worlds.
  - Matches and content: a multi-machine match tool, signed content manifests, `mods://` and Steam Workshop,
    leaderboards and voice processing.
  - A two-account session on real Steam passed both legs (lobby and sockets, and the `net-game` template match); the
    Steam leg ran without the network conditioner, which is proven on the mock and over UDP.
- **Platforms.**
  - Android: frame pacing is rewritten and schedules run single-threaded by default. The Arena sample went from about 20
    to 60 fps on a Pixel 9 Pro. TalkBack works through AccessKit, and Adreno phones get a trimmed forward shader.
  - Steam Deck: the `Deck` preset is the runtime default, and the showcase holds its fps caps on a Steam Deck OLED.
  - Windows: native runs on an Intel N150 laptop, Intel driver and NVIDIA driver workarounds, owner-only key files,
    executable resources and launchers.
  - macOS stays testing-only. The rc6 Metal workspace run on a MacBook Air M5 read 33,788 passed, 6 failed, 417 ignored;
    two visibility-buffer close tests and the half-scale TSR thin-orbit row stay open (see Known issues). XR runs on the
    simulator only.
  - Product viewer: a lean viewer for native, WebGPU and WebGL2 and as an Android app.
- **Regia Forest Demo.**
  - The demo: `showcase --demo` turns the forest scene into an interactive demo, with a free camera, time-of-day and
    weather, a tier picker, screenshots, a benchmark and credits.
  - Packaging: an agent-free `--features demo` build, Windows and Linux packages with checksums and a Steam depot
    layout, third-party notices, an SBOM, a draft EULA and a Steam upload dry run.
  - Readiness: the demo ships with the agent protocol compiled out (`--no-default-features --features demo`,
    ADR-5419, ADR-5460). The release candidates pass their SSH steps on a Steam Deck OLED; Game Mode,
    sleep/resume and a physical pad on the Deck, and the counsel-reviewed EULA, are still open (see Known issues).

## Added

### Rendering

- `.cube` color LUTs can be cooked and ship in paks, and `regia_render::lut::load_named` resolves authored LUT paths
  (ADR-4830).
- Post volumes v3: `PostVolumeShape::ConvexHull` (1 to 16 half-spaces, exact world-meter blend under any transform) and
  per-volume `.cube`/PNG-strip LUTs blended by weight and priority, off by default (`render.set_post_volume_luts`).
  Headless gizmo data (`render.post_volume_gizmos`, `post_volume_outline`), a `post_volumes` gizmo group, editor LUT
  loading and the `render_lab post_volumes` sample come with it (ADR-2323)
- `PhysicalCamera` is a reflected component (format `physical-camera` v1), so a lens saves with the level (ADR-2323)
- `regia_render::budget_ledger::BudgetLedger`: named integer counters (pipeline and bind-group creates capped at 0)
  checked after a warm frame, beside `HitchReport` (ADR-3943)
- `create_window_instance` and `request_device_locked` let a host create its own wgpu instance and device under the
  engine's loader-list lock (ADR-2306, ADR-2113)
- `set_validation_bundle_hook` writes one `bug` feedback bundle with `gpu_breadcrumbs.json` per minute on an uncaptured
  validation or internal GPU error
- Lens effects are a reflected scene component (`regia_render::lens_effects::LensEffects`, format `lens-effects` v1), so
  a camera's lens effects set with `render.set_lens_effects` save with the level and come back on load; `regia
  upgrade-project` lists and rewrites them. New `LensEffects::starburst` (0-10: six spikes from bright pixels, drawn in
  the existing composite pass) and `LensEffects::after_dof` (the pass runs after depth of field and motion blur, so
  flare, dirt and starburst stay sharp over a defocused frame); `render.set_lens_effects` takes both and
  `render.lens_effects` reports them with `starburst_active`. Off by default; an off frame is byte-identical. (ADR-3196)
- Material AOVs in render-out: `render.out_start` and `render.out_job` `aovs` also take `normal`, `normal_view`,
  `roughness`, `metalness`, `base_color` (linear albedo before lighting) and `material_id` (`u32`, stable for the job),
  drawn by a material pass over the id pass's draw list only when asked for. `aov_format` `"png"`/`"png16"` for the
  float AOVs; repeat runs write byte-identical files covered by `aov.frame_hashes`, and `aov.ids.materials` lists the
  material ids. `aovs` takes up to 9 entries (was 3); `.render_job.ron` `output.aovs` takes the same names (format
  unchanged). New codes `unknown_aov` and `aov_format_unsupported`, capability `render_out_aov_material`. (ADR-3256)

#### Lighting and shading

- Opt-in energy-conserving BRDF `BrdfEnergy::Compensated` (multiple-scattering lobe plus coupled diffuse) in the forward
  shader, visibility resolve, lite Mobile shader and path tracer: a white furnace stays at or below 1 where the legacy
  model reaches 1.68 at grazing dielectrics. Select it with `RenderProfile::brdf_energy`, `set_process_brdf_energy`,
  `REGIA_BRDF_ENERGY` or `render.set_brdf_energy`; `Legacy` stays the default. (ADR-2632)
- Opt-in stochastic many-light sampling in the production frame (`RenderProfile::many_light_sampling`,
  `render.set_many_light_sampling { mode: "production" }`, `render.many_light_stats`), plus spatiotemporal blue noise
  for shadow kernel rotation, hashed alpha and pane SSR under `FrameQuality::blue_noise`. (ADR-2652, ADR-2470, ADR-2075)
- Tangent-plane subsurface kernel (`SubsurfaceKernel::Tangent`, `render.set_subsurface { kernel }`), foliage thin-slab
  transmission on terrain grass and scatter classes, and stable `SubsurfaceUnsupported` reason codes in
  `render.subsurface`. The screen kernel stays the default. (ADR-3170)
- Thin film draws on transmissive surfaces, masked instances and lightmap receivers (forward and path tracer),
  `HairAlpha::Sorted` hair cards go through OIT, and glTF `extras.regia_transmission_roughness` imports. (ADR-2732) A
  later docs audit says the shipped pass does not draw the transmissive, masked, lightmap or sorted-hair routes yet
  (ADR-5029, see Changed).
- Forward studio axis: the ground plane, local reflection captures and image-light debug views compile only while one is
  on (`ForwardStudioMode`, `render.forward_studio`, `REGIA_FORWARD_STUDIO=full`). Pixels are unchanged. (ADR-2308)
- Shadow-caster two-phase occlusion becomes a profile column (`RenderProfile::shadow_occlusion`,
  `RenderSettings::shadow_occlusion`), off in every preset. (ADR-2482, ADR-2072)
- Scatter shadow LOD hint (`ScatterField::set_shadow_lod_hint`, `terrain.set_scatter { shadow_lod }`) and
  collision-proxy counts in the model report. (ADR-2471)
- Forward decals (`regia_render::forward_decals`, off by default): with `RenderProfile::forward_decals`,
  `REGIA_FORWARD_DECALS=1` or `set_process_forward_decals(true)`, a decal's albedo, normal, roughness and metallic blend
  into the surface in the forward pass before lighting, so the BRDF and the frame AOVs see them. The CPU bins up to 64
  decals into 32 px screen tiles; `regia_vfx::route_decals` shades each decal `post`, `forward` or `split` (terrain
  receivers and emission stay on the projected pass), and the VFX runtime feeds the frame through
  `TranslucentLayer::forward_decal_feed`, so a game needs no extra calls. `.decal.ron` v2 adds `metallic`, a metallic
  channel and `shading` (`auto` | `post`), with normal pages cooked from each layer's luma
  (`generate_decal_atlas_pages`). On lavapipe the spec 27 decal rows pass: `decal_aovs` 0.36° and 0.001 (was 17.51° and
  0.547), `decal_flip` 0.003 (was 0.755). (ADR-3255)
- Rect and tube lights on tiers with no emitter budget, opt-in: `RenderProfile::area_light_fallback` (off in every
  preset; `RenderSettings::preset()` ignores it). On `DesktopLow`, `MobileLow` and `MobileHigh`, where rect and tube
  lights emitted nothing, each rect face stands in as a cosine spot of its on-axis intensity and each tube as three
  radial cosine spots (120° apart, flux kept), with the equal-area source size. `light_profiles::area_light_fallback`
  builds them for hosts that make `FrameScene` themselves; cost counter `LightExtras::area_fallback_lights`; agent
  `render.area_light_fallback` and `render.set_area_light_fallback { enabled }`. No default frame moves. (ADR-3247)
- IES profiles on the Mobile lite tiers: `forward_mobile.wgsl` reads the profile band from the emitter atlas the frame
  already binds; a light without a profile takes no new instruction. IES `TILT=INCLUDE` is applied
  (`IesFile::tilt_factor`, `candela_tilted`, `candela_at_angles_tilted`, `peak_candela_tilted`,
  `CookedIesProfile::from_file_tilted`); the plain lookups apply the factor at 0°. The terrain's shared light pass
  gains a profile hook (`shared_local_lights_profiled_wgsl`, `shared_light_profiles_layout_entry`,
  `SharedPassDraw::set_engine_light_profiles`, default no-op). (ADR-3247)
- Opt-in sun specular disc: the forward pass and the visibility-buffer resolve can light the sun's specular from its
  0.27° disc (the closed-form mean of each isotropic GGX lobe over the disc), as the path tracer samples it, so smooth
  highlights get wider and dimmer at the core. Off in every preset (`RenderProfile::sun_specular_disc`,
  `render.set_sun_specular_disc`); anisotropic and hair lobes and the Mobile shader keep the point sun. Against a
  converged trace the disc scores 0.45 % rel. RMSE and the point sun 1.06 %. (ADR-3190)

#### Material graphs

- Material Graph editor panel: node canvas, live preview sphere, errors on their node, shared undo, an editor-only
  `.matgraph.layout.ron`, and tabs for function canvases (`.matfunc.ron`), collapse/expand to functions, signature fixes
  and instance overrides. Agent methods `editor.matgraph_*` drive all of it. (ADR-2318, ADR-2490)
- `Noise` node (fractal gradient noise, 1 to 8 octaves, the same on every device) in `.matgraph.ron` v3. (ADR-2318)
- Material graphs read world positions across a floating-origin rebase through an origin-slot adapter. Engine rebase
  holds (`RebaseHolds`, `stream.origin` `holds`/`offset_m`) and `TerrainStrokeSession` keep strokes and vehicle contacts
  consistent across a rebase. (ADR-2435)
- Bounded graphs: at most 16 static switches and 4096 lowered nodes. Each cook checks graph sets against mobile and
  desktop binding limits, and `material.compile` reports `lowered_nodes`, `function_calls`, `limits` and per-error
  `code`/`authored_node`. (ADR-4901, ADR-3002)

#### Visibility buffer

- Masked (alpha-tested) and swaying foliage (`FoliageSway::adopt`) draw as clusters, and the instance cull skips cluster
  levels an instance's distance cannot select. (ADR-3915, ADR-3916)
- Opt-in cluster residency (`ClusterResidency`, `render.set_visbuffer_residency`) with per-tier VRAM, upload, RAM and IO
  budgets. Bands stream from the cooked pak, and shadow jobs request their own bands. (ADR-3118, ADR-3708)
- Cluster LOD hysteresis (`VisBufferSettings::lod_hysteresis_px`, `render.vis_lod_hysteresis`), cleared on camera cuts.
  (ADR-4631)
- Dense-scene default switch `RenderSettings::visibility_default` (`render.set_visbuffer { default }`, off in every
  preset) with crossover tools (`DenseCrossover`, `DensityMetrics`). Also: warm frames through the path, per-material
  lightmap receivers, and double-sided clusters in the software raster. (ADR-3149, ADR-2689)

#### Upscalers and temporal

- `regia_render::temporal_chart` and agent `seeing.temporal_chart` score TSR, TAAU and FSR 3.1 on the spec 27 § 2.3
  chart (motion clarity, thin geometry, ghosting, disocclusion smear, stability). (ADR-2638)
- Optional XeSS SR Vulkan adapter (`regia_xess`, `xess` feature) in the vendor slot, with zeroed temporary heaps and
  `REGIA_XESS_SMOKE_*` diagnostics. (ADR-4656, ADR-4907)
- DLSS real-SDK proof surface (`dlss-proof` guide topic) and opt-in packaging of the SR runtime and notices with
  `REGIA_PACKAGE_DLSS=1`. (ADR-4758)
- Upscaler reason `dlss_needs_vulkan` (`regia_render::upscaler::reason::DLSS_NEEDS_VULKAN`): the DLSS binding drives NGX
  through Vulkan only, so an NVIDIA DX12 device's DLSS row names it. (ADR-7695)
- Card measurement aids in `temporal_chart` (ignored tests): `tsr_scale_timings` (TSR 0.5, TSR 0.67 and native TAA
  interleaved on one device, median and 90th percentile GPU time) and the `half_scale_probe` part `orbittrack`. (ADR-7694)
- Vendor seam: `render.get_upscalers` rows carry `unavailable_code`, `fallback_backend` and a `reactive_mask` report,
  and `PreferredRange` keeps dynamic resolution inside a vendor's range. A vendor conformance checklist test is
  included. (ADR-2679, ADR-3101)

#### Frame core

- Every built-in record site in `render_into` now enters its frame-graph node, and the dispatch is checked and reported
  (`FrameGraphReport::dispatch`, `render.graph` `dispatch`, counts of skipped, repeated and out-of-order nodes);
  two-phase occlusion is node `prepass_occlusion`. The passes still record in `render_into`, not through the nodes' own
  record callbacks (ledger M02). (ADR-4905, ADR-4856)
- `RenderThreadHost::post` and `RenderThreadSettings::queued_frames` for a host that presents from a render thread.
  (ADR-2798)
- `FrameRenderer::set_complete_frames` renders offline captures with no speed-dependent work (inline graph builds and
  sky, frame-thread emissive fits, full texture streaming, ordered cull). (ADR-2582)
- `FrameRenderer::begin_offscreen_live` renders an offscreen frame with the renderer's own settings (no capture
  overrides), as the windowed path does; the editor's GPU viewport pane draws through it, while captures and
  goldens keep `begin_offscreen`. `SharedImage::pixels_arc` hands out pixels as a shared `Arc<[u8]>`, and
  `regia_render::alias_mesh_key` lets a second `Arc<CpuMesh>` address share an existing mesh cache id. (ADR-5535)

#### Performance and diagnostics

- Hitch tooling: `play.hitch_report` names every create after warm-up, plus a `hitchsoak` job on game content and
  `TranslucentLayer::is_active`. (ADR-2303, ADR-2585)
- Cost tooling: `render.frame_work` with a per-preset frame-work ratchet, `cargo xtask gpu-bench --suite features`,
  `regia bench --on/--preset`, a stochastic SSR cost probe and opt-in bound (`SssrCostBound::WET`,
  `render.set_ssr_cost`), lens-effects budget and memory APIs, and `app.startup_work` / `memory.idle_ledger`. (ADR-2711,
  ADR-2309, ADR-2557, ADR-2482, ADR-2987)
- GPU breadcrumbs at every frame pass. A device loss writes `crash-<time>.txt` naming the suspect passes, timestamps are
  opt-in, `telemetry.screenshot_mark` is new, and trace files are written off the frame thread. (ADR-2307)
- `seeing.frame_aov` returns the real frame's shading normal or roughness/metallic as a PNG with summary numbers.
  (ADR-2951)
- Named heap owners (`regia_core::memory::{MemoryOwner, enter_owner, scoped_owner, current_owner, owner_snapshot}`, up
  to 63 per process) and the renderer's owners (`regia_render::heap_owners`: `render.device`, `render.pipelines`,
  `render.shader_modules`, `render.frame` and others). `memory.report` answers `owners` and `since_mark.owners`;
  `memory.idle_ledger` answers `owners` and `largest_owner`. Composer cache control:
  `composer_cache_stats`, `release_composer_cache`, `release_idle_composers`, `COMPOSER_IDLE_FRAMES`. (ADR-3351)
- `MemoryReport::device_allocated_bytes`: the whole device's allocated bytes (the allocator's live total on Vulkan and
  D3D12, `MTLDevice.currentAllocatedSize` on Metal, `None` on GL and WebGPU), the one figure to hold against a GPU
  memory cap on every backend; `seeing.memory_report` answers it. (ADR-5860)
- The `Static` marker reaches the extracted frame (`FrameMesh::is_static`), the GPU cull and the paged shadow caches;
  `DrawStats::static_batches` and `ShadowCacheReport::static_casters` count it as a diagnostic (static and dynamic
  instances still share batches). (ADR-5704, ADR-5906)
- New `tracing` spans: the XR frame (`xr_wait_frame`, `xr_begin_frame`, `xr_locate_views`, `xr_end_frame`,
  `xr_render_eye`), `voice_chat_decode` (PCM) and `voice_chat_denoise`, `caption_layout`, `stream_plan_step` and
  `stream_teleports`; the `camera_driver` span records its entity. (ADR-5642, ADR-5783, ADR-3265, ADR-5778)
- GPU passes by graph node: `GpuTimings::node_passes()` lists the frame's GPU-timed passes in graph order, each named
  after the built-in node that records it (`GpuNodePass { node, pass, ms }`), and `GpuTimings::stamps()` lists every
  stamp with the node it bounds and the node that wrote it (`GpuStamp`, `in_owner`). `play.hitch_report` gains
  `gpu_passes.nodes` and `gpu_passes.stamps_outside_nodes`; existing interval numbers are unchanged. (ADR-5386)
- Heavy-caster occlusion cost case (`occlusion_gain_cost`, ignored `heavy_caster_occlusion_cost`): 4 096 masked,
  swaying or opaque casters behind a wall, under a roof or in the open, timed with both culls off, the prepass cull on
  and the shadow cull on, and checked byte for byte against the unculled frame; card results are in
  `book/agents/occlusion.md` "Which scenes gain". (ADR-5389)

#### Shaders and cooking

- `cargo xtask check-shaders --matrix` runs every shipped forward and graph permutation through naga, the layout scan,
  `spirv-val`, Vulkan pipelines, HLSL SM 6.0/5.1 and MSL, and checks bound structs and per-preset binding counts against
  limit classes. (ADR-2644, ADR-3002)
- `regia cook` reports each profile's shipped forward-shader cost under a `ShaderBytesBudget` (`render.permutations {
  shader_costs }`), and `regia package` fails on overruns for shipping platforms. (ADR-2664)

#### Path tracer

- Quality presets `PtPreset { Draft, Preview, Final, Reference }`, a built-in a-trous denoiser, adaptive sampling,
  `CausticsMode` and indirect clamp, exposed on `seeing.reference_trace` and `regia render --reference --preset`.
  (ADR-2433)
- The tracer shades material graphs with the code the forward pass runs, and adds Burley subsurface and frame decals
  (`PtDecal`, `PtImage::roughness`). (ADR-2725, ADR-2951)
- Spec 27 § 2.2 calibration suite scores every row against the tracer and writes an evidence page. (ADR-2660, ADR-2951)

#### Water

- Opt-in water extras: underwater view, ring frustum culling, per-ring snap, sea state from scene wind, and water GPU
  timing. Opt-in caustics and underwater sun shafts on High/Ultra (`water.set_caustics`). (ADR-2342, ADR-2432)
- Opt-in lingering foam on the FFT ocean (`FftOcean::foam_decay`, `foam_growth`; `water_body::ocean_foam`): each band
  keeps a foam history stepped by a new compute stage after the FFT, restarting on camera cuts, body changes, clock
  jumps and `WaterRenderer::reset_ocean_foam`. Opt-in whitecaps that hold with distance (`FftOcean::whitecap_rescale`)
  and queries that follow the drawn surface (`WaterSettings::queries_follow_tier`: on Low, or with `ocean_draw` off, a
  floater rides the water the tier draws). Agent `water.set { ocean: { foam_decay, foam_growth, whitecap_rescale } }`,
  `water.set { queries_follow_tier }`, and history fields in `water.ocean_cost` and `water.state`. Defaults allocate,
  dispatch and draw what they did before. (ADR-2843)
- Underwater shafts: the march tests an orthographic sun shadow map bound with `WaterRenderer::set_sun_shadow`, so
  piers, hulls and cave roofs cut the beams; opt-in temporal accumulation (`shaft_accumulate`, up to 16 frames, reset
  on camera cuts) and shafts seen from above (`shafts_from_above`), both in `water.set_caustics`. An FFT ocean grid the
  device cannot hold is refused with a named `OceanGridError` (`water.ocean_cost` `refused`/`error`). With
  `queries_follow_tier` on, surface queries answer the swell the renderer drew. Frames are unchanged with the switches
  off. (ADR-3251)

#### Frame graph

- `shadow_occlusion` (phase 1 of the shadow casters' two-phase cull) is an optional built-in frame-graph node, held only
  while `FrameRenderer::set_shadow_occlusion` / `render.set_shadow_occlusion` is on; `render.graph` lists it and the
  `custom_pass` example switches both optional nodes on and exits 1 on a dispatch violation. (ADR-5178, ADR-2072)

### Lightmaps, GI and lighting

#### Baking

- Lightmap denoiser: an edge-avoiding a-trous filter guided by normal, position and chart, scaled by measured noise, on
  by default in `regia bake-lightmaps` (`--denoise on|off`, `--denoise-iterations N`). Taps that leave a chart now
  follow the same surface across the border, so a wall of several blocks denoises as one wall. (ADR-2314, ADR-4108)
- Crease-aware texel placement, least-squares seam stitching and automatic density for small curved receivers
  (`--crease`, `--stitch`, `--auto-density`, `--seam-budget`; all on in the CLI, off in
  `LightmapBakeSettings::default()`). The bake JSON gains `density { auto, budget, stitched, entries, warnings }` with
  `lightmap_density_low`. (ADR-2404)
- Checkpointed, resumable bakes: `regia bake-lightmaps --checkpoint` and `regia bake-gi --checkpoint` write a `.bakeck`
  beside the output and resume byte-identically after a crash or driver reset (`--resume`, `--checkpoint-every`,
  `--checkpoint-seconds`, `--checkpoint-batch`). Progress lines show `(N left, eta S s)`; the editor's Lightmaps panel
  offers **Discard**. (ADR-3095)
- Meshes with only `TEXCOORD_0` unwrap at bake time; the UV2 lives in the `.flmap` and the cook carries it, leaving
  source meshes byte-identical. (ADR-3001)
- Bake reporting at scale: a `timings` object per phase, a `density_scaled` headline and a `lightmap_atlas_scaled`
  warning that names the remedy (`--max-pages`, `--max-atlas`, split the level, lower `--density`). (ADR-2955, ADR-3121,
  ADR-3300)
- Per-cell lightmap bakes of a streamed world: `regia bake-lightmaps --scene <streamed level> --cells` bakes each
  streaming cell as `<level lightmap stem>.cells/<x>_<z>.flmap`, with the cells within `--cell-ring N` (default 1) as
  occluders and bounce surfaces, so light does not leak through a cell edge. A seam pass makes border texels agree
  (irradiance and direction texels), `cells.json` records each cell's input hash so an unchanged cell is skipped
  (`--cells-force` re-bakes all), and `--checkpoint` checkpoints each cell. On a generated 3 × 3 world the p95 border
  step goes from 0.098 to 0.0043; a cell bakes in about 0.03 s on the CPU at 16 samples, 8 × 8 cells in 2.2 s. Library:
  `regia_pt::lightmap::cells`. (ADR-3318, ADR-4559)

#### Atlas, encoding and tiers

- Multi-page lightmap atlases: `regia bake-lightmaps --max-pages N` keeps the requested density on up to N pages instead
  of scaling down, and the runtime draws one texture-array layer per page. The default is 1 page until the frame patch
  that binds texture arrays is applied, then 4. (ADR-3300)
- Block-compressed texels: BC6H and ASTC 4x4 HDR (1 byte a texel instead of 4), plus BC7 / ASTC LDR direction sections,
  picked per platform at cook with `encoding: auto` and `directions: auto`. (ADR-2314, ADR-2635)
- One bake for every package tier: `regia cook --lightmap-tiers [--json]` derives each tier's `.flmap` from the
  highest-density bake, and `Regia.toml` `[lightmaps.tiers.<name>]` and `[profiles.<name>] lightmap_tier` choose what
  `regia package` ships. The defaults are ultra 16, high 8, low 4 and ultra_low 2 texels/m, with budgets of 320 / 208 /
  96 / 48 MiB. (ADR-3001, ADR-3120)
- The `lightmap_device` sample ships lightmap tier packages from one bake: `regia package --profile lm_low|lm_high`
  (shipping code with the agent kept, signed by a key the environment names) and `lm_low_dev|lm_high_dev`; both are
  `auto` encoded (ASTC HDR on Android, BC6H on desktop). On the Pixel 9 Pro the LOW package adds 5.6 MiB of PSS at
  MobileLow and the HIGH package 47.1 MiB at MobileHigh, both drawing native blocks. `book/agents/lightmaps.md` shows
  how to check a tier package on a device. (ADR-5382)

#### Runtime and levels

- Levels name their lightmap (`LevelLook::lightmap`, `--write-level`). `LightmapLevelPlugin` installs it on open, in the
  game and in the editor, attaches receivers to every mesh part (`MeshPartOf`), and stacks the lightmaps of additive
  levels into one atlas. (ADR-2313, ADR-2635)
- Indirect specular from the lightmap: receivers reflect the baked light at the GGX lobe's mean vector on every preset
  (`LightmapSettings::specular`, default on). On the gallery floor this lifts indirect luminance from 90.02 % to 97.47 %
  of the path tracer. (ADR-4762)
- Capsule occluders (`render.set_gi_occluders`) darken lightmap receivers on every draw path when a baked volume lights
  the frame. (ADR-2656)
- Debug views `lightmap_density`, `lightmap_only` and `lightmap_invalid` in `render.set_debug_view` and in the editor
  viewport. `.flmap` entries now record a source-mesh hash, so stale receivers are counted. (ADR-2656, ADR-2840)
- Agent surface: `render.get_lightmaps` reports `texel_encoding`, `direction_encoding`, `denoise`, `pages`, `specular`,
  `level`/`levels`/`level.additive[]` and `forward.uploaded_bytes`. `render.lightmap_encode_report` and the `lightmaps`
  block of `render.get_gi_mode` are also new. (ADR-2314, ADR-2313, ADR-2656, ADR-3145)
- Samples: `samples/render_lab/lightmap_room` (a baked room with a dynamic visitor) and `samples/lightmap_device` (phone
  memory and cost, with shader overrides staged from the data dir). (ADR-2635, ADR-3145)
- Streamed cells stack their lightmaps at run time: when a cell of the open level streams in (`CellLoaded`) its
  lightmap stacks under the level's in a fixed slot and leaves on `CellUnloading`; other cells' frames do not move.
  `render.get_lightmaps` gains `level.cells` and `level.cell_stack`; new codes `lightmap_cells_not_streamed`,
  `lightmap_cells_index`, `lightmap_cells_version`, `lightmap_cell_stack_full`. (ADR-3318)
- Stale receiver policy `LightmapSettings::stale_receivers`: a receiver whose mesh moved since the bake keeps drawing
  (`keep_drawing`, the default) or falls back to the frame's other light, the baked volume where one covers it
  (`fall_back`, reported as `lightmap_receiver_stale`). Agent `render.set_lightmaps` / `render.get_lightmaps`
  `stale_receivers`, `receivers.stale_fallback`. (ADR-3006)

#### Baked GI and probe volumes

- Split baked GI volumes: a layout over the portable 128 MiB binding bakes as up to four `.fgiv` parts that blend across
  a shared band and match the unsplit volume. The screen pass gets four volume storage slots, levels and streaming
  install every part, and a brick-visit histogram probe is available (`render.set_baked_gi_brick_probe`). (ADR-2618)
- Brick volumes stacked in Y blend across the shared horizontal face when `blend_distance` equals the overlap.
  (ADR-3998)
- Cropped split bricks: when no split keeps every brick whole under the 128 MiB part budget, the bake crops the bricks
  that cross a cut, with a margin that keeps the light continuous across it. A layout with a brick past one binding no
  longer fails when a cropped split fits: on `SyntheticLevel::tall` a 454.6 MiB layout (one 193.5 MiB brick) bakes as
  four parts of 119.0 to 126.7 MiB. Reported as `plan.split_cropped_bricks` (`bake-gi --dry-run`), `split` per part
  (`--check-volume`) and `parts.installed` (`level.get_baked_gi`). (ADR-3237)
- A memory answer before a bake: `regia bake-gi --dry-run`, `--check-volume` and `level.get_baked_gi` print `memory`,
  the bytes per tier against `regia_render::memory_budget`, what is over (`baked_gi_over_budget`,
  `baked_gi_part_over_binding`) and what fixes it, largest saving first. `level.get_baked_gi` adds `parts` and
  `host_layers`; `bake_sizing --level tall` with a `split_study` comparing cuts per axis. (ADR-3006)

#### Dynamic GI and radiance cache

- DDGI probe seeding, a visibility blend between cascades, a recent-weighted running average and a per-tier screen
  lookup region; the `low` and `medium` tiers turn them on. (ADR-2605)
- Radiance cache gather tunables (`rays`, `steps`, `sky_early_out`, `economy()`), GPU work counters, and per-term
  attribution (`RadianceCacheSettings::attribution`, `render.set_radiance_cache {gather, count_work, attribution}`).
  (ADR-2605, ADR-4106)
- Opt-in quarter-resolution screen-space GI gather (`ScreenSpaceGiSettings::quarter_gather`, default `false`;
  `set_ssgi_settings`, `SsgiGatherGuard`): texel step 4 gathers the bounce alone and joint-bilateral-upsamples it with
  the half-resolution filter. Agent `render.get_ssgi_gather` and `render.set_ssgi_gather { step }` (4 turns it on, 1 or
  2 clear it; error `ssgi_gather_bad_step`). No preset turns it on, and shipped frames are unchanged while it is off.
  (ADR-3964)
- New dynamic GI settings: `DdgiSettings::finest_headroom_m` (default `FINEST_HEADROOM_M`, 2 m), `front_weights`
  (a probe behind the plane of the point's surface counts a thousandth; every tier sets it) and `edge_visibility`
  (a probe past the edge of the point's surface must see it without doubt; Low and Medium set it), plus the constants
  `dynamic_gi::{HIGH_SPACING_M, HIGH_SPACING_RATIO, HIGH_LAYERS, HIGH_FINEST_HEADROOM_M}` and the read-back
  `DdgiReadback::crease_irradiance(slot, face, side)`. (ADR-5001, ADR-5290, ADR-5552, ADR-5566, ADR-5716)

#### ReSTIR and ray tracing

- Opt-in ReSTIR GI on hardware ray queries (`RenderProfile::restir_gi`, `render.restir_gi`, `render.set_restir_gi`). It
  falls back to the SDF probe tier, and temporal reservoirs now reject hits on objects that moved more than 0.05 m.
  (ADR-3349, ADR-4001)
- `regia_render::restir_di`: reservoir-resampled many-light direct lighting with CPU reference, GPU passes and a debug
  evaluation pass (`render.set_many_light_sampling`, `render.many_light_stats`); not yet hooked into the forward pass.
  (ADR-2470)
- Traced sun shadows (`sun_shadow_trace`) now include a terrain proxy (`regia_terrain::TraceProxy`), persistent
  visibility-buffer sets, TLAS refit and alpha-masked coverage casters. Unread sun cascades are skipped
  (`terrain.trace_proxy`, `terrain.set_trace_proxy`). (ADR-2310)
- Traced sun shadows (`sun_shadow_trace`) run an any-hit alpha test for masked casters on the ray-query path and on the
  software BVH walk: a masked caster stops the share of rays its stored coverage names (one half), opaque casters skip
  the test, and a rejected candidate does not hide a later triangle. `ShadowBvh::build_with_coverage` stores the
  coverage for the software walk, which now takes alpha-masked rigid casters like the ray-query build, and `render.hwrt`
  reports `sun_shadows.frame.masked_casters`. Frames without the trace are unchanged. (ADR-4557)

#### Lights and reflections

- IES LM-63 profiles (`LightProfile`, cooked `FIES` v1), `RectLight` and `TubeLight` area lights through GGX LTC tables,
  `LightSourceLtc` for sized point/spot lights, and path-tracer `PtLight::SphereSpot` (`render.set_light_profile`).
  (ADR-2592)
- Local reflection captures become level content: the `.fcap` bake, scene v13 `LevelLook::captures`, up to 32 captures
  with per-tier nearest-box selection, `regia bake-captures`, and the `render.save_local_captures` /
  `load_local_captures` / `get_capture_bake` methods. (ADR-2860)
- Opt-in `ProbeSpecular::LobeHue` (`"lobe_hue"`): the probe specular lobe keeps its hue and sees the image's ground
  plane, so a mirror aimed at the nadir under a blue sky no longer shows a red-to-yellow spot. Set it per source
  (`DdgiSettings::specular`, `BakedGiSettings::specular`) or on both with `render.set_probe_specular {"mode":
  "lobe_hue"}`; `render.get_probe_specular` reports `lobe_hue` and `hue_floor`, and `render.probe_specular_lobe` takes
  `cube`, `direction` and `mode`. `ProbeSpecular::Lobe` stays the default and its frames are unchanged. (ADR-2996)

#### Sky, fog, clouds and weather

- Aerial perspective inside interiors: the composite no longer adds sky in-scatter inside `InteriorVolume` boxes, and
  the rain veil skips them, with no pop at doors (`render.aerial_interiors`, `render.set_aerial_interiors`, on by
  default). (ADR-4757)
- Opt-in haze and rain extinction from the atmosphere tables (`RenderProfile::aerial_from_tables`, `TableAir`,
  `render.set_weather { aerial_from_tables }`), which shrinks the horizon seam. (ADR-3294)
- Opt-in calibrated fog sun shadow (`VolumetricFogSettings::sun_shadow: FogSunShadowRule::Calibrated`,
  `render.fog_shadow`), which keeps the air under a roof dark. (ADR-3122)
- Fibrous and crisp cirrus: strands, shear, breakup and hooks, plus a supersampled B-spline `crisp` sheet that
  cross-fades in smoothly. All are off by default and settable through `render.set_clouds` and `sky.ron` `cloud_look`.
  (ADR-1509, ADR-1516, ADR-4845)
- Wet and snowy grass blades follow the ground layer's weather response, and precipitation can render at `Half` /
  `Quarter` resolution (`PrecipResolution`, `render.set_precipitation { resolution }`). (ADR-3249)
- `FogVolume::interior(interior, density)`: fog that exists only inside an `InteriorVolume`, for a tunnel's dust or a
  cave's haze. (ADR-3257)
- With `CloudSettings::shadow_map` on, the air under a cloud layer takes the layer's shadow: the aerial perspective
  drops the sun's in-scatter along the shadowed stretches of each view ray, its multiple scattering follows the light on
  the ground under the layer, and the analytic haze loses its sun glow (its horizon sky term down to a floor of 0.12).
  The planet surface beyond the terrain, rain and snow veils, the image light's ground (with `overcast_ibl` too), the
  terrain's ground bounce and the GI probes' sun (Desktop High and Ultra) follow the cover, so a high view under a
  closed cover no longer washes out to blue-gray and trees and cliffs no longer take a sunlit ground's bounce. Frames
  with `shadow_map` off are byte-identical. New `AtmosphereLuts::aerial_sun`, `sample_aerial_sun`,
  `sample_aerial_under_clouds`, `cloud_air_span`, `cloud_shadow_on_ray` and `FrameRenderer::cloud_air_frames()`.
  (ADR-5944)

#### Shadows and AO

- Opt-in horizon-slice AO (`horizon_ao`) and angular sun penumbra (`angular_sun_penumbra`, shadow filter mode 4),
  calibrated against the path tracer (`render.set_contact_calibration`). (ADR-3202)

### World

- Procgen exposes `prefab_instance_guid` and `tiles::cell_scope` (ADR-4991).
- Prefab variants (`regia_scene::prefab_variant`, `.variant.ron`, format `prefab-variant` v1): a variant stores only its
  edits over a base, base changes propagate, chains cap at 16 and cycles are refused (ADR-2323)
- `StreamedOpen::AlwaysLoaded` and new open reasons (`stream_settings_invalid`, `stream_index_corrupt`,
  `stream_index_decode`, `stream_spawn_failed`, `stream_no_assets`)
- `TerrainMap::sample_spacing_z` for non-square grids (ADR-5083)

#### Terrain

- A level names its terrain: `LevelSettings::terrain` (scene format v12) points at the `.terrain.layers.ron`, its map
  and world mapping, and `regia_terrain::level_terrain` plus the opt-in `LevelTerrainPlugin` build it. The editor names
  side files after that map. (ADR-2411)
- Grass density brush with `<map>.grassdensity.bin` (v1) and exact undo, plus an editor Terrain panel (16-bit PNG and
  RAW heightmap export/import, density target, `.scatter.ron` rule editor). Agent `terrain.grass_density_*`,
  `terrain.*level_terrain`, `terrain.scatter_rules`, `terrain.set_scatter_rule`. (ADR-2411)
- Opt-in ORM blue channel as a layer's wet-frame porosity (`porosity_from_orm`, `.terrain.layers.ron` v5). Macro grids
  up to 16 x 16 tiles keep per-tile streaming residency (256-bit mask). (ADR-3367)
- Android tiled terrain: `regia cook --platform android` writes the same terrain tiles as desktop, as ASTC 4x4 LDR
  (sRGB albedo; UNORM normal, AO, height, ORM and splat masks), replacing the heightfield-only interim cook.
  `assets.terrain_tiles { asset }` reports tile counts and `bcn` or `astc` (codes `terrain_tiles_*`). (ADR-5294)
- Sampler-path LOD offset for the terrain's anisotropic layer taps: `regia_terrain::anisotropy_lod_offset_for(..)`
  returns 0.25 mips on Mesa's AMD Vulkan driver (RADV) and 0 elsewhere, `REGIA_TERRAIN_ANISO_LOD_OFFSET=<mips>` sets it
  on any adapter (0 turns it off), `TerrainGpu::set_anisotropy_lod_offset` sets it per draw, and `terrain.get` reports
  `anisotropy_lod_offset`. (ADR-5459)

#### Foliage and wind

- `FoliageSway::adopt(&LabMaterial)` makes imported trees and scatter meshes sway in the scene wind, shadows included.
  Painted instances can stay still (`PAINTED_FLAG_STILL`, `ScatterBrush::wind`), material graphs gain a `Wind` node, and
  agent `material.sway` / `material.set_sway` and `terrain.scatter_stroke { wind }` drive it; samples `terrain_lab
  --foliage-wind` and `showcase --foliage-wind`. (ADR-2304)
- Forest flythrough kit (`regia_terrain::scatter::forest`, opt-in and deterministic): `ScatterQuery` finds scatter
  instances in a box or along a segment from placement, with trunk capsules and crown spheroids; `corridor_report` /
  `carve_corridor` measure a camera path's clearance to trunks, crowns and ground and carve per-class `.scatter.ron`
  exclusions; `canopy_gap_map`; motion LOD lookahead for fast cameras (`ScatterField::set_motion_lod`, `flight_check`,
  `tune_fade_bands`); trees that sway in the camera's wake with matching motion vectors (`TrackWake`, `GustField`,
  `SwayField`); a `PopMeter`; and a scripted stand-in flythrough. `ForestFlightPlugin` adds agent
  `terrain.forest_query`, `terrain.forest_corridor`, `terrain.forest_carve`, `terrain.forest_flight` and
  `terrain.set_forest_flight`. With motion LOD off (the default) the emitted frame is unchanged. (ADR-1503)
- Per scatter class, opt-in: lower-detail leaf shadow casters (`ScatterField::set_class_caster_hint`,
  `ScatterCasterHint`, `terrain.set_scatter { class, class_caster }`), so far cascades lose triangles while near
  shadows keep their detail, and a season per instanced class (`set_class_season`, `ScatterSeason`,
  `terrain.set_scatter { class, class_season }`) with the same rule as `season::leaf_present` on the mesh path and in
  the impostor cull shader; a season change rewrites no instance. `terrain.scatter` reports caster triangles, leafless
  counts, `class_casters` and `class_seasons`. (ADR-3146)

#### Voxel far field

- Voxel far levels for placed meshes through `meshlet_bridge::VoxelFarSource`, drawn as sub-voxel boxes with sampled
  per-level box errors, so the switch moves nearer at the same silhouette error. `.fvox` is v4; agent
  `render.voxel_far_stats` and `assets.voxel_far` (boxes, `box_error`, `mean_opacity`, `coverage_at_switch`). (ADR-2655,
  ADR-3108, ADR-3227)
- Alpha-tested (`alphaMode: MASK`) far cook voxelizes only what the alpha test keeps and fits opacity to the drawn box,
  so leaf-card canopies keep their near coverage. New `synthetic_forest` benchmark (100 000 procedural trees).
  (ADR-3108, ADR-3227)

#### HLOD

- Baked HLOD proxy atlases (opt-in `hlod_atlas_px`, `hlod_atlas_max_px`): base color, normal and ORM baked at cook,
  compressed per platform, baked through alpha cutouts with coverage alpha, graph materials folded to constants. Agent
  `world.hlod_proxy`. (ADR-2483, ADR-2713)
- Terrain scatter reaches the HLOD tree as voxel proxies (`StreamAssets::scatter`, `.wvoxels` v1), and opt-in far proxy
  shadows per tier (`HlodProxyShadows`, `MeshShadowCast`). (ADR-3253)

#### Streaming

- Opt-in per-tile terrain collision (`TerrainTileCollision`), VRAM as a hard planner bound, and render-residency-aware
  `visible` (`StreamRenderResidency`). Agent `stream.bounds_set`, `stream.collision_tiles`. (ADR-2317)
- Net clients buffer binds for replicated level entities whose cell has not loaded yet (cap `MAX_BUFFERED_LEVEL_BINDS` =
  16,384); the streamed nav cook walks a terrain (`WithNavTerrain`); new cook warnings `stream_terrain_misaligned`,
  `stream_relevance_radius`. (ADR-2317)
- Replication relevance by loaded stream cell: while an enabled streamed level is open on a server, the `stream_cells`
  filter drops a replicated entity whose cell lies outside a peer's loaded cells plus a one-cell margin; entities with
  no cell stay relevant (`StreamCellRelevancePlugin`, agent method `stream.cell_relevance`). (ADR-4011)
- Streaming soak and budget counters: `StreamStats` high-water marks (`plan_cells_evaluated`, `peak_plan_cells`,
  `peak_inflight`, `peak_pending_bytes`, `peak_apply_entities`, `peak_loads_per_step`) in `stream.stats` and the
  `play.wait_until` / `play.expect` snapshot; `cargo xtask stream-bench --soak [--seed N]` runs a 6 km, 5,000-step soak
  headless; `stream-bench` JSON adds `p50_ms`, `p95_ms`, `settled_over_33ms`, `stream_share` and `worst_five`.
  (ADR-3265, ADR-2863)

#### Navigation

- Jump and drop arcs (`NavLinkArcConfig`, `NavLinkTraversal` message), off-mesh links streamed with their tiles (nav
  cell v2) with a mid-link cancel rule, and tile-cache grid growth (`NavTileCache::grow_to`). Agent
  `nav.link_traversals`, `nav.crowd_set_link_arcs`, `nav.tile_links`, `nav.tile_cache_grow`. (ADR-2589)
- Link reservation queues, lanes from road splines (`NavRoadNetwork::add_road`), per-triangle area paint, runtime
  link/lane files with the `NavLinkAnchor` component, and collider-aware `generate_level_links`, each with `nav.*` agent
  methods. (ADR-2420)
- `regia_core::math::{det_sin, det_sin_cos, det_atan2}`: software trig that gives the same bits on every target.
  (ADR-4769)

#### Procedural generation

- Parallel graph evaluation whose output equals the serial run (`Evaluator::with_parallelism`,
  `ProcGenSettings::threads`, `REGIA_PROCGEN_THREADS`, `procgen.run { threads }`, `procgen.profile`), and copy-on-write
  point columns. (ADR-2724, ADR-2731)
- `spawn.prefab` attribute overrides, checked at compile and applied by the world host. `wfc.solve` node with
  symmetries, tile classes, 3D vertical sockets, `seed`, `bounds` and `tileset` inputs. (ADR-2724, ADR-3027)
- Plant builds from the agent (`procgen.plant_build`) with mesh envelopes and an impostor billboard; nodes
  `plant.envelope_mesh`, `density.ground_layer`, `road.pieces`; `in.mask` reads PNG pixels and `in.ground` reads layers.
  (ADR-2731)
- More node settings: `graph.call graph_from`, `attr.lookup nearest_within`, `points.align keep_yaw`, `points.project`
  onto splines/polygons/boxes, `in.rays phys_material`, shape-exact `in.overlap`, mesh bounds for `in.entities`.
  (ADR-3027)
- Runtime: `OnLoad` generators run with their level behind `ProcGenLoadGate` (`procgen.load_report`), `PerTier` bakes,
  static colliders for `spawn.mesh` instances, and `regia package` strips `editor_only` generators. (ADR-2421)
- Editor and CLI: Path and Area tools with road and forest presets, `regia procgen bake` / `regia procgen verify`,
  tiles, volume and biome overlays, generate-around-view, and `procgen.*` lifecycle events on `events.read`. (ADR-2422,
  ADR-2907)
- Procedural evaluation fingerprint: `regia_procgen::fingerprint` digests caller-quantized instance records (node,
  instance, position in mm, yaw in millidegrees) to an order-independent 64-bit value, so two bakes compare without the
  world file; agent `procgen.fingerprint` (codes `procgen_fingerprint_bad_params`, `procgen_fingerprint_overflow`,
  cap 1,048,576 records). Not collision-resistant. (ADR-4593)
- `regia procgen bake <level> --prune` deletes, after a whole-level bake, the `.pgout` files no `Baked` generator of the
  level writes (`procgen verify` tags them `orphan_bake`). `filter.compare` `rhs_attr` compares against another
  attribute of the same element, `debug.log` takes `{attr:<sel>}`, and `cargo bench -p regia_procgen --bench graph`
  measures a 200-node compile and the 1 km ground pipeline. (ADR-5644)
- Anchored tiled generators report `tier_reach_order` when a finer tier builds farther than a coarser one, in the log
  and in `procgen.tiles` `warnings`. (ADR-5646)

#### Fluids

- Optional crate `regia_fluid` (feature `fluid`, off by default): deterministic PIC/FLIP solver, whitewater,
  marching-cubes surface, `.fluidcache` v1 bakes cooked into paks, presets, and a desktop GPU compute path (feature
  `gpu`). Agent `fluid.*` methods. (ADR-2441, ADR-2577)
- Moving solids (box, sphere, capsule) and two-way rigid coupling with `regia_physics` (`FluidPhysicsPlugin`,
  `FluidBody`); up to 16 moving solids on the GPU. (ADR-2577)
- `regia_fluid::sleep::cells_to_integrate` and read-only agent `fluid.sleep_mask` to skip still cells; the solver does
  not call it yet. (ADR-3949)

#### VFX

- `.vfx.ron` v7: scene wind on particles, event spawns and sub-emitters, mesh-surface and skinned-mesh spawn shapes,
  along-normal velocity, kill volumes and ribbons. Agent `vfx.systems`, `vfx.describe`, `vfx.spawn`, `vfx.set_param`,
  `vfx.spawn_probe`. (ADR-2642, ADR-2800)
- VFX Editor dock tab: emitter and module editing, curve and gradient editors, live preview, per-emitter cost table;
  agent `editor.vfx_open`, `editor.vfx_state`, `editor.vfx_edit`, `editor.vfx_preview`. (ADR-3171, proposed)
- Ambient life, opt-in (`regia_vfx::ambient`, `AmbientLifeSettings`, added by `VfxPlugin` and idle until enabled):
  pollen and dust motes that glint in the sun's shafts, gnat swarms, butterflies, fireflies at summer dusk, seasonal
  falling leaves, and bird flocks and soaring birds, driven by the scene's time of day, season, rain, wind and clouds;
  the Low tier and below draw nothing. `AmbientBirdCallsPlugin` moves a nearby one-shot bird call onto the nearest
  visible bird. Agent `render.ambient_life`, `render.set_ambient_life`, `render.set_ambient_birds`; showcase
  `--ambient-life`. Render-out jobs restart its clock, so two fresh apps deliver the same frames. Off, frames are
  bit-identical. (ADR-3024)
- Scene-wide VFX budget, off by default: `regia_vfx::significance` ranks every live effect instance by distance,
  projected size, visibility, priority and age under `SignificanceCaps` (live and full-rate instances, spawns per
  update, live particles, off-screen pausing with a bounded warm-up, hysteresis, dwell, per-system caps with a
  `StealPolicy`), with states `Full`, `Reduced`, `Paused` and `Culled`. `VfxScene::significance_mut`,
  `quality::significance_caps(QualityTier)` (design values per tier) and agent methods `vfx.significance` and
  `vfx.significance_set` come with it; a paused or culled instance with nothing live gives its GPU emitter run back.
  (ADR-3275)
- Crash blast front (`regia_vfx::crash`): one blast-front clock (`blast_front_radius`, `blast_arrival_seconds`,
  `blast_front_reached`, `blast_arrival_step`) drives the camera shake, a dust wall of `front_wall_<k>` ring emitters
  out to `CrashScale::wall_reach` (464 m at 250 t) that grows at 343 m/s and never leads the front, the blast wind
  (`CrashScale::blast_wind`, `BlastWind::speed_at`, `BlastGusts`), a gust line of `crash_gust_snow` puffs at
  `CrashSequence::gust_sites`, and grass that bends to the front (`GrassGust`, `TerrainGrassHandle::set_gust`,
  `BlastGustGrassPlugin`). `CrashBurst::contact_downrange` tilts the contact puff downrange of a slanting fall. Agent
  `vfx.blast_gust`. (ADR-1497)

#### 2D and tilemaps

- Isometric, staggered and hexagonal maps (`.tilemap.ron` v2), Wang corner sets and Tiled Wang and terrain import
  (`.autotile.ron` v2, cooked), multi-cell stamps, geometric auto-tile neighbors, Tiled objects in world space, and
  animated tiles in the editor pane. (ADR-2597, ADR-2714)
- `regia_2d::autotile::edge16_mask(north, east, south, west)`, the documented Edge16 index helper.

### Gameplay systems

- Physics iteration ceilings: `MAX_SOLVER_ITERATIONS` 64, `MAX_CCD_SUBSTEPS` 16, `MAX_STEP_FIXED_COUNT` 1024 and
  `MAX_QUEUED_DAMAGE` 65536 (ADR-4916).
- Fixed-wing aircraft on the physics world (`regia_physics::aero`): lifting surfaces, propeller and jet engines, gear,
  an offline trim solver, `.aircraft.ron` v1, `AircraftPlugin`, four `physics.aircraft*` agent methods and the
  `samples/flight_lab` sample (ADR-3374, proposed)
- Player data resilience: save slots keep `<slot>.prev` and load it when the current file is damaged; a damaged
  `settings.ron` or `input_overrides.ron` is moved to `.bad-<n>` instead of being overwritten (ADR-3102)
- Stable entity references in saves (`regia_framework::save_entity_refs`): every saved entity gets a save id, and an
  `Entity` inside a saved component (field, `Option`, enum, `Vec`, array or map value) is written as the target's save
  id and remapped on load, including level entities found again by `EntityGuid`. A pawn's target, carried items,
  vehicle riders and `ChildOf` hierarchies survive a save and a load. Agent `save.inspect { slot }`. (ADR-3239)

#### Physics: vehicles and collision

- Convex decomposition at glTF import (`FCHS` v1 hull sets), `.vehicle.ron` v1, hover craft (`regia_physics::hover`),
  and layer-aware vehicle rays; agent `physics.vehicle_config_check`, `physics.hovercraft`. (ADR-2301)

#### Physics: collision, CCD, materials, fields

- Continuous collision queries in `regia_physics::ccd`: `time_of_impact`, `time_of_impact_angular` and
  `time_of_impact_motion`, which sweeps any number of turns, plus `convex_distance`, `sphere_sweep` and
  `swept_box_vs_thin_wall`. Agent method `physics.time_of_impact` takes literal shapes or entities. (ADR-2248, ADR-2423)
- Opt-in `SweptCcd` component sweeps a dynamic body's step motion, rotation included, against kinematic and dynamic
  targets and bullet against bullet. It reports `hits`, `failures` and `last_toi`. (ADR-2423)
- `PhysicsMaterial` and the `.physmat.ron` v1 format: friction, restitution, density and combine rules, a
  `PhysicsMaterialRef` component and agent `physics.material_check` / `physics.material_register`. (ADR-2424)
- Physics debug draw of contacts, joints and centers of mass (`physics.debug_draw`), `JointBreakTorque` for joints that
  break by torque, and the headless `samples/physics_lab`. (ADR-2424)
- Gameplay force volumes (`ForceField`): directional, radial, vortex, gravity override, linear and angular drag, and
  impulse pads, in global, sphere, box or capsule volumes. `WindDrag` adds quadratic air drag from `SceneWind`; agent
  `physics.fields`, `physics.field_set`, `physics.field_probe`. (ADR-3348)
- Contact sounds from physics (`PhysicsAudioPlugin`, opt-in): impacts, scrape and roll loops, and break sounds, driven
  by the new `.impactbank.ron` v1 `ImpactBank`. Footstep surfaces can come from physics materials; agent
  `audio.impacts`, `audio.impact_probe`, `audio.impacts_set`. (ADR-3270)

#### Cloth

- Vertex-triangle self-collision now really runs, with garment layers (inner layers push outer ones), reaction torque on
  the carrying body, swept fast colliders and optional parallel stepping that gives the same bits as serial. Agent
  `physics.cloth_set_layers`; `physics.cloth_inspect` reports layers and self-crossings. (ADR-2425)
- Nine cloth presets (`ClothPreset::LightFabric` through `RopeHemp`), pin helpers `pin_edge` / `pin_by_map`, the
  optional `.cloth.ron` `preset` field, and agent `physics.cloth_presets`. (ADR-2439)
- A runaway guard (`ClothGuard`, on by default) rolls a blown step back to the last good step or the rest pose and emits
  `ClothFault` events. Stiff-link convergence keeps inextensible links within 1.02x; `ClothHealth` and agent
  `physics.cloth_health` report it. (ADR-3273, ADR-4846)
- Scene wind on cloth: the weather's `SceneWind` blows on every cloth placed through `ClothSim` (`ClothGluePlugin`),
  scaled by `ClothParams::wind_scale` (0..=10, default 1, optional in `.cloth.ron`); `physics.cloth_inspect` reports the
  scene wind and each solver's global wind. (ADR-2884)
- Editor Cloth panel: an inspector for `.cloth.ron` parameters, starting point, self-collision, wind model and the
  instance's blend and LOD distances; paint mode on the sim mesh with a map picker, a value ramp in meters and a
  simulate preview; and a `cloth` gizmo group (off by default). Agent `editor.cloth_panel_state`,
  `editor.cloth_panel_edit`, `editor.cloth_paint_stroke`; `regia_agent::cloth::edit_cloth_map` is the same code
  `physics.cloth_edit_map` runs. (ADR-2884)

#### Destruction and explosions

- `regia_framework::explosion`: one call pushes bodies, breaks joints, damages `Destruction`, plays effects and camera
  shake (feature `explosion-fx`) and gusts nearby cloth. Presets `grenade`, `barrel`, `bomb`, `shell`; agent
  `gameplay.explode`, `gameplay.explosion_stats`. (ADR-2430)
- Structural load in `.destructible.ron` v2: weight flows through bonds to supports, so a table that loses a leg
  collapses. Unsupported sections fall as one body and split on impact. `DestructionFxPlugin` with `.destructionfx.ron`
  v1 maps `fx_tag` to sound, VFX and decal. (ADR-2734)
- Impact damage: a car or heavy crate that hits an opted-in structure breaks it, server-side, with speed, impulse,
  cooldown and per-tick gates. Agent `physics.destruction_impact_damage` and `_set`. (ADR-3272)
- `DebrisBudget` caps secondary rigid debris per break and per frame; agent `physics.debris_budget`. (ADR-3929)
- `samples/destruction_lab` runs destruction level 2 end to end with a server and two clients. Agent
  `physics.destruction_playback` compares what two processes play. (ADR-2573)

#### Ragdolls

- Active ragdolls (`RagdollMode::Active`): per-bone PD motors follow the animated pose, hits weaken a body group,
  standing balance plants the feet, and a get-up clip is chosen by facing and pose distance. Agent `ragdoll.state`,
  `ragdoll.active_set`. (ADR-2498, ADR-2564)
- Opt-in recovery (`RagdollRecovery::full()`): capture-point stepping, landing from a drop, and feet that hold on moving
  crates and lifts. Also `RagdollShape::Sole`, `RagdollProfile::humanoid_active` and agent `ragdoll.push`. (ADR-2963)
- Ragdoll caps follow the quality tier: Mobile Low 2, Mobile High 4, Deck 6, Low 8, High 16, Ultra 24. (ADR-2424)

#### Vehicles

- Geared drivetrain: torque curve, gearbox with automatic or manual shifts, clutch, and open, locked or limited-slip
  differentials. Also anti-roll bars, aero, rolling resistance, ABS and traction control, agent
  `physics.vehicle_telemetry` / `physics.vehicle_shift`, and `samples/vehicle_lab`. (ADR-2598)
- Slip-angle tires (`TireModel::Slip`) with brush or Magic Formula curves, load sensitivity, a friction ellipse, static
  hold on slopes and load transfer at the contact. Tire transients add relaxation length, self-aligning torque, camber
  thrust and slip-weighted combined slip. (ADR-2719, ADR-3276)
- `VehicleFeedback` turns steering torque and wheel-load jolts into gamepad rumble; agent `physics.vehicle_tire_curve`
  plots a tire. (ADR-3276)
- Trailers: tow-ball or fifth-wheel couplings, a jackknife readout, follow or surge brakes, and road trains up to 8
  units. Includes the `.trailer.ron` v1 format, `TrailerPlugin` and agent `physics.trailers`, `trailer_couple`,
  `trailer_uncouple`, `trailer_config_check`. (ADR-3281, proposed)
- AI drivers (`VehicleDriver`, `VehicleAiPlugin`) follow lane-graph routes with a curvature speed plan, pure-pursuit
  steering, car following and stuck recovery. They drive through the same physics as the player. Agent
  `vehicle_ai.drive_to`, `status`, `set_params`, `stop`. (ADR-3117)
- Vehicle audio (`.vehicleaudio.ron` v1, `VehicleAudioPlugin`): engine layers by rpm and load, tire squeal, surface
  rolling noise, bumps, wind and shifts, with a voice budget for traffic. Placeholder sounds are included. (ADR-3271)
- Wheel surface effects (`VehicleFxPlugin`): tire smoke, dust, spray, skid strips and ruts; agent `vfx.vehicle_fx`.
  (ADR-3274)
- A standalone two-wheel lean model (`regia_physics::vehicle_bike`) with agent `physics.bike_step` /
  `physics.bike_state` and `samples/bike_lab`. (ADR-4017)
- Skid-steer tracks: `regia_physics::vehicle_track` is a planar two-track body (center speed the tracks' average, yaw
  rate their difference over a 2 m separation, 8 m/s full throttle, a 4 m/s² friction cap per step, 1 m/s² coast drag)
  with a closed-form yaw check and a run digest. Agent `physics.track_step` and `physics.track_state`; non-finite input
  answers `track_bad_input`. (ADR-4629)

#### Character and traversal

- `regia_framework::traversal`: swim, ladders, mantle and vault for a character with `TraversalSettings`. A nav bridge
  lets AI crowd agents use ladders and jump points, and the state is predicted and replicated over the network. Agent
  `physics.character_traversal` and `_probe`. (ADR-2971)
- `CharacterTraversal::request_in(order, origin)` queues a traversal order whose points are in the local frame of the
  given `OriginOffset`, for orders issued after a floating-origin rebase or before a character's first step. (ADR-5573)

#### Animation and motion matching

- Motion-matching databases cook with `regia cook` and load as assets. Motion matching can drive the `Animator`, and the
  editor gets a Motion Matching panel. Clips with NaN or infinite keys fail the cook with `clip_non_finite`. (ADR-2409)
- Foot contact labels and gait phase, foot locks for foot IK, a trajectory predictor (`MotionMatchCharacter` drives it
  from `CharacterInput`) and montage-scoped matching; agent `anim.mm_predict`. (ADR-2858)
- BVH motion-capture import: `regia cook` turns `.bvh` files into a skeleton and clip, with optional retargeting. Agent
  `editor.bvh_inspect`. (ADR-2532, proposed)
- Library modules without runtime wiring: `regia_animation::clip_compress`, an object-space-error clip compressor, and
  `regia_animation::pose_search`, an offline pose-search database. (ADR-4869)

#### AI

- Opt-in `PerceptionPlugin` (sight, hearing and damage memory, `Attitudes` team table), `UtilityPlugin` (hot-reloaded
  `.utility.ron`, now frozen at v1) and `GoapPlugin` (async planning that lands on a fixed step). Agent
  `ai.perception_get`, `ai.utility_explain`, `ai.goap_plan` and more. (ADR-2565, ADR-2336)
- Squads with leader re-election, attack tokens, formation slots, an `AiTickBudget` with distance tiers, and custom
  spatial-query generators and tests (`.spatialquery.ron` v2). (ADR-2709)
- Editor Behavior Tree panel and node-view State Tree Editor, both with live status, validation and undo. Running agents
  follow `.statetree.ron` reloads, and agent `ai.bt_*`, `ai.statetree_*`, `editor.bt_*` and `editor.statetree_*` methods
  are added.
- Reactive behavior trees (`.bt.ron` v2): `compare` nodes test a blackboard key against a constant or a second key
  (`eq`, `ne`, `lt`, `le`, `gt`, `ge`, `is_set`, `in_range` on bool, int, float, tag and entity); observer aborts
  (`none`, `self`, `lower_priority`, `both`) re-check a node when one of its keys changes and reset the running branch
  (span `bt_observer_dispatch`); a `state_tree` node runs a `.statetree.ron`, and a `bt:<file>.bt.ron` state-tree task
  runs a behavior tree (nesting stops at four, `bridge_cycle`). `ai.bt_edit`, `ai.bt_get` and the Behavior Tree panel
  palette carry the new nodes. (ADR-4543)
- `smartobject.release { handle, agent }` releases only that agent's claim; another agent gets `F0002` with reason
  `not_holder`. (ADR-5636)

#### Scripting

- Lua script components: `regia.component(name, { props, on_update })` declares typed per-entity properties that the
  editor inspector edits and the scene saves. Hot reload reconciles stored values; agent `lua.components` and
  `lua.props.*`. (ADR-3356)
- `ScriptCtx::physics()` returns `WorldQueries` (`raycast`, `raycast_all`, `shapecast`, `overlap`, `closest_point`)
  over the world's `PhysicsWorld`, filtered by the `PhysicsLayers` query matrix when present; `None` without physics.
  (ADR-5613)

#### Templates and samples

- Third Person template: procedural body with foot placement, collectibles, save flow, sword combo, enemies, death and
  respawn, title and settings. Top Down gets a navmesh enemy, First Person gets interaction and quicksave, and each
  ships an agent smoke test. (ADR-2559, ADR-2738)
- `samples/third_person` is a playable combat slice: melee and ranged enemies on behavior trees and a navmesh crowd,
  combos, ragdoll deaths, checkpoints, menus and procedural sound. (ADR-2596)
- The `net_game` sample and `net-game` template have a HUD (`src/hud.rs`): a scoreboard on Tab, a minimap, a radar,
  world markers, a bracket on the nearest player and, with lobbies, a lobby list. (ADR-2958)

#### AI and physics

- Sight hysteresis: `Perception::sight_lose_radius` and `sight_lose_half_angle` keep a remembered target until it leaves
  a wider lose cone; agent method `ai.perception_hysteresis` refuses a lose cone smaller than the acquire cone with
  `perception_bad_hysteresis`. (ADR-4586)

#### Determinism

- Digest traces: `nav_world_lab --trace` and `crowd_lab --trace` write one `section step digest` line per fixed step so
  two hosts' first divergence is named; `nav_world_lab` feature `cross-platform-determinism` forwards to the physics
  cross-platform feature. (ADR-5128)
- `regia_framework::abilities::abilities_state_hash_keyed(world, key_of)` hashes ability state with a host key (a spawn
  index, a save slot) for entities without an `EntityGuid`, so an unrelated spawn no longer moves the hash;
  `abilities_state_hash` is unchanged. (ADR-5960)

### Networking and Steam

- Networked rigid bodies: `ReplicatedBody` and `NetBodyPlugin` replicate crates and thrown objects as kinematic proxies,
  with no traffic for a body at rest; agent `net.bodies`. (ADR-2902)
- Networked vehicles: `NetVehiclePlugin` with owner prediction in a private world, correction smoothing, seats that hand
  over `NetOwner`, and `Vehicle::snapshot`/`restore`; format `.vehicle-snapshot.ron` v1, agent `net.vehicles`.
  (ADR-3080)
- Lag compensation can pose bone-attached hit shapes at the exact server tick (`exact_tick_joints`, off by default), and
  `NetAnimationPlugin` animates remote characters from replicated state. Agent `lagcomp.rewound_shapes`,
  `net.anim_state`. (ADR-2551)
- Server bots fill matches and cover dropped players during reconnect grace (`NetBotsPlugin`, `ServerBotAiPlugin`), with
  difficulty settings, `[bots]` in `server.toml` and `--bots N`; agent `net.bots`. (ADR-3346, ADR-4854)
- Destruction over the network handles snapshots of any length, instances past 64 KiB, per-peer relevance anchors and
  `snapshot_part_bytes` for slow links; `DestructionDrawPlugin` draws cooked chunk meshes once a structure breaks.
  (ADR-2305)
- `NetSet::Gameplay` between `NetSet::Apply` and `NetSet::Predict` for systems this tick's prediction must see
- `spawn_kind_of` and `ReplicationRegistry::debug_value_applied` for client copies; injected session clocks
  (`set_unix_now_s` / `now_unix_s` on `SessionServer`, `NetServer`, `NetSession`) (ADR-4772)

#### Transport and security

- Trust on first use for server keys: `NetLink` clients (`LinkOptions::known_hosts`, `host_id`) and `session.connect`
  pin a host's key in `user://known_hosts.ron`, and a changed key ends with `net_host_key_changed` before any password
  leaves the client. Agent `net.known_hosts`, `net.trust`, `net.forget`; `allow_unpinned` skips the check for LAN
  development. (ADR-2416, ADR-3283)
- Session rekeying (`RekeyPolicy`, about 2^20 records or 1 GiB per key by default) and NAT rebinding of sealed peers, so
  a client whose NAT rebinds keeps its session. (ADR-2416)
- A listen host keeps a persistent key in `user://listen-server.key` (`NetSession::set_listen_identity_path`), so
  returning players are not locked out after a restart. (ADR-4936)
- Exact-address bans next to IPv6 /64 block bans: `Session::ban_exact`, `NetServer::ban_ip_exact`,
  `NetLink::refuse_ip_exact`; a `bans.ron` entry spelled `addr/128` is exact on both ports. `regia_net::addr_block`
  exposes the block rule. (ADR-3283, ADR-4936)
- A server-wide pre-proof answer cap, `ServerConfig::pre_proof_answers_per_s` (default 512), stops a spoofed-address
  flood from turning the server into a packet reflector; counter `pre_proof_shed`. (ADR-4936)
- Optional QUIC datagram socket behind the `net-quic` feature (off by default, quinn 0.11.12), with reliability still in
  `regia_net`; agent `net.quic_status`. (ADR-3970)

#### Replication

- `#[derive(NetMessage)]` writes the message impl, direction marker and field codec from `#[net(...)]` attributes;
  messages carry a stable id and `VERSION`. (ADR-2415)
- Replicated `Handle<T>` fields, in components and in net messages, travel as the asset's guid or path and resolve on
  each receiver; meshes, images, textures and materials loaded through `AssetServer` register on their own. Agent
  `net.assets` lists misses by code. (ADR-2415, ADR-2665)
- Opt-in entropy coding of state frames (`replication.entropy: Range`, `[net] entropy` in `server.toml`), negotiated per
  connection at protocol 3; agent `net.entropy_stats`. Saves about 2 to 9 % of state-frame bytes on the harness
  workloads. (ADR-3315)
- `net_relevance` gizmo group (off by default) shows each entity's relevance per connection; agent
  `net.relevance_gizmos`. (ADR-2665)
- Position grid (`position_step_m`, `PositionQuantizer`) and per-entity reconcile ticks on the state channel, so a frame
  the server skipped no longer causes a false correction. (ADR-2413)
- `NetPriorityScale`, a per-entity factor on the relevance priority gain. (ADR-2902)
- Net replay: a server records what a client would have received (`regia_net::recorder`, `start_recording` /
  `stop_recording`, a view of everything or of one player's held set) to a `.netdemo` file, or keeps the last N seconds
  in a ring (`start_ring`, `export_ring`) for kill-cams and instant replays. Real clients receive the same bytes with
  it and without it. `regia_framework::net_replay` (feature `net`, in `DefaultPlugins`) plays a file with no server
  through the normal client path: play, pause, silent seek, speed 1/16 to 16, free or follow camera. Agent
  `net.replay_*` methods, guide topic `net-replay`. (ADR-3021)

#### Sessions and dedicated servers

- `GameRules` hears remote players (`on_player_joined`, `on_player_left`), a leaving peer's entities are handed on
  (`NetSuccessor`, agent `net.handoffs`), and per-player ping and loss are a resource, agent `net.player_stats` and an
  in-game overlay. (ADR-2413)
- Replication games run under the dedicated-server host (`HostedLink`), with join codes (`--relay local[:PORT]`, console
  `joincode`), kick and ban of link peers from the console and agent, and `--shared-port` to put link and session on one
  port. (ADR-2440, ADR-2665)
- Dedicated servers keep their world across restarts (`[persistence]` in `server.toml`, autosave, atomic saves,
  corrupt-save fallback) and per-player state (`App::register_player_persistent::<T>()`); console `save`/`restore`,
  agent `server.save_now`, `server.persistence_status`, `server.restore`. (ADR-3354)
- A native fault in a Linux server exits 70. (ADR-2440)
- `cargo xtask net-match` plays the `net-game` template across machines under stated conditions and writes
  `report.json`/`report.md`; conditioner specs (`ConditionerConfig::parse_spec`, `--conditioner SPEC --seed N`,
  `net.conditioner_set { spec }`). (ADR-3075)
- Dedicated server: `--upgrade-config` rewrites an older `server.toml` to the current `format_version` (comments kept)
  and exits; the console's `players` prints each player's ping and loss, and `status` adds one line per player
  (`NetSession::peer_link_stats`). (ADR-5650)

#### Content delivery and mods

- Signed content manifests (Ed25519 `trusted_keys` in `Regia.toml`), `regia assets keygen`/`sign`, agent
  `content.update`, the `regia assets chunks --max-size` budget gate and an `https://` shared cook cache. (ADR-2710)
- `mods://` mounts mod folders, `.fpak` archives and in-memory maps in load order with confinement checks, a content-set
  hash for lobby matching, and a per-mod Lua entity cap (default 4,096); agent `mods.*`, `lua.entity_cap`. (ADR-2418)

#### Steam

- Steam Workshop upload, subscribe and unsubscribe (`SteamWorkshopPlugin`); installed items mount at `mods://` and the
  owned lobby's `mods.content_set` follows them. Agent `steam.workshop_*`. (ADR-2418)
- Leaderboards through `regia_framework::leaderboard` (local, unavailable or Steam service), app and DLC ownership
  (`Steam::owns_app`, `dlc_installed`,...) and game-recording timeline markers; agent `steam.leaderboard_*`,
  `steam.owns_app`, `steam.timeline*`. (ADR-2340)
- The `net-game` template follows a Steam id launch through `LobbySteamTransport`, with a two-account real-client test.
  (ADR-3169)
- `cargo xtask steam-demo-upload --dry-run` stages a packaged demo into the steamcmd layout with VDF and store-page
  templates in `steamworks/`; it never logs in or runs steamcmd. (ADR-4899)
- `regia_steam::real_run` turns the two-account Steam acceptance into a step machine with leave/reconnect, a lobby-data
  content check, per-account Remote Storage round trips, per-endpoint evidence logs and a `steam_real_run_verdict`
  example (`PASS`, `NOT RUN`, `INCOMPLETE`, `FAIL`). (ADR-4896)
- Template match over Steam: `net_game::steam_leg::SteamLegEndpoint` (feature `steam`; `steamworks` links the real
  client) plays the `net-game` template over a friends-only Steam lobby and sockets, and
  `regia_steam::real_run_net::verify_net_leg` judges the two endpoint logs (connected guest, at least 300 server
  datagrams, matching app id, commit and lobby, at most 20 corrections and 2 resyncs per client). The owner session
  script `docs/evidence/steam/2026-11-10/steam-owner-session.sh` and a 30-minute checklist in
  `book/user-guide/steam-real-run.md` § 4a come with it. (ADR-5375)

#### Voice chat

- Microphone processing: echo cancellation (128 ms tail), `light` noise suppression and automatic gain, all off by
  default; agent `voice_chat.processing`. Unknown `voice_chat` user-settings keys round-trip. (ADR-2608)

#### Play-in-editor networking

- One ECS world and one view per play-in-editor client, with a client picker and key focus in the Net tab; agent
  `editor.pie_net_clients`, `pie_net_entities`, `pie_net_focus`, `pie_net_key`. (ADR-2623)
- Per-client conditioner presets in play-in-editor, agent `editor.pie_net_client_conditioner`. (ADR-3996)
- Play-in-editor with separate processes: with **Separate processes** checked in the Net tab, Play runs a dedicated
  server process and N client processes (or N clients joined to the editor's listen session) on real UDP ports, started
  the way `regia server --clients n` starts them; **Windowed clients** gives each its own window. The open level goes to
  a play copy, child output goes to the Output Log with `[server]`/`[client k]` prefixes, and Stop or the editor ending
  kills the children (Linux parent-death signal, Windows job object). Agent `editor.pie_processes`, and
  `separate_processes`/`windowed` on `editor.pie_net_config`, `editor.pie_net_run` and `editor.play`; codes
  `pie_process_*`. (ADR-2682)

#### Networking on mobile and Windows

- `NetSession::resume()`/`resume_on()` reconnect a suspended client with its reconnect token; `NetSessionPlugin`
  suspends and resumes from the `Lifecycle` schedule.

### Platforms

- Android writes default outputs to app-private directories (`regia_platform::paths::cache_dir`). Windows adds
  `open_file_identity` for the crash uploader.

#### Android: frame pacing and schedules

- `pacing.trace` returns the display pacer's last frames (up to 1024) with medians, fps, tick gaps, missed ticks, the
  software cap's sleep and a `summary.breakdown` into `work`/`swapchain`/`wait`/`cap`/`loop` with a `limit` that names
  what held frames back. `log: true` mirrors rows to logcat (target `regia_pacing`). (ADR-2320, ADR-2339, ADR-4913)
- New pacing controls: `PacingCost` (`Work` default, `Frame`), `PacingHint` (`Double` default, `Slot`, `Off`), slot
  probes, and a simulator with swapchain images, GPU/latch time and clock `stretch`
  (`pacing.set`/`pacing.get`/`pacing.simulate`). (ADR-2320, ADR-2339)
- `regia_app::ScheduleExecutor` (`MultiThreaded`, `SingleThreaded`, `platform_default()`), `App::set_schedule_executor`,
  and agent methods `executor.get`/`executor.set`. (ADR-4933, proposed)
- `PacingModel::set_queue_drain`, `PacingStats::drains` and `pacing.get` `drains` (ADR-5564), and
  `regia_render::pipeline_cache::save_installed_in_background`, which saves the installed pipeline cache on a
  short-lived `regia-pcache-save` thread (ADR-5857).
- Wake-ahead for display-paced frames (`regia_platform::frame_pacing::FrameWakeAhead`, on by default, 2.5 ms): the
  frame loop sleeps until shortly before its release tick and then polls for it. Agent `pacing.set` `wake_ahead_ms`
  (0..=4, 0 or null off) and `pacing.get` `wake_ahead_ms`, `wake_ahead_polled`, `wake_ahead_predicted`. (ADR-5461)
- Android performance hint session (`FramePerfHint`, off by default): `APerformanceHint` (API 33+) gets the slot as
  target and each paced frame's work as actual duration; `pacing.set` `perf_hint`, `pacing.get` `perf_hint`.
  `pacing.trace` rows gain `tick_delivered_ms` and `woke_ms`, so a late start splits into delivery, hand-off and event
  loop. (ADR-5461)
- Paced frames start on the vsync tick grid: `FrameSteadyStart` (`grid`, `grid_follow`, `start_offset_ns`, `drain_run`,
  `grid_releases`) and `DisplayPacer::set_grid_release`, `set_grid_follow`, `set_start_offset`, `set_drain_run`.
  `pacing.set` takes `release` (`grid` | `tick`), `grid_follow`, `start_offset_ms` (-8..=8) and `drain_run` (1..=1024);
  `pacing.get` reports `steady_start` and `grid_moves`; `pacing.trace` rows gain the frame loop thread's scheduler
  counters on Android (`work_run_delay_ms`, `wait_run_delay_ms`, `preemptions`, `wake_cpu`) plus `release` and
  `sleep_late_ms`. (ADR-5946)

#### Android: accessibility

- TalkBack adapter on Android (`accesskit_android` 0.7.5) behind the existing `accesskit` feature; actions route through
  the desktop `BridgeHandler`, and `ui.a11y_platform_tree`/`ui.a11y_platform_connect` report an `android` status object.
  (ADR-3454, amends ADR-1998)
- `SystemTextScale` follows Android's font size setting, labels fold in descriptions for TalkBack, an update gate
  resyncs the full tree when accessibility comes back, and the First Person template gains a touch controls screen
  (`ControlsLayout::Touch`, Android default). (ADR-4932)

#### Platform services

- `platform.power_state` and `regia_platform::power_state()` report `battery`/`ac`/`unknown`, percent and `low_power`
  (below 20 %); Linux reads sysfs, Android reads the battery intent. (ADR-3997)
