- `regia_platform::process_memory()` reports resident and peak resident bytes on Linux, Android and Windows. (ADR-3288)
- `renderer.minspec_explain` says why optional features are off and which limit clamps apply for `pascal` or `mobile`.

#### Steam Deck

- `RenderProfilePreset::runtime_default()`, `runtime_default_for(bool)` and `regia_render::is_steam_deck_model()` pick
  `Deck` on a Steam Deck (`SteamDeck=1` or a `Jupiter`/`Galileo` board); `render.get_profile` reports `runtime_default`.
  (ADR-3289)
- stream_world's teleport bench reports VRAM budget, per-cell and resident VRAM, GPU allocated/reserved bytes and RSS,
  the inputs for a future streaming VRAM bound. (ADR-2952)
- Controller layouts (`regia_steam::deck_layout`): `layout_sheet` and `layout_sheets` give the rows of a "Controls"
  screen, one per bound gamepad control with the family's label, texture key and actions, and `check_layout` reports
  `no_gamepad_binding` (error), `reserved_control`, `control_conflict` and `missing_glyphs`. Agent `steam.layout`, CLI
  `regia steam layout ... [--check]`, and `regia package --steam` warns about each action a controller cannot reach.
  Glyphs follow the controller type Steam Input reports (`SteamBackend::input_controller_type`,
  `follow_steam_input_glyphs`), so a DualSense through Steam Input on a Deck shows PlayStation glyphs. (ADR-1332)
- `vfx_lab --deck-check --preset NAME [--panel lcd|oled]` measures one performance preset, `--deck-presets` measures all
  four in separate processes with a cool-down, and `--deck-validate DIR` judges folders copied back from a Deck; each
  preset gets pass / warn / fail with stable codes in `deck_presets.json` and `deck_presets.md`, and a fail exits 1.
  (ADR-1332)

#### Windows

- Template game executables embed an icon and VERSIONINFO through `winresource`; `regia package --platform windows`
  checks the PE resources, and `regia upgrade-project` adds the build script to older projects. (ADR-2645)
- `cargo xtask dist --target windows` writes `run-<bin>.cmd` launchers and ships DLLs (`--dlls`) and DXC (`--dxc`) for
  Direct3D 12. (ADR-2055)
- `play.hitch_report` names the call sites of blocking GPU polls (`blocking_poll_sites`). (ADR-4050)

#### Packaging and CLI

- `docs/release/play-internal-testing-asset-packs.md`: the Play internal-testing check of Android asset packs, with no
  key material in the repository. (ADR-7696)
- `regia clean [--cache|--all] [--dry-run]`, Android shipping symbols (`dist/android/symbols/`, `symbols.json`), Linux
  AppImage output, and a validated per-platform `render_profile` written to `build_info.json`. (ADR-2631)
- `regia package` writes a deterministic `sbom.json` (Cargo package names, versions, licence labels) into every package
  kind. (ADR-3935)
- Startup playback in dev and shipping builds: `--play-input PATH` or `REGIA_PLAY_INPUT` plays a `.frec` recording at
  start (a missing file warns and the game continues), `REGIA_SMOKE_SECONDS` exits cleanly that many seconds after
  playback completes, and `REGIA_JSON_LOG` writes the JSON-lines log. On Android the same switch reads
  `files/regia-play-input.frec` when `files/regia-play-input.on` exists. `cargo xtask package-smoke` packages dev and
  shipping builds, plays a scripted recording for 60 s and fails on an `ERROR` log record, a missing playback line, a
  dirty `summary.json` or a shipping APK of 150 MB or more (`--android`, `--android-emulator`, `--windows`,
  `--dry-run`). (ADR-3968)
- Every `regia new` template defines `[profile.shipping]` (fat LTO, one codegen unit, abort, line tables), and
  `regia clean --json` prints the plan as one JSON object. (ADR-5627)
- `cargo xtask dist --notices FILE` ships `FILE` as `licenses/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`, and `--no-default-features` /
  `--features LIST` pass through to the cargo build. (ADR-5419)

#### GPU-specific paths

- Terrain anisotropic footprint path for drivers that drop sampler anisotropy on array textures, `terrain.get`
  `anisotropy_path`, and `REGIA_TERRAIN_ANISO_FOOTPRINT=1|0`. (ADR-3957)
- Material sampler calibration: at pipeline build a one-dispatch probe (cached per device) measures how the device's
  anisotropic sampler blends between mips, and a sharper-than-trilinear blend becomes a mip offset added to every
  material sample's bias; `render.get_capabilities` reports `material_anisotropy_lod_offset`, and
  `REGIA_MATERIAL_ANISO_LOD_OFFSET=<mips>` (0..=0.5, `0` off) replaces it. The terrain's sampler-path offset is measured
  per device the same way (`terrain.get` `anisotropy_lod_offset`), with a listed value for Mesa RADV and ARM's Mali
  driver where the probe cannot run. (ADR-5670, ADR-5797)

#### XR

- XR walk mode (`XrWalkPlugin`): smooth locomotion, arc teleport, ground follow, seated height calibration, comfort
  presets and a per-refresh render budget; agent `xr.walk_start`/`xr.walk_set` and showcase `--xr
  simulator|openxr|auto`. Runs on the Simulator only. (ADR-2457)

#### Product viewer

- `regia_viewer`: a lean forward renderer (uniform buffers and textures only) for native, WebGPU and WebGL2, with the
  `.renv` v1 baked environment and `viewer.*` agent methods. (ADR-2539)
- `regia_looks`: low-cost product looks (metals, anodized metals, stones, opal, cabochon, frosted) on WebGL2 limits, a
  stand-in material library and `looks.*` agent methods; step facets and blasted metal grain follow. (ADR-2538,
  ADR-2587)
- `regia_viewer_view` (shared drag view clamped to the recipe's `view_range`), `regia_viewer_pack` with the
  `viewer-package` v1 format, and CLI `regia viewer pack|verify|standin`. (ADR-2540)
- `regia_web` web player (`RegiaViewer` JS class, WebGPU with WebGL2 fallback, idle at zero draws) and `cargo xtask
  web-player` with size reports against a 2 MB target. (ADR-2541)
- `samples/product_viewer`: the viewer as a sideloaded Android app and desktop window, with a pipeline cache between
  runs and launch extras to force GLES or MSAA off. (ADR-2533, ADR-2835)
- `regia_configurator`: four JSON contract formats with a JSON Schema 2020-12 validator and contract checks, plus the
  runtime configurator (`resolve()`, picks, build) and `configurator.*` agent methods. (ADR-2535, ADR-2534, ADR-2693)
- Product capture: `.render_job.ron` v4 `product` block, `regia render --job`, deterministic `step`/`frameHash` in the
  web player and `viewer_app.step` on Android. (ADR-2757, ADR-3369)
- The web player's `stats().problems` reports `warm_set_miss` when a pipeline is created after an opened product's
  first drawn frame. (ADR-5637)

#### Android budgets

- `play.hitch_report` gains `work` (per-frame max of CPU work and GPU time: p50/p99/longest, CPU p50/p99), and the
  Android budget gains a `presented_p99` row (presented interval p99 vs the measured refresh interval, limit 1.05 via
  `presented_p99_refresh_ratio`). (ADR-4947)

### Tools, editor and pipeline

- Headless editor: `regia-editor --headless [--no-gpu] [--exit-on-stdin-eof]` and `regia editor [PROJECT_DIR]...` run
  the edit world, live cooker, hot-reload host and agent server with no window, printing `ready`/`exit` JSON lines. New
  methods `editor.headless_status`, `editor.open_level`, `agent.shutdown`. (ADR-2412, ADR-2821)
- Editor baseline: typed Preferences, persistent and editable keymap (import/export, conflicts), dock layout saved per
  project and per user (floating windows included), and a Project Settings page with platform targets, `render_profile`,
  and an input-map editor for actions, bindings, context priority, modifiers and triggers. Every page has
  `editor.prefs_*`, `editor.keymap_*`, `editor.layout_*` and `editor.project_*` agent twins. (ADR-2321, ADR-2771)
- View ▸ Diagnostics: GPU breadcrumb counters with "Load last crash bundle", and a `.regiatrace` timeline with Record
  (`editor.diagnostics`, `editor.trace_timeline`, `editor.trace_record`). (ADR-2771)
- Viewport tools: vertex snap (V), drop to floor with align to surface (End), collider handles (C), a World Settings
  panel for `LevelSettings`, clickable `path:line[:col]` links in the Output Log, and the `code.editor_command`
  preference. Fly and outliner keys are rebindable palette commands. (ADR-2696)
- Inspector multi-select editing: shared values show, differing ones show an em dash, single-axis edits keep other axes,
  "on k of n" components stay editable, and one edit is one undo. `editor.inspect_selection` and `entities: [ids]` on
  `editor.set_property` / `editor.set_transform`; prefab Revert/Apply act on the instance. (ADR-3948)
- Terrain scatter brush class picker and density slider (`editor.terrain_scatter_brush`), and off-screen culling in the
  procedural panel (`editor.procgen_panel_culling`). (ADR-2786)
- Entity kinds and the project map: declared kinds (`EntityKind`, `App::register_kind`, `.kind.ron` v1), derived roles,
  Outliner icons, `kind:` search and Group by category, and `[kinds] strictness` linted by the new `regia preflight` and
  by `regia cook`. `regia describe [--live]` and `project.describe` write `project-map.md`/`.json`; new methods
  `kinds.list`, `kinds.of`, `kinds.lint`. (ADR-2488)
- Content Browser provenance badges (`hand`, `agent`, `gen`, `import`, review state, policy findings) and agent methods
  `editor.asset_provenance`, `editor.asset_repair_metas`, `editor.asset_subasset_changes`. (ADR-2428)
- `regia_core::ron_pretty()` is the one pretty-RON writer, so RON output uses `\n` line endings on every host
  (ADR-4922).
- In-editor profiler: `.rprof.json` captures (`profile-capture` v1), a flame view and Capture button (View -> Profiler),
  and agent methods `profiler.set_capture`, `profiler.frame`, `profiler.capture` (ADR-2323, ADR-1518)
- Editor prefab-variant editing (`editor.create_prefab_variant`, `editor.prefab_variant`, `editor.set_variant_override`)
- `ship_check::check_windows_subsystem`, run by `regia package --platform windows` on shipping packages (ADR-2306)
- `regia_asset_pipeline::gltf_anim::skin_slot_joints(document, skin)` exposes the importer's skin-slot to joint map.
- Repository checks: `cargo xtask loc-length` (pseudo-localized Fluent length budgets), `cargo xtask check-provenance`
  (row-level source classes incl. `licensed-vendor-sdk` with an approval ADR), `cargo xtask release-ledger` (v0.5.0
  evidence ledger, `--strict`, `--json`) and `cargo xtask android-cfg` (Android-only source scan and CLI/template
  aarch64 check). (ADR-4564, ADR-4581, ADR-4931, ADR-4935)
- Conversation panel: a `.conv.ron` drawn as a graph (line blocks, `If`, `Choice` and `End` nodes, arrows for every
  jump, branch and option) with a stable automatic layout, node positions kept in an editor-side sidecar, zoom, pan,
  fit and search. Every edit is undoable and writes the canonical form (an unedited file saves byte for byte),
  validator findings show on the nodes, a file that does not parse opens read-only with its error, and a preview steps
  lines and choices with a mocked condition table and voice playback. Agent
  `editor.conversation_open|graph|edit|save|preview_step` (`book/agents/conversation-editor.md`). (ADR-3298)
- `cargo xtask walkthrough` follows the book's getting-started path as a stranger would (a temporary home, no `REGIA_*`
  variables, no repository on `PATH`): it packages an engine release archive, then runs `regia engine verify`, `regia
  doctor` and, for every template, `regia new`, a 5-frame headless smoke, `regia cook`, `regia package --platform
  linux`, the package from another directory, `regia upgrade-project` and the Android `cargo ndk` check, and writes
  `target/walkthrough/report.json` (nightly job `licensee-walkthrough`). Every project `regia new` writes, and the
  `net_game` sample client, accept `--exit-after-frames N`; template mains return `std::process::ExitCode` from the
  app's `AppExit`, so `AppExit::Error` exits non-zero. (ADR-2954)
- Dynamic extensions: the stamp is read from the exported `REGIA_EXTENSION_STAMP` symbol by bounds-checked ELF64, PE32+
  and Mach-O 64 readers, so a record planted elsewhere in the file no longer counts; the dynamic-`std` check reads the
  editor executable's needed libraries on Windows and macOS; warm discovery caches parsed manifests in
  `<project>/.regia/extension-discovery.json`; and `AssetServer::load_content` reaches extension loaders through
  `ContentLoaderHook`. (ADR-2844)
- Editor: Alt+P starts play-in-editor when no session runs (it does nothing while one runs). (ADR-5615)
- Look-dev panel (`lookdev`, a tab of the default bottom group): traces the open level with the reference path tracer
  through the editor camera or the level's game camera, or a tracer test scene (`cornell`, `furnace`), on its own
  thread and GPU device so the editor keeps drawing; writes `reference.exr` with every AOV, `reference_preview.png` and
  `reference.json`. Agent methods `editor.lookdev_trace`, `editor.lookdev_status`, `editor.lookdev_cancel`, error codes
  `lookdev_*`. (ADR-5896)
- UI Animation panel, opened from the UI Designer's Animations button: list, create, rename and delete the animations of
  a `.uianim.ron` library, tracks per property, keys with time, value and ease on a curve view, `once`, `loop`,
  `ping_pong`, scrub and play, one undo step per edit, atomic saves. Agent methods `editor.ui_anim_open`, `_list`,
  `_edit`, `_set_key`, `_remove_key`, `_scrub`, `_validate`, `_save`; validation codes `ui_anim_missing_node`,
  `ui_anim_ambiguous_node`, `ui_anim_property_unsupported`, `ui_anim_invalid`. `.uianim.ron` libraries ship in the pak,
  and `regia_ui::anim_pack::load_uianim_file` loads them for `ui.play_anim`. (ADR-3092)
- Content Browser Rename / Move… moves a source with its `.meta` (the asset id is kept, so `asset:<guid>` references
  need no edit) and rewrites whole-path mentions in referencing `.ron` files, as one undo step. Agent method
  `editor.rename_asset { from, to }`; the shared move and referencer scan live in `regia_asset::project_ops`.
  (ADR-5700)
- Play-mode guard: a `Static` entity whose `GlobalTransform` changes at runtime warns once per entity and is counted
  (`editor.static_move_warnings`, `PieStatus::static_move_warnings`). (ADR-5704)
- Extension settings: `settings.get` / `settings.set` reach an extension's `declare_settings::<T>()` resource as `<id>`
  or `<id>.<field>` (live writes, `dry_run`, answers carry `provider: "extension:<id>"`; not persisted yet). New
  `regia_framework::extension::SettingsAccess`. (ADR-5783)
- `regia new` and `editor.new_project` use an existing empty target folder, and `--template empty` (spec 61's id) makes
  the `blank` template. The CLI and the editor share one project-name rule and writer
  (`regia_templates::project_crate_name`, `write_project_dir`). (ADR-5643)
- `agent.guide` topic `asset-validators` summarizes the editor's asset validation, graph, size map, audit and
  save-validation methods. (ADR-5632)
- Editor crash reports: the editor installs the panic hook before its window opens and writes `crash-*.txt` (panic
  message, backtrace, editor build, OS, GPU adapter, last 200 log lines, home paths scrubbed, size-capped) into its log
  folder. A game-code panic during play-in-editor is a `pie:` report: play stops and the editor keeps running. The next
  launch offers View report, Copy to clipboard and Open folder; agent `editor.crash_reports` and
  `editor.crash_report_read` (module `regia_editor::crash_reports`). (ADR-3931)

#### Assets

- Texture-set materials: `regia assets texture-sets <folder>` and `assets.texture_sets` turn a folder of
  `<base>_<suffix>.png` maps into `.material.ron` files. New `.meta` options are `normal_green` and `orm_channel_order`.
  (ADR-3320)

#### Library modules

- Library-only encoders and generators, not yet used by the cook or the text atlas: `regia_asset_pipeline::bc6h_encode`
  (all 14 BC6H modes), `regia_asset_pipeline::etc2_encode` (all five ETC2 modes plus EAC) and `regia_ui::msdf` (MSDF
  glyphs plus a TrueType `glyf` reader). (ADR-2331, ADR-2333)
- Safe-Rust library modules, not yet wired into the cook or renderer: `astc_encode` (LDR ASTC) and `bc7_encode`.
- Library modules for game code: `regia_ai::goap` planner (ADR-2325), `regia_net::snapshot_delta` codec,
  `regia_render::masked_occlusion` CPU culler (ADR-2329), `regia_terrain::volume_mesh` dual contouring (ADR-2295), and
  `regia_procgen::wfc` with the `procgen.wfc_solve` agent method.

#### Agent protocol: transports, jobs and review

- `regia mcp --http [ADDR]` serves MCP Streamable HTTP next to stdio with the same tools, behind a per-launch bearer
  token, `Origin`/`Host` checks and `--allow-origin`, `--allow-remote`, `--http-max-body`, `--token-file`. `regia mcp
  --headless [--no-gpu] [--editor-bin] [--editor-timeout]` starts a headless editor when no app answers and ends it with
  the adapter. (ADR-2346, ADR-2821)
- One front door for long work: `agent.job_status` / `agent.job_cancel` (cursor-paged events, forwarding
  `render_out:<n>` and `dcc_bake:<id>`), `agent.run_tests` streaming `cargo test` outcomes as job `tests:<n>`, and
  `agent.open_in_editor` for an entity, prefab or file line. (ADR-2346)
- Staged review: `review.propose|list|diff|accept|reject` plus the editor's Agent Changes panel; accepting writes
  atomically and stamps `.meta` provenance `reviewer: owner`. With `[provenance] require_review_for_shipping = true`,
  `regia cook --quality final` and `regia package` refuse unreviewed agent- or generator-made assets. (ADR-2346)
- Golden tools: `agent.golden_test` runs a named golden as job `golden:<n>` and returns expected/actual/diff paths;
  `agent.bless_golden` only stages a proposal with a reason. The owner installs it with `regia golden list | accept <id>
  | reject <id>` at a terminal. (ADR-2821)
- Agent benchmark: 25 scripted tasks in `bench/agent_tasks/` (new frozen format `agent-task` v1) and `cargo xtask
  agent-bench` (`--oracle`, `--oracle --empty`, `--serve`, `--check-only --attach`, `--probe`, `--json`). An MCP
  conformance test drives `regia mcp` over stdio with an MCP client. (ADR-2621)
- Protocol stability: `docs/api-snapshots/agent-protocol.txt` plus `cargo xtask agent-snapshot --update|--check`, which
  classifies diffs as breaking or additive and requires a matching release-fragment line. (ADR-3335)
- Agent guide: call references (params, results, error codes, a captured headless answer) on sixteen pages, and `cargo
  xtask agent-examples` checks every JSON fence against the schema with a ratchet baseline. (ADR-3918)
- Local agent transport: next to the loopback WebSocket, an app with the agent feature serves the same JSON-RPC surface
  over a Unix domain socket (`<project>/.regia/agent/agent.sock`, mode 0600 in a 0700 directory) or a Windows named
  pipe (`\\.\pipe\regia-agent-<pid>`, owner-only DACL). One `{"bearer":"<token>"}` handshake frame, then 4-byte
  length-prefixed JSON frames (16 MiB cap). The address is advertised in the owner-only `<project>/.regia/agent/socket`
  file; `Client::connect_project` prefers it and falls back to the WebSocket, and `Client::connect_local` connects
  explicitly. Android keeps `adb forward` to the WebSocket port. Book page `agents/local-transport.md`. (ADR-5708)
- Code methods: `agent.build_game` (MCP tool `build_game`, permission `build`) runs `cargo build --message-format=json`
  as job `build:<n>` and streams structured `diagnostic` events (level, code, primary span, rustc's suggested
  replacement), `artifact` and `build-finished`; the result carries the diagnostics, counts, artifacts and a bounded
  raw tail. `agent.hot_reload` (tool `hot_reload`) asks the running editor to rebuild and swap the game code as job
  `hot:<n>` and returns the reload outcome (`accepted`, `phase`, `generation`, `elapsed_ms`, ...). (ADR-5707)
- Editor asset operations, each an MCP tool: `editor.edit_asset` patches a `.material.ron`, `.prefab.ron`,
  `.level.ron` or `.variant.ron` through its typed document's JSON view and saves it canonically as one undo entry;
  `editor.reimport`; `editor.move_asset` (moves the `.meta` too, refused with `asset_move_referenced` while referenced);
  `editor.delete_asset` (first call answers `needs_confirmation`, `confirm: true` deletes); `editor.set_import_settings`
  (writes the `.meta` settings over folder rules and re-cooks). Book page `agents/asset-ops.md`. (ADR-5706)
- Runtime gameplay methods: `scene.spawn_prefab { prefab, transform?, root_guid? }` (a template still loading answers
  `pending: true`), `level.open { level, mode? }` (`replace` or `additive`), `level.unload`, `rules.get`, `timers.list`
  and `tweens.list`, backed by the new `TimerHub::infos()` and the provided `GameRules::type_path()`; new error codes
  `bad_guid` and `spawn_refused`. Book page `agents/runtime-gameplay.md`.
- `regia mcp` attached to an editor (or started with `--headless`) lists the editor's own tool-named methods
  (`save_level`, `undo`, `redo`, `select`, `focus`, `import`, `play`, `stop`, ...), read from its live `agent.schema`
  each time the client lists tools. (ADR-5628)

#### Import and export

- `.exr` and `.hdr` import as linear float textures: BC6H on desktop, RGBA16F on Android, `hdr_encoding` and
  `cube_face_px` (equirect to cube at cook). `regia_asset::hdr_texture` decodes cooked HDR on the CPU and
  `render.set_studio_environment` accepts a cooked `asset`. (ADR-2546)
- Aseprite (`.aseprite`, `.ase`) and LDtk (`.ldtk`, `.ldtkl`) import into `SpriteAtlas` strips and tile maps, with the
  read-only `import.report` method. (ADR-3358)
- MaterialX (`.mtlx`) import of `standard_surface` and `open_pbr_surface` into a cooked material graph, with `.meta`
  settings and `import.materialx_report`. (ADR-3341, proposed)
- glTF collision proxies (`collision_box_*`, `_sphere_`, `_capsule_`, `_convex_`, `_mesh_`, other `collision_*` as
  hulls) cook into a new `collision` sub-asset (format `collision-set` v1); `AuthoredCollision { mesh }` turns them into
  a `Collider`, and `assets.collision_report` lists them. (ADR-2341)
- Import folder rules: `config/import_rules.ron` v1 applies glob-matched importer settings at cook time and keys them
  into the cook; check them with `regia assets import-rules check` or `assets.import_rules`. (ADR-3321)
- Scene export to one `.glb`: `regia export glb (--level | --prefab)...`, `asset.export_glb`, and File → Export
  selection to glTF, with hierarchy, meshes, materials, textures, lights and cameras. (ADR-2554)
- glTF extensions on import: `KHR_mesh_quantization` (integer positions, normals, tangents, UVs and morph deltas; a
  material's `KHR_texture_transform` over quantized UVs is baked into the vertices), `EXT_meshopt_compression` (every
  mode and filter; a `fallback` buffer is never read), `EXT_mesh_gpu_instancing` (a `prefab/instances` sub-asset, format
  `prefab-instances` v1, with the geometry cooked once), `KHR_materials_variants` (`regia_asset::PrefabVariantSelection`
  switches a model by name), `EXT_texture_webp` and `KHR_lights_punctual` (the named hierarchy spawns `PointLight`,
  `SpotLight` and `DirectionalLight`; `lights: false` leaves them out). A required extension the importer cannot read
  (`KHR_draco_mesh_compression`, `KHR_texture_basisu`, anything unknown) fails as `gltf_ext_unsupported` naming it; an
  optional one falls back with a warning. Read-only agent `asset.gltf_extensions { path }` reports support, fallbacks,
  compressed views, instanced nodes, variants and lights without cooking. (ADR-3016)
- Scene export falls back to the cook when a mesh, material or texture source is gone (warnings `mesh_from_cooked`,
  `mesh_from_cooked_rigid`, `material_from_cooked`, `texture_from_cooked`, `cooked_unreadable`), writes each copy of an
  instanced source (`instances_truncated` past 4096), and exports alpha mode, cutoff and double-sidedness. (ADR-3016)
- PLY (ASCII and binary; meters and Y up by default), STL (binary and ASCII; millimeters and Z up by default, vertex
  welding, smooth normals inside `crease_angle_degrees`) and PSD (the merged composite at 8 or 16 bits, RGB or
  grayscale, or one named top-level layer with `layer`) cook directly and import from the Content Browser; PLY and STL
  convert through the glTF importer, PSD cooks like a PNG. Read-only agent `editor.ply_inspect`, `editor.stl_inspect`
  and `editor.psd_inspect`, error codes `ply_*`, `stl_*` and `psd_*`. (ADR-3244, proposed)
- USD import (`.usda`, `.usdc`, `.usd`, `.usdz`) through the pure-Rust `openusd` reader (pipeline only): composition
  (sublayers, references, payloads, variant selections, inherits, instancing), the Xform hierarchy, meshes with every UV
  set and `materialBind` subsets, UsdPreviewSurface materials with embedded textures, cameras and lights, converted to
  meters and +Y up; instances of one prototype share one mesh. Every file is read and confined before composing, and
  reference cycles and chains deeper than 16 are refused. `regia cook` (importer `usd` 1.0.0, `.meta` settings `scale`,
  `meters_per_unit`, `up_axis`, `import_cameras`, `import_lights`, `limits`), `regia import <file> [--out GLB] [--scale
  F] [--json]` and agent `editor.usd_inspect` (MCP `usd_inspect`). (ADR-3235, proposed)
- `regia export usda --glb FILE.glb --out FILE.usda` writes one USDA stage: default prim `World`, an `Xform` per glTF
  node and a `Mesh` per triangle primitive with points, face indices, normals and the first UV set; refusals
  `usda_glb_missing`, `usda_glb_invalid`, `usda_empty`, `usda_write`. CLI only. (ADR-4604)
- DDS import of DX9 and DX10 cube maps, cube arrays and texture arrays into one KTX2 with the file's mip chain (BC
  blocks pass through on desktop, ASTC per face on Android); BC6H UF16/SF16 pass through as `BC6H_UFLOAT`/`BC6H_SFLOAT`
  on desktop and decode to RGBA16F or ASTC HDR on Android or with `hdr_encoding`; signed BC4/BC5 decode, remap to
  unsigned and cook as a normal map or mask. New `Ktx2Layout`, `write_ktx2_layered`, `dds_import::import_dds_bytes`,
  `DdsImage::{subresources, subresource, is_hdr, is_layered}` and an `import.dds` fuzz target. (ADR-3266)

#### Cook, paks and content

- Shared cook cache: `[cook.shared_cache]` (directory or plain `http://`, `read` or `read_write`), hash-verified entries
  keyed by importer inputs plus engine build id, `regia cook --shared-cache|--read-only|--no-shared-cache`, `regia cook
  cache stats|prune`, and `cook.cache_stats`. (ADR-2612)
- Provenance policy `config/provenance.ron` v1 checked by `regia assets validate`, final cooks and `regia package`;
  orphaned `.meta` files re-pair with moved sources, and renamed glTF/FBX sub-assets keep their ids. (ADR-2428)
- Pak update diffs: `regia assets pak-diff <old> <new>` and `assets.pak_diff` report added/removed/changed/moved assets
  and an estimated chunked patch download. (ADR-3322)
- Paks keep GPU-ready mesh submesh ranges (`gpu-mesh` v2) so destruction cut faces keep their material; cooked sounds
  ship inside paks (`cooked-sound` v1); `NavSurface { area }` paints nav areas per triangle. (ADR-2788)
- Generators: `generators/<name>.toml` (format `generator` v1, `command` or built-in `heightfield`), `regia gen
  list|run|verify`, and `gen.list|run|verify` running as cancellable `gen:<n>` jobs. (ADR-2412, ADR-2821)
- Content validation runs on save in the editor (`editor.save_validation`), and a new `dialogue_assets` validator joins
  `regia assets validate` and `regia cook`. Test sessions gain `keep.screenshots_on_failure` and
  `REGIA_SESSION_LIMIT_FRAMES`. (ADR-2755)
- `regia doctor` checks free disk, NDK version, Android SDK platform and build tools, and prints `--verify-paks DIR
  --json`. (ADR-2051)
- Formats: `cargo xtask check-formats` guards 24 more versioned files and `regia upgrade-project` knows 22 of them, and
  an old-bytes corpus of every older version of each frozen format runs in tests. (ADR-2316, ADR-2743)
- Texture `.meta` `alpha_cutoff: Some(0..1)` scales each mip so an alpha-tested texture keeps mip 0's coverage
  (Castano 2010) on every encoder (BC7, BC3, ASTC, ETC2/EAC); off by default. `.texpack.ron` v1 recipes build a
  texture's channels from up to four images or nested recipes (`Source(source, channel, invert)` or `Constant`), with a
  size-mismatch policy. Agent methods `assets.texture_coverage` and `assets.texpack_dry_run`, error codes `texpack_*`.
  (ADR-3090)
- A pak's TOC `dependencies` list orders mounting: every listed dependency mounts before the pak that declares it, so a
  patch pak declares its base instead of needing a file name that sorts after it. A cycle or an unmatched entry still
  mounts. (ADR-5700, ADR-5877)

### Regia Forest Demo

- `showcase --demo` runs the forest scene as an offline interactive demo: free-fly camera (keyboard, mouse and gamepad),
  time-of-day and weather sliders, a tier picker that explains its detected tier, an fps overlay, F12 PNG screenshots,
  an F9 benchmark that writes `showcase-table.md`/`.json`, and a credits page built from the scene's
  `asset_manifest.json` (v1). Agents drive it through `demo.*` methods (capability `showcase_demo`), and `--headless`,
  `--demo-out DIR` and `--tier NAME` go with it. (ADR-3013)
- `cargo xtask demo-package` builds the demo with the `shipping` profile for Windows and Linux and writes a Steam depot
  folder with steamcmd scripts, plain archives with `CHECKSUMS.b3` and `DECK-CHECK.md`. It refuses any asset that has no
  licence row and uploads nothing; `cargo xtask dist --overlay DIR` and `regia_cli::distribution` checksum helpers come
  with it. (ADR-2827)
- An agent-free demo build: `showcase --no-default-features --features demo` links no agent protocol, net transport,
  Steamworks or upload crate. `cargo xtask demo-notices [--check] [--verify DIR]` writes
  `docs/demo/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES-demo.md` from that exact tree, fails on any crate that can upload data, and checks a
  packaged folder's binaries, notices and EULA. (ADR-3111, ADR-4847)
- A draft demo EULA (`docs/demo/EULA-DRAFT.md`) and the staged plan for the showcase video and free-camera demo
  (`docs/showcase-plan.md`). A package cannot ship the draft EULA. (ADR-3111, ADR-2447)
- The third-person sample adds a guide NPC conversation (English and German), a cloth cape and soft ball, and `F6`/`F7`
  demo record, play and scrub (ADR-2946, ADR-4939)
- Cold start: a "REGIA FOREST DEMO - LOADING" boot splash covers terrain install and pipeline warm-up
  (`showcase::demo::configure_loading`, `WarmTerrainBuild`, `BootSplash::set_warm_queue`), and `quality.get_detected`
  gains a `host` answer (`regia_agent::HostQualityDetection`) so the demo reports its own tier pick. (ADR-5131)
- Player-ready bundles: a player `README.txt` (version, `Build <git>`, controls, what the demo sends and writes), build
  ids via `cargo xtask dist --git REV` / `--readme FILE` and `demo-package --git-windows/--git-linux`, new `--verify`
  checks, per-depot `git` and `install_mb` in the Steam upload plan, and Steam client icons (184 x 184 JPG, 512 x 512
  PNG). (ADR-5002)
- `showcase::demo::sun::{daylight_hours, player_hour, PLAYER_MIN_SUN_ELEVATION, PLAYER_MAX_COVERAGE,
  PLAYER_WEATHER_PRESETS}`, and `demo-package` puts `licenses/EULA.md` and the demo's own notices in each bundle and
  depot (codes `demo_package_legal_missing`, `demo_package_legal`). (ADR-5419)
- `showcase --demo --exit-after-frames N` in a window (`ExitAfterSceneFrames`, `DemoOptions::exit_after_frames`)
  exits 0 after N frames drawn past the loading splash. (ADR-5452)
- `WINDOWS-CHECK.md` in the `demo-package` staging directory: the Windows minimum-spec check steps beside
  `DECK-CHECK.md` (hash check with `certutil`, a read-only install in a Unicode path, launch times, tier, input, close,
  logs and the Visual C++ runtime dialog). (ADR-5578)

#### Showcase: look, camera and ambience

- Opt-in camera feel: `Handheld` sway and breathing, `PhotographicWalk` (shutter scales with speed, depth of field on
  focus pulls), and a first-person `walk_bob` whose footfalls follow ground walked. Agents use `camera.handheld`,
  `camera.photographic_walk`, `camera.walk_bob` and `camera.set_walk_bob`. (ADR-2449, ADR-2486)
- Opt-in look controls: soft leaf shadows, seasons (`SeasonPlugin`), 4K video capture presets on `render.out_start`,
  8-bit output dither, star flux that keeps stars visible at 4K, and `set_sun_shadow_range` to bring tree shadows back
  on Mobile Low. Each has `render.*`/`scene.season` agent methods and leaves default frames bit-identical. (ADR-2449,
  ADR-2486)
- `WindStyle::Natural` gives grass and foliage gusts with calm spells, slower sway for taller plants, no regular wave
  and per-species flutter. Set it with `terrain.set_wind { style }` or `material.set_sway`. (ADR-2446)
- Weather-driven ambience: `AmbienceBed` layers follow `SceneWind` and `SurfaceWeather` and stay silent under 0.3 m/s
  wind, `AmbientSpot` adds water loops and scattered bird calls, and `FootstepBank` plays surface footsteps from
  animation notifies. `audio.ambience` checks the bed against the renderer's foliage wind; `test_tones` gives audible
  placeholder loops. (ADR-2442)
- Ecology validator: a 36-species temperate and boreal table, `validate_ecology` with `ecology_*` reasons,
  `.ecology.ron` profiles (format `ecology` v1), six biome presets, a `biome.ecology` node, `biome.validate_ecology` and
  `cargo xtask check-ecology`. (ADR-2456)
- Voxel far proxies: `voxel_far: Some((resolution: 32))` in a glTF `.meta` cooks a sparse-brick `.fvox` (v1) stand-in
  that the visibility buffer's LOD cut uses as coarser cluster levels, in the main view and every shadow job, with no
  impostor pop. `assets.voxel_far` reports levels, memory and switch distances. (ADR-2445)
- Benchmarks: `cargo xtask showcase-table` measures each quality tier over `perf_views.json` at 2560x1440 into
  `docs/perf/showcase-table.md`, and `regia bench` gains `synthetic_grass_1m` and `template_npc_200` scenes with
  `counters` (`--small` for software rasterizers). (ADR-2444, ADR-2695)

### Audio, media, UI and accessibility

- `Audio::is_capturing`, `capture_channels`, `capture_dropped_frames` and `is_talking`; dropped frames count since
  capture started, across restarts (ADR-4870, renumbered from ADR-1553f)
- `Localization::add_locale` merges one locale's texts into a loaded `Localization` (ADR-4939)
- `regia_media::opus::gate_packet` and `OpusMode`, with a fuzz target for untrusted Opus packets (ADR-2306)
- Video: `.videolist.ron` playlists play in order, `video.select_track` with `index: null` disables audio, and
  `DecodeCaps::for_host` / `av1_process_budget` size the decoder per host

#### UI: world space

- World-space UI panels and camera-target UI (`UiTarget::WorldSpace`, `UiTarget::Camera`, `WorldPanel` billboarding and
  fade), with pointer injection and agent `ui.world_click`, `ui.set_target`, `ui.targets`. (ADR-2406)

#### Sequencer and cameras

- Sequencer v3 (`.seq.ron` v5 at the candidate): spatial sound sections that follow a binding or sit at a position, a
  per-section voice time scale (varispeed), looping sub-sequence sections (`SubRepeat::{Once, Count, UntilStop}`, up to
  4 deep), and `rename_binding` as one undo step. v4 added video sections; v5 adds spawnable bindings and voice
  Doppler/occlusion sections. Editor strip rows and `sequencer.spatial_voices`, `sequencer.sub_loops`,
  `sequencer.rename_binding` cover them. (ADR-2405, ADR-2862, ADR-2948, ADR-3197)
- Sequence subtitles: a sound section that plays a dialogue line shows its caption cues in sync with seek, scrub, pause,
  loops and time scale; opt out with a `.nosubs` track, and burn in on render-out with
  `SequenceSubtitleSettings::burn_in`. (ADR-3334)
- Marker sheets: export and import a sequence's markers as CSV (`marker_sheet`, agent `sequencer.marker_sheet`); a bad
  row names its row and writes nothing. (ADR-4597)
- Rail camera (`Node::Rail`, arc-length dolly on a spline) and crane (`Node::Crane`, arm and mount with limits, optional
  rail base), keyable from sequences (`camera.rail.position`, `camera.crane.*`) and inspectable via `camera.rail_state`,
  `camera.crane_state`, `camera.set_rail`, `camera.set_crane`. (ADR-3231)
- The camera as a physical actor (`regia_framework::camera_body`, `CameraBodyPlugin`): `CameraBody` follows the shot
  sequence, a `CameraBodyFollow` entity or the host's drive, and its closed-form air wake (a Mach cone above 343 m/s)
  flicks grass blades (`TerrainGrassHandle::set_wake`, with motion vectors), pushes dynamic physics bodies, lifts dust
  or snow on fast low passes and startles birds. `camera_body::proximity` and `camera_body::clearance` check a path
  against ground and trees, and `lift_keys` / `lift_shot` propose raised keys. It is off until `camera_body.json` or
  `camera_body.set {"enabled": true}` turns it on. (ADR-1502)
- A startled bird (`regia_framework::bird`, `BirdPlugin`, `bird.json` v1): five species sizes, a startle time derived
  from the camera path (distance, closing speed or the wake front), then a flinch, a panicked take-off and a seeded
  flight the wake throws about. The model is procedural (14 rigid parts, per-part motion vectors) and fixed-step from
  take-off. Agent `camera_body.*` and `bird.*` (`book/agents/camera-body.md`); the showcase reads optional
  `camera_body.json` and `birds.json` from the scene directory. (ADR-1502)
- Cutscene flow (`regia_framework::sequencer::cutscene`): `start_cutscene`/`skip_cutscene`/`stop_cutscene` with input
  context lock, letterbox bars, a hidden `HudLayer`, dialogue and subtitles stopped on skip, and hand-back through the
  camera takeover; `NetCutscenes` starts a cutscene at a tick on every peer and a late joiner skips to it. Spawnable
  bindings (`Sequence::spawnables`) pre-roll their prefabs and exist only while the clock is in their range;
  `SequencePlayer::skip_to`/`skip_to_end` deliver skipped markers in order, and `restore_on_stop` writes back the
  properties the player wrote. Editor **Binding kind** row; agent `cutscene.*` and `sequence.player` action `skip`.
  (ADR-2948)
- Take recorder (`regia_framework::sequencer::recorder`, client builds): play the game or PIE and record transforms,
  cameras with their cuts, graph animator clips, named or `reflect:` properties and `TakeCue` audio cues into a
  `<name>_take<N>.seq.ron` that joins the source sequence as a sub-sequence section. Keys are reduced to a stated error
  (defaults 0.001 m, 0.1°). Editor **Record take** section in the Sequence Curves panel; agent `sequence.record_start`,
  `record_stop`, `record_state` with `take_*` codes. (ADR-2637)
- Video sections play on the sequence clock: fullscreen, on a player, on a world screen or in a UI image, with drift
  correction in real time and frame-exact decoding under a manual clock (`render.out`, `render.out_job`, `play.step`),
  so two render-outs show the same pictures. They need the `media` feature and `MediaPlugin`; otherwise the sequence
  reports `seq_video_unavailable` and plays the rest. Editor stills on the section strip; agent
  `sequencer.video_sections`. (ADR-2862)
- Pitch-preserving sound sections (`AudioSection::time_scale_mode: PreservePitch`, scales 0.5 to 2, stretched once by
  WSOLA into a 256 MiB cache, bit for bit the plain voice at rate 1), Doppler on spatial sections
  (`AudioSpatial::doppler`, 0 to 4, from the section's motion on the sequence clock) and occlusion through the audio
  occlusion query (`AudioSpatial::occlusion`). Seeks and render-outs sound the same as playing through. Library:
  `regia_audio::stretch_asset`, `Audio::start_asset_lowpass`/`set_voice_lowpass`, `AudioOcclusion::set_extra_rays_for`.
  (ADR-3197)

#### Audio

- Music on the audio clock: `music_stinger` on the next beat, bar or segment, rule-based `music_transition` with
  crossfades, `MusicBeat` messages, and `.music.ron` v2. `AudioEmitter::fade_start` fades inverse and log rolloff to
  silence at `max_distance`. (ADR-2517)
- Audio Mixer dock and Cue Editor panels with undo, plus per-bus peak, RMS and short-term loudness meters
  (`audio.bus_meters`, `editor.audio_mixer_get/set`, `editor.cue_edit`). (ADR-2530)
- Cook-time transcode of WAV and FLAC to Ogg Vorbis via `audio_cook.toml` or `.meta`, with a separate Android quality
  and an `audio_ship.json` report. `regia package` now ships the loudness table as `assets/loudness.ron` so a packaged
  game can turn normalization on. (ADR-3230, ADR-4898)
- `read_wav_cues` and agent `audio.cues` read RIFF cue points and labels from a WAV. (ADR-4632)
- Convolution reverb on any bus: `BusEffect::Convolution { ir, mix, predelay_secs }` with impulse responses from cooked
  sounds (`ImpulseResponse::from_sound`, `from_encoded_bytes`, `Audio::register_impulse_response`). Uniformly
  partitioned with 256-frame partitions (5.3 ms wet latency at 48 kHz), stereo IRs, resampling at load, crossfaded IR
  swaps, no allocation on the audio thread, and per-tier IR caps (`ConvolutionTier`: desktop 4 s, mobile 1.5 s, low
  off). Reverb zones can name an IR (`AudioReverbZone::ir`, `with_ir`) and crossfade click-free to and from it; a sound
  whose `.meta` sets `impulse_response: Some(true)` is checked as an IR and never loudness-normalized. (ADR-2721)
- Pitch-preserving time stretch (WSOLA, rates 0.5 to 2): `regia_audio::time_stretch` offline, and
  `Audio::play_time_stretched` / `set_time_stretch_rate` / `stop_time_stretched` on a playing voice. (ADR-2721)
- `.ambience.ron` v1 (`AmbienceDef`, `AmbiencePlayer`, `Audio::play_ambience` / `stop_ambience`, system
  `ambience_file_sync`): looping beds plus seeded random one-shots, with cook validation and a fuzz target. Agent
  `audio.bus_effects`, `audio.bus_effect_set`, `audio.convolution_stats`, `audio.ambience_play|stop|list` and
  `audio.time_stretch_probe` (`book/agents/audio-convolution.md`). (ADR-2721)
- Runtime audio: sound assets carry playback defaults (`SoundAsset::with_default_volume_db`, `with_default_pitch`,
  `with_default_loop_region`, `with_default_bus`); cues play in 3D (`Audio::play_cue_at` at a position, `play_cue_on`
  attached to an emitter, with seeded and event variants) under their weights, ranges, no-repeat, cooldown, limit and
  steal rules; an `AudioEmitter` plays a `SoundCue` (`cue`, `cue_name`, the `CueAssets` resource); and
  `regia::DefaultPlugins` loads `config/audio.ron` into `AudioSettings::buses`. `audio.set_emitter` takes a cue.
  (ADR-5699, ADR-5926)

#### Video and media

- Boot movies from `Regia.toml` `[boot] movies`, a `skip_video` input action, fullscreen video above the UI,
  `PauseDecode`/`PauseAll` when hidden, WebM text cues routed to captions, a decode pool (4 desktop, 2 Android) with a
  thermal hook, and `.webm`/`.mkv` cooking with validation. `media.*` agent methods cover each. (ADR-2410)
- Built-in AV1 + Opus WebM encoding (feature `media-encode`): render-out format `webm`, `.render_job.ron` v3
  `output.encode`, and `regia media encode` for game movies, with no external tool. (ADR-3369)
- Editor Video panel: preview, frame stepping, stream info, decode stats and the boot movie list, with
  `editor.video_open/seek/state`. (ADR-2720)

#### UI

- `.ui.ron` gains collection `repeat` templates with keyed row diffing and six data-only binding converters (v6),
  isolated group opacity, rounded clips, image masks, integer scale and a DPI curve (v7, plus `.uisettings.ron` v1),
  reusable components with typed parameters and slots (v8), and pictures by asset path or icon with nine-slice (v9).
  (ADR-2489, ADR-2866, ADR-3147, ADR-3148)
- Game UI widgets (`.ui.ron` v10): a lobby list, scoreboard, radar and minimap, world markers and target brackets
  (`regia_ui::game_widgets`), rules bridging (`GameRules::scoreboard`, `ScoreSheet`, `GameUiBridgePlugin` driving a
  lobby list from `regia_net::Lobbies`) and agent methods `ui.lobby_list`, `ui.scoreboard`, `ui.radar`,
  `ui.world_markers`, `ui.target_bracket`. (ADR-2958)
- A `repeat` on a `list` widget is virtualized (about 15 live rows for 10,000 items, anchored scroll), and the UI
  designer gains a themed GPU preview, comment-keeping saves, a component palette and Picture rows. (ADR-2715, ADR-2489,
  ADR-3147, ADR-3148)
- `.svg` assets cook to textures and atlas sprites through `resvg`, with size limits and refusals for scripts, external
  refs and text. (ADR-3350)
- Runtime UI inspector: `ui.inspect`, `ui.pick_at`, `ui.highlight` and an editor "UI Inspector" panel that shows which
  source won each style property. (ADR-2703)
- `ui.click { modifiers }` holds `shift`, `ctrl`, `alt` or `meta` through a click as real key messages, so agents can
  Shift- and Ctrl-click list rows; a menu row with an empty `shortcut` shows the chord the shortcut registry binds to it
  (`UiShortcuts::chord_label`). (ADR-5665)
- Backdrop blur render-target counts: `BackdropStats` gains `live_targets`, `live_target_bytes`, `targets_created` and
  `targets_freed`, read from the pool when `UiRenderer::backdrop_stats()` is called, and `ui.backdrop_stats` reports
  the same; the blur's scratch textures go through `TrackedDevice`. A frosted panel opened and closed 50 times returns
  to the baseline count and creates nothing after the warm cycle. (ADR-5420)
- Dialogue UI (`regia_ui::choice`, `captions::karaoke`, `captions::anchor`): choice widgets (vertical list and radial
  wheel, keyboard, gamepad, pointer and touch, an optional timer that picks a default, disabled options with a reason,
  theme classes, accessible names, right-to-left mirroring), karaoke caption highlighting
  (`DialogueCaptionSettings::karaoke`, off by default) and captions anchored to speakers inside the safe area
  (`DialogueCaptionSettings::placement`, `CaptionAnchors`). `ConversationChoiceBridgePlugin` opens a widget for a
  conversation `Choice` step. Agent `ui.choice_state`, `ui.choice_pick`, `captions.anchor_state`,
  `captions.set_dialogue_style`. (ADR-3234)

#### Localization and fonts

- Culture formatting in `regia_ui::loc_format` (numbers, currency, dates, relative times, lists) for about thirty
  locales, with Fluent `NUMBER()` and `DATETIME()` including ordinal plurals, and `ui.format_preview`. (ADR-2698,
  ADR-3359)
- Translator tools: XLIFF 2.0 exchange, `regia loc status`, `regia loc check-fit` (overflow at real layout), glyph
  coverage (`regia loc check --glyphs`), a `glossary` v1 format, translation-memory notes on export, and WebVTT/TTML cue
  exchange. (ADR-2550, ADR-3359)
- Cook-time font subsetting to the shipped locales' characters (`regia assets font-subset`, `assets.font_subset`),
  keeping glyph ids and shaping tables. (ADR-2697)
- Thai, Lao, Khmer and Myanmar text wraps and caret-steps at dictionary word boundaries in UI, captions and subtitles
  (`ui.text_breaks`). (ADR-3323)
- Localization panel in the editor (bottom tab group): every key against every locale, each cell marked translated,
  stale, placeholder or missing, with filters, search, paging and per-locale progress. Editing a cell rewrites only
  that `.ftl` entry, atomically, as one undo step, and records the `# regia-source:` hash so it leaves the stale
  filter. **Gather**, **Find usages** and **Pseudo (viewport)** buttons; agent `editor.loc_table`, `editor.loc_set`,
  `editor.loc_gather`, `editor.loc_usages`, `editor.loc_pseudo` with `loc_panel_*` codes. (ADR-3260)
- `regia loc export` counts the terms PO export leaves out (`skipped_terms`, also in `--json`) and adds `#:`
  references for dialogue keys; `loc_po_bad_pattern` names the line inside the translation. (ADR-5778)

#### Accessibility

- Player assists: toggle, minimum hold, repeat and cool-down per action, per player or gamepad; rumble and phone
  vibration scales; mono audio; color-vision filters; camera shake scale, motion blur and FOV override; look sensitivity
  and Y invert on named look actions. All persist in `GameSettings` and apply live. (ADR-2646, ADR-3464)
- `ui.a11y_audit` checks the live screen for unnamed controls, contrast, target size, focus reachability, text clipped
  at 200 % and motion, with WCAG 2.2 criteria and fix hints; `TouchTarget` enlarges hit boxes. (ADR-3025, ADR-4903)
- Photosensitivity flash check (WCAG 2.2 SC 2.3.1) over PNG folders or live captures: `regia_core::flash`,
  `seeing.flash_check`, `cargo xtask flash-check`. It is an aid, not a certification. (ADR-3804)
- `ScreenReader::sync_cost()` and `ui.a11y_tree` `sync_cost` report what the screen-reader sync cost over its last 512
  live frames, per stage. On the Pixel 9 Pro (release build, TalkBack on, 81 nodes): mean 0.27-0.58 ms, p99
  0.54-0.86 ms per frame. (ADR-5379)

### Core, licence and security

- `regia_scene::hierarchy::despawn_tree` despawns any depth without recursion; agent error `request_too_large` caps
  request parsing at 128 MiB (ADR-2306)
- `TypePathLookup` / `lookup_type` tell an unknown type path from an ambiguous short path
- `DISCONTINUITY_GAP` (30 s), `locale_from_env`, `ThermalStatus::token`, `MIN_THROTTLED_FPS` / `MAX_THROTTLED_FPS`,
  `SWIPE_MAX_SECS` and `track_pick_order` (ADR-2273, ADR-5065)

#### Licence, legal and security

- `cargo xtask sbom` writes a deterministic CycloneDX 1.5 bill of materials (`engine` or `game` profile), and
  `docs/asset-licences.toml` gives every tracked binary asset a licence row checked by `notices --check`. (ADR-2760)
- A written threat model (`docs/security/threat-model.md`, book Security page) with fifteen trust boundaries, each
  mitigation tied to code and a test. `regia mcp token` prints the running HTTP server's token only after checking its
  advertisement. (ADR-3206, ADR-3285)

  (ADR-5953), a terrain layer-tap IR cost test, a forward render on a 2 MiB stack, and Mobile lite lightmap tests that
  hold the lite tiers to the full shader.

## Changed

### Rendering

- The water caustics pass filters the ripple map itself in f32, so the tile matches its CPU mirror (ADR-2529). The
  engine shader hash now covers `regia_fluid` (ADR-2528).
- Calibrated fog uses a half-texel constant shadow bias (`FOG_SHADOW_BIAS_TEXELS` 0.5); the legacy rule stays the
  default.
- The sun page cache and the local-light atlas cost scales with what changed, not with the caster count (ADR-3079,
  ADR-2028).
- Thin-film and OIT text follows the shipped pass: transmissive, masked, lightmap and sorted-hair routes are not drawn,
  `render.oit` has no `hair` field (ADR-5029; ADR-2732 stays proposed)
- `render.set_upscaler` reports `vendor_frame` from the runtime renderer, and DLSS availability asks NGX for the current
  device's extensions
- The post pass reuses a same-edge 3D LUT texture; a surface-graph slot's inline pixels are part of the material texture
  key
- Material cache keys are generation ids per `Arc<CpuMaterial>` (not addresses); graph-material back faces negate the
  tangent frame like the standard surface; the GPU tier table adds AMD laptop rows (RX 7900M High, 7800M-class Medium,
  other M/S Low) and matches Adreno 7xx/8xx by number. `SdfScene`/`SdfShape`/`HeightfieldGrid`/`MeshSdf` get
  `PartialEq`; `FrameRenderer::interior_scene()` / `light_extras()` read back host state. (ADR-4950)
- `render_into` records every built-in pass and slot in one loop over graph record sites; the `before_post` copy and the
  depth-match copy come from the drawn view's transient pool and show in `render.graph` `transients`. Same commands,
  same order. (ADR-4945, ADR-4905)
- Emissive probe gathers for new emitters transform and filter triangles on the worker; the frame thread only copies
  positions (about 1 ms saved for a 16 384-triangle lamp), with bit-identical results.
- A rigid blended draw sorts by its mesh bounds center (a mesh with no bounds uses its cached vertex centroid), so
  sort order can change where the two differ. Time-reading material graphs read one frame clock in forward, shadow and
  velocity passes when a material clock or scene wind is set. (ADR-5160)
- `admit` and the agent's `render.memory_budget` admission no longer approve a load set while one side of current use
  is unmeasured (no GPU allocator report on GL, Metal or WebGPU, or no resident-set sample on macOS and the web):
  `fits` is false and `reason` names the missing side. Vulkan and D3D12 on Linux, Android and Windows admit as before.
- A `.cube` LUT whose `DOMAIN_MIN` is not below `DOMAIN_MAX` on every channel, or is not finite, is refused with
  `LutError::Cube`.
- With `REGIA_RESIDENCY_FEEDBACK` on, the residency copy and clear ride the frame submit, so the readout arrives one
  frame later; the default (off) is unchanged.
- The local-light shadow atlas caches a `Static` caster like any other caster (ADR-5906). A held Quick 3D frame
  resubmits its last lists and leaves `Draw3d` alone, so drawings made while it is held wait for the next live frame
  (ADR-5456).
- Material textures on Mesa RADV (RX 7900, Steam Deck) and Mali phones (Pixel 9 Pro, Mali-G715) filter like NVIDIA:
  both measure a 0.25-mip anisotropic offset, so material draws through the full forward shader and the terrain's
  sampler path filter a quarter mip softer there than before. NVIDIA, lavapipe and an Apple M5 measure 0; their frames
  are unchanged and no golden moves. (ADR-5670, ADR-5797)
- The shader composer cache is a process-wide pool that `RenderPlugin` drops after 30 app frames without a composition,
  instead of one composer per thread kept for the process's life (about 10.8 MB of parsed shader library in the
  start-up scene); the staging belt's spill chunk grows on the first spill instead of holding 1 MiB. The cluster
  light-index buffer is sized by the frame's per-cluster cap. (ADR-3351)
- Visibility buffer: cluster residency retries a budget refusal once the free budget plus resident bands no instance
  needs cover the mesh's base, and the mesh then draws through clusters for good; a band larger than
  `io_bytes_per_frame` goes as the frame's first read; foliage with voxel far levels finishes its sway fade before the
  held voxel level hands over (about 11 % nearer with the default band). Key-0 emissive meshes trace their distance
  probes on the frame thread when the fit worker is on. The Adreno shader-matrix permutations carry every runtime
  workaround define, `FORWARD_TRIM` included. (ADR-5579)

#### Upscalers and temporal

- TSR resolve (opt-in): converged sub-Nyquist detail, thin surfaces found in last frame's depth, single-layer history
  restarts, uncovered-pixel luma carried in history alpha, and young-pixel placeholders. Tracked locks: a lock carries
  its thin feature's sub-pixel offset (the aux motion target is RGBA16F, +8 bytes per output pixel), only the pixel the
  moved point lands in claims, follows or inherits it, an owned lock's color is point-fetched, and a pixel that owns no
  thin feature keeps other pixels' thin samples out of its clip box. TSR 0.5 now meets every § 2.3 row: orbit thin
  geometry 0.683 to 0.905 (bar 0.90) on an RTX 5090 and on lavapipe; TSR 0.67 orbit thin 0.937 to 1.000. No preset uses
  TSR or a 0.5 render scale, so Desktop High stays native with TAAU. (ADR-2638, ADR-4859, ADR-4934, ADR-7694)
- A vendor upscaler keeps its mode across dynamic-resolution steps, and XeSS reports `not_bundled` instead of `null`.
  Vendor probe tests expect the state of the build they run in. (ADR-2679, ADR-4907)
- XeSS paces each context: before recording an execute it waits until the execute four evaluations back has finished
  on the GPU, so a CPU-bound offscreen loop now waits. On an NVIDIA DX12 device the DLSS row's `blockers` start with
  `dlss_needs_vulkan`. In a Windows SDK-linked build `regia_dlss::SDK_VERSION` is the runtime DLL's file version
  (`310.9.16`). DLSS and XeSS stay opt-in. (ADR-7695)
- The temporal chart's ghost scene draws its ground stripe at (0.18, 0.1515, 0.1212), which renders to one 8-bit color,
  so the ghosting number no longer depends on adapter rounding. Readings move: TSR 0.5 reads 0.0185 on the RX 7900
  XTX, RTX 5080 and RTX 5090 (0.0199 to 0.0203 before); FSR 3.1 reads 0.029 at 0.67 (now above the 0.02 target) and
  0.061 to 0.062 at 0.5. (ADR-5890)

#### Determinism and render-out

- The GPU-driven instanced color cull is order-fixed by default (`set_ordered_gpu_cull(false)` opts back into the
  atomicAdd pack). Offscreen captures force ordered culling, complete emissive fits and sync DDGI bakes. (ADR-4777,
  ADR-4780)
- Frames no longer follow the wall clock: complete frames key cache eviction and streaming on the frame index, zone
  exposure holds the eye without a host `dt`, and texture-stream OOM back-off counts frames. The CPU sun, atmosphere
  tables and glass crack pages use a software `libm`. (ADR-4777)
- Ties follow entity order: the primary window, the sun (lowest-entity `DirectionalLight`), equal-order cameras and
  light lists.
- Render-out jobs run under complete frames and restart effect seeds, particle clocks and scatter LOD memory. They take
  no VFX warm frames, and a held job restores live effects and camera shakes. (ADR-2582, ADR-2633, ADR-2672, ADR-2741,
  ADR-2808, ADR-2814)

#### Performance

- Fewer frame-path creates and less steady work: prewarmed bind caches survive into the first frame, and lazily or
  raw-created groups are counted and cached. An empty VFX layer and the translucent step are skipped, and the
  precipitation occlusion view redraws about once per snap. (ADR-2303, ADR-2585)
- Lens effects allocate nothing of their own on a steady frame, and the shadow-caster cull uses four compute passes
  instead of six. The staging belt's warm-up no longer depends on GPU timing and reserves 640 B per reserved instance
  (cap 32 MiB). (ADR-2482, ADR-3189)
- `FrameRenderer::render_into` is split into named steps and a recording context (2,398 lines to about 1,520) with no
  pixel change. (ADR-2300, ADR-4856, ADR-4905)
- Steady frames stop rebuilding what did not change: the draw list, instanced shadow and depth lists, local-light,
  near and precipitation caster lists and the skin palette upload allocate nothing; cluster overflow masks, cluster
  froxel boxes and light masks, mesh LOD level lists (`MeshLodSnaps`), mesh-occluder blocks, the TAAU velocity object
  pack and GPU-driven instance lists are reused while their inputs hold; a dynamic-texture flush takes the pending image
  instead of cloning it. (ADR-5160, ADR-5206)
- Gizmos and wireframe: a batch locks the edge cache once, rigid segments stay in local space and are shared across
  frames, a retained gizmo expands once, scratch vectors and bind groups are reused, and a skinned triangle skins each
  vertex once (3 evaluations, not 6). (ADR-5159, ADR-5162)
- Frame-generation optical flow scores the zero correction first and stops a candidate at the first pixel past the
  best SAD (a 24x16 perfect guess reads 2,384 pixels instead of 110,976); the luma pyramid stores one byte per pixel
  (2,764,744 bytes instead of 11,058,840 at 1920x1080). (ADR-5162)
- Post-volume LUT blends and overcast weather maps are cached on a 1/256 step, so a camera inside a blend band no
  longer rebuilds the n³ table and a 3D texture every frame, and an altitude shift packs no new weather map; render
  overrides resolve only the overridden hierarchy (40 unrelated meshes plus one overridden child: 1 resolve, not 43).
  (ADR-5159)
- Emissive and lab paths: a mesh submitted with key 0 fits and traces its distance probes once (ADR-5208); the
  per-frame emissive-map signature is hashed word-wise, once per material per frame, about 8x cheaper per byte
  (ADR-5451); large lab textures and meshes hash once per allocation; debug surface twins skip skinning when the pose
  matches, and the material-id legend is a map (48 visits instead of 1,128 name scans).
- Shadows, decals and overlays: compute-fallback sun shadows write a moving caster's BVH into the existing buffers
  when it fits; paged sun shadows refill page lists in place; the decal receiver skips an unchanged upload (4,800 bytes
  for 60 stamps); a steady scalar-overlay pose is compared in place (12,288 bytes not copied for 256 joints); the
  daylit fallback box stays put while the eye is inside it, so a 0.25 m step no longer reruns the bake.
- `apply_material_graphs` keeps its path set on `GraphMaterials` and rebuilds it only when a graph reference or its
  mesh renderer changes (64 entities sharing 4 paths copy 4 strings once, then none).
- `regia_quick`: a filled rect takes two shared-state writes, unchanged text is shaped once per `QuickRenderer`, the 2D
  command list and 3D lists keep their capacity, a plain miter stroke or arrow skips lyon, and a repeated overlay
  generation skips the vertex upload. (ADR-5305)
- The `Static` marker no longer splits GPU cull batches: static and dynamic instances of one mesh and material share
  one indirect batch again (8 meshes x 4 materials x 16 instances, half `Static`: 64 batches with the split, 32 now).
  A static shadow caster keeps the cascade's sub-texel move tolerance like any other caster, and the editor's
  play-mode Static guard visits only entities whose `GlobalTransform` changed and keeps one warning per moving entity
  (`editor.static_move_warnings` still counts every move). Merging static meshes into fewer draws is not implemented.
  (ADR-5906)
- Path-tracer image ranges wider than 65,535 workgroups write, submit and poll once per sample batch instead of once per
  split, and the lightmap denoiser finds across-chart texels through a median-texel grid with the same result (ties
  still break by texel index).
- Renderer audit performance: unpaged local-light shadow tiles share one cached caster list (Mobile High redraws every
  face); a structural sun-trace rebuild deduplicates meshes with one lookup per instance; HLOD proxy shadow mode no
  longer walks every proxy vertex; `settle_lod`'s CPU mirror builds the instance matrix once per instance (128k
  meshlets in about 1.12 ms per frame); residency keeps its `cluster_base → mesh key` map across frames and a band flip
  redraws cached shadow maps only for casting meshes; `combine_lightmaps` stacks same-size, same-exposure sources
  verbatim; `PtTexture` shares a `SharedImage`'s pixels instead of copying them on every extract; a visible cloth
  rewrites one resident mesh in place instead of a new mesh arc and full upload every frame. (ADR-5535)
- Swaying casters take part in both two-phase culls: a vertex-stage material graph that declares its largest offset
  (`SurfaceGraph::max_vertex_offset` > 0, as every `FoliageSway` material does) is tested on its mesh bound grown by
  that offset in the depth prepass cull and the shadow casters' cull, as the color pass's Hi-Z cull already did.
  Swaying foliage behind an occluder now skips its depth prepass draws on Desktop High and Ultra; frames and shadow
  maps stay byte-identical to the unculled ones, and the shadow cull stays off in every preset. (ADR-5389)
- Skinned casters page by their joint-palette bound and join the dynamic-caster shadow class: a vertex-shader-skinned
  mesh with a palette bound is cached in the sun pages and atlas tiles like a rigid caster (one skinned caster used to
  force a whole-cascade redraw every frame), a pose change inside the box is redrawn in the refreshed cascades, and a
  held pose is not refreshed. A standing crowd's shadow time drops from 0.04-0.19 ms to 0.003 ms on an RTX 5090.
  Compute-skinned units and skins without a palette bound keep the whole-cascade redraw.
  `DynamicCasterReport::skinned_casters` (`render.foliage_wind` `last_frame.skinned_casters`) counts them. (ADR-5418)
- Terrain forward pass: the sampler-path LOD offset is read once per pixel, which removes its 1.5 % cost on every
  adapter (RTX 5090 `terrain_forward` 2.542 ms to 2.484 ms, GPU frame 5.627 ms to 5.560 ms, mean of per-run p50 over 8
  interleaved runs; same frame bytes). RADV keeps its 0.25-mip offset. LOD cross-fades reuse one per-object uniform per
  fade side instead of creating a buffer and bind group per fading draw per frame, and the shadow view-projection cache
  and late overlay warm no longer allocate per frame. (ADR-5943)

#### Materials and assets

- Importers recook once: PNG/JPEG 1.2.0, `material_ron` 1.5.0 (the header lists texture asset ids for pak chunking) and
  glTF 1.13.0. (ADR-3320, ADR-2732)
- Morphing scenes under TAA/TSR now write motion vectors, so resolved frames can differ from earlier releases.
  (ADR-2652)

#### Rendering on constrained devices

- `DdgiSettings::clamped` also keeps both probe buffers inside one 128 MiB storage binding.
- Level-look and post-volume LUTs resolve through the asset server first and accept `\` separators. (ADR-4830)

### Lightmaps, GI and lighting

- New CLI bakes differ from older bakes of the same scene (denoise, crease, stitch, auto density, Sobol-stratified first
  bounce). `--denoise-iterations` now defaults by density (2 / 3 / 4 / 5 at 2 / 4 / 8 / 16 texels/m), and the denoiser
  runs on the bake's worker threads. (ADR-2314, ADR-2404, ADR-4108)
- Atlas layout charts and packs on all `--threads` workers with a thread-independent result, and `--scene` resolves
  parts by instance. (ADR-2955)
- `lightmap_only` also removes sky IBL, flat ambient and all air (fog, aerial, clouds, mist). Capsule changes on a held
  view show on the next frame. (ADR-2840)
- The baked diffuse reserves the split-sum specular share. In levels with image light, the lightmap's reflection
  replaces the sky's on receivers. (ADR-4762)
- The v0.3.0 definition of done becomes "a lightmapped Regia-built interior passes the plate review"; the lightmap tier
  policy is recorded. (ADR-3001)
- Receiver specular is absorbed into the lightmap line (masked indirect FLIP 0.084 to 0.057/0.064 at gallery settings);
  the Mobile Low room test enforces mean FLIP 0.08 / p95 0.20; tier evidence classifies dark points (`dark_classes`,
  diagnostic only). (ADR-4995)
- A split baked GI volume with a part that does not load installs the other parts and reports the failure with its
  stable code (`volume_not_found`, `volume_unreadable`), part 0 included; before, nothing installed. A reload of the
  same path keeps the lit parts when it finds fewer than the frame has. `--check-volume` and `--sample` still refuse an
  incomplete set. (ADR-3006)
- Derived lightmap tiers repack their charts at their own resolution when fewer than their `padding` gutter texels
  are left (`[lightmaps.tiers.<name>] padding`, 1..=16, default 1, 2 on the default ULTRA_LOW): each chart keeps its
  size, moves by whole texels and gets its own gutter; only UV2 moves and the tier's `.flmap` carries its own unwrap.
  `regia cook --lightmap-tiers --json` reports `derive.repacked_charts` and `derive.chart_padding`. The gallery's
  ULTRA_LOW grows from 375,440 to 440,976 bytes. The derived-tier dark-point check takes its neighborhood from the bake
  in world units. (ADR-5125)
- The lightmap denoiser reads across a chart border only where the two texels see each other (no geometry on the
  segment between them); denoised bakes can change at chart borders behind a wall or on faces buried between two
  blocks. (ADR-7590)

#### GI, reflections, sky and weather

- Interiors with `InteriorVolume` lose their sky-colored haze by default; `set_aerial_interiors(false)` restores the old
  look. (ADR-4757)
- `RadianceCacheSettings::miss_cap` defaults to 0 (was 4). The `low` and `medium` DDGI tiers shade with seeding and the
  visibility blend and use smaller probe buffers. (ADR-2605)
- SSR/SSSR misses inside a local capture's box use the capture's HDR map instead of the coarse reflection probe.
  (ADR-2860)
- A sized spot light becomes `PtLight::SphereSpot` in reference traces, and terrain shades a sized light's specular like
  the forward pass. (ADR-2592, ADR-1367)
- Rain-only frames skip the VFX layer's empty simulation and draw passes. (ADR-3249)
- `DynamicGiBudget::rays_per_frame` (agent `render.get_dynamic_gi`, `tiers[].rays_per_frame`,
  `renderer.budget.rays_per_frame`) now counts the coarser cascades' slower re-trace: Low 8,640 (was 18,432), Medium
  30,720 (was 65,536), High and Ultra 60,000 (was 128,000). Only the reported figure changes.
- Baked GI builds its brick lookup grid when a volume is installed or uploaded again, not every frame and every
  `baked_gi_status` call; the brick-grid word count is cached per volume. DDGI prepare reuses its instance, list and
  emitter scratch and an unchanged emissive mesh's clusters; multi-bounce probe updates reuse one previous-bounce grid.
- Probe bakes pack and upload the scene once per content hash, placement and leak tests walk mesh BVHs on an inline
  stack, the lightmap crease probe stops once the remaining rays cannot change the answer, and an unchanged incremental
  plan copies its stored cell hashes. (ADR-5154)
- Path tracer: one sample batch is one GPU submission; `PtSession::run`, `run_with` and `run_ranges` queue work and
  return, and the next `image` waits for it. Adaptive stopping and the halfway snapshot read back 32 bytes per pixel
  instead of 176 into a reused staging buffer; `cluster_boxes` bins boxes on a grid; `LeakGeometry::triangle_count`
  counts each mesh once.
- Dynamic GI High and Ultra trace 72,000 rays a frame on the 12-layer High layout (spec 79 § 7). SSAO keeps its
  gradient-noise lattice and takes the blue-noise table as a sixteenth-turn jitter against flicker (ADR-5457).
- High and Ultra dynamic GI layout: the finest probes are 1 m apart with each coarser cascade 3.8 times wider (reach
  549 m), four cascades of 20 x 12 x 20 probes (19 200 probes, 153 600 rays a settled frame, 60.1 MB of probe buffers;
  were 16 000, 128 000, 50.1 MB), and the tiers turn on probe seeding, the visibility blend and running-average
  weighting. Low and Medium sit a metre higher (`finest_headroom_m` 3), and the finest cascade holds half a cell more
  at each end of its window on every tier. A fresh renderer starts at the High tier's settings;
  `DdgiSettings::default()` keeps the old 2 m layout. (ADR-5001, ADR-5290, ADR-5909)
- One probe lookup on every layout: probes are weighted at the surface point (the normal and view bias move only the
  visibility test), irradiance tiles hold exact axis lobes and the irradiance at the surface behind a probe, the light
  between two probes blends as its cube root, a probe holds the light in the creases around it, and light that
  reaches a probe through a doorway, window or tunnel mouth is weighed from the opening. `visibility_blend` now selects
  only the blend between cascades. (ADR-5374, ADR-5457, ADR-5506, ADR-5552, ADR-5909)
- Probe rays continue through the coarser cascades' mesh lists (far ground and walls are seen and cast sun shadows),
  hit a mesh where they cross its surface (openings no longer read half a brick voxel narrower), reflect as the
  material's dielectric from its `roughness` and `metallic` factors, and trace persistent instance sets (up to 16 384
  instances a set) and the image light's ground plane (`set_environment_ground`). A new probe traces every frame for
  its first sixteen updates, and the shading copy follows a settled probe through a second filter (the held-view
  frame-to-frame change falls by about a third). (ADR-5374, ADR-5457, ADR-5506, ADR-5716)
- Probe distance bricks have a sample plane on every face of a mesh's bounds and compute closest points in f64:
  boxes, plates and their edges are exact, at up to 7 % more brick memory for a compact mesh and 36 % for a thin bar.
  A probe sitting on a surface relocates along the surface's way out and no longer slides along it. (ADR-5338)
- A probe field whose traced scene stays unchanged freezes after 512 updates of a finest probe at the tier's re-trace
  rate (4096 frames on High, Medium and Ultra, 8192 on Low), not after 600 frames. (ADR-5001)
- Result: Desktop High passes the `specs/27` § 2.2 bars on all five acceptance scenes (mean FLIP against the path
  tracer, bar 0.08: trench 0.0646, color room 0.0785, door room 0.0653, tunnel 0.0633, shaft 0.0653; RTX 5080); the
  Low tier reads 0.0760, 0.1577, 0.1361, 0.0704, 0.1015. Probe update GPU time: 0.572 ms at 1440p High on an RTX 5080
  (bar 0.6), 0.257 ms at 800p Low on an RTX 5080 and 4.44 ms on the Steam Deck. Every frame with dynamic GI probes
  changes in value. (ADR-5909, ADR-5938)

### World

- A world index newer than the build opens the always-loaded set without cell streaming; `WorldStreaming::pin` counts
  entity sources toward the 64 cap and checks `radius_scale` in `0.1..=8`
- A procgen reader starts an idle source first; `procgen.run` on a scheduled generator re-queues cells instead of
  starting a run
- Terrain: floaters use at most 64 points, shores bake on the next 32-frame boundary, `detail_normal_strength` up to 2
  reaches the shader, and `GapSun::Morning`/`Evening` swap to east/west (ADR-5083)
- Nav: an obstacle set carves the mesh once until the mesh or obstacles change, A* expands each polygon once, crowd
  steps reuse their buffers, and a tile-cache rebuild replans only agents whose corridor used a replaced tile. ORCA
  uses at most six neighbors inside the time horizon (dense crowds get new velocities), and without a
  `NavRegionPlugin` the crowd step rebuilds at most 16 queued tiles per fixed step. (ADR-5143)
- World and fluid steady-state costs: a still cell member no longer walks its children every fixed step, the streaming
  step dedups cell sources without allocating, and the HLOD held-node set is reused across frames; calm whitewater
  builds no surface normals, a finite step allocates no drop mask, and the GPU solid kernels skip the SDF outside each
  solid's bounding sphere. Digests and the pinned GPU particle hash are unchanged.

#### Terrain

- A height edit redraws only the smallest rectangle holding changed samples (GPU textures, pyramids, VT pages, RT
  proxy); a refresh with unchanged heights changes nothing and keeps the frame bit-identical. (ADR-2450, ADR-2301)
- `MAX_MACRO_TILES_PER_SIDE` is 16, the terrain `Material` uniform is 416 bytes, and `TerrainMap::min_max_height` is
  cached. (ADR-3367)
- Terrain CPU prepare skips work a held camera already did: a repeated virtual-texture selection key does not repaint
  or upload the node map; an unchanged camera, radius and texture description does not rescan the tile grid (the first
  scan queries the window around the camera: 119 tiles for a 300 m radius on a 4096 m map, against 341 in the full mip
  chain); the page plan does not rescore resident pages for the same eye; draw-list words, cliff-relief flags, grass
  `select_patches` and the sky-irradiance scale upload are reused when unchanged. Counts are pinned by tests; no timed
  speedup is claimed. (ADR-5219)
- Terrain CPU costs (ADR-5234): the bounce grid reuses its previous result while the sun stays in a 1/1024 bin and
  writes only the newly uncovered strip on a camera scroll; grass selection copies the last patches for an unchanged
  view, budget, class range and height generation and does not sample nodes the height slab already places outside the
  frustum; `TerrainDrawList` rebuilds only when the eye XZ or the terrain inputs change; a ray that misses the map's
  extent and height range returns without marching.
- Terrain raycasts (`raycast_with_holes`, `raycast_terrain`, `pick_terrain`, `terrain.raycast`) treat an origin within 1
  mm of the surface (plus a few `f32` steps of the origin's magnitude) as on the surface and record no crossing at
  distance zero; a ray from farther below still records the upward crossing. `LevelTerrain::validate` bounds
  `height_scale_m` to `0..=1e6`, non-finite `ErosionParams` are refused instead of writing NaN heights, and
  `synthetic_mountains` validates its inputs and uses the deterministic `powf` kernel. (ADR-5588)

#### HLOD and streaming

- `hlod_budget_bytes` counts the texture bytes actually written and never evicts proxies on screen or asked for by the
  cut (0 now means "no prefetch"); parent proxies bake through a two-level index. (ADR-2713, ADR-3253)
- A dedicated server adds `NavStreamPlugin`, and `nav_world::level_triangles_in` uses composed world poses and includes
  heightfield colliders. (ADR-2317)
- World streaming hot paths: a steady HLOD view rebuilds its cut once and reuses it until the selection, proxy residency
  or full-draw visibility changes (authored cross-fades still advance); a view inside the movement threshold keeps
  handing out the rest of its plan without walking the cell registry; idle steps no longer walk every cell record; a
  body that stays in its cell no longer scans the cell's root list; replay timing reads the trace in place. New counter
  methods `WorldStreaming::hlod_repairs()`, `plan_cell_visits()`, `cell_record_visits()`, `pin_wait_scans()`,
  `guard_root_visits()`, `replay_events_copied()` and `hlod_leaf_walks()`, `FadeState::settled`, and `INFO` spans
  `stream_plan_step` and `hlod_repair_leaves`.
- Data layers govern streamed cell members: a cell entity of a `Parked` or `Absent` layer arrives parked and shows on a
  switch to `Live` without a cell reload. `stream.unpin` (and a pin's `ttl_steps` running out) cancels the teleport
  move queued on that pin, and a new move on the same pin replaces the old one; `WorldStreaming::queued_moves()`,
  `regia_terrain::STROKE_LOG_CAP` and `stream_cook::CacheAssets::with_manifest` are new. (ADR-7411)

#### Procedural generation

- `Evaluator::new()` schedules on the compute pool; `points.jitter`, `filter.range`, `density.noise`, `points.prune` and
  `spawn.mesh` run faster with bit-identical output. (ADR-2724)
- Generators go stale when a file they read changes; `FromShape`/`FromSpline` bounds come from the entity's own
  `Shape`/`Spline`; `in.overlap`, `in.entities` and `in.rays` outputs change as noted in Upgrade. (ADR-2421, ADR-3027)
- `plant.export` billboards are CPU-baked impostors, so `.glb` bytes change for plants with `billboard: true`.
  (ADR-2731)
- Anchored generators recompile their graph when the path, parameters or a source file's bytes change, otherwise
  every `file_check_frames` updates; the scheduler and instance extract reuse the wanted-cell set and draw list while
  inputs hold (culling can lag by up to one meter); one cache eviction inspects one key. (ADR-5153)
- An idle `procgen_update` no longer walks every tagged entity; `biome.areas`, grass masks, `biome.exclude` (AABB
  grid), `points.stamp` and mask rules stop repeating work; `biome.layer` takes a claim mask's weight from
  `MaskData::sample`, so a candidate off a texel center can differ from a fresh `claims_at`.
- `ProcGenQueue` starts queued runs in `EntityGuid` order (guid-less last), so a one-slot platform (the Android
  default) picks the same run as a four-slot desktop for runs requested in the same frame. Tool runs are unchanged.
  (ADR-5447)
- `procgen.profile` profiles a `Baked` generator with a fixed `quality_tier` at that tier; `procgen.run`,
  `procgen.tiles_rebuild` and `request_run` on an anchored generator re-hash its graph files at once, so an unsaved
  `procgen.graph_edit` reaches the re-queued cells. Input hashes separate query dependency tags with a NUL, so multi-tag
  query deps re-bake once, and `in.overlap` skips convex hulls with fewer than three points.

#### Fluids, 2D, UI, VFX

- `FluidSim::state_digest` is version 2 and hashes every input of the next step, so every digest value changes;
  `GpuFluid` clamps particle speed to `MAX_FIXEDPOINT_SPEED`. (ADR-4753)
- Auto-tile rules on staggered and hexagonal maps read the cells that touch on screen; the 2D pane draws animated tiles
  at its clock's frame. (ADR-2714)
- The window pointer no longer hits camera-texture or world-space UI roots (`ui.click` answers `ui_node_off_window`).
  (ADR-2406)
- VFX particle pools add an event buffer and child ring (about 1.3 MB Desktop, 160 KB Mobile) even without events; the
  trail pipeline binds the sprite atlas. (ADR-2642, ADR-2800)
