- `FluidSim::solid_impulses` is empty after `GpuFluid::sync_into` until the next CPU step (the GPU path computes no
  impulses). GPU fluid repeats bit for bit on one device; across devices the contract is the CPU tolerance, because WGSL
  division is 2.5 ULP and an RTX 5090 reads back other bits than lavapipe. (ADR-5598)
- Fluid steps reuse their scratch (pressure, extrapolation, binning, level-set band and mesh), whitewater skips surface
  normals with no fast particle, and separation considers at most `SEPARATE_CELL_CAP` (12) particles per cell, which
  changes separation and the digests of denser cells. A fluid publish rewrites a uniquely owned mesh in place under a
  stable `mesh_key` (`MeshRefresh`, `note_mesh_content_changed`). (ADR-5313)
- `VfxSystemDef::validate` rejects a cone `angle` outside 0..89, a directional `spread` outside 0..=180, and over-life
  curve or color keys whose times decrease; such assets now fail to load with a message.
- VFX GPU event staging keeps the first `event_capacity` fired events in slot order. `VfxProfile` desktop High and
  Ultra return 8192 events per frame again and Mobile High 1024; staging uses about 11 MB more GPU memory at the desktop
  High pool and about 1.2 MB at Mobile High.
- Water CPU queries skip work: an ocean query outside the rectangle widened by the shift bound runs no inverse FFT and
  allocates no scratch, three ticks allocate the FFT scratch at most once, swell waves for one instant live in a stack
  array, the swell uniform buffer is reused, a ribbon query outside the centerline box walks nothing and a hit reuses
  its Manning speeds, and `WaterGpu::set_shores` skips an unchanged shore list. With ring culling on and no query frame
  yet, the ring uses the larger spectral bound; a ribbon's normal is the slope of the winning centerline segment.
- VFX frame work follows live content: decal culling keeps one tile rectangle per survivor instead of a tile vector per
  screen tile, particle death bookkeeping is a pooled treap, ribbon and glow builds reuse scratch buffers, unchanged
  emitter records are not uploaded and a dirty run is one `write_buffer` (span `vfx_emitter_flush`), settled debris
  stops rewriting `Transform`, a wrapped decal pool no longer copies every decal per upload (3 allocations to 0), decal
  aging allocates nothing, and a warm or paused entry streak allocates 0 times instead of 24 (`EntryScratch`,
  `step_entry_streaks`). New counters `DecalBin::tile_visits` and `VfxGpu::emitter_writes`. (ADR-5252)
- Dark-flight damage smoke is deposited on a grid four times coarser than the tier step (1 s at Full, 4 s at Cheap),
  which changes the dark ribbon's look; `VfxBudgetStats.death_records` counts distinct death times; an event-child
  emitter with no scheduled parent reserves no sort or soft-layer space. `DecalPool::decals` and its parallel slices
  stay oldest-first (spawn order) after the pool wraps; the drawn order does not change. (ADR-5252)
- Dark-flight damage smoke deposits one sample per four tier steps (a 1 s grid at the Full tier): a fainter, cheaper
  dark stain; the glowing wake keeps the fine step. No golden re-blessed. (ADR-5535)
- Impostor cards hold a whole tree: `ScatterField::bake_impostors` captures every material part of LOD 0 and cuts
  alpha-masked holes (before, part 0 only, no holes); a class with a season at bake time also bakes a bare card. A
  one-part unmasked mesh bakes the same bytes. (ADR-3146)
- `vfx.spawn` answers `F0002` with reason `refused_by_cap` (was `F0003`, "not loaded") when a per-system significance
  cap refuses a loaded system. (ADR-3275)
- 2D pass: the sprite renderer keeps one instance buffer and view uniform per device, extraction double-buffers the
  sorted frame (`PreparedFrame::generation`), animated tiles come from a prefix table, each tileset image is stored once
  per frame, and a run of equal tints converts to sRGB once (a 4096-quad white layer converts once, not 4096 times);
  same pixels. New `Frame2d::clear`, `Frame2d::prepare_into`, `SpriteRenderer::instance_buffer_creates`,
  `SpriteRenderer::view_bind_creates`, `SpritePass::retained_instance_capacity`, `Orientation::cell_outline_points`,
  `TileMapAsset::tile_world_outline_points`.
- Crash effects: the `crash_dust` system budget is 6144 (was 2048), `crash_effects` returns 16 definitions (adds
  `crash_gust_snow`), and `BlastArrived` is measured from the detonation frame's clock with `blast_front_reached`.
  (ADR-1497)

#### World and physics

- The stream cook's `CellRecord::vram_bytes` counts built-in primitive vertex/index bytes, so primitive worlds no longer
  cook at 0 VRAM and the opt-in VRAM bound sees them. (ADR-4993, ADR-2317)
- 2D physics writes contact ends before begins within a step and treats negative `Collider2d`
  friction/restitution/density as defaults counted in `non_finite_inputs`. (ADR-1507)
- Fluid: CFL substeps include moving solids, `SolidImpulse::angular` is taken about the start-of-step pose,
  `with_colliders` refuses malformed heightfields (`fluid_bad_param`), and GPU particle-to-grid sums are 64-bit fixed
  point. (ADR-4963)
- Slip-tire transients and the AI driver use `regia_core::math` kernels, so their bits no longer follow the host libm.
  (ADR-5128)

### Gameplay systems

- Music crossfades are equal-power, including on the untimed path. Opus packets that would be concealed decode as
  silence (ADR-2385). Rumble follows the haptics intensity.
- `request_quit` with no primary window sends the close request to another window (ADR-4916).
- `ClothPlugin` steps a set it inserts in parallel (bit-identical to serial); `MaxLocalPlayers` follows changes;
  `Health::new` stores a NaN or negative maximum as 0
- A dedicated server keeps the game's `FixedTime::max_substeps` (default 5) and closes one profiler frame per update
- Damage on a dead entity (heals included) is ignored; `PrefabPool::recycle` only takes its own members and despawned
  pooled entities leave the pool; `default_spawn_point` follows spawn order; `state_hash` ranks tied entities by
  reference class; caption-less dialogue runs on game time; duplicate dialogue names/voice ids are skipped as
  `dialogue_duplicate_id`.
- Framework: `net.bots add` bots stay until `net.bots remove` (the `min_players` fill no longer counts or removes them;
  they still use `max_bots`); `PlayerManager` reuses free player indices after 255; `ShotPlayer::clear` releases the
  camera it drove last; a save slot's old file becomes `<slot>.prev` only after the new file is in place;
  `world_snapshot` and `world_demo_hash` sort entity sets and maps by entity key, so such hashes change once and then no
  longer depend on spawn order; traversal facing, swim drag and ledge slope use the deterministic math kernel. A frame
  that runs several fixed steps applies its mouse motion on the last step (was the first). (ADR-5573, ADR-5599)
- Animation: motion-matching trajectory offsets are meters in the root frame on scaled roots too (meter-scale databases
  are bit-identical); an additive node with weight 0 neither samples its child nor advances its clock; a frame that does
  not evaluate (LOD or visibility) skips joints, skin palette, spring bones and gaze; `MontagePlayback::new` resolves a
  `start_offset` past its section end through the section links; constant-rate montage time is summed per step from
  checkpoints, so a seeking and a stepping peer agree (a 0.3 s self-jump can differ by about 3e-6 s from the old walk).
  (ADR-5100)
- AI: `Changed<BehaviorTreeStatus>` fires only when a status byte changes; a state-tree enter that stays rejected
  records one trace line per streak; hearing tests only agents in the 8 m cells the event radius reaches; smart-object
  slots materialize in definition spawn order and behavior-tree instant seeds key on `SpawnOrder<Blackboard>`;
  perception hysteresis clamps a stored lose range up to the acquire range.
- Lua lookups (`regia.find`, component `on_update` passes, the entity-cap count) follow `Name`, `LuaComponent` and
  `LuaSpawned` edits made through `&mut` (inspector, reflection, game systems); `ComponentDecl::props` is in name order.
  (ADR-5606)
- Steady-tick allocations removed: behavior trees (14,400 allocations over 24 ticks to 0), utility decisions (4,608 over
  16 ticks to 0, new `Reasoner::decide_into`), state trees with a rejected enter (about 264,000 per tick to 0),
  smart-object moves and spatial-query filters reach 0; sight reads at most two poses per check. Animation keeps its
  pose, mask, curve, IK, spring, gaze, morph and search buffers (`EvalReuse`, `Pose::delta_into`,
  `Skeleton::globals_into`, `solve_two_bone_into` and other `*_into` helpers, `BlendRuntime`), montage step queries
  advance from a cursor cache instead of replaying the play, parameter writes no longer allocate for stored names
  (`ParamLayout::declared_shared`), and property animation keeps its component-name table and warns once per bad path.
  (ADR-5100)

#### Physics and gameplay behavior

- `Joint::break_force` now applies at the documented load, which is about `solver_iterations` times (4x at defaults)
  lower than the old effective threshold. Divide old values by `solver_iterations` to keep the old feel.
- A second blast pushes debris in flight, `ExplosionStats::chunks_hit` counts at the blast, and destruction poses follow
  network relevance. (ADR-2573)
- Root-motion yaw is a twist about +Y in object space, and warp windows split a frame at their edges. (ADR-2604)
- `ClothHealth` strain and the runaway test cover zero-compliance links only. Cloth that stretches stiff links past
  `stiff_limit` steps differently (better converged); other cloth is bit-identical. (ADR-3273, ADR-4846)
- `regia_ai::can_hear` is a plain range test across teams; it used to pass only for the same team. (ADR-2565)
- `AudioBusSettings` defaults to 1.0 per bus (was 0.0). `graphics.render_scale`, `graphics.shadow_quality` and the audio
  bus settings now take effect. (ADR-2237)
- Abilities idle memory: 1,000 idle entities cost 29,700 bytes (was 317,428), with no behavior change. (ADR-2426)
- A net client whose stall outlived the server's handshake state restarts the handshake, at most twice per connect.
  (ADR-4929)
- Cloth in a scene with a `SceneWind` (weather or manual) now moves with it; set `wind_scale: 0.0` to keep the old
  behavior for a cloth. Calm or absent scene wind steps exactly as before. (ADR-2884)
- `NavCrowd::sync_tile_revision` applies a tile patch only when the crowd saw the revision just before it, and
  otherwise replans every agent; every patch also replans stranded agents. Server bots finish their current leg while
  nothing is perceived (bot input sequences change and stay deterministic). (ADR-5598)
- `MontagePlayback::new` caps the combined play rate at `MAX_PLAYBACK_RATE` (1000), and `PredictorSettings` halflives
  clamp to [1 ms, 10 000 s]. Conversation captions are stamped on the caption clock when engine audio plays voices,
  and a peer's destruction op stream waits during a relevance gain that spans several ticks. (ADR-5572)
- Physics hot paths: cloth snapshot triangles are binned (one particle against 4,096 triangles visits 1), GJK distance
  keeps its simplex on the stack (26 allocations to 0), destruction expiry and cold steps reuse their buffers,
  stationary platformer solids skip the actor scan (3,200 actors to 0) and solids sit in a 64-pixel grid (909 solids
  examined to 9), and cloth self-collision cells follow the median edge. A terrain-collision tile no longer copies the
  heightfield, swept CCD reuses its buffers, a settled terrain-tile step returns early, 2D contacts follow begin/end
  events, and a character move allocates 9 times instead of 11. (ADR-5147, ADR-5299)
- Settled cosmetic debris stays asleep: write-back no longer wakes bodies rapier has put to sleep; a contact, a write
  or a gravity change still wakes them. (ADR-5147)
- Gameplay hot paths: camera drivers index names once per frame, replay presentation and sequencer sampling reuse
  their indexes and pose buffers (ADR-5135); feature packs, choosers, dialogue voices, ability grants and refused
  `WhileTagged` grants skip unchanged work; skinned palettes (32 allocations to 0), kinematic ragdolls (25 to 0),
  active ragdolls (10 to 0) and prefab pool keys (2 to 0) stop allocating on steady frames; a buoyancy sample checks
  only lakes whose bounds contain it (6 calls to 1); an unchanged cloth step reuses its world snapshot.
- Physics audio occlusion stops a ray after 16 distinct solids (the muffling already saturates at 16; 64 boxes
  examine 16) and keeps its buffers; repeated animation parameter writes reuse the map key; exact-tick lag
  compensation reuses one pose scratch per skeleton.
- The duplicate camera-target-name warning fires once per driver and name instead of on every lookup. (ADR-5415)
- A paused state tree's inbox (or one whose agent a layer parks) keeps the newest 64 events (`MAX_PENDING_EVENTS`) and
  counts the rest in the new `StateTreeInbox::dropped`, warning on the first drop; it grew without bound during a long
  cutscene. `#[derive(BtTask)]` on a union is a proc-macro error naming the type. (ADR-5905)
- `physics.field_set` `position`/`rotation` are world space for child entities too (the child's local `Transform` is
  written relative to its parent's composed pose); `physics.field_probe` `layer` above 65535, `physics.field_set`
  `mask` above 4294967295 or `priority` outside i32, an empty `curve` falloff and negative ranges are refused with
  `invalid_value`. `audio.impacts` pair entries gain `sound`, `played`/`breaks_played` count only plays that started a
  voice, and `audio.impacts_set { seed }` with no bank answers `impacts_no_bank`. New
  `regia_physics::{world_field_pose, ancestor_world_affine, localize_under_parent}` and `FieldScratch::forget_field`.
  (ADR-5899)
- Retarget rest correction breaks a tie between equally deep mapped descendants by joint name instead of target storage
  order, so a legal reorder of the target skeleton gives the same motion; a rig whose joint has two equally deep mapped
  children may aim at the other child than before. `SmartObjectIndex::search` ties on the slot's ordinal (role, then
  arrival) on every path, grid or linear, with or without a `RejectionLog`, so a rejection log never changes the winner
  (ADR-5373).
- Gameplay audit performance: CPU picking rejects off-ray skinned meshes by posed bounds, sweeps pick caches once per
  query, visits only mesh and terrain archetypes and prunes with the running best distance (new
  `regia_pick::raycast_mesh_entity_within`, `pick_cache_scope`, `MeshBvh::nearest_within`); the terrain pick march walks
  a min/max pyramid; resting 2D contacts, idle destructibles and live debris stop allocating per step; a Magic Formula
  tire searches its peak slip once for a steady load; explosions pre-filter bodies by radius; steady replicated
  animation, kinematic ragdolls and the vehicle-audio wheel readout (`Vehicle::wheel_states_into`) make no per-step
  heap requests. (ADR-5533, ADR-5535, ADR-5669, ADR-5922)
- `ImpactBank` caps its per-body variant history at 4096 entries (`IMPACT_HISTORY_CAP`), evicting the oldest other
  key by insertion order, so replays evict identically; a body absent for more than 4096 other impacts can repeat a
  variant. (ADR-5922)
- A paused state tree leaves its `StateTreeInbox` alone: events wait in order and are delivered on resume (a burst of
  more than 64 was dropped before). Lua script component properties are listed sorted by name on every load.
  `drive_cameras` does no per-frame work while no `CameraDriver` is enabled. (ADR-5779, ADR-5517, ADR-5778)
- Force fields fold in spawn order, latest spawned first (`SpawnOrder<ForceField>`), so an equal-priority gravity
  override goes to the latest spawned field (`physics.field_probe` too); fresh entity ids fold as before. Disabled
  bodies (streaming-frozen, parked chassis) gain no velocity from fields and keep the impulse pads they were inside.
  (ADR-5583)
- Over moving kinematic ground a character moves in two sweeps, first with the ground (stopped only by what does not
  move with it) and then its own move; an anchored, networked character treats every kinematic body it touches as
  standing still. Ground that is not kinematic or stands still is unchanged. (ADR-5972)
- A cloth world snapshot of a convex collider is the hull when it has at most 64 vertices and 64 face planes (larger
  hulls stay the bounding box), so particles inside the box but outside the hull are no longer pushed out.
- `physics.bike_step` takes a wheel-ray length past `f32` range as a miss; a v1 `.trailer.ron` that carries v4 tire
  transient fields is refused, matching `.vehicle.ron`'s v3 gate.

### Networking and Steam

- The net codec's `Vec<u8>` is capped at 65,535 bytes (`WireError::TooLong`), peer ids are never reused in a session,
  and `net.bandwidth` reads its window on the world's tick rate (ADR-1643, ADR-1662)
- Shipping builds refuse `net.set_client_rate`, `net.grant_relevance`, `net.set_interp_delay` and
  `net.force_correction` with `net_dev_only`, and `lagcomp.raycast` and `lagcomp.rewound_shapes` with
  `lagcomp_dev_only`
- Predicted, lag-compensation and interpolation pose math uses fixed-order scalar float operations instead of SIMD and
  libm paths, for the same bits across platforms (ADR-4770, ADR-4772)

#### Transport and security

- The handshake budget charges only packets that returned a valid cookie; a banned source gets a `Challenge` and is
  refused after its cookie proves the address. (ADR-3283, ADR-4936)
- Every per-address rule (peer cap, bans, token buckets, relay lobbies) counts an IPv6 source by its /64 and an
  IPv4-mapped address as IPv4. (ADR-3283)
- `CRYPTO_VERSION` is 2 (key epoch in the counter field); records are sealed over the handshake's split keys. (ADR-2416)
- Wrong passwords and admin tokens slow an address block instead of locking it out: from the tenth, each wrong try makes
  the block wait 4 s, doubling up to 30 s, and a try inside the wait is refused unchecked (`bad_password` /
  `auth_failed`), so a household behind one NAT address keeps getting in
  (`regia_net::session::lifecycle::{auth_backoff_ms, AUTH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS, AUTH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS}`). (ADR-5993)
- Ten wrong session passwords or remote admin tokens from one address block (an IPv4 address or an IPv6 /64) within a
  minute refuse that block's further tries until the minute ends (`MAX_AUTH_STRIKES`, `AUTH_STRIKE_WINDOW_MS`). A voice
  client mixes at most 256 remote speakers and binds at most 1,024 sender slots (`overflow_drops` counts the rest).
  A Lua mod's component `on_update` passes share one instruction budget and watchdog per update. (ADR-5870)
- Transport link ids (`Session::add_peer`) start again at 1 past `u16::MAX` and skip ids live peers hold (session
  `PeerId`s are never reused); a `Slowed` peer's odd per-type rate halves exactly over twice `per_ticks`;
  `TickDrivenMotion` accepts two keys at one time (an instant step). (ADR-5580)

#### Replication and sessions

- `NET_PROTOCOL_VERSION` is 3; protocol 2 peers still join with entropy coding off. The protocol hash includes each
  message's version. (ADR-3315, ADR-2415)
- Prediction, ack RTT and smoothing follow the world's `FixedTime` instead of a fixed 60 Hz, and the interpolation clock
  takes the server's tick rate from `Welcome`.
- A hidden proxy has no collider until it is shown. `net.set_owner` answers `net_dev_only` in shipping builds.
  (ADR-2413, ADR-2415)
- The destruction stream waits during a joiner's snapshot and then catches up; navmesh geometry uses chunk hulls;
  message layout is `DestructionMessage/2`. (ADR-2305)
- `terrain_settings_hash` is platform neutral, so Android and desktop builds of the same content agree; `/health` adds
  `game_port` and `join_code`. (ADR-2440)
- `PlayerManager` tracks the local-player cap apart from server bots. (ADR-3346)
- The `net-game` template follows `REGIA_NET_CONDITIONER` in dev builds and optimizes engine dependencies in dev builds.
  (ADR-3075)
- In play-in-editor with client worlds, the play world steps one fixed tick per editor frame in lockstep with the
  clients. (ADR-2623)
- Replication: an acknowledged quiet connection walks only dirty, in-flight or unacked copies (one change among 48
  asleep copies walked 48); `Audience::Predicate` results are cached per entity and recomputed when that entity's
  components or a resource change (ADR-5328); `NetPlayer` controllers, trusted-position adoption and prediction
  history reuse per-tick work; despawns clear the bandwidth-by-entity row; `TickDrivenMotion::pose_at` binary-searches.
- `NetVec::clone` shares element allocations and copies on write; a lag-compensation query on the present tick stages
  the pose that `LagCompRecord` adopts, so history keeps the pose the query saw; the relevance cone compares squared
  lengths (a point exactly on the old boundary can differ by an ulp). Wire formats are unchanged. (ADR-5297)
- Send path: `Link::queue` and reliable fragments keep the caller's buffer, `BitWriter::write_bytes` and
  `BitReader::read_bytes` copy slices, `SlotTable::remove_many` rebuilds the id index once, a root caches its position
  quantizer, lag compensation caches owner walks, and relevance gizmos reuse one snapshot.
- A `NetVisualOffset` that is zero or decays to zero is removed; readers using `unwrap_or_default` still see zero.
  (ADR-4770)
- Net message logs are capped: rejected messages log at most 10 lines per peer per second and over-cap broadcasts warn
  at most once per second (counters still count every drop); the server walks an entity's owner chain once per entity
  per message pass (`OwnerCache::owner`). (ADR-5647)
- A desktop clean exit says a `client_quit` goodbye, so the server drops the player at once instead of holding a suspend
  grace slot, and banning a target that already has an entry refreshes its expiry and reason instead of adding a row.
  (ADR-5789)

#### Steam and voice chat

- Lobby polling keeps the last known owner when a poll reports owner id 0.
- `config/voice_chat.ron` `echo_cancel`, `agc` and `noise_suppression` now take effect; `strong` runs the `light` stage.
  (ADR-2608)
- `content.check_host` returns a `signature` object. (ADR-2710)
- Achievement thresholds outside the `i32` stat range fail `AchievementsConfig::validate` (or are cleared by
  `salvage`), `Steam::stat_set_f32` runs the threshold auto-unlock like `stat_add`, and a locked lobby joined through
  the real backend reads as `Private` with `joinable: false`. (ADR-5124)
- Steam quiet frames cost less: an unchanged lobby is snapshotted once (no persona-name rebuilds), Steam Input formats
  action names and resolves handles once per context, the Workshop scans the subscribed library every 120 polls and a
  downloading item every 30, and a datagram batch is copied into one scratch buffer. New default trait methods
  (`LobbyBackend::still_in`, `SteamBackend::input_digital_handle`/`input_analog_handle`/`input_read_digital`/
  `input_read_analog`, `P2pMessages::receive_into`) make that path cheap for a custom backend. (ADR-5217)
- `steam_real_run_verdict` judges either leg (echo or template match) from its logs, and the echo leg's lobby is
  friends-only instead of public (a stranger could take the guest's slot on the shared test app). (ADR-5375)
- `VoiceChatSettings::validate` refuses `redundancy: true` with `frames_per_packet: 2` (a two-frame packet has no slot
  for a spare frame). `MockSteam::set_input_activation_fails(true)` also models an action manifest that is not loaded:
  handles resolve to 0 and action reads return `None`.

### Platforms

- Android asset packs: chunk state changes (`chunk state`, target `regia_asset`) are logged at info, state label
  changes only, and the generated activity of a project with asset packs logs one `regia_pack` logcat line per store
  status change of a pack, so a store-track build without an agent can be checked. (ADR-7696)
- Automatic frame pacing treats 20 fps as a 50 ms slot, so a 240 Hz panel may use a swap interval up to 12
  (`PacingModel::slot_ceiling`); forced intervals stay 1..=8 (ADR-2252, ADR-5070)
- `RealTime::tick` treats a gap of 30 s or more as a discontinuity; residency sweeps stop while suspended
- A window whose surface the OS refuses as occluded (macOS: a locked console, a sleeping display) steps at the hidden
  rate (`LoopControl::throttled_fps`, default 10 per second) from the third refused tick instead of on every redraw;
  the editor draws such frames into a stand-in texture, so UI, viewport panes and agent screenshots keep working
  (`regia_platform::OccludedAcquires`). The agent's Unix socket binds in `$TMPDIR/regia-agent-<key>/` (macOS) or
  `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/regia-agent-<key>/` (Linux) when the project path is too long for the socket path limit. (ADR-7335)
- Load-set admission measures VRAM on Metal from the device's allocated size (every admission on macOS was refused as
  "VRAM not measured"), and macOS reports `rss_bytes` and `peak_rss_bytes` (`proc_pid_rusage`, `getrusage`) in
  `seeing.memory_report`, `memory.report`, telemetry and crash bundles (they were `null`). (ADR-5860)
- Linux `power_state` reads peripheral (`scope=Device`) and empty-slot (`present=0`) supplies as `unknown`.
  `regia_platform` reads Android Back every frame whatever `ClosePolicy::back_closes_primary` says, so a Back that met a
  disabled policy is never acted on later. `Residency::stats` reports cumulative `sweeps`, `restored_pages` and
  `max_sweep_us` across suspends. (ADR-5600)

#### Android

- `Auto` pacing counts a frame's work, not its swapchain blocking; it drops to a lower slot in one step after a hold,
  asks the compositor for a rate that keeps two vsyncs a frame, and probes a shorter slot when frame cost grows with the
  slot. `pacing.set { cost: "frame" }` and `{ hint: "slot" }` restore the old model. (ADR-2339)
- Android runs every schedule on the single-threaded executor by default; desktop keeps multi-threaded. The process
  subscriber no longer creates `bevy_ecs` helper spans (builds with `tracy` keep them). (ADR-4933)
- Adreno phones compose the forward shader with `FORWARD_TRIM` (no fitted-emitter or LTC sphere light, surface weather
  or weather aerial haze there); `REGIA_SUN_SHADOW_PCF_ONLY=0` turns the workaround off. (ADR-3145)
- Frame encoding is cut into bounded segments of at most five passes on Android; `set_encode_threads(0)` finishes them
  on the calling thread. (ADR-4657, proposed)
- `regia cook --platform android` writes each terrain's tiled heightfield and a texture-less terrain index. (ADR-4914)
- Input and UI: Back arrives as `BrowserBack`, the touch audit measures targets in dp, a sub-frame tap is held for one
  frame, and scroll offsets are applied once. (ADR-4932)
- The Android `frame_p99` budget row reads `play.hitch_report` `work.p99_ms` against 16.6 ms instead of the vsync-paced
  presented interval; `android-pass` prints `work p99`. (ADR-4947)
- With `cost: work` (the default), Auto and forced-interval pacing drain a display queue that stays one image full:
  after 8 frames in a row that the swapchain held back and that started at once on a missed tick, the next frame waits
  for the following tick; a queue that refills at once doubles the next run, up to 1024 frames. `cost: frame`, direct
  `PacingModel` users and `pacing.simulate` are unchanged. (ADR-5564)
- The renderer saves the persistent pipeline cache once right after the first game frame, off the frame path, as well
  as when the warm queue drains; on a first launch `render.pipeline_warm` `cache.saves` reads 2. (ADR-5857)
- `pacing.simulate` turns late start on for `cost: "work"` as `FramePacer::pace_frame` does: without a swapchain `work`
  and `frame` runs now differ, and the growing-cost probe settles at 25 ms instead of 33.3 ms on a 120 Hz panel.
  (ADR-4913)
- Gamepad discovery walks `InputManager` once on a stable device set and then every 600 frames, or on the next frame
  when a button or stick edge arrives with no pad connected; a silent new pad can take up to 10 s at 60 Hz to appear.
  (ADR-5301)
- Packaged media extracts through a `media_index.ron` that `regia package` writes (paths plus length and FNV-1a
  stamps), to `<content>/movies/...`, prunes media the index drops, and an unchanged cold start opens neither copy.
- Display-paced frames poll for up to about 2.5 ms per frame on one core before their tick, and a tick whose
  Choreographer callback runs more than 0.6 ms late releases the frame on its predicted time;
  `pacing.set { "wake_ahead_ms": 0 }` restores the old behavior. (ADR-5461)
- On Android the wgpu device asks for `wgpu::MemoryHints::MemoryUsage` (host-visible blocks 4 MiB doubling to 32 MiB,
  device-local 8-64 MiB) instead of 64 then 128 MiB blocks, because the allocator's free space counts in PSS; desktop
  is unchanged. (ADR-5423)
- A paced frame starts when its tick is due on the vsync grid (tick time plus the usual callback delay), not when the
  Choreographer callback is seen (0.3 to 1.0 ms later on the Pixel 9 Pro); `pacing.set { "release": "tick" }`
  restores the old release. The runner drains a display queue that stays full after 2 held late starts (was 8), and with
  `cost: work` a refresh-rate change that would round the slot up tries the shorter slot first. (ADR-5946)

#### Steam Deck

- `RenderSettings::default()` is `Deck` on a Steam Deck (render scale 0.7, 40 fps cap, temporal upscale); cooks keep
  `platform_default()`. (ADR-3289)
- The memory report files UI glyph and VFX atlases under `other` and the lightmap atlas under `probes_luts`. The book
  notes that Deck binaries need a build host with glibc 2.41 or older. (ADR-2952)

#### Windows and dedicated servers

- A Windows dedicated server exits with code 70 on a native fault, refuses an `admin.token` other users can read,
  confines `net.identity_file` to the server directory, and reports failed `bans.ron` saves.
- `TrackedDevice::poll` is `#[track_caller]`; the default Windows dist bundle lists `run-vfx_lab.cmd`; new projects
  carry `winresource`, `png` and `toml` build-dependencies. (ADR-4050, ADR-2055, ADR-2645)
- Windows exes built from the engine checkout (`regia.exe`, the editor, samples and tools) link with an 8 MiB main
  thread stack through `.cargo/config.toml` (a `RUSTFLAGS` environment variable replaces those flags), and `regia new
  --template net_game` gives the dedicated server the client's `build.rs` (8 MiB stack, icon, version info). The
  generated `app/build.gradle.kts` is restricted on Windows only when it holds a literal signing secret.
  (ADR-5617, ADR-5616)

#### Packaging

- Packaging fails closed when `sbom.json` cannot be built, and `[platforms.linux] formats` (`tar`, `dir`, `appimage`) is
  read. (ADR-3935, ADR-2631)
- `regia doctor` with no `--platform` reports Android problems as warnings, so `regia doctor && regia package` works
  for a desktop game without the Android SDK; `--platform android` and `all` still fail. `regia new --template`
  accepts `first-person`, `third-person` and `top-down`.
- `regia package` leaves symlinks out of `media_index.ron` and leaves out, with a warning, media whose path the runtime
  refuses (`:` or `\` in a name); Android packages always carry `media_index.ron` (an empty list without media).
  (ADR-5795)

#### XR

- `xr_no_loader` now says the OpenXR binding is not built in this version; `ActionPoses` empties on session end; `xr.get
  refresh_hz` reports the pending rate. (ADR-4900)
- `NullXr` keeps the last 32 submits and 32 haptic pulses (`MAX_KEPT_SUBMITS`, `MAX_KEPT_HAPTICS`);
  `SessionCore::frames_ended` still counts every frame, and `XrRuntime::frame_layers` reuses its quad list. (ADR-5301)
- Simulator stereo costs: an empty panel list copies and submits nothing, quad panels keep one bind group per slot that
  both eyes share, each eye's uniforms and the multiview object uniforms go out in one `write_buffer` each
  (`multiview::OBJECT_SLOT`, 256 bytes), the multiview mesh cache re-uploads a keyed mesh only when its byte length
  changes (`StereoForward::release_meshes` drops released keys), two-pass stereo moves the draw list into the second
  eye, and eye and mirror views are created once. New `regia_xr::perf` counters.

#### Product viewer

- `resolve()` follows option graph 1.2.0 and the owner's published reason codes (`reasons[].code`, `statement`,
  `product_key`, `geometry_key`); picks accept any ASCII case. (ADR-2558, ADR-2693; ADR-2688 superseded by ADR-2693)
- Configurator paths follow the project-relative request-path rule and refuse `:`, control characters, device names and
  over-long segments. (ADR-1816, ADR-2600)
- Look and viewer refinements: warmer golds and finer opal play, exact kit decimals, a deterministic player clock,
  pixel-ratio-aware gestures, and no vsync thread for a lazy loop. (ADR-2587)
- `Camera::clamped` keeps pan within ±1000 mm and the lens within the build schema (a non-positive focal length, sensor
  width, focus distance or f-stop resets to 100 mm, 36 mm, 60 mm, f/11; larger values clamp to 2000 mm, 200 mm, f/256);
  wear turns wrap beyond ±360° and non-finite ones become 0; option graph records whose group names or labels are equal
  ignoring ASCII case are refused (`configurator_bad_document`); a schema needing more than 1,000,000 plus 256
  evaluations per value stops with `contract_too_deep` (`maxSteps`). (ADR-5587)
- `viewer.view_simulate` refuses a `viewport` that is not two positive finite numbers or a malformed `set_view` field
  (`view_bad_event`) and scripts over 1,000,000 frames (`view_too_many_events`, `regia_viewer_view::MAX_SIM_FRAMES`);
  `widest_angle_deg` includes poses applied by an immediate `set_view`. (ADR-5597)

#### Media, assets and packaging

- `.webm`, `.mkv`, `.video.ron` and `.videolist.ron` now cook; `.mp4` and other containers fail with
  `container_unsupported`. `regia_cli` links the AV1 encoder by default. (ADR-2410, ADR-3369)
- `.svg` files and project fonts are cooked assets now: SVGs go into the pak, and packaged fonts are subset, so a
  character outside the shipped set draws nothing unless the font sets `subset: off` or `extra_chars`. (ADR-3350,
  ADR-2697)
- `cook_project` refuses unknown platform tags with `CookError::UnsupportedPlatform`. (ADR-4898)
- Video publish reuses one RGBA allocation for same-size pictures, fills `DynamicTexture` only when a `VideoMaterial`
  is bound and the picture is newer, shares decoder planes with GPU convert, and reuses above-UI bind groups.
  (ADR-5296)

### Tools, editor and pipeline

- Edits to `config/input.ron` are undoable and do not mark the level dirty, and a priority drag commits once. (ADR-2771,
  ADR-4849)
- The viewport toolbar gains a Tools menu, and only Ctrl+Y and Ctrl+Shift+P stay fixed keys. The edit world registers
  `Collider` so the Inspector can edit it. (ADR-2696)
- `editor.terrain_scatter_brush` validates every parameter before applying any.
- `regia package` defaults its cook validation gate to `Fail` (ADR-4908). `CookReport::font_subsets` lists every font in
  the cache.
- Agent methods reject wrong-typed optional parameters with `invalid_params` and `data.param` (ADR-2622).
  `viewer.render_still` and `looks.render_board` require `play` (ADR-4760).
- Scoped tools stage reviews under their own `tool:<id>` session. Editor terrain rays go through `regia_pick`, so they
  honor `OriginOffset`.
- Generated LODs simplify with normal and UV0 weights.
- `regia package` fails unless `config/validation.ron` names `cook` (ADR-4908); a material-graph edit is its own
  document and no longer dirties the level
- `agent.shutdown` and the headless exit save list the tile map in `saved`; `hierarchy_cycle` also refuses delete,
  duplicate and group on a malformed `ChildOf` cycle.
- The default editor layout gains a Conversation tab. A heightmap import too large to undo clears the undo and redo
  history; the UI designer shows a picture path the runtime cannot find as missing (`found: false`) and draws the
  runtime fallback; `editor.project_input_edit { edits: [] }` writes nothing; input-map conflicts follow the runtime's
  context order; `procgen.graph_edit` refuses every file and path spelling of an open variant's chain
  (`procgen_read_only`). (ADR-5575)
- `regia loc cues import --json` refuses to replace a different `.subs.ron`, `regia loc import` and `regia loc
  dialogue-sheet import` refuse a locale that is not a plain tag, and `regia test --level` with a level outside the
  project names no level. (ADR-5581)
- `scene_budget` resolves each distinct mesh path once instead of once per instance, and `editor.budget` counts a
  model's own materials when any instance uses them (a mix of own and overridden materials was dropped). (ADR-5413)
- `regia dcc-feed send` sends a skin joint that owns a mesh, camera or light, or has non-joint nodes below it, as an
  ordinary item too. (ADR-5291)
- Hot reload: an editor built with `hot-dylib` fingerprints the engine dylib at start and refuses every later reload
  once it was relinked ("engine changed — restart the editor"); from the 50th mapped library an accepted reload's toast
  suggests a restart. `regia describe --live` asks the running app again (at most four calls) while
  `project.describe` says its answer is incomplete. (ADR-5627, ADR-7386)

#### Agent protocol

- **Upscalers (rc6):** on an NVIDIA DX12 device, `render.get_upscalers` and `render.set_vendor_upscaler` list the new
  reason `dlss_needs_vulkan` first in the DLSS row's `blockers`; `unavailable_code` is still the runtime state while the
  runtime is not ready, and `dlss_needs_vulkan` once it is. (ADR-7695)
- A wrong-typed optional parameter now returns `invalid_params` (`F0002`, `data.param`, `data.expected`) instead of
  silently using the default (about 430 handler sites). Wrong-typed condition fields make
  `play.wait_until`/`play.expect` conditions `broken`. (ADR-2622)
- No method may reach a path under a `goldens` directory (`golden_path_denied`), `agent.run_tests` drops
  `REGIA_BLESS`/`REGIA_SKIP_GOLDENS`, and `review.accept` returns `golden_owner_only` for golden proposals. (ADR-2821)
- `gen.run`/`gen.verify` return `{ job, state: "running" }` after `wait_ms` (default 5 s), and a second call returns
  `gen_busy`. `agent.run_tests` and `agent.golden_test` share one `run_tests_busy` lock. (ADR-2821)
- A queued request the app does not take within its timeout returns `F0006`, and an expired request is never run later.
  (ADR-2394, ADR-2396)
- `render.graph` lists `shadow_occlusion` in `dispatch.inactive` on a CPU-culled frame or one that redraws no shadow
  map. `editor.move_asset` is refused only while another file names the asset's path (id references move with the
  `.meta`) and lists those in `id_referencers`; `editor.edit_asset` on the open level answers `asset_level_open`;
  `editor.rename_asset` refusals carry `data.reason`; `editor.reimport` and "Reimport (forget cache)" recook with the
  cook cache ignored. `seeing.reference_trace` and `seeing.compare` `scene_info` name `brdf_energy`, `material_clock`,
  `origin_offset` and `foliage_wind`. (ADR-5626, ADR-5876, ADR-5877)
- Read-only agent profiles: `editor.procgen_pin` and the editing forms `editor.procgen_starters { starter }`,
  `editor.procgen_reload { take_theirs }` and `editor.procgen_capture { out }` need `edit_scene` (`F0012` otherwise);
  a read-only profile could edit the open graph or write panel PNGs before. (ADR-5646)
- Where an editor method shares an MCP tool name with a runtime method (`pause`, `step`, `set_property`,
  `list_assets`), the tool calls the runtime method; call the editor one by its JSON-RPC name. (ADR-5628)
- `level.place_level` honors `transform.rotation`; `level.layers { layer }` without the scene plugin answers
  `layer_unknown`; `anim.retarget_check` `mapped` is ordered by rest correction, largest first. (ADR-5779, ADR-5816)
- `kinds.list` and `kinds.of` memoise the merged kind registry on an assets fingerprint
  (`regia_scene::kind::scan::assets_stamp`), so a script calling them in a loop pays one directory walk per change.
  (ADR-5609)
- `viewer.render_still` refuses a `contact_shadow` outside 0..1 (`viewer_bad_value`) before making a device;
  `viewer.view_simulate` refuses a script that would run more than `MAX_SIM_FRAMES` (1,000,000) frames
  (`view_too_many_events`); configurator builds refuse a bad caller option or tenant field with
  `configurator_bad_options` instead of `configurator_build_invalid`; a call refused by the mutating window, a scoped
  token's window or the golden-path guard no longer spends the heavy-call budget; `profiler.capture` uses the shared
  project path resolver. (ADR-5895, ADR-5600)

#### Cook and import

- All cooks of one project take turns on `<project state dir>/cook.lock`, and cook outputs are written atomically.
  (ADR-2396)
- Recooks drop manifest rows for outputs and sources that are gone. A `.glb`'s external buffers and images count as part
  of its import, and material-graph texture ids are part of the graph's cook key. (ADR-2394)
- glTF/FBX sub-asset ids come from the `.meta`. glTFs with collision proxies and cooked sounds recook once. (ADR-2428,
  ADR-2341, ADR-2788)
- `regia preview` keeps its asset argument inside the project's `assets/` folder before cooking. (ADR-4390)
- Network files are frozen at this merge, so even a field with a serde default needs a version bump. (ADR-2316)
- Import-rule globs: `**/` matches whole folders only (`**/props/` no longer matches `myprops/`); check projects with
  `regia assets import-rules check`. Import rules reach the images a glTF references (their `.meta` still wins) and
  enter the glTF's cook key; FBX and OBJ importers are 1.2.0; LDtk IntGrid value ids are keyed by level, layer and
  cell; texture-set generation leaves a set that already has a material untouched. (ADR-5586, ADR-5570)
- The voxel far cook skips a masked primitive whose alpha test cuts it away everywhere instead of failing the asset,
  and MaterialX inputs the lit model drops are reported as `mtlx_lossy_input` without cooking or binding their
  textures. (ADR-5571)
- `regia cook --stats --platform android` counts every terrain slot at ASTC 4×4's 16 bytes per block and prints
  `android: ASTC 4x4 for every slot`; new `terrain_map_budget_for_platform` and `format_budget_report_for_platform`.
  (ADR-5799)
- glTF opens through the importer's `open_gltf` path run the security review's checks (node forest, view-less accessor
  budget, image header budget before decode, URIs beside the file); `import_gltf_checked` and `parse_gltf_checked` are
  thin wrappers over it. The editor viewport renders at the pane's physical pixels with the pane's aspect. (ADR-5923)
- `regia cook` / `regia package` chunk reference errors start with their reason code (`chunk_base_reference: ...`,
  `chunk_unlisted_reference: ...`, `chunk_cycle: ...`). (ADR-5636)
- `.uianim.ron` libraries are cooked, and a malformed one fails the cook; HLOD proxy atlases with holes cook their base
  color with coverage-preserving mips (cutoff 0.5); a malformed `.meta` audio `settings` string is a
  `CookReport::warnings` line. (ADR-3092, ADR-3090, ADR-5821)
- Android terrain: no terrain input goes through the generic single-texture cook any more, a terrain input change
  re-cooks the terrain layer only, and the cook key changed, so the first Android cook re-cooks every terrain.
  (ADR-5294)

#### Runtime behavior from audit waves

- Hot-reload copies are named `game-<N>-<pid>-<serial>`. Behavior and state trees tick in arrival order, and the
  animation origin rebase runs in `Update`. (ADR-2396)
- Save loads are all or nothing, save writes use unique temp files, and Windows device-name slots get a trailing `_`.
  Relay join codes derive from the relay secret. (ADR-2385)
- `TileCache::tick` cancels stale queued loads, and procgen `FromSpline`/`FromShape` bounds follow the entity.
  (ADR-2391)

#### Core and assets

- `regia_ecs` serialization checks map/set keys on the loaded value, bounds postcard decodes to 128 nesting levels, and
  orders partially ordered (NaN) keys canonically. (ADR-4951)
- glTF `.meta` gains `forward` (`GltfForward`) and `scale`, with a cook-key axis term so changed settings recook; every
  pretty RON writer goes through `regia_core::ron_pretty()` (LF on Windows too); `MediaSources` resolves project paths
  with `resolve_read`. (ADR-4922)
- Core and asset costs: the flash analyzer skips its window search on frames whose flashing cells did not change, and
  `det_cbrt_f32` stops at its fixed point instead of always running 48 Newton steps (same bits); block-encoded and RGBA8
  directional lightmap loads keep one copy of their texels, GPU-mesh cooks encode vertex bytes once, and import-rule
  globs match without a per-rule table.
- Asset and app hot paths: steady lightmap receiver frames skip entry-name lookups; `AssetManifest::id_for_path`
  remembers hits and misses until `note_entries_changed`; a baked GI volume builds its brick lookup grid once per change
  and unchanged level volumes skip the manifest scan; finished content requests leave the live set; the stream cook
  looks up manifest paths by stem bucket (32 props against 203 entries examined 13,195 entries, now 269), stats each
  cooked file once and decodes a shared HLOD texture once; `DiagnosticsPlugin`'s live entity count sums archetype
  lengths (2,000 calls 101.03 ms before, 10.22 µs after) and a full frame-time window allocates nothing. (ADR-5103)

### Regia Forest Demo

#### Regia Forest Demo and showcase

- The showcase on Mobile Low holds a sunlit EV100 14.5 when the scene sets no exposure, instead of a white frame. The
  showcase director stops its scripted weather while the demo owns the weather. (ADR-2486, ADR-3013)
- `cargo xtask showcase-table` shares one writer with the demo; its JSON gains `generator`, and its statistics use an
  incremental mean that rejects non-finite samples. (ADR-3013)
- The showcase's non-plain Desktop High and Desktop Ultra runs request the dense-scene visibility path
  (`RenderProfile::dense_scenes`); probe GI and the temporal upscaler stay the preset's, and plain runs and other
  presets draw as before.
- The panel's hour slider stops at sunrise and sunset of the chosen day, cloud cover stops at 0.6, and the weather
  presets are clear, fair and cloudy (overcast, rain, storm and snow are gone from the panel; agent calls keep the
  whole range). Store text says "from sunrise to sunset" and "from clear to cloudy". `demo-notices --verify` reports a
  forbidden crate only by a trace a linked crate leaves, not by its bare name. (ADR-5419)
- Package text: the README's privacy paragraph says the shipped demo opens no network port, `DECK-CHECK.md` compares
  `sha256sum` (SteamOS ships no `b3sum`), and the player README says the first start prepares the graphics behind the
  loading screen and can take a minute or more on a low-end PC. (ADR-5452, ADR-5578)

#### Regia Forest Demo: Steam

- The Windows bundle drops `run-showcase.cmd`; store texts list only what the build shows; requirements give
  per-platform storage, a Windows minimum from the Intel N150 laptop and a Linux minimum from the GTX 1080 table;
  `steam-demo-upload` accepts depots from different commits of one version. The owner kit is one page,
  `steamworks/STEAM-DEMO-KIT.md`. (ADR-5002)

### Audio, media, UI and accessibility

- Music: a bad tempo is `AudioError::Invalid` (0 < bpm <= 1000), and a transition segment equal-power fades the outgoing
  track
- `MediaSettings.language` defaults to the OS locale; the AV1 desktop thread pool is `cores / 4` clamped to 1..=4
- `.vfx.ron`, `.decal.ron`, `.smartobject.ron` and theme files refuse non-finite numbers that used to draw NaN pixels
  (ADR-2077)
- Entry-streak shed pieces trail the head as short cooling streaks, ten by default with glow capped at 0.6 of the head
  (ADR-1511, ADR-1517)
- UI and audio hot paths: scrolling a virtual list runs layout incrementally instead of a full flexbox pass, extract
  skips children outside the clip, and an idle focused text field, dropdown, tooltip or caret blink no longer rebuilds
  or clones per frame; a settled tween drops its identity `UiTransform`. Audio occlusion queues, the propagation sync,
  warm voice routes and impact planning reuse their buffers. (ADR-5534, ADR-5900, ADR-5471)

#### Audio

- The listener follows the active camera when no entity has an `AudioListener` (`AudioListenerFollow::Off` restores the
  origin). K-weighting is exact at every sample rate and true peak follows BS.1770-4 Annex 2, so readings move by up to
  about 0.3 dB. (ADR-2517)
- Voice and Ambience volumes and all five bus mutes persist in `GameSettings.audio` and apply on load and on change.
  (ADR-3464)
- `lewton` is a patched vendored copy; 8 channels at blocksize 8192 no longer decodes. (ADR-2164)
- Every bus that sends into `Reverb` also sends silently into a hidden `ZoneIr` send bus (the default mix is
  bit-identical), and a music change with no clock (suspended output) fades the new stems in at equal power. (ADR-2721)
- `VoiceChatSession::played()` returns every frame played since the last `acknowledge_output` or `take_output`; the
  microphone ring holds `VoiceCaptureSettings::buffer_secs` in full for any callback of 64 frames or more (768 KiB at
  the default 2 s, 48 kHz); a vehicle limiter `rate_hz` above half the step rate cuts every other step. (ADR-5589)
- `video.select_track` on a `.videolist.ron` reopens only the entry that plays now and keeps its position
  (`regia_media::plugin::command_reopen_tracks`), and `VideoPlayer::phase_offset` seeks only the first entry a list
  opens. (ADR-5602)
- `audio.set_emitter` with both a non-empty `sound` and a non-empty `cue` answers `INVALID_PARAMS` (the cue used to
  win); `VoiceEntry::effective_volume_db` includes the asset's default volume and loudness gain, and
  `VoiceEntry::effective_pitch` is new. Voice chat `frames_per_packet: 2` and `redundancy: true` are documented as
  mutually exclusive (a config with both fails to load). (ADR-5926)
- The low-pass voice (`Audio::start_asset_lowpass`) resolves its bus like `start_asset`: the params' bus, else the
  asset's default bus, else `Master`, recorded in the voice's params. (ADR-5924)
- A steady mix allocates nothing once its scratch is warm: microphone blocks publish into pre-sized ring slots,
  `Audio::tick` and emitter sync keep their scratch, propagation copies only what changed and sends volume, pan and
  pitch only past 0.05 dB, about 0.001 pan or 0.005 pitch, and voice chat reuses its buffers
  (`VoiceChatSession::played`/`acknowledge_output`, `VoiceSpeaker::pull_frame_into`,
  `VoiceChatSimulator::poll_packet_into`, `AudioCapture::push_callback_block`, `crossfade_weights_into`).

#### UI and accessibility

- Reduced motion now reaches 3D cameras (shake, handheld, walk bob, motion blur off), animated UI materials and scroll
  flings. Rebind panel controls are touch-sized (44 UI px), described per action and device, and grouped for D-pad
  focus. (ADR-3464, ADR-3025, ADR-4903)
- New theme variations `Panel.field` and `Button.field` give text fields a 3:1 edge. (ADR-3025)
- `regia loc dialogue-sheet import --strict` writes nothing when any row is stale or fuzzy.
- Turning subtitles or caption mode off hides the rows it filters, on screen too, without ending them: their sets still
  advance and finish, a row turned back on before its end shows again, and a row pushed while the option is off is still
  dropped (spec 36 § 8.4). Karaoke syllable estimates count English silent endings, consonant `y` before a vowel and
  word-final `ia`/`io`, so karaoke word timings of existing captions move, and a dash standing alone between spaces no
  longer joins the next word. A paused shot sequence with no named camera moves to a new top camera on the next frame;
  an unnamed `ShotPlayer` still hands the shot to a higher-order camera, and `clear` releases the camera it drove last.
  (ADR-5993, ADR-5959)
- Retained UI held-frame costs: a held caption allocates nothing and keeps its row (least-recently-used measure cache
  past 2048 lines), a stationary world panel reuses its depth bind group, target refresh and tagging skip a stable tree,
  a live screen reader reuses its last tree, a playing UI video of constant size rewrites one texture
  (`UiRenderer::queue_dynamic_image`), animated material time no longer marks nodes changed, the software cursor is an
  overlay tail, an unchanged binding is not reflected or formatted again, image-mask hit tests lock the renderer once
  per frame, and a focused search box re-ranks only when its query, items or limit change (`fuzzy_rank` keeps the best
  rows of a positive limit; ADR-5247).
- `regia loc cues import` follows the WebVTT and TTML text rules (header metadata lines skipped, lone-CR line ends,
  character references resolved, cue tags dropped, a TTML `<p>`'s source layout ignored), and `--format webvtt` export
  writes `&`, `<`, `>` as references. Translation-memory pre-fills are `fuzzy` in PO and `state="initial"` in XLIFF and
  import only once approved (`loc_po_fuzzy`; `--strict` fails). `Localization::add_locale` moves the generation, so
  replaced texts show on the next frame. (ADR-5576, ADR-5577)

### Core, licence and security

- Agent contracts tightened to match the specs: `loop.set`, `pacing.simulate`, `world.pick`, `anim.clip_report`,
  `editor.set_theme`, `steam.glyphs`, `settings.set` (`dry_run`), `spectator.step`, `scene.apply_edits` and more refuse
  or accept exactly the documented values (ADR-3285)
- Five editor methods that changed state move from `read` to `edit_scene` and become mutating (ADR-3285)
- Frame profiler span exits fold into per-thread tables (a 4,000-chunk fan-out took the frame lock 4,000 times, now 0),
  flame capture stops building paths past 65,536 edges per frame, and `log::ring::recent` clones at most `limit`
  records under the lock (1,000 to 500). (ADR-5132)
- `regia_ecs` reflected postcard decode skips type-graph walks for leaves and cached lists, builds one container
  default, probes key defaults once per type, and canonical map ordering encodes each key once (376 encodes to 32).
  Bytes are unchanged.
- The `game` SBOM profile lists only ported sources that reach runtime layers.

#### Determinism (cross-platform and cross-thread-count)

- Simulation math no longer uses the platform libm. `regia_core::math` gains bit-pinned `det_*` kernels (sin/cos,
  atan/atan2, asin/acos, exp/ln/log10, powf, cbrt, tan, slerp/nlerp, yaw and Euler quaternions). AI, animation, camera,
  character yaw, ragdoll, cloth, CCD, vehicle tires, input smoothing, easing and VFX now use them, so float bits can
  move by about 1 ulp once and then match on every target. (ADR-4651, ADR-4652, ADR-4653, ADR-4754, ADR-4764, ADR-4765,
  ADR-4773, ADR-4811, ADR-4645)
- Ordering that used entity ids now follows spawn arrival, rapier handle order or `EntityGuid`. This covers swept CCD,
  collider attach, 2D contacts, query results, cloth colliders and wind, camera and shot ties, perception, smart objects
  and spatial queries, chooser and ability-chance streams, and death messages. (ADR-1347, ADR-4651, ADR-4756, ADR-4764,
  ADR-4773, ADR-4645)
- Fixed-step clocks: input actions, property animation, ragdoll get-up decay, conversations, chooser evaluation, timers,
  tweens and `wait_seconds` advance per fixed step. Root-motion sweeps run in fixed-step quanta, and springs and gaze
  step by consumed animation time. (ADR-4768, ADR-4653, ADR-4652, ADR-4756, ADR-4645, ADR-4765)
- Physics plugins panic at build when spawn-order tracking cannot start, and canonical serialization gives map key ties
  a total order. (ADR-4755, ADR-1347)
- `ClothBudget::default()` is 8,192 particles on every target (Android was 4,096). (ADR-4764)
- `det_nlerp` writes out its dot products, so aarch64 (Android, macOS) and `cross-platform-determinism` builds give the
  same bits as x86_64 (x86_64 is unchanged). Slip-tire trail, combined weight, camber, slip angle and relaxation, bike
  lean and rapier2d body angles use the `det_*` kernels. (ADR-4773)
- Chooser guid streams mix each guid half through splitmix64, which changes every guid-keyed chooser draw; a
  `ChooserBinding`'s per-evaluation cache is part of a demo snapshot, and `ChooserBinding::stream_id` exposes the
  stream key. A voiced conversation line ends on the runner clock, and `ConversationClock` is set from
  `Time::elapsed()` (removing about 144 ms per hour of drift). (ADR-5415)
- `det_yaw_pitch_roll` takes the gimbal-pole branch (roll 0, folded yaw) at pitch ±90 degrees, so a top-down camera
  no longer snaps its heading; the fov-kick decay and 2D-follow easing use pinned helpers. (ADR-5415)
- `rotation_yaw` reads the YXZ Euler yaw on the `det_yaw_of` kernel again (a pitched or rolled facing read back
  wrong); cloth and soft bodies share one arrival counter (`SharedSpawnOrder`) so budget ties follow spawn arrival
  across types. (ADR-5416)
- Gaze, the aim/two-bone/look-at/chain IK blends, montage warp facing, `MoveResult::ground_angle` and
  `CharacterNetInput::from_character_input` use the `det` kernels; pose bits move by up to an ulp once and then match
  on every platform. (ADR-5410)
- The built-in `laminate_d1` glass stamps use the software libm; D1 stamp texels can move by an ulp on every host.

#### Licence, legal and security

- The licence is reissued as the Regia Engine Licence Agreement v1.1 (effective 2026-10-22) with recorded counsel
  review; terms are otherwise unchanged.
- Every crash-report field and upload is scrubbed, including emails (`[email]`), `file://` and percent-encoded home
  paths, more secret-key spellings and cookies. (ADR-3285)
- Project paths refuse trailing dots or spaces, `COM¹²³`/`LPT¹²³` and network or device forms on every host; the agent
  audit log rotates at 64 MiB and is owner-only. (ADR-3206)
- `regia mcp --http` keeps unauthenticated connections in their own pool (128, 10 s head timeout) so idle sockets cannot
  lock out the token holder; eight agent methods moved from `read` to `play` or `edit_scene`. (ADR-3285)
- `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` names the bundled Noto Sans Hebrew, Devanagari and Thai faces (OFL-1.1), which already
  shipped without a notice row. (ADR-5615)
- `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` and `cargo xtask sbom` list the crates behind off-by-default features (`regia/accesskit`,
  `regia/net-quic`, `regia/physics2d`, `regia/static-dxc`, `regia_steam/steamworks` and similar): `cargo xtask notices`
  resolves each shipped crate with `--all-features`, as `deny.toml` does. The demo's notices are unchanged. (ADR-5556)

#### Integration: versions, renumbered ADRs and renumbered formats

- The workspace version steps 0.2.14 → 0.2.15 → 0.2.16 → 0.2.17 → 0.2.18 → 0.2.19 → 0.2.20 → 0.2.21 → 0.2.22 → 0.2.23.
  v0.2.15 has no tag: its content ships in v0.2.16, gated once (ADR-2979).
- Renumbered ADRs: the networked physics props ADR-6000 → ADR-2902. FORWARD_TRIM is cited as ADR-3145 everywhere;
  ADR-2991 never had a heading. The `regia preview` asset-path confinement ADR is ADR-4390.
- Removed duplicate ADR copies: ADR-2412, 2413, 2415 and 2440; ADR-2489, 2866 and 2642; ADR-2489, 2866, 2979, 3001, the
  3105 heading, 3210, 3286, 3372, 3373 and 3915; the second ADR-2635; the doubled ADR-2470 rows; and the kbatch4
  duplicates. After v041 no ADR number is used twice, and the ADR index grows from 989 to 999.
- Renumbered formats: `.matgraph.ron` v4 and `.matfunc.ron` v3 (earlier fragments said v3/v2). `.ui.ron` components
  stay v8 and image assets move to v9. `.render_job.ron` encoder settings stay v3 and the product block moves to v4.
- Renumbered doc sections: `docs/06` § 18 start-up memory, § 19 toolchain canary, § 20 mutation testing. Spec 98: § 8c
  cluster residency, § 8d foliage sway, § 8e band streaming. The lightmap evidence SUMMARY puts the interior plate at §
  8.

### Tests, docs and tooling

- A panicking warm job reports `warm job panicked: <text>`. Book, spec 13 and rustdoc are corrected for SSR, probes,
  cascades and SMAA, and spec 27 § 2.2 stars are updated with no target loosened. (ADR-2681, ADR-2660)
- `docs/specs/lightmaps.md` describes what shipped, and `lightmap_forward_quad_box_golden` now fails when its reference
  is missing. GI thresholds are re-recorded 3-10x tighter, GI plates settle at 64 probe frames, and GI records keep full
  `f32` precision. (ADR-3211, ADR-3701)
- Terrain, procgen and VFX docs and spec sections corrected to match the code; nav documents and pins its fixed-step
  order; `regia upgrade-project` migrates `.matfunc.ron`.
- Specs, book pages and rustdocs for animation, AI, framework, physics and Lua now match shipped behavior. Template and
  CLI guides were corrected, and template CI uses the six-template registry. (ADR-2233, ADR-2237, ADR-2250)
- `android-pass --cycles` defaults to 50; `windows-pass` compiles each step before running it; `showcase-table` records
  the NVIDIA driver stamp only for NVIDIA adapters; a new `amd_rdna3` plate floor. (ADR-4904, ADR-4050, ADR-3957)
- Docs truth pass, release-note consolidation, unused dependency removal, duplicate unification, and `STABLE_CRATES`
  grown to 32. (ADR-2312, ADR-2527, ADR-2760, ADR-3301)
- Integration batches merged the audited repairs in order, with a compile check after each merge. Gate test devices
  are shared through locks, and some GPU fixtures run serially (`--solo`). Rustdoc and book pages were corrected to
  match actual behavior.
- About forty docs repairs bring the book, specs, rustdoc and agent schema text in line with the code across every
  subsystem (ADR-2238, ADR-5029, ADR-5065, ADR-5070, ADR-5081, ADR-5083)
- `book-check`, CI and preflight run the provenance check; every provenance row declares a primary source; the
  two-account Steam test is `#[ignore]`d instead of passing silently; `grass_weather` sealed-layer tolerance and corpus
  exceptions for `gpu-mesh` v1 / `voxel-far` v3; ADR duplicates (3101, 4762, 4862, 4899, 4945) collapsed; release-ledger
  docs are `docs/06` § 24. (ADR-5123)
- Repository tools: `cargo xtask android-pass --uninstall` removes the adb forwards it added and prints a `forwards:`
  line (ADR-5423); `cargo xtask windows-pass` records a step whose log says it skipped as `skipped` with `skip_reason`
  and reports BLOCKED instead of PASS, and `xtask/tests/native_crash.rs` runs on Windows (ADR-5806).

## Fixed

### Rendering

- The performance overlay and the first UI frame no longer create bind groups (ADR-2406). Thin film no longer flickers
  after `prewarm` (ADR-2308).
- Shader portability fixes: the restir shaders pass the shader gate (VUID-10684, FXC X3550), and VFX event reservations
  avoid the invalid SPIR-V local-array layout. DDGI probe buffers stay within the storage-binding byte limit.
- A staging reservation guarantees one large enough chunk (ADR-3189). The lite weather helper matches the lab shader
  again (ADR-3145). Render-out keeps per-job `timings` (ADR-2444).
- Numeric guards: radiance-cache NaN settings, non-finite white-balance kelvin, atlas overflow, YUV/texture shift
  panics, EXR layer dedup, and a path-traced IOR of 1.0.
- Cloud steps above 128 now reach the fade span (cinematic cap 256); an 8-bit UNORM fade is sRGB-encoded once; an
  invalid `Primitive` update drops its mesh
- Quick draw: a frozen frame keeps its 3D meshes and 2D logical size, negative extents draw mirrored, and an uncolored
  line uses the stroke color
- The editor creates its GPU instance and device under the loader-list lock (ADR-2113, ADR-2306)
- Renderer audit fixes: reused material slots no longer draw a dropped material's textures; window and hidden-window
  screenshots install the enclosure shell and pass `shadow_occlusion`; texture views use this frame's IES/LTC/area
  lights and release idle GPU state; down-looking sky near the ground shows sunlit ground; `QualityTier::Medium`
  round-trips; empty-format swapchain replans retry; frame-generation screenshots are no longer dropped; adopted foliage
  keeps vertex alpha. (ADR-4950)
- A texture view and the main view no longer share one `before_post` copy. (ADR-4945)

#### Determinism

- Cluster shadows past capacity keep a stable tail (prefix sums), and UV2 residency is keyed by content. (ADR-4778)
- The frame-generation game-field walk no longer depends on scheduling, and async sky never pairs a sun with another
  key's tables. (ADR-4779)
- DDGI brick bakes and emitter clusters are order-deterministic, stream-view camera ties break by spawn index, and HLOD
  proxies insert in entity order. (ADR-4780)
- Complete frames meter auto exposure deterministically, SSR dormancy follows frame order, and `assets.texture_memory`
  ties are stable. (ADR-4782)
- Emissive fits follow texel content, the lent-mesh trust actually attaches, and equal-age eviction has a total order.
  (ADR-4783)
- Two identical render-out jobs repeat their color and id frames, including the first job in a fresh host and minimized
  windows. The first `render.out_progress` answers within the first render (`warming`), and a cancel behind
  board-answered polls dispatches at once. (ADR-2672, ADR-2728, ADR-2741, ADR-2808, ADR-2814, ADR-4894)
- A render-out job that ends during a warm restores the host's exposure-readback wait instead of leaving it on; a
  shader-epoch rebuild keeps the exposure wait; texture memory report rows that tie sort on every remaining field, so
  two processes list them identically.
- VFX particle lists gather in pool-slot order on every `VfxProfile` (three compute kernels,
  `regia_vfx::gpu::shaders::GATHER`), so the Mobile High crash tier and overlapping additive particles (sparks, flashes,
  embers) draw the same bytes on every run. Cost: three small dispatches per simulated frame and about `sort_n * 8`
  bytes more list buffer (about 132 KB on Mobile High, 1 MB on Desktop High). Frames with particles may differ once from
  earlier captures (same particles, another blend order). (ADR-5991)

#### Correctness

- SSSR resolve and reproject detect NaN/Infinity by exponent bits, so NaN can no longer enter history on drivers that
  keep it.
- An equal-priority frame-generation motion tie keeps one whole vector instead of mixing axes.
- Visibility buffer: voxel-far receivers keep probe light instead of sampling the UV2 chart origin, and over-cap
  `set_instance_set` returns false instead of drawing a prefix. A 10-storage-buffer device stays on the classic path,
  sub-pixel moves no longer swap threshold clusters, and runtime path-off keeps caster shadows. (ADR-4906, ADR-4631,
  ADR-3915, ADR-3916)
- Draws sharing an `object_key` with opposite `lod_dither` keep separate object records.
- Material graphs: a position read with all 15 parameter slots is a compile error, failed reloads keep the last good
  generations, back-face tangent normals flip, and parallax `min_steps > max_steps` is an error.
- `restir_di` no longer drops lights on the last G-buffer row and column. (ADR-2652)
- Robustness: `GiField` probe-count overflow, cloud `map_resolution` clamp, 64-bit emissive texel checks, `DdgiSettings`
  non-finite/non-positive clamps, and per-level streaming gate keys.
- Portability: the Adreno workaround applies only on Android, adapter pick compares across Vulkan and DX12, and the
  low-RAM warm-thread cap applies on Windows and macOS. (ADR-2275)
- Hitches: game-content soak creates (first decal, first cut fog, held-view history/post groups) and per-frame fade, HDR
  display and video creates are gone, and a camera cut no longer creates two 1 MiB belt buffers. (ADR-2303, ADR-2585,
  ADR-3189)
- Subsurface on a lite forward frame reports `forward_lite`, not `no_material`.
- Renderer audit fixes: a KTX2 mip chain longer than the image is `CompressedTextureError::Ktx2` instead of a load
  panic; scene export compares a surface graph's `uses_time` and `max_vertex_offset`, so path-traced references of
  swaying foliage move; a non-finite or extreme camera eye no longer overflows the DDGI cascade grid; non-finite or
  inverted sway boxes stay out of the sun page cache, and a caster move followed by a matrix snap no longer leaves
  stale pages; the half-resolution baked-GI screen lookup always fits its storage binding; an async sky result that
  finishes after the camera came back inside the height hysteresis is dropped.
- Visibility buffer and cluster residency: clusters with a non-finite own error or a NaN parent error no longer leave
  holes; a corrupt base band is refused for later frames; a band read that lands after its mesh was dropped or replaced
  no longer panics or marks the new mesh `band_failed`; turning residency off after a failed read keeps resident bands;
  a non-finite LOD threshold gives zero hysteresis; non-finite cloud genus, look and cirrus blends stay finite.
- Images and LUTs: `.cube` `DOMAIN_MIN`/`DOMAIN_MAX` map the input range (resampled onto the 64³ grid) instead of
  dividing outputs; odd-sized mip edges average the orphan texel (RGBA8 and the glass normal chain); studio HDRI
  shrink bins no longer overlap; the fade overlay on an 8-bit UNORM target encodes sRGB once (mid gray drew at 0.873
  instead of 0.735); `TextureViewSettings::with_texture` clamps to `MAX_EDGE` (16,384) and still copies; large mip,
  equirect and staging sizes no longer wrap (a 4 GiB upload returns `RenderError::UploadTooLarge`).
- Lights and shadows: a shadowed spot's lit cone is clamped to the 1.3 rad half-angle its shadow tile covers; the
  light budget tests view-space x and y against the true frustum rectangle, so on-frame corner lights are not demoted;
  mesh occluders sample the belly shadow above the ground (about 900 cd/m² dropped to about 45 in tests),
  orthonormalize sheared transforms and clip the sun-shadow polygon at the ground; paged shadow scissors match pages at
  any cascade resolution; `box_project` handles an origin outside the box; `sphere_radiance_scale` treats an
  underflowing radius as a point light; the CPU precipitation exposure matches the shader filter.
- Materials and geometry: parallax occlusion mapping divides by the rounded step count (deep flat heights overshot by
  up to about 7%); mesh LOD bounds use the operator norm (a sheared chain sized its sphere up to about 27% small); glass
  crack normals read flat outside the stamp; valley mist caps its concavity radius and bakes zero for a non-finite one;
  the `natural` foliage wind no longer pops when the material clock wraps at 3,600 s and a non-finite time gives a zero
  offset; immediate images with a short pixel buffer are skipped instead of drawing garbage; the wireframe edge cache
  checks its index list; cooked materials keep parallax `height_map` and `curvature_map`.
- Frame state: a warm pipeline handoff keeps the half-resolution SSGI upsample layout (it panicked); SSGI, its upsample
  and SSR rebuild when the HDR color format changes; a texture upload whose bytes do not match the level counts as
  failed and skips `mark_resident`; an older shader edit can no longer commit over a newer failed one, and a panicked
  validation worker fails every pending job; renaming a `LocalCapture` id frees the old slot, and a second entity with
  the same id is refused; the scene export records scalar material edits under an unchanged key.
- The lite Mobile forward shader under `BrdfEnergy::Compensated` takes the specular layer's energy share once for the
  sun, local lights and baked-specular lightmap receivers (it was taken twice: about 4 % too dark for a rough
  dielectric, more at grazing angles); `BrdfEnergy::Legacy` frames are unchanged. (ADR-5622)
- `seeing.frame_aov` on the lite forward tier (Mobile profiles on tile GPUs) forces the full forward shader for its two
  capture frames and restores the tier afterwards; it used to decode lit color as normals and roughness with no error.
  (ADR-1874)
- Render graph: a dev shader hot swap (`FrameRenderer::sync_shader_epoch`) keeps the host's `register_pass` passes and
  the two-phase prepass switch with its `prepass_occlusion` node, and `shadow_occlusion` is admitted at the `shadows`
  site by the same rule as every node (`FrameDispatch::admit`), so a graph that gains it mid-frame no longer records
  the shadow cull that frame. (ADR-5626, ADR-5901)
- A render-out on a host that already has complete frames on with the exposure wait off now waits for each exposure
  readback and puts the host's wait back when the job ends; it metered histograms on frames chosen by GPU speed.
  (ADR-5935)
- Material-graph parallax divides by the rounded step count like the hand-written march, so `hull_panel` matches the
  forward path again (it differed by 155/255); recook graphs that should pick it up. A skinned shadow caster takes its
  joint-palette bound (`Draw::skin_bound`) instead of the mesh's rest pose; switching `ProbeSpecular` on DDGI or a baked
  volume drops the still-frame composite, so the switch shows on a held view; the cluster index buffer is sized by the
  frame's lights-per-cluster cap (16 on Mobile, at most 256). (ADR-5828)
- One mesh drawn both rigid and skinned no longer loses the rigid draw when the skinned visit comes last: a cache entry
  keeps its rest-pose sphere for its life. (ADR-4577)
- Material graph cache: tagged hits register their owners, and replacing or removing a library alias invalidates every
  entry it used, so a stale compiled graph is not served after an alias edit.
- A held camera over swaying plants no longer reuses AO, SSGI and SSR from a frame behind the sway; point and spot
  shadow atlas tiles of a `Static` caster redraw on any pose change, like the sun pages; `render.precipitation`
  `drawn.splashes` is 0 while no precipitation occlusion view exists; a `render.set_foliage_wind` refused with `F0003`
  no longer changes `SceneWind`. (ADR-5554, ADR-5704, ADR-5823, ADR-5656)
- A `DynamicTexture` written in place (a video's picture on a mesh) moves the material epoch, so a held camera on a
  profile with SSGI and paged sun shadows no longer freezes the previous frame's screen passes or composite.
  (ADR-9603)
- A host-built skinned `FrameMesh` that weights a joint at or past its palette's length or the 256-joint cap
  (`MAX_JOINTS_PER_SKIN`) is refused in every pass and counted (`DrawIntegrity::skinned_refused`,
  `render.draw_integrity` `skinned.refused`) instead of skinning from the next draw's joints. (ADR-5418)
- Renderer audit fixes: an in-place mesh rewrite (`with_unique_mesh_mut` + `note_mesh_content_changed`) now rebuilds the
  second UV stream, visibility-buffer meshlets, compute-skinning twin, skinned bounds and the paged shadow cache, and
  reaches LOD, shadow-LOD and dithered variants; a moving key-0 emissive mesh no longer goes dark, and key-0 occluders
  no longer keep their first frame's bounds; a dynamic texture write is no longer lost to a second renderer; a retained
  gizmo edited in place redraws; the texel-density and wireframe debug views no longer show stale data; a local capture
  can be renamed with all 32 slots in use; a development-build engine-shader hot swap keeps host frame passes, occlusion
  modes, the installed lightmap, the forward-lite override, camera-cut detection and ReSTIR GI settings; a graph
  material's slot 0 is written once a material clock is unset; texture views no longer shorten the texture-streaming
  back-off; baked GI capsule occluder words no longer overrun a reused screen lookup buffer; `CirrusSheet::lerp` returns
  its end sheets exactly. New CPU twin of the masked alpha test, `alpha_hash::rejects`. (ADR-5594)
- Visibility buffer: with residency off, stream-shaped meshes no longer re-read their whole pak every frame and drains
  validate bands (`band_corrupt`); a missing pak or corrupt base band stays refused; dense streamed meshes reach their
  finest band on the Mobile and Deck tiers; non-finite cooked LOD errors no longer leave holes or drop a mesh at
  distance; a cooked voxel level of `u32::MAX` no longer stalls the frame thread (about 24 s per call for a small mesh).
  (ADR-5579)

#### Upscalers

- DLSS: depth is no longer flagged inverted, an NGX start failure reads `init_failed`, and device ownership uses live
  Vulkan identity with EXT1 creation. Aliased input/output, undersized views and a bad exposure texture are refused, and
  a reply-slot out-of-bounds panic is fixed. (ADR-2679, ADR-4758)
- XeSS output repeats for identical inputs (zeroed temporary heaps): 50 of 51 smoke runs pass on an Intel N150 Windows
  laptop, up from 3 of 20. (ADR-4907)
- XeSS output repeats under load: XeSS corrupted queued frames when more than 10 executes were outstanding on the GPU,
  so the smoke's release build diverged in every Performance-first run on the N150 (0 of 11). With per-context pacing
  206 of 206 runs pass in both orders; this was the one unexplained divergence above. (ADR-7695)
- XeSS evaluate reuses its bind group while the color, motion, depth and output views and the depth layout are
  unchanged, instead of allocating one every frame.
- FSR 3 clears its thin-feature locks with the reconstructed depth, so the first frame after a resize no longer locks
  pixels from undefined buffer contents for tens of frames.
- Builds with `regia_dlss`'s `sdk` feature and `DLSS_SDK` compile again on Linux and Windows; a device extension name
  with no terminator is an error on the device path too.
- The SDK-linked DLSS build compiles for Windows: NGX result codes are read as bits (bindgen types C enums `i32` under
  MSVC and `u32` on Linux), and the NGX check stub follows the target's enum type so the Windows check-stub build
  catches such mismatches. The DLSS probe no longer reports an NVIDIA DX12 device as API-capable. (ADR-7695)
- TSR 0.5 no longer loses about a third of a 1 px lattice's members under an orbit (drawn a column ahead, or restarted
  as the wall behind them). (ADR-7694)

#### Path tracer

- IOR 1.0 dielectrics keep F0 near 0, and bakes use a solid-angle-weighted sky. `bounces: 0` and CPU-unsupported quality
  controls are refused, and HDRI downsampling fits the 2048 x 1024 cap.
- Path tracer audit fixes: a NaN sun in the cloud slab reference is the lowest sun instead of tracing to the vertex cap;
  zero-rotation or non-finite decals are left out of the packed buffer; the `pt.wgsl` `Material` contract row names
  `graph`, `sss` and `sss_tint` (ADR-3002). Lightmap denoise counts a texel reached by two kernel offsets once, and seam
  stitch samples stay on their own page, including stacked atlases with no gutter.
- `seeing.reference_trace` takes the renderer's `origin_offset`, `foliage_wind` and `material_clock` (a
  position-reading graph traced kilometers off after a rebase, and wind-reading graphs traced at zero wind), and a
  material graph the tracer refuses keeps its standard emissive instead of tracing black.
- Path tracer audit fixes: adaptive presets at 4K and above no longer leave the lower half of the frame undersampled at
  `min_spp`; material graphs on back faces get the forward pass's tangent frame; a subsurface channel with zero reach
  keeps its light; readbacks of one `PtSession` from several threads take turns; `adaptive_bricks` refuses a huge region
  with `placement_invalid`; EXR decode refuses more than 64 channels or samples the file cannot hold, EXR encode refuses
  oversized scanlines, and EXR, PNG and metadata writes go through a temp file and rename. (ADR-5585)

#### Shaders

- Shaders with function-local struct/array `var`s are rewritten (skinning, DDGI, radiance cache, SSGI cached), and
  shaders no check reached are now checked. The shader contract no longer breaks the Android build without `frame-gen`.
