  (ADR-026, ADR-2644, ADR-3002, ADR-4901)
- Material-function calls no longer expand exponentially before any check runs. (ADR-4901)
- `prepare`'s pipeline-cache stamp key folds the content salt on Metal, DX12 and GL too, so an update with new material
  graphs warms again.

### Lightmaps, GI and lighting

- Mottled walls and brightness steps along chart borders on the gallery plate. Coplanar-chart seams of separate blocks
  are now measured and stitched. (ADR-4108)
- Room creases keep their contact darkening, and small curved props pass the seam budget at the default density.
  (ADR-2404)
- SSAO no longer darkens a receiver's baked diffuse light a second time. Additive-level receivers draw their own level's
  texels, and the editor's cooked-order parts carry tangents. (ADR-2635)
- The Mobile lite shader keeps sun shadows on every planned cascade; the forced-lite receivers' energy goes from 2.327
  to 1.036. (ADR-2403)
- Fairphone 5 and Galaxy Tab A 8.0 no longer crash in the vendor shader compiler at launch. Dev shader edits reach
  GPU-driven pipelines. (ADR-3145)
- A level's load is linear in its props (`.bake.ron` version read), and the at-8192 remedy text is correct. Layout of a
  2561-prop level drops from 67.7 s to 0.76 s. (ADR-3121, ADR-2955)
- The lightmap-only view no longer saturates to sky color or tints through fog. (ADR-2840)
- Equirectangular skies bake as a solid-angle weighted mean. `exposure_for` no longer depends on the host's libm, and
  `bounces == 0` is rejected.
- `regia bake-gi --cpu` refuses a cloud field or a bad heightfield instead of baking a clear sky or no ground; a
  cloud-only edit re-bakes every incremental cell
- HDRI sun extraction refuses a latitude band (`bake_hdri_sun`), and adaptive path tracing no longer double-counts a
  band past 65535 workgroups
- Derived tiers keep the bake's stitched seams via a bounded least-squares border fit (gallery LOW seam p95 0.3678 to
  0.1119, `one_bake_gives_every_tier` passes); derived gutters keep chart-border gradients; Mobile lite direct and
  lightmapped indirect use the reference BRDF and DFG lookup; the lightmap device sample initializes render settings so
  `render.set_profile` works. (ADR-4995)
- Baked GI and lightmap installs stamp source kind and asset id, so late-joining volumes load; a failed additive-level
  combine no longer renames receivers onto the primary level.
- `regia bake-lightmaps` and `regia bake-gi` keep the checkpoint until the output is on disk, so a failed write no
  longer loses the bake, and a `bake-gi --from-scene --bake` checkpoint error prints one JSON line with its code. `regia
  cook --lightmap-tiers` refuses an invalid `Regia.toml` instead of cooking against the default tier table, derived
  tiers keep the bake's `source_hashes`, and atlas sizes that overflow `usize` are `LightmapError::Layout`. (ADR-5581)
- Lightmap tiers: a project tier with a 4×4 block encoding (`encoding = "auto"`, `"bc6h"` or `"astc_hdr"`) and no
  `padding` pads charts by 2 texels like the built-in ULTRA_LOW (1 texel let a BC6H block hold two charts' borders:
  gallery seam 0.253 against the 0.25 budget; RGB9E5 tiers still pad 1); a `padding` outside 1..=16 is refused with
  `lightmap_tier_rule` when the tier table loads, `none = true` tiers included; the tier repack keeps distinct imported
  UV2 charts apart even when they lie closer than the bake's padding; `derive.border_frozen` also counts the on-chart
  texels the border fit holds on steep borders. Derived-tier dark-point checks take their window from two bake texels
  in world units (ADR-5125, now accepted). (ADR-5621, ADR-5125)
- Host baked GI layers survive levels: a level with a split volume or a streamed level no longer replaces or clears
  layers set with `set_baked_gi_layers`. `BakedGiVolume::join_split_set` refuses a split section naming more columns
  or bricks than its parts hold before allocating. (ADR-3006)
- Derived lightmap tiers pass the seam bar: ULTRA_LOW tiers no longer open seams where charts abut (gallery seam p95
  1.065 to 0.147, budget 0.25), and narrow charts at a coarse tier read the bake's border light again (derived LOW seam
  p95 0.112 to 0.063); bake, LOW and ULTRA_LOW read 0 dark points. (ADR-5125)
- A sealed room no longer reads the sunlit ground outside its walls through the lightmap denoiser (295 lux to 0 lux on
  a floor whose raw bake is 0 lux), and faces buried between two abutting blocks no longer pick up a neighbor's light.
  Re-bake to drop the leak from existing `.flmap` files. (ADR-7590)
- Mobile Low and Mobile High lightmap receivers: metal receivers keep their baked light (the lite shader now adds the
  multi-scatter share; a rough alloy floor was up to 11.1 code values darker than the full shader, now 0.85 at most),
  and occlusion maps darken baked diffuse with the multi-bounce form as on Desktop. A bare `LabMaterial` draws the same
  frame bit for bit; no golden moves. (ADR-5945)
- A seam sample on stacked lightmap pages no longer rewrites the previous page's last row; a paged lightmap combination
  whose last source has no entries no longer fails with `lightmap_layout`; a level lightmap cooked or mounted from a
  chunk pak after the level opened installs; lightmap denoise on mixed texel densities no longer scans fine interiors
  for every off-chart tap (same texels); the `.flmap` reader uses checked `u64` arithmetic, so forged counts on 32-bit
  targets return `lightmap_payload` instead of panicking. (ADR-5585, ADR-5573)

#### GI, reflections and sky

- Closed interiors no longer read bluer or purple than the path tracer. (ADR-4757, ADR-4108)
- A sealed 6 m room's ceiling no longer leaks on the `low` DDGI tier (10.9 % to 0.11 %), GI plates lose their probe
  deficit, and fast-camera stops recover sooner. (ADR-2605)
- The radiance cache no longer picks a floor's cell layer by the sign of depth rounding, a bug that changed shadows by
  GPU. (ADR-4106)
- Local captures in closed rooms are no longer darkened by sky visibility or replaced by DDGI probe specular. Levels can
  save captures without re-rendering them on load. (ADR-2860)
- Blended baked GI sets larger than one binding are no longer cut down, and stacked brick volumes no longer pop at the
  ceiling. (ADR-2618, ADR-3998)
- ReSTIR GI drops stale hits after an object moves, and the opt-in no longer makes the preset custom. (ADR-4001,
  ADR-3349)
- Crisp cirrus: the CPU composite keeps strand peaks, small crisp amounts blend continuously, and `shear_deg` blends
  across the +/-180 degree wrap. (ADR-4845)
- Steady rain and snow allocate nothing on the heap inside the VFX layer. (ADR-3249)
- GI probes no longer store unsampled depth cells as a 12 m miss (receivers saw through geometry with 8 rays); a probe
  replaced by the inside-a-solid sentinel no longer darkens receivers; `GiField::intensity` scales multi-bounce light
  once; a fast radiance-cache scroll restarts the trimmed level's refresh instead of reporting settled with stale cells.
- A DDGI bake skips triangles with non-finite positions or normals (they baked inside-out black bricks), and an
  oversized `InteriorVolume` coarsens its probe grid instead of overflowing or panicking.
- A CPU geometry-only probe trace returns zero spherical harmonics like the GPU path; baked volumes are unchanged.
  (ADR-5154)
- `ProbeSpecular::LobeHue` with a ground plane on traced probes no longer counts the plane twice (its green bled 24
  levels over a mirror's horizon and softened it): the lobe takes the plane swap only for a baked volume's lookup. The
  default `Lobe`, frames without a plane and baked volumes keep their bytes. (ADR-9232)
- Clouds no longer leave specks on trees and roofs against a cloudy sky on any tier that draws the cloud layer: the
  composite leaves a surface alone when it is nearer than the point where its view ray enters the layer. On an RTX 5080
  the frame-to-frame change of tree pixels under fair clouds fell from 0.217 to 0.042 luma levels. (ADR-5792)
- DDGI on a floor or wall lying exactly on a probe plane (the quick 3D ground with 1 m probes) no longer shows
  horizontal dark streaks: the lookup picks its cell a hundredth of a cell along the normal. (ADR-5517)
- Clouds after a weather transition to an off-grid coverage no longer keep an overshoot's map: a running transition
  clamps coverage, type and density to the segment between its two states. (ADR-5667)
- Dynamic GI leaks: a held view no longer keeps the probes' start-up light (a sealed 2 m box read 7.49 % of the sunlit
  ground on High, now 0.07-0.40 %); probes no longer read sunlit floors and roofs thicker than a few centimeters as
  shadowed; a sealed 6 m room's ceiling on Desktop High reads 0.0002 % (was 2.16 %); tunnels lose the bright tears
  along roof/wall seams and the light under walls standing on a floor; walls around a probe inside a thick closed
  mesh are no longer black. (ADR-4948, ADR-5290, ADR-5338, ADR-5457)
- Dynamic GI accuracy: a room lit through a door no longer over-reads on Desktop High (1.15 to 1.02 times the path
  tracer), a tunnel's walls no longer under-read because the sunlit ground past 20 m was sky to the probes, a sunlit
  surface no longer adds its own radiance to its indirect light, a wall between two probe planes no longer reads the
  light half a cell in front of it, and on the Low tier a probe behind a wall no longer lights its front through a door
  in it. (ADR-5374, ADR-5457, ADR-5566, ADR-5909)
- Dynamic GI stability: the probe field no longer changes with the camera's position along a long corridor, light
  returns the same after a walk and back, a fast camera stop on High settles within the `ddgi_temporal` bar, a floor
  on a probe plane keeps its probe light on the first frame, and blue noise no longer raises the flicker of probe-lit
  frames (SSAO takes the blue-noise table as a sixteenth-turn jitter of its gradient-noise rotation). (ADR-5001,
  ADR-5338, ADR-5457)
- Dynamic GI on NVIDIA cards: a mesh without thickness (a ground quad, a foliage card) no longer bakes a non-finite
  distance brick on its front side, so a software rasterizer and an NVIDIA card light it the same and an instance set
  matches a placed mesh; the screen lookup no longer shows single wrong texels along the frame's top and left edge or
  along creases near the camera on Low and Medium. (ADR-5716, ADR-5796)
- Dynamic GI materials: a swaying plant placed as a mesh reflects its adopted base color and emissive maps (it
  reflected its base color factor, usually white), and with a ground mesh under the image light's ground plane the
  plane is no longer counted twice. (ADR-5716)

### World

- Procedural generators spawn prefab instances again; they had spawned none since ADR-4806 (ADR-4991).
- World streaming contracts: the `holes` gauge, the 64-source limit with pins, per-level sources, `cell_corrupt` and
  non-finite sources (ADR-4922).
- Terrain, nav, 2D and scene guards: no aborts on empty patches, corrupt nav meshes, nav link hangs, huge 2D boxes or
  zero-scale reparents (ADR-4921, ADR-4916, ADR-4917).
- `world.pick` hits morphed meshes and dual-quaternion or eight-influence skins in the drawn pose; equal-distance picks
  resolve by spawn order (ADR-2273)
- A terrain ray crossing from below hits; terrain layer names with `..` are refused before the cook probes them;
  `nav.links` counts obstacle boxes
- A failed dedicated-server level open no longer leaks the parse or moves the `Baked` generator directory

#### Terrain and far field

- CPU layer weights match the shader (a flat plateau no longer falls back to layer 0); odd `2^n + 1` height pyramids
  include the last row and column; `macro_resident` lists are sorted and deduplicated; the altitude range refreshes
  after height edits. (ADR-3367)
- Masked far canopies no longer fill in or swell at the switch (32³/16³/8³ levels at 0.91/0.91/1.02 of near coverage);
  the coverage test honors `KHR_texture_transform` and ignores `vertex_ao`. (ADR-3108, ADR-3227)
- An HLOD budget of 0 no longer collapses the cut to the root proxy and hides the viewer's cell. (ADR-3253)
- Scattered and imported trees now sway. (ADR-2304)
- Terrain audit fixes: height patches wider than the map or past its last row no longer spill into the next row or
  panic; shore uploads use only the declared rectangle; floater drag and queried volume skip non-finite or
  non-positive radii; ring vertices with a non-finite plane center are skipped instead of aborting; terrain rays march
  at half the finer of the X and Z spacings, treat a crossing inside a hole as a miss, and rectangular hole margins
  reach their full extent, so the collider no longer keeps ground the renderer cut.
- The virtual-texture overflow slot is an avalanche hash of the packed key (LOD included), in `vt.wgsl` and the Rust
  mirror; a coherent LOD-0 window no longer collapses to a strip and starves the rest of the map.
- Terrain audit fixes: the heightfield collider keeps holes on non-integer spacing ratios (`HolesMask::cuts_xz` probes
  `ceil(reach / finer)` steps, at most 8, out to the exact reach); the terrain pick no longer misses drawn ground about
  one cell around every hole (`raycast_with_holes` falls through only inside the rendered hole mask); tile-stream
  eviction keeps every view's tiles (the union of requests since the last tick, nearest distance per tile), not only
  the last view's (ADR-5219); a dirty virtual-texture page queued for an in-place rebake is no longer evicted in the
  same `plan` (two bake jobs for one atlas texel); the smooth brush at the map border no longer counts the center
  sample; `VtLayout::new` returns `TerrainError::VirtualTexture` instead of panicking past 2^31 pages; the macro bake's
  concavity AO is no longer too low or negative near map borders; a 1×1 grass paint map samples texel 0 everywhere
  instead of falling back to procedural weights on the +X/+Z half.
- Virtual-texture overflow survivors no longer crowd the window's last rows: the allocation list keeps the LOD in its
  top bits and permutes the position bits, so every 32-row band of a coherent 256×256 window holds 493..532 of the
  4,096 survivors (3,550 sat in rows 224..256 before).
- New `TileCache::loads_issued()` and `TerrainTileStream::loads_issued()` count the loads handed to the tile loader
  (they never go down, unlike `pending_count()`). (ADR-5975)
- Hard straight cuts across terrain at every lattice cell of the anti-tiling blend on NVIDIA RTX cards (2 m apart on the
  demo's meadow, at every tier) are gone: the lattice's vertex ids stay integers until they are hashed. (ADR-5792) An
  HLOD fade reversed rapidly keeps its progress instead of retiring both drawables early.
- `terrain.load_paint` reads every weights file `terrain.save_paint` writes (cap 1 GiB + 1 MiB; a 16385² map with three
  or more painted layers saved but did not load). Scatter v2 placement is the same bits on every platform when
  `scale_bias` is not 1 (`det_powf`, `det_acos`; scales move by a few ulps once). (ADR-5815, ADR-5629)
- Water: opt-in ring culling culls again over an FFT ocean; an oversized FFT ocean grid warns once per grid; a floater
  that leaves the water drops the buoyancy `ExternalForce`; a baked shore field reaches its far edge. (ADR-5555,
  ADR-5826, ADR-5817)
- Terrain audit fixes: the virtual-texture node map follows a shadow cascade reselect under a held camera, and pages
  that come back into view are scored again; every view keeps its tiles wanted when earlier views used up
  `max_requests`; grass with several classes near the 4096-patch cap no longer loses a class; `ShoreField::stats` keeps
  `wet_fraction` at or below 1; the water shore texture is sized from consistent fields only and clamped to the device
  limit (an oversized field was a fatal validation error); a camera cut rebuilds the bounce grid on the compute pool
  again; `visible_radius = f32::INFINITY` no longer starves tile requests; `set_anisotropy_footprint` overrides survive
  pipeline-warm draws; a terrain moved to another level claims its new cells at once.

#### Navigation, streaming, procgen

- Nav: west/south grid growth refuses shifts not exact in f32 instead of misplacing the floor; huge level triangles are
  still clearance-tested; links landing on unloaded tiles stay unresolved; an agent on a take-off point takes the link.
  (ADR-5007, ADR-2589)
- Streaming: no more `net.level_entity_missing` or stray copies for entities whose cell has not loaded on the client.
  (ADR-2317)
- Procgen inputs: negative extents clamp to 0, out-of-range mesh indices fail with `mesh_bad_index` instead of aborting,
  `Int` params and overrides keep exact `i64` values, `plant.export` refuses path names, `editor_only` generators no
  longer ship, `PerTier` bakes every tier, `points.repeat` honors `chain: false`. (ADR-2251, ADR-2421, ADR-2731)
- Procgen editor: anchors no longer double-count or clobber host anchors, selected volumes draw past the 256 cap, spline
  gizmos are cached, `requested_cells` no longer panics without `ProcGenTiles`; the editor cache budget is 512 MiB.
- Nav: a floating-origin shift moves every agent's plan, parked agents included, whichever nav system drains it; crowd
  arrival no longer flickers for radii under 0.01 m; a crowd agent stranded by an obstacle walks again when a partial
  tile rebuild opens its way; area-cost, link, grow or origin changes followed by a tile rebuild replan the whole crowd;
  `NavCrowd::shift_origin` moves a mid-jump ballistic arc (it flew 1 km away after a 1 km rebase). (ADR-5598)
- World streaming: an over-budget async despawn finalizes its cell and ends the run; a replayed failure releases its
  pending-bytes reservation once; a `ChildOf` cycle no longer places a streaming source kilometers away; a stationary
  streaming member no longer walks its descendants every step. (ADR-5481)
- Procgen: prefab integer overrides keep every `i64` bit and refuse non-finite or inexact floats
  (`override_type_mismatch`); integer parameters clamp in `i64`; `road.pieces` lays pieces flat on a rolled ribbon;
  `attr` nearest-within rotation uses `detmath::acos`; `graph.call` capture resolves `Bind`s and keeps the declared
  graph under the 256-graph cap; `MeshEnvelope::contains` counts points on a face or a shared edge.
- Nav: every system that drains the origin-shift cursor shifts all `NavAgentState`s, so a crowd or lane-graph step that
  ran first no longer leaves ECS agents a cell off; authored-link sync drains the cursor before reading anchors, so link
  endpoints are no longer shifted twice.
- Procgen: a failed reread keeps resident cells instead of despawning them on the next good read (ADR-5153), and
  `rotation_between` keeps the normalize guard for overflowing inputs (ADR-5448).
- World streaming: an over-budget despawn ends its run even when the cell has more hierarchies left, so the same step
  no longer announces new unloads or (async timing) spawns another cell's hierarchy; lockstep announces them one step
  later (ADR-5940). A replayed `Failed` read while `Loading` frees its `StreamStats::pending_bytes` reservation once,
  not twice, and `step_pose`'s parent-cycle check is linear at any depth (a malformed chain near the 1024 cap cost about
  0.5 M compares per entity per step).
- Procgen: a `procgen.tiles` error clears when a broken graph file is reverted, when a missing called graph, variant
  parent or base file is created or fixed, and when a variant is repointed at a renamed base (the cells kept the old
  graph with no error); an unchanged broken file is no longer recompiled every `file_check_frames`. `plant.export`
  refuses Windows device names with an extension (`nul.v2`, `com1.txt`) with `plant_bad_name`.
- Nav funnel: a portal that holds the path's start or goal counts as crossed, so a path starting on its first portal no
  longer bends around a portal end (22.944 m instead of the 22.472 m optimum on the oracle case). The nav triangle
  gather refuses a heightfield whose `nx * nz` overflows instead of indexing past the end in a shipping build. World
  streaming reservations are per read: a replay's retry no longer loses its pending-bytes reservation to a cancelled
  read's late result, and an over-budget async despawn finalizes its cell. (ADR-5495)
- Funnel: a portal that holds the path's start or goal is skipped as already crossed; a start on the first portal bent
  the path around a portal end (22.944 m instead of 22.472 m in the test corridor).
- `nav.generate_links { apply: true }` on a streamed `NavTileCache` no longer copies the tiles' streamed links into the
  cache's own list. `filter.compare` on a vector attribute without a component suffix fails with `attr_type_mismatch`
  (it read `.x` silently). Biome spacers are no longer counted as placed in `biome.stats` and `biome.areas`, and a
  misspelled `.road.ron` piece `side` is a load error (it became `Left`). (ADR-5820, ADR-5644, ADR-5645)
- Procgen audit fixes: a mesh or material that loads or hot-reloads after the first frame draws for generator instances
  under a still camera; an anchored generator reports a compile error in `procgen.tiles` until an input changes instead
  of recompiling every update; generator read order follows graph and parameter changes; `density.ground_layer` feeds a
  following `$prev`; `in.ground` takes heights and layer weights from the lowest-guid terrain; removing a `Tags`
  component invalidates query dependencies; cloned `StaticSnapshot`s no longer return another clone's content hash.
- `StreamStats.holes` stays a gauge on a steady camera (it grew without bound with the default instant HLOD fade); a
  `.wvoxels` sidecar that exists but cannot be read logs a warning; a nav jump crossing takes the arc height of its own
  jump point, and traversal orders from nav crossings are not shifted by the floating origin; the nav triangle gather
  skips a heightfield collider whose `nx * nz` overflows instead of aborting in shipping builds. (ADR-5599, ADR-5573)

#### Fluids, VFX, 2D, XR, UI

- Fluid: a solid cell sharing an open face with liquid counts as liquid (tanks no longer drain into solids); non-finite
  particles are skipped in the splat; a cache keyframe interval of 0 writes as 1. (ADR-2577)
- `apply_shake_requests` applies the camera-shake distance falloff.
- 2D sprites at z = -0.0 sort correctly; XR input no longer sticks after a session ends; camera and world UI roots no
  longer draw over the window. (ADR-2406)
- Fluid audit fixes: a solid crossing a domain wall no longer traps particles (the wall slide steps along the inward
  normal, and probes both directions of each free axis when the nearest face lies outside the domain); non-finite
  particles are ignored by separation and the liquid-cell mark, and the GPU particle-to-grid kernel skips them; box
  radii and trapped-air speed use software square roots; a finite pressure impulse past about 1e19 clamps to the cap
  instead of to zero. (ADR-4753)
- VFX: a cleared GPU event epoch no longer spawns children on emitter 0 on the first advancing frame. 2D: encoded sRGB
  tint channels above 1 stay on the curve and non-finite channels become 0. XR: `xr.drive input` and `*.xrsim.ron`
  refuse non-finite values (`xr_invalid_value`), and a failed session re-create reports the "binding is not built"
  message under `xr_no_loader`.
- VFX: a non-finite `dt` no longer moves GPU time; an emitter-count reload keeps the old GPU emitter run until its
  particles expire; readbacks unmap on every error; debris `apply_budget` gives unused room to the other group and
  keeps one fragment per asking class; directional `spread` is clamped; GPU events are no longer dropped by slot
  position (one OnSpawn at ordinal 8,999 fired no child), and the CPU probe keeps the same first fired events.
- `FluidSim::state_digest` hashes moving solids and their shapes before the first step, and a same-size rewrite of a
  live fluid mesh updates the GPU buffers. (ADR-5313, ADR-5414)
- 2D: `AnimatedSprite` saves load again after the clock change (the serialized field is `time_ns`; the phase restarts,
  the frame is kept); animated tiles are back on the presentation clock with sprite sheets, so a render-out `time_map`
  no longer freezes them; sRGB tints above 1 keep brightening and NaN propagates instead of asserting; the rounded
  animation remainder stays below one period (72 fps stepped on a paused clock).
- Fluids: `GpuFluid::new` drops particles with a non-finite position or velocity at upload, as the CPU solver does, and
  the GPU particle-to-grid guard `finite3` uses the shift form `(bits << 1) >= 0xff000000`, which fast-math shader
  compilers do not fold (Metal could fold the old `v.x == v.x` test, and NVIDIA's Windows Vulkan compiler folded the
  mask form on a `dot` result). `SolidField::normal` uses the software square root, which moves the moving-solids
  digests. (ADR-5730, ADR-5875, ADR-5915)
- VFX GPU events: `event_prepare` clears each fired-slot mask word it consumes, so a scan visits only slots that fired
  since the last prepare (one late event costs 1 bit, where it cost 33; up to about 349k reads per frame on one GPU
  thread at desktop_high before); child-ring events are no longer lost when the main window shrinks; never-written
  event ranks and parent or child slots outside the emitter table are still ignored. The CPU probe keeps the same
  events as the GPU under event overflow. (ADR-5976)
- VFX: `DecalLibrary::load_dir` fails on a directory-entry IO error instead of staging a truncated prefab set, and the
  entry-streak focal length at a zero, negative or tiny `fov_y` is restored (`focal_px(720, 0.0)` is `720_000_000.0`).
- `AnimatedSprite` no longer steps a frame early at rates whose period is not a whole number of nanoseconds (at 72 fps
  five updates showed frame 27 one nanosecond early); the frame count is the floor of the summed update time however it
  is sliced. (ADR-5959)
- Ring culling over an FFT ocean culled nothing (`ring_chunks_culled` stayed 0); with culling on, the tick's query frame
  is built again. Culling off (the default) is unchanged. (ADR-5555)
- The LOD cross-fade and impostor hand-over share one lattice-free hashed-alpha threshold on moving views, removing the
  interleaved-gradient-noise lattice in the hand-over band; held views keep their bytes. (ADR-3146)
- `.vfx.ron` vortex `radial` acts on the GPU and on CPU ribbons (it was ignored; effects with `radial: 0` are
  unchanged), and a small VFX pool with a larger event budget builds. `AnimatedSprite` keeps an exact sub-nanosecond
  leftover, so a 2D animation no longer gains or loses a frame depending on how the same time is sliced, a paused
  `update(0.0)` no longer steps, and a rate under about 2.5e-29 fps no longer divides by zero. (ADR-5629, ADR-5795)
- VFX: event-child draw bounds no longer drop to zero while reservations are still held (64-bit totals); `VfxHarness`
  draws precipitation at `Half` and `Quarter` resolution; `DebrisSpec::apply_budget` sums class counts in 64 bits.
- 2D: pixel-perfect mode composites with premultiplied alpha; wide tile maps keep `f64` cell rectangles until the GPU
  instance, so cells past 2^24 map pixels stay distinct; tint alpha caps at 1, and non-finite or non-positive alpha
  draws nothing; `TileMapAsset::pixel_size` rounds fractional isometric, staggered and hexagonal extents up. (ADR-4634)
- XR: the multiview forward set rewrites a mesh edited in place at the same size and recomputes its bounds; released
  mesh keys reach the multiview cache on every XR frame and an ended session drops it (it leaked GPU buffers);
  `XrRuntime::frame_layers` keeps the last accepted layers when `end_frame` fails.

#### World, assets and physics

- glTF/stream cook: cubic-spline bakes no longer hang on huge key times (bounded at 2^20 samples), zero-scale joints are
  a skin error instead of NaN skinning, `StreamPriorityVolume` covers its world box and huge or out-of-range volumes no
  longer hang or pass silently, and a refused recook leaves the previous cook's files.
- `stream_world`'s generated ground is a static body, so dropped bodies no longer fall through. (ADR-4993)
- Physics audit fixes: non-finite joint anchors are clamped, same-step `StreamFrozen` add/remove no longer disables
  bodies for good, 2D despawn/park reports contact ends, free 2D collider offsets rotate, terrain tile unload reports
  `CollisionEnded`, rotating-platform carry turns about the center of mass, autostep checks `autostep_min_width`, and
  swept CCD sees compound heightfields.
- Fluid audit fixes: no repeated pressure impulse while paused, fast thin solids no longer tunnel, GPU solid/liquid cell
  and face-velocity rules match the CPU, kinematic spin no longer depends on quaternion sign, cache playback draws the
  liquid surface, and several overflow and stale-impulse cases. (ADR-4963)
- Cooked levels lost their `LevelSettings` (gravity, atmosphere, look, audio medium) when `terrain`, `look.lightmap`
  or `look.captures` was unset; the scene cook now keeps the RON text of any value that does not decode back. Re-run
  the stream cook for streamed levels. (ADR-5608)
- A failed level-directory header write no longer follows the deletion of removed entities' files. A v1 asset set is
  upgraded to v2 in memory, so it no longer saves v2 `tags` under a v1 header. (ADR-5779, ADR-5632)
- Asset audit fixes: a GPU-mesh file whose payload length crossed a varint boundary no longer puts its vertices at an
  unaligned offset, and a LOD whose `index_count` is not a multiple of 3 is refused at write; `mods://` `list_dir` lists
  children that a mounted file shadows; `mesh_from_heightfield` returns `TooManyVertices` instead of wrapping on 32-bit
  targets; the content HTTP server answers `400 Bad Request` to a truncated or over-16 KiB header block instead of
  serving the file.

### Gameplay systems

- A listener inside an audio zone mouth's radius is routed through the mouth again. Vehicle audio layers stay alive.
- Particle spawns queued in a headless, agent-driven host are no longer dropped (ADR-3307).
- AI, 2D, input and Lua fixes: `ChildOf` cycles, spawn-order draws, a `f64` animation clock, non-finite input, pinch
  baselines, zero-length haptics, and Lua `require`/restart.
- Template fixes: F9 quickload restores the character, the mouse and right stick keep separate look scales, and the
  `net_game` bot aims at the nearest avatar with a fire cooldown.
- Mirrored cloth normals point outward; a non-finite montage step is 0; `ridged_fbm` with zero octaves returns 0
- Functional-test `near_*` asserts fail on NaN; non-finite setting overrides stay text

#### Physics, cloth, destruction

- NaN and degenerate inputs are refused: CCD returns `DegenerateMotion` instead of `Separated`. Non-finite joint fields
  and non-positive explicit mass or inertia never reach the solver; they are counted in `non_finite_inputs`. (ADR-2423,
  ADR-1507)
- The cloth budget setting (4 substeps, 1 iteration) no longer blows up to NaN, fast colliders no longer tunnel through
  cloth, and a faulted step is never torn. (ADR-3273, ADR-2425, ADR-4846)
- Flying debris no longer shields bodies from a blast. `set_body_locked` stops an already-locked body that is moving.
  After a floating-origin rebase, vehicle wheel contacts follow the new origin. (ADR-2573, ADR-2963, ADR-2598)
- Physics audit fixes: a force-field capsule with a non-finite half-height or radius is empty instead of panicking in
  every fixed step; falling destruction sections use the gravity of the last physics step, so a zero-gravity world no
  longer splits free sections with phantom impacts; a cloth sim-LOD switch always builds a world snapshot for the new
  solver; destruction breaks during a split relevance gain reach the client, and a gain abandoned or replaced by a
  snapshot no longer breaks the stream. (ADR-4773, ADR-5572)
- A root-motion character on a moving platform after a frame hitch is carried once per fixed step, not once per sweep
  quantum (0.92 m against 0.76 m steady on a 2 m/s platform), and the quantum clamp covers the whole bankable window.
  (ADR-5410)
- Destruction, debris and `DebrisSpawner` spawns store the authored quaternion bits, and a zero spawn rotation parks
  at identity and counts in `PhysicsStats::non_finite_inputs` instead of reaching rapier. (ADR-5447)
- `AircraftPlugin` panics at build when spawn-order tracking cannot start, like the physics plugins, instead of sorting
  by entity id. (ADR-5411)
- Force fields: a field on a child entity acts, and is reported by `physics.fields` and `physics.field_probe`, at its
  composed `Transform` chain pose (current in the fixed step) instead of last frame's `GlobalTransform`; a body counts
  as on a layer when any collider attached to it (its own, a child's, compound bodies included) is; a body whose
  colliders are all on layers past 31 is affected by no field; a gravity override under non-finite level gravity
  cancels the last-finite gravity the step integrates; a non-positive `AirDensity` applies no wind drag; removing or
  despawning a `ForceField` forgets only its own impulse-pad occupants, so other pads no longer re-fire.
  `apply_wind_drag` runs in rapier handle order and allocates nothing once warm. (ADR-5899)
- Physics audio: the footstep-surface ray skips the walker's whole body (a capsule on the walker, on a child, or on the
  body a child "feet" walker hangs under); an impact that starts no voice starts the body cooldown but does not charge
  the per-step cap or count as played, so inaudible far impacts no longer starve an audible one; a scrape or roll voice
  restarts when its resolved clip changes; an out-of-range rattle no longer logs an impact every step. (ADR-5899)
- Explosions pick the bodies to push by center of mass: bodies with child-entity colliders, sensor-only colliders,
  colliders on filtered layers, or no collider but an explicit mass are pushed again, and a compound body is no longer
  shielded by its own child collider (`Occlusion::RayCast`). (ADR-5898)
- Fracture clipping keeps a source triangle lying on a cut plane only in the cell it faces out of; it was kept by both
  cells and capped again, so a coplanar step wall had three times its area. The tire peak-angle cache key covers
  `load_exponent`, so a tire edited at run time with the same contact load no longer reuses a stale peak angle.
- Physics audit fixes: a destruction section whose chunk hull rapier cannot build no longer panics or maps later chunks
  to the wrong collider, and a landed section no longer splits under its own support force; `SlipWeighted` tires without
  camber or relaxation stay in the friction circle; non-unit spawn rotations no longer skew rapier's rotation matrix; a
  clockwise vortex respects `max_speed`; joint break forces above 64 solver iterations are not overstated; tiled terrain
  gets its collision back after a park and unpark, an all-holes tile no longer blocks `terrain_collision` cells, and
  `contact_sample` answers on streamed ground; a body rebuilt with `StreamFrozen` stays frozen; cloth rescues fast
  particles across grid cells and its broad phase no longer goes all-pairs on coarse cloth. (ADR-5583)
- A negative `Sphere` or `Capsule` force-field radius is an empty volume; a non-finite world gravity measures overrides
  against the last finite gravity; `VehicleSimWorld` clamps solver iterations and CCD substeps like the live step; the
  bike balance model refuses a non-finite lean and its clamped lean step stops at the target; a grip-clamped tire scales
  its `camber_thrust` readout; the cloth strain readout skips non-finite ratios; `MovingSolid::is_valid` requires a unit
  quaternion.

#### Animation, AI, input, scripting

- Yaw root motion on Z-up pitched roots turns correctly, and warped frames no longer warp motion outside the window.
  Frame hitches no longer tunnel thin walls, and spring substeps catch up instead of dropping time. (ADR-2604, ADR-4652)
- Socket and spring-bone panics on short or swapped skeletons are fixed. `UpdateMode::Lod` uses world position, and
  finished state trees stop counting ticks. (ADR-2233)
- A jump no longer fires three times on a 50 ms hitch. Quick clicks are kept, long sessions no longer flip tap
  classification, 0..1 triggers rest at 0, and SpaceMouse padding bits are ignored. (ADR-4768)
- Lua mods: no panic when the host cap is below the clamp floor, restart and failed loads despawn the mod's entities,
  and `require`, `math.random` and `string.gsub` follow Lua 5.1 edge cases.
- Decal libraries load in sorted order and all-or-nothing, and over-capacity GPU event frames keep events
  deterministically. (ADR-4811)
- Animation audit fixes: corrupt joint or morph-target ids no longer make clip measurement or morph paths ask for
  gigabytes and abort (`MAX_MEASURED_JOINTS`, `MAX_MORPH_TARGETS`); huge `exclude_window`s, montage rates and
  self-jumping sections no longer overflow or hang; montage time past 2^24 steps stays in `f64`; cooked motion
  databases with a bad sample rate are refused; pose search keeps statistics in `f64`, takes Z-up root yaw as the
  twist about +Y and uses the deterministic facing kernel; parked motion-matching rigs no longer bank root motion; stale
  layer and montage masks, address-keyed IK caches, reused aim-IK buffers and the growing morph-list pool are fixed;
  non-finite pose-space correctives are refused; springs under 1 Hz step; `det_sin_cos` stays in range past 1e6 rad.
- AI audit fixes: replacing a behavior or state tree drops its spatial queries; the sight sweep visits targets in
  perceivable arrival order under a sight budget; hearing uses the listener's current position; the fixed-step
  smart-object index sees `GlobalTransform`-only moves and its rebuild no longer allocates; untracked spatial-query
  owners keep distinct seeds; server bots plan from the local pose with `det_atan2` and spawn-order ties;
  `RunSpatialQuery` clears stale `_error` and `_position` keys; an expired attack-token waiter rejoins behind surviving
  peers. (ADR-1347, ADR-4651, ADR-5598)
- Input audit fixes: assist cool-down and `Smooth` hold on a zero-delta frame, `exp_f32(88.4)` is finite, and NaN
  trigger, button or stick samples store 0. (ADR-5381)
- AI: smart-object distance ties and perception sight order follow slot and spawn order with or without a log;
  fixed-step smart-object refresh sees `GlobalTransform`-only moves and is not rebuilt again in `PostUpdate`;
  `InsideZone` uses the current local transform chain; `NearbyEntities(name: ...)` emits in `Name` arrival order.
- Input: a press on a frame whose fixed loop banks reaches the next fixed step; input-recording playback injects per
  fixed step; `FixedTime::would_run` honors an `hz` change; gilrs trigger ranges latch per pad (a −1..1 pad no longer
  collapses at half pull); descriptor-less SpaceMouse layouts keep buttons 3+ on Windows, and SpaceMouse button
  messages and `SpaceMouseNav` toggles follow the merged state.
- Animation: the montage cursor computes elapsed time in `f64` and keeps moving past 2^24 steps; a huge finite step
  through a self-jumping section consumes whole loops with one division; `skin_points` refuses weights whose sum
  overflows with `SkinPointsError::BadValue` (`skin_bad_value`) instead of collapsing the vertex to the origin;
  `MontagePlayback::new` resolves a `start_offset` across section links. Marker-synced followers no longer count a
  shared loop wrap twice (a walk/run blend added an extra lap of root motion each leader cycle). (ADR-5733)
- Input and time: a `Tap` pressed on a stepped frame and released on a banked frame triggers on the next fixed step.
  After `Time::set_clocks` moves the clock forward (a render-out job handing the session time back), the fixed loop
  re-anchors, so `FixedUpdate` and `Update` read the same `Time::elapsed()` within one fixed step; Lua `elapsed_secs`
  and the conversation clock disagreed after every render-out job.
- Lua mods: a failed hot reload rolls script-component writes back newest-first (a changed-then-removed component gets
  its pre-trial values back); a component `on_update` pass is charged inside its own budgeted call, and an empty pass
  no longer leaves a sticky charge that disabled the mod; non-finite `vec3` properties and non-finite `min`/`max`/`step`
  bounds are refused with `lua_bad_value`, as is a table whose number key and text key would merge (`[1]` and `["1"]`);
  a refused `regia.emit` no longer uses emit budget.
- Lua cost: with the plugin's name index, `regia.find` charges one probe instead of the world's entity count (an
  equal-name `find_cost` of 32 is now 1); a component pass visits only the entities carrying that declaration (3
  instead of 43 with 40 bystanders); `e:set_prop` writes one field; the directory watch compares size and modification
  time before rereading file bodies; handles for one entity are one Lua userdata, so they are one table key.
- The smart-object index rebuild no longer allocates a scratch buffer per slot move (115,200 bytes per rebuild at 1600
  slots); it sorts once by role and arrival, and the order is unchanged. (ADR-5718)
- Montage playback without a rate curve covers the same montage time every step (the per-step length is `f64` into the
  section walk; it was off by up to 0.7 % of a 60 Hz step after half an hour), a step past several loops of a
  self-jumping section consumes them at once, `MontagePlayback::new` resolves a `start_offset` at a section end through
  the section links, `skin_points` refuses finite weights that sum to `+inf`, and a key release that only a banked
  zero-step frame saw no longer restarts a binding's `Hold`/`Tap` timer. (ADR-5711, ADR-5733)
- Touch input: a tracked touch cancelled in a message batch stays cancelled when a delayed `Ended` follows (no tap), and
  an untracked Android joystick touch end or cancel releases the stick to center.
- Touch `VirtualStick` and `VirtualButton` drive their input actions (they filled only `VirtualAxes`/`VirtualButtons`,
  which nothing read); the widget releases only the holds it made. (ADR-5610)
- `ScriptsSet::Late` runs `late_update`, so systems ordered against it get real ordering (ADR-5613);
  `EyeLimits::default()` uses exactly 35°/40°/26°/31° (ADR-5614); the retargeter tie-breaks equal-depth mapped
  descendants by joint name, so a legal sibling reorder no longer changes the chest's output (ADR-5863);
  `MontagePlayback` steps in `f64`, so a networked tick keeps moving past step 2^24 and an over-wide step stops the
  section walk instead of spinning (ADR-5535).
- `.montagevar.ron` refuses unknown fields (a variant naming `sections`, an override naming `rate`); they loaded and
  were ignored. Abilities log `unknown_attribute` once per application, including live modifiers, and not for
  suspended effects. (ADR-5633, ADR-5639)
- Lua: a component declaration whose `on_update` no entity carries no longer runs a charged empty pass every frame (it
  could disable a mod with a small instruction budget); script passes keep their entity query (9 allocations per update
  gone); the smart-object index rebuild allocates nothing again. (ADR-5816, ADR-5554, ADR-5632)
- `curves.import` without `CurveTablePlugin` answers `{ available: false }` before parsing; a NaN in a chooser
  `Value(..)`, emit cell or catch-all reports `chooser_bad_number`; a `.functest.ron` `do` action the input service
  refuses fails its step (the pass reported "all steps passed"). (ADR-5638, ADR-5637)
- Input: mouse look read in `Update` works below the fixed rate (at 30 fps with the 60 Hz fixed rate it did not move at
  all); `Hold` and `Tap` time their own binding instead of the action's `elapsed`, which a sibling binding had advanced;
  Android gamepad discovery keeps its connect hint under the multi-threaded executor (the first pad was seen up to 600
  frames late). (ADR-5599)
- Animation: scripted rotation warps extrapolate the remaining yaw at the translation's rate, so a rig no longer turns
  off its facing and snaps on the last frame; marker-synced followers keep a multi-lap leader step's laps; chain IK on a
  short parent list is an invalid chain instead of a panic; long montage playbacks take step lengths in `f64` (from step
  2^24, about 77.7 h at 60 Hz, every other step stalled); `skin_points` refuses weights that sum to `+inf`
  (`skin_bad_value`); CPU-skinned normals use the inverse-transpose under non-uniform scale; a failed motion-match
  search keeps `last_query()`; pose search at the end of a non-looping clip jumps back to the matched frame; a
  `BlendSpace2D` sample moved in place re-triangulates.
- Lua: handles a mod no longer holds are collected, so long sessions no longer grow toward the mod's memory limit; a
  failed `lua.restart` removes the old run's entities even when `on_start` fails; a mod whose fresh load failed keeps
  its entry chunk's despawns; the mod watch no longer rereads every body after a touch or unchanged save, and catches an
  atomic same-size save that kept the modification time (Unix). (ADR-5606, ADR-5895)

#### Gameplay and agent methods

- Framework fixes: chooser `drop_beyond` with non-finite gaps, `save_count` after a failed save, spectator follow at
  identity pose, `regia run --level` reload for `.level.d` levels, and active ragdoll hit timing.
- Agent input validation: cloth, weather, GI debug, `biome.claim`, `physics.overlap`, `audio.propagation`,
  `procgen.tiles_set`, `functest.create` and `nav.cook_triangle_areas` validate before mutating;
  `localization.set_locale` persists the resolved tag; rejected `ui.list` / `anim.scripted_warp` edits keep prior state;
  `types.describe` reports correct enum/tuple/`Vec3` schemas; `render.set_upscaler` returns `F0003` before changing
  quality. (ADR-4641 proposed)
- Offscreen pipeline warm startup preserves the original renderer on worker failure. (ADR-4641 proposed)
- Framework audit fixes: the conversation clock follows `Time::elapsed` (it lost 667 ns per 1/60 s step) and keeps
  running after `Time::set_clocks`; a voiced line's deadline no longer adds a device clock lead; unvoiced captions show
  when the audio clock is ahead; overlapping subtitle cues resolve to the later cue; wind drag ignores negative density
  and non-finite `dt`; a world-snapshot string equal to an entity id is not a reference; save migration reads the
  CRC-covered payload version; a `.prev` over `MAX_SAVE_FILE_BYTES` is refused from its length; a rail named by several
  splines follows the lowest entity id and ignores a non-finite follow target; `WhileTagged` abilities start once a
  reload, cheat toggle, level change or same-turn grant allows them. (ADR-4756, ADR-5572)
- `ShotPlayer` with no named camera re-picks the top camera each frame, so a higher-order camera spawned mid-shot takes
  over; sidecar-less level ids fold `.` and `..` segments. (ADR-5416)
- `conversation.say` and `conversation.play` stamp lines from `ConversationClock`, not the audio device clock.
  (ADR-5415)
- Framework: saved volumes, mutes, look sensitivity and invert, phone vibration and input assists apply at launch; a
  failed save no longer loses `<slot>.prev`, and `.bad-N` copies drop the oldest first; long-running servers keep adding
  bots after 255 bot joins; editing a middle sequence section's end updates the sequence duration; marker CSV sheets
  with blank lines load. (ADR-5573)
- A moving kinematic platform carries a character once, whatever the character touches: over 240 steps a character
  against a platform's railing slid 0.6 m at 3 m/s, a walking one jumped 5 cm ahead, and one on a rising platform
  counted as airborne; now each keeps its place within 2 mm and stays grounded. (ADR-5972)
- Fixed-step `Time::elapsed` no longer jumps by time the substep cap discarded (a 100 ms frame at 100 Hz with a 3-step
  cap reported 110 ms instead of 40 ms); a frozen `RunFrames` loop no longer simulates the frame that clears pause;
  `FixedTime::overstep_fraction` is 0 while the bank was accumulated at another rate.

### Networking and Steam

- Forged `ConnectRequest`s no longer keep a victim from joining; one IPv6 /64 can no longer take every slot or dodge a
  ban; `session.connect` no longer sends a password to an unpinned key. (ADR-3283, ADR-4936)
- A new connection resets prediction, the interpolation clock and proxy snapshot rings; state-ack compares survive the
  2^32 wrap; uplink loss and `NetVec` id exhaustion are counted correctly.
- `InterestGrid::query` no longer loops over 2^64 cells for a huge radius.
- The default state channel places root positions in the client's floating-origin frame, and rapier-moved bodies are
  gathered. (ADR-2902)
- A server decodes client messages without loading assets the client names. (ADR-2665)
- Destruction snapshots past 64 KiB or 256 ticks no longer restart for ever. (ADR-2305)
- Clients no longer start prediction with a 0 ms RTT, and a short lead resyncs once instead of in a chain. (ADR-4915)
- Dedicated apps accept input from live link peers alongside session players. (ADR-4861)
- The connect timeout counts from the handshake's last step, so a stalled windowed client no longer times out; a client
  built together with the server refuses to start instead of running as a server (`regia::SERVER_BUILD`). (ADR-3075)
- Joint-attached hit shapes no longer lag a frame with `exact_tick_joints`. A level keeps `Replicated`, `NetGroups`,
  `NetAsleep` and `NetPredicted` in the editor. (ADR-2551, ADR-2623)
- A child whose parent stops replicating is sent in the shared frame again. `NetSessionPlugin` applies the `session`
  section of `net.ron`. Exact-address bans expire on injected time.
- `#[replicate]` rejects literal `bits` outside the codec range. Steam lobby diffs and achievement queries were made
  cheaper.
- A forged plain `Challenge` no longer aborts a sealed handshake; redundant input commands carry their own interpolation
  view; an oversized parent spawn no longer holds back children
- State acks and in-flight packets survive the sequence wrap, and unreliable dedup no longer drops honest reordered
  messages
- Flood forgiveness follows the simulation clock, ban checks use the injected clock, net RON writers save `\n` on
  Windows, and codec stats no longer depend on the pool size (ADR-4772, ADR-3150)
- Client `net.replicated` and `net.entity_state` report the real copy; a 65,536-byte payload no longer encodes empty
- Client backlog overflow ends the connection (`ReliableOverflow`) instead of splicing frames; reused asset slots unbind
  old keys; only breaching ticks count toward the flood disconnect; `net.bandwidth` uses the server tick clock.
- Net audit fixes: client lead resync across tick 2^31 and lead adjustments past 2^24 compute in integers;
  `stable_pow2` stays finite just below 128; non-finite lag-compensation rotations record identity; an unreliable
  sequence half the `u32` space ahead no longer becomes the window floor and blocks the sender; registering an asset
  key on a second live handle moves the key to the newer handle. `#[derive(Replicate)]` rejects out-of-range `bits`
  written parenthesized, cast, hexadecimal, suffixed or negated, and clamps const widths so the protocol fingerprint
  matches the wire.
- Bans: a failed `bans.ron` save still kicks the peer and reports `ban_store_save_failed`; timed bans on a directly
  driven `NetServer` expire (they were stored in 1970); a `refuse_ip` right after hosting lasts its full duration.
  (ADR-5444)
- Sessions: `suspend()` sends every goodbye copy before returning, `disconnect()` after `suspend()` cancels the pending
  auto-resume, and a second `suspend()` keeps it; `stall_forgive_ms` above 250 works under automatic time; the Android
  listen cap applies from `NetSessionPlugin::finish`, and `SessionServer::set_options` clamps `max_players` to it.
- The coupled vehicle bridge stores a rotation offset that the nlerp decay returns to the drawn rotation (a large
  correction rendered up to about 2 degrees off); `stable_pow2` no longer returns infinity on [127.5, 128).
- Bans: the console `ban`/`unban`/`bans`, agent `server.ban`/`server.unban`/`server.bans` and game-port bans stamp and
  judge expiry on the session clock (`NetSession::now_unix_s`) the join check reads, so an NTP step no longer ends a
  `ban <id> 60s` at once or stretches it by an hour, and `unban` no longer prunes entries by the system clock. A ban
  against a `bans.ron` that was unreadable at start answers `ban_store_save_failed` again (the player is still
  removed). (ADR-5973, ADR-5833)
- `matchmaking.start` (`Lobbies::start_matchmaking`) with `create_if_none: false` lists and joins again when its unused
  create spec would be refused (`members_invalid`, `too_many_keys`); that check runs only when `create_if_none`.
- A `#[replicate(skip)]` field keeps the client's own value through updates and full resends (the state wire reset it
  to `Default`). (ADR-5648)
- Prediction: a confirmed predicted spawn keeps its prediction at bind and a rollback across an unbound one restarts
  it from its spawn-tick state (a projectile jumped 7.5 m at 6-tick latency and counted a correction); the
  `net.predict_writes_unregistered` checker sees the first predicted system too. (ADR-5649)
- Dedicated server: `server.toml` `[net]` `client_rate_bytes`, `server_rate_bytes` and `send_every_ticks` reach the
  replication sender at start and on every reload (they reached only the handshake); `HitShapes` and
  `NoLagCompensation` are registered on every process, so clients and the editor load them. `ALIGN_TO_VIEW` messages
  are released after `interpolation.max_delay_ms`. (ADR-5650, ADR-5647)
- `matchmaking.start` with `create_if_none` checks its create fields at once (`members_invalid`, `too_many_keys`)
  instead of failing rounds later as `create_failed`. (ADR-5656)
- The `net_game` sample client and the `net-game` template's client take `--exit-after-frames N` like every other
  template's game (it exited with `unknown argument`); projects generated earlier can copy the flag from a new project.
- Net audit fixes: a slowed peer sending a one-per-tick type at its legal rate is no longer disconnected after 600
  ticks; 65,535 joins and leaves no longer make the server refuse every later join; a reliable message holds about its
  own size in memory (one 256 KiB message took about 29 MiB); a mover with a step key decodes on clients; lag
  compensation no longer freezes the pre-movement pose for a present-tick claim, reaches targets with an unbounded ray
  and records swapped parents; `KnownHosts::load_or_recover` returns a read error instead of moving an unopenable store
  aside; `is_locally_controlled_owned` tells a remote player's pawn on a listen server from the host's own. (ADR-5580)
- A stale private-file temporary from a crashed run with the same PID no longer blocks server key or known-hosts writes;
  narrowing an IPv6 `/64` ban to a `/128` reopens the rest of the block; the prediction trust cache sees an owner change
  through a non-predicted parent; a component changed outside the predicted schedule is recorded at the next tick; an
  infinite frame over an infinite recovery half-life no longer turns the recovery offset into NaN; a schedule swapped in
  one frame gets the requested executor; `regia run --level` no longer re-asserts the start level's bake directory every
  frame. (ADR-5789)
- A predicted character riding a physics-driven base no longer jumps ahead on the client and gets corrected:
  `move_anchored_character` no longer lets rapier carry the rider a second time. (ADR-5972)

#### Steam

- Workshop uploads send absolute paths; a mock Workshop update installs while a file inside is open; concurrent cloud
  saves of one slot no longer tear; a `Regia.toml` with a BOM or UTF-16 no longer hides the app id.
- Steam Input action set names fold to ASCII, a collision is `ManifestError::NameCollision`, and a rejected activation
  is retried every frame.
- `SteamSocket::send_to` uses the destination's channel; `Steam::language()` is empty until `Ready`; the Deck thermal
  governor ignores failed temperature reads; lobby server keys and owner recovery raise no spurious events.
- A Steam id launch in the template is refused once and leaves the lobby instead of failing silently;
  `MockSteam::set_rich_presence` rejects NUL bytes like the real backend. (ADR-3169)
- A failed local cloud save removes its temp file (Auto-Cloud's `*` pattern would upload it), and a mock Workshop
  update with no installable files reports `SubscribeFailed` instead of wiping the installed tree.
- `LocalSaveBackend::save` removes its temp file when a save fails, a slot at the 248-byte limit saves again (temp
  names no longer embed the slot), and the mock Workshop sweeps stale `.trash-<id>-*` folders on the next install.
- Steam audit fixes: a failed cloud write restores the previous save instead of deleting it (`steamworks` 0.13's
  writer commits on drop); failed lobby joins report `LobbyError::Full`, `NotFound` or a platform error from
  `LobbyEnter` instead of always "full", and an IO failure is `LobbyError::Platform`; a Workshop download that cannot
  start is retried and reported (`WorkshopEvent::DownloadFailed`), a failed Workshop replace keeps the old install, and
  a failed unsubscribe is reported (`WorkshopEvent::UnsubscribeFailed`); the shutdown stats flush waits for its own
  `StatsStored`; a non-finite Steam Input axis reads as not held. (ADR-5124)
- `LocalSaveBackend`: a temp file left by a crashed process no longer fails every first save of a later process with
  the same pid. (ADR-5795)
- A `steamworks` build initializes an injected backend: `SteamPlugin` asks the backend it holds whether to restart, so
  a mock no longer exits before init (8 of 9 `shared_client` tests failed under `--features steamworks`). A failed
  cloud write no longer destroys the previous save; lobby enter responses map to the right errors and a locked enter is
  not reported as public; the mock Workshop install keeps the old folder when the swap fails; download failures are
  reported once per streak. (ADR-5375)
- Steam Input actions resolved before Steam loaded the action manifest no longer stay dead for the session, and a UI
  button held when the last input context goes away is released; Workshop updates installed while the game runs are
  picked up and `mods.content_set` follows them, and an accepted `download_item` resets the item's failure streak; lobby
  member names follow persona updates; the locale flag read no longer panics on a non-Unicode argument.

#### Voice chat

- `echo_cancel`, `agc` and `strong` suppression were stored but never run; user settings dropped unknown keys.
  (ADR-2608)
- A speaker let into a full audible set (`max_mixed_speakers`) at a re-ranking gets its "started"
  `VoiceChatEvent::SpeakerActivity` edge no later than the first frame it is heard (speaking icons and captions got
  only the "stopped" edge), and a waiting speaker keeps being decoded, not mixed. (ADR-5928)
- A muted account's voice no longer arrives over the network: muting queues a `VoiceControl::MuteHint` for every sender
  slot bound to the account, and for slots bound later. No more than `max_mixed_speakers` voices are mixed at once.
  (ADR-5642)
- A pending encoder reset survives until the packet that carries it goes out (with `frames_per_packet: 2` or after a
  failed encode), so the remote decoder no longer stays synced to a stale encoder; `VoiceChatSession::playout` returns
  only the last tick's frames.

### Platforms

- Cursor grab maps to the mode winit supports on X11, Xwayland and macOS, and retries next frame after a refusal
- Android: a zero-duration vibrate does nothing, and gamepad samples wait for a real device id
- A shipped Windows template game no longer opens a console window (ADR-2306)
- DX12 without DXC no longer crashes in FXC: the cloud-shadow and ground-bounce switches are rewritten and a
  `shader_hlsl` FXC assertion rule keeps the pattern out. Native on the Intel N150 laptop (DX12, no DXC) the lightmap
  set passes 35/35 and `forward_small_stack` passes where the old binary overflowed. FXC is slow there (about 9 minutes
  for one Desktop High renderer, 27 s on Vulkan), so DXC stays the recommended DX12 compiler. (ADR-5971)
- A cursor-grab refusal the OS never supports (winit `NotSupported`, for example on Android) is logged once instead of
  retried every frame; `platform.power_state { dir }` no longer blocks on a FIFO attribute file; `pacing.trace` no
  longer counts ticks the tick channel dropped as missed vsyncs (`VsyncTicks::drain_marked`). (ADR-5789, ADR-5895)

#### Templates and platform

- Android `SaveDirectory::default()` writes to the app's internal data path; every autosave used to fail on a read-only
  path. A cold 18-25 s first frame no longer ends a windowed net-game client with `connect_timeout`. (ADR-4929)
- Template fixes: Blank and Quick honor `--exit-after-frames`, the First Person floor faces up, and Top Down uses the
  current engine line. Upgrade migrations keep comments and multiline user text and refuse mixed future-version levels
  before writing. (ADR-4864)
- The `net-game` template and `samples/net_game`: a client's non-finite stick values no longer make its avatar's
  position NaN on the server (projects made from the template should copy the `step_position` and `judge_shots` guards);
  `--ticks 0` closes the windowed client on the first fixed step; `--conditioner` overrides a malformed
  `REGIA_NET_CONDITIONER`. An injected Android Back that closed the first-person touch Controls screen no longer quits
  the game on the next frame. (ADR-5600)

#### Android

- Arena on a Pixel 9 Pro (shipping, Mobile High, Auto, 60 Hz) went from about 20 fps to 60 fps with the single-threaded
  executor, and a frame that overran its slot now starts at once on the missed tick. (ADR-4933, ADR-4913)
- `Auto` pacing no longer halves its own rate through the compositor hint (Fairphone 5 arena from 7.5-10 fps to about 19
  fps; `docs/perf-log.md`, Android pass of the v0.2.15 candidate). (ADR-2339)
- The Fairphone 5 GPU driver no longer crashes about 1 s after launching the arena. (ADR-3145)
- Accessibility: correct TalkBack labels, no SIGABRT after TalkBack leaves, a touch-sized and openable controls screen,
  Back no longer quits or gets bound during rebind, and the font size setting scales text. (ADR-4932)
- Packaged media (boot movies, cutscenes) open on Android, and a level's named terrain loads there. (ADR-4826, ADR-4914)
- Agent WAV and preview output (`audio.loopgen_render`, `audio.render_check`, `ui.theme_preview`) defaults to an
  app-private directory instead of unwritable `/data/local/tmp`. (ADR-4815)
- Frame generation releases its swapchain image before the surface drops on suspend; a Home press sends the net
  `suspended` goodbye and resumes with the token; the Android listen cap (`android_listen_max_clients`, default 8) is
  enforced.
- After pacing `Off`, a preset change or a hitch, Auto pacing on a 60 Hz phone could stay with every frame blocked
  about 8 ms in the swapchain (presented p99 1.24-1.29 x the refresh against the 1.05 limit). (ADR-5564)
- A first launch closed or killed within a few seconds of its first frame lost every compiled pipeline, so the next
  start compiled them all again (cold first frame about 5 s instead of about 2 s on the Pixel 9 Pro). (ADR-5857)
- TalkBack no longer loses partial accessibility updates after Android asks for a resync of an update already taken: a
  full-tree update now meets the pending request. (ADR-4932)
- Nested packaged media (`movies/` and other subfolders) is extracted from the APK through the packaged media index
  (`regia::PackagedMediaIndex`, `media_index.ron`), also for packages built from a named profile. The generated Play
  asset-pack listener updates a pack under one lock, keeps a canceled fetch canceled and a removed pack removed.
- Asset packs: cancelling a fetch right after starting it is reported (the pack no longer reads `PENDING` for good),
  and a state query posted before a remove can no longer restore the removed pack's row. Projects pick this up on the
  next `regia package`. (ADR-5291)
- Play asset packs: cancelling a pack that is already done no longer hides a later cancel; the store listener and the
  native helpers share one lock, so an old download's state cannot overwrite a retry's; a real cancel of a retry
  (download notification or store side) lands instead of being taken for the old download's echo. Projects get the new
  helper code on the next `regia package`. (ADR-5623, ADR-5765)
- Android fast-follow packs are requested at every launch: a pack the store finished while the game was closed is
  taken in place, a running download is followed, and a cancelled or failed one is fetched again. Before, the store's
  local testing never delivered one and a pack cancelled from the download notification never came back. (ADR-7696)
- A sound in a fast-follow, on-demand or external chunk no longer ships in the base package too: sounds ship raw as
  well as cooked, and the raw copy ignored chunk assignment (a 475 MB on-demand chunk of sounds put the same 475 MB into
  the base module; the check app's AAB shrinks from 1,059,564,983 to 561,338,071 bytes). `regia package` prints how many
  raw files it left to their chunks. (ADR-7696)
- Generated Android asset-pack helpers take one lock around the pack bookkeeping and call the store after releasing it;
  `regiaPackState` returns null after posting a state query; a cancel of a pack in a terminal state leaves its row alone
  (regenerated on every `regia package --platform android`). A named build profile packaged without media ships an empty
  media index, so a later version that drops its last clip prunes the clips an earlier one extracted. (ADR-5674,
  ADR-5922)
- Settings > Display size changes the UI without a restart: the runner re-reads `densityDpi` after resume, focus gain
  and configuration changes and writes `WindowScaleFactorChanged`. After a suspend the renderer calls
  `Breadcrumbs::note_resumed`, so a later device loss no longer blames passes of lost work. (ADR-5379, ADR-5640)
- Media extraction skips `media_index.ron` entries with a `..`, `.` or empty part, a leading `/`, a backslash, `:`, NUL
  or a non-media name (they could be copied outside the extraction directory), and an update that drops its last video
  clip prunes the old clips. (ADR-5795)
- `presented_p99` over 1.05 on the Pixel 9 Pro: a sleeping frame loop sometimes took 0.6-2.4 ms to wake after the
  vsync thread handed it the tick. With the wake-ahead and predicted release four `android-pass` runs read 1.037 /
  1.033 / 1.033 / 1.030 with every budget row passing. (ADR-5461)
- Arena PSS over the 700 MB budget at Mobile High (717-722 MB): after the lifecycle drill the switch to Mobile High
  reserved a third 128 MiB allocator block. With the smaller blocks three `android-pass` runs read 631 / 630 / 598 MB.
  (ADR-5423)
- `presented_p99` on the Pixel 9 Pro reads 1.007 to 1.009 x the refresh in five full passes (it sat at 1.03 and
  once reached 1.060 against the 1.05 limit): paced frames no longer carry the callback's delivery jitter. A half-period
  move of the app's vsync ticks no longer starts a frame early and keeps the display queue one image fuller, and the
  arena no longer sits at 30 fps for minutes after a cold start (6 of 8 cold starts before). (ADR-5946)

#### Steam Deck

- A game that names no render preset gets `Deck` instead of Desktop High. (ADR-2952, ADR-3289)
- The memory report no longer files every atlas under `shadows`, and `arena --bench` prints its lavapipe note only on a
  CPU adapter. (ADR-2952)
- The terrain's anisotropic shimmer on RADV (Steam Deck, Radeon on Linux): RADV moves the trilinear blend toward the
  sharper mip once an anisotropic sampler is bound, so the layer taps filter a quarter mip softer there. On the Deck
  flicker per unit of detail goes from 0.132 to 0.103 (bar 0.128); every other adapter's frames are unchanged.
  (ADR-5459)
- `vfx_lab --deck-check` times its run on the app's clock from the first frame, so a start-up longer than the run no
  longer reports no frames. (ADR-9600)
- A Deck starts on Deck glyphs before a pad connects, Steam's `Steam Virtual Gamepad` keeps them, an action bound to
  both D-pad axes shows one prompt, SDL's `PS3 Controller`, `PS4 Controller` and `PS5 Controller` names read as
  PlayStation, and `--preset` with an explicit `--cap` keeps that cap. (ADR-1332)

#### Windows

- Intel GPUs on Windows Vulkan no longer crash in the driver on Desktop High: the engine pipeline cache is skipped there
  (`REGIA_PIPELINE_CACHE=1` re-enables it). Windows now reports resident memory. (ADR-3288)
- Showcase live play no longer stalls every second frame on its exposure readbacks on GPU-bound devices. (ADR-4050)
- NaN and infinity guards in DDGI and gizmo shaders hold on the NVIDIA 610.88 driver. (ADR-3150)
- Saves survive a destination held open (editor saves, pipeline cache, WebM output, read-only bake descriptions), editor
  config always uses `%APPDATA%`, and path case/separator mismatches no longer break terrain, GI assignment or map
  origins. (ADR-4824, ADR-4825)
- Secrets and uploads: `server.key` and `known_hosts.ron` get owner-only DACLs and an exposed key is refused; crash
  reports replaced during upload are kept; quarantine works with an existing `.rejected` twin. (ADR-4828, ADR-4821)
- Networking and text: UPnP discovery and UDP sends survive a stale `ConnectionReset`, long private-file paths work, and
  `.ron` files and Y4M input use or accept `\n`/CRLF consistently. (ADR-3150)
- On Windows, the generated Android `app/build.gradle.kts` with a literal signing password and the `regia assets keygen`
  private key are restricted to the current account with `icacls` before writing. (ADR-5126)
- `server.key` and `known_hosts.ron` DACLs are protected from inheritance, the exposure check is an allowlist (owner,
  `SYSTEM`, `Administrators`), object and callback ACEs are parsed safely, security buffers are 8-byte aligned, and
  both files load and save through verbatim long paths; the admin-token check flags write and other non-read grants and
  no longer reads a write-only grant as readable; `create_private_file` checks short writes. (ADR-4828, ADR-5444)
- Editor: saves never tear a locked or read-only destination (the read-only attribute is cleared, a sharing lock is
  retried, then the save fails with the old document intact); the hot-reload module probe no longer stops at 1,024
  modules; the recent-projects file uses the same `%APPDATA%\regia` resolver as prefs, and a list at a pre-fix location
  is read once and moved. (ADR-5411, ADR-5413, ADR-5414)
- Windows secret files are private from the moment they exist: `regia_net::private_file::create_private` sets a
  protected DACL for the process token's user, `SYSTEM` and `Administrators` in the create call, with no sharing, for
  the Android signing file with a literal secret, the `regia assets keygen` key and the agent's owner-only files
  (`.regia/agent/token`, `endpoint.json`, the `regia mcp --http` token and advert, DCC-feed files). The old `icacls`
  step after creation left a window in which another local account could open the file. Android packaging under a
  LocalSystem service grants by the token's SID. (ADR-5616)
- Windows ACL checks: remote admin works on Windows dedicated servers (the owner was missing from the read descriptor,
  so every `admin.token` read as exposed and remote admin was always off); an elevated administrator's fresh
  `server.key` is no longer refused when `Administrators` owns it; object ACEs are read after their GUIDs, and callback,
  compound and unknown allow ACEs are an error. (ADR-5880, ADR-5833)
- The editor's recent-projects list from older Windows builds is read from both old locations
  (`%USERPROFILE%\<crate>`, `%HOME%\.config\<crate>`) and moved to `%APPDATA%` on the next write. (ADR-4825)
- DX12: visibility-buffer frames no longer shade background pixels as a cluster's first triangle on the Intel N150
  (the `R32Uint` ID clear goes through `f32` in the DX12 backend); DX12 clears to zero and writes the empty ID with a
  full-screen draw (`backend_policy::clears_integer_targets_exactly`). (ADR-5807)
- SpaceMouse Pro, Pro Wireless and Enterprise buttons 3 and up work again without a report descriptor (always the case
  on Windows); only the two-button models read two bits. DACL checks of the server key, known-hosts files and
  `admin.token` work on paths of 260 characters or more and parse an object ACE from its own bytes. (ADR-5599, ADR-5580)

#### MacOS

- A damaged extension library is refused instead of crashing the editor, and the hot-reload dynamic-std check has a real
  implementation on Windows and macOS (compile-checked only). (ADR-3373)
- Sky light no longer leaks into sealed rooms in lightmap bakes, paused apps keep queued particles, and editor
  screenshots no longer swap red and blue. (ADR-3287, ADR-4824)
- Console locked: the windowed showcase ran about 1,500 empty frames a second and the editor drew none, and window
  screenshots timed out (F0006); they are now served offscreen. `regia describe --live` and the DCC feed accept a
  local-transport advertisement whose path is spelled through a link (`/var` vs `/private/var`); a deep project can
  bind its socket; the Metal 2D sprite pass no longer draws a non-finite alpha white. (ADR-7335)
- The microphone callback no longer allocates on first use of each capture slot (std's macOS mutex allocates on its
  first lock). (ADR-5550)

#### GPU-specific

- Terrain gets an anisotropic footprint path for drivers that drop sampler anisotropy on array textures (seen on Intel
  Arc under Mesa ANV), and Arc test runs select ANV only. (ADR-3957, ADR-3286)
- DLSS on pre-Turing NVIDIA cards reports `runtime_reports_unsupported` to tools. (ADR-4937)
- Frame generation requires five storage buffers per stage, and an oversized host DDGI grid no longer aborts buffer
  creation.
- Linux: a process no longer loses its NVIDIA card after a few dozen device creations. Each creation made the Vulkan
  loader load and unload every installed driver, and NVIDIA's driver leaked glibc static TLS each time, so the 27th
  device found no NVIDIA adapter (the 19th with Mesa's drivers beside it) and fell back to the CPU rasterizer or failed
  a strict `REGIA_GPU_UUID`. The renderer keeps its first Vulkan instance until `run_clean_exit`, and 400 devices in a
  row open on an RTX 5090 and an RTX 5080. A refused `REGIA_GPU_STRICT` selection also writes `GPU selection failed:
  <reason>` to stderr. (ADR-5794)
- Screen reflections of the linear march on the RX 7900 XTX (RADV, Mesa 26.2.6) returned two different images for the
  same still frame; the march now records the pixel it hit and reads it after its loop. NVIDIA, lavapipe and the
  Steam Deck render the same frame as before. Terrain shimmer under TAAU on Mali (Pixel 9 Pro) is fixed by the
  measured sampler offset. (ADR-5713, ADR-5797)

#### File and path safety on every OS

- Fixed temp names that followed planted symlinks or raced between processes (DCC feed recordings, remembered clients,
  material graphs, profile captures, `status.json`), and montage/bake paths now refuse read-only targets and Windows
  device names on every host. (ADR-1816, ADR-2396)
- `.cube` files with a UTF-8 BOM parse, and CPU mip sizing no longer wraps at 2^32 bytes.

#### Product viewer

- The Android viewer shows a refusal instead of a black screen, option rows drag-scroll on phones, the last row is not
  cut, and both players draw the real looks. (ADR-2615)
- All 695 published option graph 1.2.0 vectors match, and gem kit round trips keep the mesh. (ADR-2693)
- Viewer audit fixes: equirect sampling, environment bakes, studio lights and `set_style` replace non-finite or
  oversized inputs instead of panicking or failing the whole bake; `set_look_texture` refuses bad sizes with
  `ViewerError::Config`; `parse_radiance_hdr` accepts CR LF headers. The view controller reads an exact back view as
  180 degrees of tilt, lets free-orbit yaw across the back settle, falls back to defaults for huge tilts and non-finite
  cameras, keeps the pivot inside the near plane below 0.01 mm, and turns the piece counter-clockwise for a positive
  `spin_deg`.
- Pack, looks and configurator audit fixes: wide pear outlines, generated gem cuts in the gem-local frame, kit cuts
  matched on length and width, object `material_slots`, GLB node cycles and `TEXCOORD_0`, symlink escapes from a
  package (`pack_unsafe_path`); anodize tables after an empty frame, substrate cycles (`material_not_drawable`), short
  or repeated metal n/k samples, non-finite look uniforms and swapped stone bounds; a saved build keeps conflicting
  ASCII-case spellings (`duplicate_value`), a non-cutting plane is not a second face, facets without a plane are
  refused, draft-04 tuple `items` no longer accepts everything, a doubling `$ref` stops at the budget instead of
  running for hours, `multipleOf` compares integers exactly, and the cost scan reads every JSON body. (ADR-5587)
- Web player: a frame skipped while a gesture settled is retried, two products opened on different threads no longer
  share a serial, view keys apply in frame order, and a sampled camera path whose pose count is not the frame count is
  refused (`view`).
- A scripted camera that keeps the piece level could be refused as spun ("spin 0.05 degrees") when its up vector's dot
  product rounded under 1; the level test measures the angle with `atan2(|a x b|, a . b)`. (ADR-5550)
- `configurator.open_product` refuses a wrong-typed inline `documents.kits` (`F0002`, `data.param: "kits"`) instead of
  opening the product without kits. (ADR-5802)
- Looks: very rough blasted metal no longer draws black (the pit shadow is clamped to 0..1), anodize table entries too
  large for `f32` become 0 instead of NaN, a negative `attenuation_per_mm` is clamped to 0, an absorption spectrum with
  a non-number sample is refused (`material_missing_field`), and `LookMesh` builders with zero counts or non-finite
  sizes build an empty mesh.
- Web player and viewer: drags, wheel, `setPick` and `setView` redraw after `create()` and after a device-loss restore;
  a refused package or pick no longer empties the canvas or stays applied; `open()` without a device starts a restore,
  and a superseded open rejects with `open_superseded`; WebGPU contact shadows match stills and WebGL2 within one 8-bit
  step; a translucent clear without hardware sRGB keeps `rgb <= alpha`; a part placed with `up` along a tilted `z_axis`
  is no longer sheared; `capture_still` past the device buffer cap returns `ViewerError::Still`;
  `ViewerRenderer::render_if_needed` draws when the target size changes. New `LazyLoop::forget_pending` and
  `Player::frame_not_requested` for custom web hosts. (ADR-5591)
- Viewer tools: `regia_viewer_bake_env --face 2048` samples a capped 2048-per-face source (about 300 MB instead of 4.8
  GB), configurator schemas compile once per interface, and a geometry-key slot collision never overwrites another
  group's entry.

#### Packaging and CLI

- `regia package` clears stale staging before building a client package and writes tarballs atomically. The generated
  Android manifest handles font, locale and color-mode changes in place.
- `regia test --level` folds `.` and `..` in the level path before `functest.run` checks it (it answered
  `bad_params`). `regia upgrade-project --write` names the right reason when it restores a deleted `build.rs`, and plans
  `manual` for an unchanged older engine script whose `Cargo.toml` it cannot edit. (ADR-5617)
