- `regia package --profile shipping` on a generated project failed with "profile `shipping` is not defined"; it now
  supplies the profile with `--config profile.shipping.<key>=<value>` and a warning when the workspace manifest has
  none. Android packages show the project icon: `ic_launcher` (and the adaptive foreground) is written into every
  density from `[platforms.android] icon`, then `assets/branding/`, else a placeholder. (ADR-5627)
- A shipping `sim_bridge` binary no longer embeds `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR`, and the third-person template declares
  `[profile.shipping]`. (ADR-3968)
- `regia package` no longer fails its SBOM step on a host that built only that platform: `sbom.json` is filtered to the
  package's target triple (every ABI for Android), so another platform's crates are no longer listed.
- A named shipping profile of a game that links the terrain no longer fails with "the glTF/PNG importer is linked"
  (the binary check masks the runtime type paths in `ship_check::RUNTIME_TYPE_PATHS`), a reflected doc comment no
  longer reads as linked editor code, and the `lightmap_device` release build no longer embeds the build machine's
  path. (ADR-5382)

### Tools, editor and pipeline

- Monkey-test finds: a debug panic with floating windows whose tab bars overflow, and non-finite numbers typed into
  Inspector fields corrupting transforms. (ADR-3290)
- The colour picker no longer overwrites non-colour `[f32; 3|4]` fields. Asset-viewer mips, clip preview root motion and
  offscreen UI capture sRGB are corrected.
- The palette keymap persists across sessions, floating windows are no longer dropped on restore, and a `DeadZone` edge
  is validated. Several selected entities are all edited, not just the primary. (ADR-2321, ADR-4849, ADR-3948)
- Editor hot reload, thumbnail, bake and terrain worker robustness fixes. (ADR-2396)
- A source moved together with its `.meta` keeps its cooked files (ADR-2528). An unchanged cook no longer rewrites
  `font_subsets.json` (ADR-530).
- Agent fixes: `errors.explain` knows `bad_params`, `agent.run_tests` keeps a failing test's whole output, and the
  bearer token goes only to `127.0.0.1`. Captures get their 60 s default deadline, and the permission audit accepts
  `vfx.spawn_probe` (ADR-4760).
- Writes are atomic for DCC and remembered-client files and for path-traced output. WebM render-out checks the agent
  encoder feature (`media_encode_unavailable`).
- Security findings 19, 20, 21, 25, 27, 30 and 33: confined paths, `.tmx` caps, a generator environment allow-list,
  audio decode limits and a heightfield side cap (ADR-4922). The rmcp advisories RUSTSEC-2026-0457, -0461 and -0462 are
  fixed by moving to rmcp 3.9.0.
- `bans.ron` v2 registers its v1 migration source. DLSS create/evaluate sizes are validated. `editor.capture_panel`
  sizes reach the anim defaults.
- glTF `.meta` `scale` and `forward` were ignored; texture rules accept `normal_green` and `orm_channel_order`
- A viewport content drop places meshes, materials and sounds at the drop hit; headless shutdown keeps a failed tile-map
  save dirty; `procgen.graph_edit` refuses an open variant
- `render.set_permutation_policy` and `profiler.capture` refuse paths that leave the project (ADR-1816)
- Editor fixes: failed placed-level Commit/Discard keeps the session; headless shutdown reports tile-map write failures
  (`shutdown_save_failed`); a failed Play keeps the selection; Play entity count no longer double-counts; skins bind
  with literally `{name}_{node}` joint names; `ChildOf` cycles no longer spawn NaN groups or hang reparenting; thumbnail
  requests no longer swallow edited sources or miscount buffered replies.
- Editor audit fixes: placed-level Commit after opening another level no longer saves over the placed level's file
  (ADR-5574); Inspector Euler `ry` past 90 degrees keeps the typed angle, non-finite Euler drags and typed values are
  dropped, and multi-selection drags, extension-drawn components, tuple-variant fields and repeated entities edit
  correctly with one undo step; the reparent cycle check walks to the root; video panel playback advances by frame
  time, a backward step pauses and stepping past the end pauses on the last picture; the UI designer decodes TGA and
  BMP by extension; scene budgets count model and override materials and one material per file spelling.
- More editor fixes: `editor.lightmap_status` reports a level bake's checkpoint beside `assets/maps/<stem>.flmap`;
  empty `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`, `APPDATA`, `USERPROFILE` or `REGIA_EDITOR_CONFIG_DIR` count as unset; the Content Chunks
  panel refuses a platform such as `../x`; PIE entity counts skip resources, observers and systems; Project Settings
  input boxes follow undo and agent edits; saves through a differently cased project root on Windows are validated;
  trace bars, tall lightmap atlas previews, procgen debug lines and anchors, and the 2D pane clock are corrected; the
  theme panel refuses to save over `regia-light-high-contrast`. (ADR-5575)
- CLI audit fixes: `verify_checksums` no longer follows symlinks or opens directories, FIFOs or Windows device names;
  `regia loc cues import` detects TTML that starts with an XML declaration, `regia loc cues export` resolves keys
  through the locale's fallback chain and refuses an unknown `--locale`, `regia loc check-fit` measures missing keys
  with the text the game shows, and a `Language: ../..` header cannot steer `regia loc import` out of
  `assets/localization`; localization and dialogue writes replace a planted temp symlink; `regia test --level` keeps
  `.` and `..`; `regia upgrade-project` skips vendored `.level.d` trees; `regia server` packaging scans split levels.
  (ADR-5581)
- Save As keeps terrain side files dirty until the whole save succeeds; viewport picking scales by the offscreen
  buffer's integer size and keeps the pane's aspect; hot-reload build errors show the parent diagnostic; Restore and
  Discard in crash recovery keep the offer when they fail; a failed material-graph save removes its temp file; a
  deleted but mapped `libstd-*.so` is recognized on Linux; path stripping canonicalizes on case-insensitive macOS and
  non-ASCII Windows names; dropping an `Other` asset in the viewport spawns nothing. (ADR-5413)
- Editor: Discard of a crash-recovery autosave that is already gone drops the offer (the window, which has no close
  button, stayed up for the session); with the inspector showing a flipped Euler reading, an `rx` or `rz` edit on a
  multi-selection no longer turns the other entities 180 degrees; hot-reload build diagnostics parse each cargo JSON
  line once.
- `.import.ron` folder rules accept the audio `impulse_response` setting, like the importer; the ambience file, the
  conversation graph layout and the cloth panel write RON with `\n` line ends on Windows too. (ADR-2721)
- Escape during play-in-editor stops the session instead of closing the editor. (ADR-5615)
- Hot reload after an engine relink: reloads after an "engine changed" refusal are refused until the editor restarts.
  (ADR-5627)
- A version migration chain stops at its 32-step ceiling before invoking a 33rd callback.
- Project names: the editor's project browser and `editor.new_project` refuse Java reserved words (the Android
  application id was invalid), `regia new` and the editor refuse Windows device names (`con`, `nul`, `com0`-`com9`,
  ...) with `BadName`, and a failed generation in the editor leaves nothing behind. (ADR-5643)
- Play in editor no longer runs a game with no game systems and no `on_load` when its `register` adds `PhysicsPlugin`
  (the `third_person` and `top_down` templates, `flight_lab`, `vehicle_lab`) and the edit level holds a `Collider`: PIE
  starts the physics spawn-order tracking before it spawns the level (`regia_physics::track_physics_spawn_order`,
  `PhysicsSpawnOrderError`), and `PhysicsPlugin` accepts tracking a host started earlier. (ADR-9180)
- The editor viewport renders at the physical pixels it covers on a HiDPI or Retina window (it rendered at its size in
  points and stretched); grid and selection-outline stamps are one physical pixel wide, and
  `editor.viewport_stats` `pane.textures.last_size` reports physical pixels.
- The trace timeline no longer drops the newest mark of a recording when its position rounds one ulp past the view's
  width. (ADR-9601)
- The `kinds.list` / `kinds.of` memo notices an edit that keeps a `.kind.ron`'s mtime but not its size, and a file whose
  mtime moved back.
- Editor: the asset-create dialog refuses drive-prefixed and Windows device names; the lightmap panel finds the project
  as the CLI does; a failed save-all restores the open tab; components with one short name keep their own prefab
  override entries; the video panel's boot list follows the project-path rule; stopping the trace recorder names the
  file it wrote; with realtime off, an unchanged stats overlay no longer keeps the editor out of its idle wait; a
  Windows icon resamples from a capped 1024 px source (a 4096 px icon built a ~270 MB copy).
- The agent audit log stops instead of growing `audit.1.jsonl` without bound when the new log fails to open after
  rotation, and a plate's sidecar records the delivered frame's camera and profile. (ADR-5895)

#### Agent protocol and DCC feed

- Test-run cancel kills the whole process tree, non-UTF-8 output no longer ends the stream, and job forwarding checks
  permissions. MCP HTTP sessions are bounded, and review-queue limits apply. (ADR-2346)
- Long generators no longer stall the editor frame. (ADR-2821, ADR-2412)
- Agent and DCC-feed bounds: `key_glob` cost, feed texture and recording caps, repeated mesh sections, FIFO/device file
  reads, connection caps, exclusive endpoint temp files, and `seeing.reference_trace` limits. (ADR-2396)
- `regia dcc-feed` places joints correctly under a non-uniform parent scale with a rotated child, and reorders the
  mesh's `JOINTS_0` with the parents-first joint order; recordings into a symlinked folder work. (ADR-5581)
- Agent audit fixes: read-only calls that could hang or abort the engine are bounded (`procgen.wfc_solve`,
  `configurator.contract_check` with a self-referencing schema, `viewer.view_simulate`, `audio.cues` on a WAV with
  many labels); FIFOs at `seeing.capture_plate` `shots_file`, `.renv`, `product.build` and `world.hlod_proxy` paths are
  refused; `scene.apply_edits` refuses a despawn plus an edit to its child before changing anything; out-of-range or
  non-finite numbers in `physics.field_set`, `physics.field_probe`, `audio.vehicle_audio_set`, `render.set_clouds`,
  `terrain.set_grass`, `functest.create` and `editor.set_property` are refused instead of truncated or stored;
  `assets.pak_diff` caps `chunk_size` at 64 MiB; `loc.check_fit` follows the request-path rule. (ADR-5597)
- More agent fixes: `xr.walk_set` keeps the height calibration when the same call turns ground follow off;
  `ai.query_abort` and `ai.query_submit` keep or drop per-request caps correctly; `input.assists` keeps saved assists
  not yet applied; `save.quick_load` falls back past an oversized current copy; `audio.impacts_set { seed }` with no
  bank answers `impacts_no_bank`; `seeing.flash_check` decodes frames up to side 8192; `review.propose` stages
  newline-only and CRLF edits as real hunks; viewer and look stills no longer write through a planted temp symlink;
  `assets.import_rules` stays inside the project; montage paths with `\` name the same file on Linux and Windows.
  (ADR-5597)
- `editor.step` rejects `frames` outside 1..=10000 or non-integers, `editor.matgraph_edit` requires `pos` to be exactly
  two numbers, `editor.import` rejects a non-string `sources` element, all with `invalid_params`; `editor.capture_panel`
  accepts sizes up to 4096. The `out_dir` descriptions of `audio.loopgen_render` and `audio.render_check` name the
  Android app cache directory.
- `regia dcc-feed send` keeps nodes under a skin joint in place, parents joints across non-joint nodes (an `Offset` or
  `Armature`) with their transforms folded in at rest, in `--animate` and in pushed clips, and sends joints parents
  first. DCC-feed recordings with non-UTF-8 file names write again. (ADR-5291)
- Asset methods: `editor.move_asset` and `editor.rename_asset` destinations follow the request-path rule (symlinked
  folders, drive, UNC and `..` paths answer `path_outside_project`, `goldens` destinations `golden_path_denied`), and
  absolute, root-relative and UNC request paths are `path_outside_project` on every asset method. A move or rename is
  all-or-nothing, rewrites only string literals naming the moved path (not comments, field names or
  `"props/rock.glb"` for `assets/rock.glb`), and a case-only rename works on Windows and macOS. `editor.reimport` keeps
  the asset's id, import settings and provenance, and `editor.edit_asset` no longer reloads the open level (which
  cleared undo). (ADR-5876, ADR-5877)
- Code methods: `agent.job_cancel` on a `hot:<n>` job ends it on the editor's next frame and frees
  `agent.hot_reload`; a pending hot job whose host went away fails with `hot_reload_no_game`; an unlisted pending
  reload answers `hot_reload_busy` instead of panicking the editor; `agent.build_game` counts every diagnostic and cuts
  `file` and `suggestion` to 4 KiB; `agent.guide code` has no raw newlines. (ADR-5879)
- Local agent transport: on Windows every named-pipe connection was closed before its handshake, so `regia agent
  call`, `regia mcp`, `regia render` and the editor's `ui_inspector` could not use it; pipe handles are overlapped, the
  first instance must create the name, and the client checks the pipe server's pid before sending the token. A wrong
  token fails `Client::connect_local`, `Client::connect_project` falls back to WebSocket, and a second app no longer
  takes over or unlinks a live app's socket. (ADR-5878)
- Generators: `agent.job_cancel` on a `gen.run` or `gen.verify` job, and a failing cancellable run, stop the whole
  process group on Linux and macOS, including processes the command left running after it exited; a `gen.verify`
  cancelled after its last generator answers `gen_cancelled`. (ADR-5974)
- A `--no-gpu` headless editor refuses a product `render.out_job`, `ui.theme_preview` and `editor.ui_designer_render`
  with `editor_headless` before opening a device, and a failed adapter request hints to install lavapipe or a GPU
  driver. (ADR-5180)
- `regia dcc-feed send`: a skin listed child first keeps its weights (joint indices follow the sent order per mesh
  node), and pushed clips carry the folded non-joint nodes, so a rig with a rotated, scaled `Armature` (90°, 0.01) no
  longer flips and jumps 100× when the clip plays. (ADR-5618)
- `sequence.record_state` is a true read and no longer inserts a `TakeRecorder`; `ui.world_markers` and
  `ui.target_bracket` check every parameter under every action; the agent registries no longer list `app.startup_work`,
  `memory.idle_ledger`, `render.set_brdf_energy`, `render.voxel_far_stats`, the `startup-idle` guide topic and the
  `idle_ledger_*` codes twice; `editor.procgen_capture`'s schema names its real 64-4096 pixel range. (ADR-5957,
  ADR-5960, ADR-5924, ADR-5712)
- Data kinds: nothing loaded `.kind.ron` files into the live registry, so the editor showed every data kind as "not
  declared" and `kinds.list`/`kinds.of` left them out; the editor now loads them on project open, the agent merges them
  per call, and a game can call `regia_scene::kind::load_project_kinds`. `project.describe`'s first answer lists the
  running `PreUpdate` under `map.unread_schedules`. (ADR-5609)
- An `edit_scene` call with `dry_run: true` on a method without a `dry_run` parameter writes its `agent.changes`
  record. (ADR-5628)
- Agent input validation: studio HDR cubes, valley mist, tilemap stamp paste, dialogue playback, camera-cut thresholds,
  thin-film edits, HDR image scales and shadow-stability requests refuse numbers that overflow `f32` (or signed 64-bit
  stamp coordinates) before changing state; state diffs apply prefix and ignore filters to both snapshots; partial
  accessibility updates keep omitted settings; IPv6 loopback agent URLs resolve; `ui.text_shape` answers
  `text_not_shaped` for stale content or locale; navigation handlers report wrong-typed ids as `F0002`; `nav.crowd_*`
  step replies follow the runtime's fixed-tick order; `agent.schema` exports valid JSON Schema types.

#### Cook, import and formats

- Props with `collision_*` proxies no longer cook without collision, and a moved source keeps its GUID. Destruction cut
  faces keep their material in desktop paks. (ADR-2341, ADR-2428, ADR-2788)
- Cook audit fixes: stale atlas keys, lock coverage, cache keys, generator stderr hangs, cloth/soft glTF loads, chunk
  repacks, case clashes in `cook_material_maps`, and partial `.meta` writes.
- Crafted input no longer crashes import: glTF accessor types, Vorbis header sizes, Tiled zlib layers, HDR/EXR headers,
  `.table.ron` trailing backslash, short accessors, and cooked files with trailing bytes or version 0. (ADR-2723,
  ADR-2394, ADR-2431, ADR-2391)
- `validate_name_invalid` is no longer reported for cooker `#` sub-assets, and with `cook: Fail` it had stopped every
  terrain cook. `check_pak_summary` ignores platform tag case, and mip generation and glTF import allocate less.
  (ADR-4928)
- Importer audit fixes: Aseprite old palettes, linked-cel position and opacity, the background layer's transparent
  index, slice pivots and 9-slice borders in frame space, reverse-clip slice keys and 6-bit palettes; Aseprite and LDtk
  size bounds (`MAX_COMPOSITE_PIXELS`, `MAX_GRID_CELLS`) instead of overflows, hours-long composites or 100 GiB grids;
  LDtk external levels stay in the source folder, merged tiles are no longer offset twice, `Free`/`GridVania` levels
  keep negative positions, values-only IntGrids import and duplicate level identifiers fail; MaterialX image paths
  follow the portable project-path rule, graph cycles are `mtlx_cycle`, long swizzles are refused and `tiledimage` no
  longer samples upside down. (ADR-5570, ADR-5571)
- Cook audit fixes: a malformed `config/import_rules.ron` fails each source with `CookError::ImportRules` instead of
  cooking without rules, and a source imported after the rules changed mid-cook is not recorded under the old key; glTF
  `orm_channel_order` reaches the packed ORM; material overlay layer maps are pak dependencies; a recook with no voxel
  HLOD nodes removes the stale `.wvoxels`; plant envelopes refuse hostile GLBs, node cycles and partial triangles;
  cubic-spline NaN keys and infinite plant vertices are errors; command generators keep their stderr under load.
  (ADR-5570, ADR-5571)
- Asset audit fixes: importer settings parse `/* */` comments and brackets inside strings; huge pak chunk sizes hash in
  64 KiB reads; texture-set conflicts keep every claimant, an AO map next to a packed ORM is listed as unreferenced, and
  a bad member `.meta` is a warning; renamed lightmap entries and reused manifest allocations resolve correctly;
  releasing a chunk ends a finished request (`Cancelled`); `encode_gpu_mesh` refuses partial triangles.
- `.cube` LUTs: the live cooker re-cooks a saved `.cube`, the cook refuses one over 16 MiB with `CookError::ColorLut`,
  and an unreadable cooked entry falls back to the loose file. (ADR-5448)
- KTX2 files with a mip chain longer than the image's, or a cubemap, array or 3D layout, are rejected
  (`CompressedTextureError::Ktx2`) instead of panicking or loading face 0; the GPU-mesh migrator refuses a LOD whose
  index count is not a multiple of 3 (`CookedMeshValidationError::NotTriangles`). (ADR-5674)
- Import rules: the cook checks a `(len, mtime)` stamp before rereading `config/import_rules.ron` and caches the parse
  per thread (a 10 k-source cook reread it about 20 k times); a stamp is trusted only when the file is at least 2 s
  older than the read, so a same-length edit within one mtime tick is not missed, and the snapshot and its stamp come
  from one read. Editing a rule that matches an FBX/OBJ-referenced image recooks the converted source. Pipeline globs
  (`lod_classes.ron`, `validation.ron`, registry roots) use the import-rules `glob_match`, so `**/props/` no longer
  matches `myprops/`. (ADR-5734, ADR-5932, ADR-5586)
- LDtk IntGrid object ids are made unique on collision (a 32-bit hash collided at about 12 % for 32 k cells); maps get
  new ids on their next cook. A texture-set write run no longer writes a set's material when a member's `.meta` does not
  parse, and the manifest path index evicts the least-recently-used entry instead of clearing every index. (ADR-5734)
- Pak mounting: a TOC dependency cycle mounts as one group after its outside dependencies (the warning names every pak
  in it), a pak depending on a cycle member mounts after it, and `mount_chunk_pak` keeps dependency order for chunks
  mounted at runtime. (ADR-5877)
- Terrain tile cook memory: `cook_tiled_texture` copies out one tile per worker instead of every tile of every tiled
  mip (8192² BC7 bench, 1365 tiles: peak RSS 1078 MiB before, 735 MiB after, same bytes and cook time), and a threaded
  tile encode runs each worker's block encoders on that worker, at most 16 encode threads instead of up to 16 × 16.
  (ADR-5799)
- glTF import: a `TEXCOORD_n` or `COLOR_n` in `BYTE` or `SHORT`, or a `JOINTS_n`/`WEIGHTS_n` in a type the reader does
  not take, no longer reaches an `unreachable!` that aborted the process under `panic = "abort"` (it reads, or fails as
  `gltf_quantization_invalid`); integer `POSITION`, `NORMAL` and `TANGENT` are no longer read as raw float bits; a
  required `KHR_materials_*`, `KHR_texture_transform` or `REGIA_materials_detail` imports; quantized signed UVs import
  when the file declares `KHR_mesh_quantization`; and the editor's viewport, hierarchy, model report and budget open the
  files the cook reads, with the same URI checks. (ADR-3016, ADR-5958)
- A cooked GPU mesh whose header sits on a postcard varint boundary no longer writes a header whose payload length
  disagrees with the payload.
- Cook audit fixes: DXT2 and DXT4 DDS files decode as premultiplied (unpremultiplied to RGBA8) instead of passing their
  blocks through; stream-cook capsule, cylinder and cone bounds follow the shape's long axis, and a child's priority
  volume is stamped at its world position; GLB export refuses NaN or infinite accessor floats that were written as
  `null` (`glb_non_finite`), and `plant.export` refuses a non-finite `lod_screen_size`; a failed `ext://` recook keeps
  the previous cooked state; the DCC feed bake refuses inconsistent geometry or a non-finite `import.scale`
  (`invalid_value`) instead of writing a GLB the importer rejects.

#### Scene and prefabs

- Prefab instances keep the names their files declare. Runtime `spawn_prefab` and placed levels derive deterministic
  guids instead of OS-random or entity-bit ids. (ADR-4928, ADR-4806 proposed)
- Fixed floating-origin level opens far from zero, layer parent loops, cleanup of failed prefab spawns, and parenting to
  added prefab children. Fresh level opens register variant base chains.
- Shape queries and keep-world reparent treat a millimeter scale as invertible and non-finite inputs as `+∞`; a nil
  `EntityGuid` is refused as a prefab or placed-level root (`missing_instance_guid`). Re-opening an `Active` level whose
  data fails validation keeps its record, a re-open after the data left the library reloads in place instead of
  spawning a second copy, and a `PendingPrefab` resolves from a replaced `PrefabLibrary`.
- Scene audit fixes: a pending level whose load fails is dropped from `LevelManager` instead of being retried every
  frame (a retried `Replace` respawned the level with new entity ids each frame); components with a `format_version`
  newer than the build's are skipped with a warning on cooked, prefab, added-child and override spawn paths; an
  undecodable component on an added child skips that component instead of aborting the scene; a placed level that is
  in the library but fails to spawn reports `spawn_failed` (`PlacedProblem::Spawn`), not `level_not_found`.
- Scene frame costs: a clean transform propagation skips resolved roots and unchanged affines, waiting prefab
  instances no longer clone their records every `PreUpdate`, `StreamPlanner::plan` considers only cells inside each
  viewer's unload window and sums the eviction ring once, and layer applies parse each record once. New counters
  `stream_plan::{last_plan_cells_visited, last_plan_ring_sums}` and
  `layers::{layer_scan_stats, reset_layer_scan_stats}`.

#### Runtime hardening from audit waves

- DLSS: feature leases, a single NGX instance per device, lock scope, per-device extension checks, and unknown NGX
  failure codes now fall back. (ADR-2396, ADR-2391)
- Saves and path tracing: corrupt saves no longer wipe the world, save files are size-capped, and GPU BVHs deeper than
  48 levels lost geometry before this fix. (ADR-2385, ADR-2395, ADR-2431)
- Numeric robustness: NaN and out-of-range inputs no longer latch NaN in character movement, camera, ragdoll, IK, nav,
  physics, frame generation, curves and DDGI. Concurrency fixes cover input, audio, video audio, 2D, animation, AI,
  physics, render-out and telemetry. (ADR-2395, ADR-2431, ADR-2396)
- Network and product viewer: undeclared local-player slots, asset-table loader bounds, and port-mapping replies are
  fixed in `regia_net`. Required add-on resolution, skipped frames, color carry-over and symlink escapes are fixed in
  the product viewer. (ADR-2686, ADR-2685)

### Regia Forest Demo

- The third-person sample's `F6` recording lands in `<data dir>/demos/run.frec`, and `camera.*` refuses an overflowing
  `context` point (ADR-4939)
- 45 pipelines built outside the warm queue no longer hold the first present (82.6 s cold on lavapipe, 2-4 s on the N150
  laptop); `quality.get_detected` reports the demo's pick; headless exit removes `endpoint.json`; the depot README no
  longer says "test bundle" or tells players to run cargo. (ADR-5131, ADR-5002)

#### Regia Forest Demo and showcase

- The showcase forest no longer renders as a white frame at `--render-profile MobileLow`. (ADR-2486)
- The demo privacy check no longer passes a tree that links the agent protocol, and `--verify` no longer accepts the
  draft EULA. (ADR-3111, ADR-4847)
- The windowed demo no longer hangs on its loading splash when the terrain warm job panics (non-shipping builds; the
  error shows in `ShowcaseStats::terrain_error`); `BootSplash::set_warm_queue(None)` opts the splash out of the warm
  queue; a `"ui"` overlay layer replaced under its name is warmed instead of compiling on the frame thread; the late
  overlay warm and `publish_detection` allocate nothing on a steady frame; `configure_loading` works before the render
  plugin; an agent host without the render plugin (a dedicated server or CLI tool) removes its `endpoint.json` at
  exit. (ADR-5620)
- The demo benchmark's `low` view flies 1.7 m over the meadow (it measured the terrain's underside from 22 m below), the
  landing-site decal is the scorch's 2.4 m dark core instead of an 18 m dark square, and the example scene's
  `terrain.layers.ron` is regenerated at layers version 5. Re-render showcase stills and trailers of trees against
  clouds, close meadow views on RTX cards and the landing site. (ADR-5792)
- `--no-default-features --features demo` compiles again (`HostQualityDetection` and `demo/agent.rs` were not gated
  under `agent`), and `demo-notices --verify <depot>` no longer fails every real demo binary on the weather preset
  string `snow`. (ADR-5419)
- `showcase --demo` in a window honors `--exit-after-frames`, so a scripted start of the shipped build ends. (ADR-5452)
- `showcase SCENE --window|--fullscreen --exit-after-frames N` exits with code 0 after N frames drawn once any loading
  splash is gone; in a window it ignored the flag (new `showcase::app::run_windowed_with_exit`). (ADR-5565)

### Audio, media, UI and accessibility

- External-clock looping video no longer rewinds the decoder feed. UI fixes: non-finite swipes, nine-slice UVs, glyph
  atlas offsets after a page wipe, and `max_chars` on bindings. Idle heap windows keep the one-frame-tail contract
  (ADR-4761).
- Input: per-binding triggers, chord press edges, overlapping injection windows and `Completed` over `Canceled` ranking;
  `Keys2D` diagonals on `Axis3D` normalize
- UI: clipboard read failures no longer paste the last in-app string, Fluent terms ship, and plural rules for `nb`,
  `no`, `nn`, `be` are corrected (ADR-336, ADR-330, ADR-344)
- Audio: NaN reverb, filter and position values no longer silence buses, and `set_bus_volume` refuses non-finite values;
  the Opus decoder no longer pins full-scale output after tiny packets (ADR-2306)
- Voice playback on the `Voice` bus raises the `Voice` duck; capture state reads "not capturing" while suspended
  (ADR-4870)

#### Sequencer and cameras

- A cutscene voice line now shows its subtitle, and prefetch sources pick up `StreamPrefetch.priority` changes on the
  next `PostUpdate`. (ADR-3334)
- Crane pitch ranges outside ±89.9° clamp to the nearest hard bound, non-finite bounds no longer panic, and a skipped
  rail or crane node leaves no stale report. (ADR-3231)

#### Audio and media

- `play_music` crossfades at equal power (it dipped to 0.39), and true peak now covers a clip's first 11 samples.
  (ADR-2517)
- Zone portals honor `radius`, and voice speaker ranking uses a 3 dB hysteresis so near-equal speakers stop swapping.
- Four crafted-Vorbis panics are now stream errors instead of aborts under `panic = "abort"`. (ADR-2164)
- WebM: HDR10 files with mastering metadata open, anamorphic clips letterbox at display aspect, cue seeks keep the video
  position, and a mid-file read error fails with `io_failed` instead of a clean end.
- A packaged game now carries its loudness table. (ADR-4898)
- Audio audit fixes: packed sounds honor `SoundDecodeLimits` (`AudioError::DecodeLimit`, ADR-4922); a 0 Hz WAV is a
  load error; gear-shift counter jumps and non-finite breaks no longer overflow or play; voice chat no longer loses
  about 40 % of the voice at 30 fps, and the default 2 s microphone buffer holds 2 s of 10 ms callbacks; revived or
  moved emitter voices keep their volume and pan; Music no longer stays ducked 6 dB after a suspend or a speaker
  quality change; a remote speaker's "stopped speaking" event is sent; the listener ignores a non-finite camera pose.
  (ADR-5589)
- Binaural voices on a 44.1 kHz mixer use a 44.1 kHz HRIR table (interaural delays were about 9 % long). (ADR-2721)
- Media audit fixes: non-finite video volumes play at 1.0; `nb`/`nob`/`nn`/`nno` and legacy `iw`/`in` tags match
  their Matroska languages; the opening clear frame uses the track display aspect; a playlist entry that fails to open
  no longer replaces the playing clip; whole-file preload reads only the checked length; a failed WebM rename keeps
  the previous output; `video.select_track` on a playlist no longer restarts the list at entry 0. (ADR-5602)
- A pending video picture of a new size with short planes no longer replaces the player's target; one short
  fullscreen picture is skipped instead of blocking every later frame; a WebM re-encode only removes and retries over
  the output on Windows access errors, so the previous take is never deleted elsewhere.
- Cue playback: a `looping: false` cue emitter plays its variation once; a sound asset's default volume, default
  pitch and loudness gain hold on positional and emitter voices (a −40 dB default played at full level through
  `play_cue_at`); a cue's authored low-pass (`filter_cutoff_hz`, `filter_offset_octaves`) applies on `play_cue_at`,
  `play_cue_on`, emitter autoplay, after a revive and across an emitter track rebuild; a cue steal waits until the new
  play started, and a two-path play counts once; an emitter's `volume_db` applies to cue emitters; a failed or
  inaudible 3D cue play does not consume the pick. (ADR-5926, ADR-5977)
- Nested pressurized rooms on an airless world are audible again (interior air through one wall), and the media feed's
  underrun counter saturates instead of wrapping. (ADR-5731)
- Setting `VideoPlayer.rate` to `0.0` on a closed, ended or failed player is a quiet no-op instead of a warning.
  (ADR-5904)
- Captions: `conversation.say` and `conversation.play` through the agent stamp unvoiced captions on the caption clock,
  so a caption no longer finishes before it is shown; `conversation.ron` caption settings reach a caption board
  created later; `settings.set` with `dry_run: true`, or a refused `settings.set`, no longer marks an extension's
  settings resource added and changed. (ADR-5833, ADR-5936)
- `SoundAssets::add_packed` honors `SoundDecodeLimits`: a cooked entry that would decode past the cap returns
  `AudioError::DecodeLimit` without allocating the rest of the clip. (ADR-4922, ADR-5471)
- Video: a refused `.video.ron` names its file; a `VideoPlayer` opened with `rate: 0.0` and `autoplay` waits paused in
  Ready; an unsupported rate at open is warned. `regia media encode` to a path that is an existing directory fails
  instead of renaming the directory away and reporting success. (ADR-5638, ADR-5795)
- A paused voice that virtualizes keeps its position, and `AmbiencePlayer::step` with a huge `dt` ends (at most 256
  calls per one-shot per step; past about 1.7e5 s the loop never ended).
- Audio audit fixes: two separate interiors on an airless world no longer share an airborne path; the smallest
  containing interior classifies a point (an airlock inside a hab); a failed cue play no longer consumes the pick; a
  propagated one-shot whose second path fails stops the first voice instead of leaking it; media feeds drain the whole
  step at any positive rate (a muted rate-8 stream drifted from the picture); `PcmFeed::channel` refuses channel counts
  outside 1..=8.

#### UI and accessibility

- Touch taps click controls, rebind captures no longer close their dialog or steal focus, and the D-pad reaches
  dead-zone sliders. (ADR-4903)
- The accessibility tree keeps list rows and names tabs, and theme text color now reaches labels under plain containers
  and labels spawned later. (ADR-3025)
- Focus repair drops disabled or hidden nodes at once, and an emptied modal refocuses its default child.
- `Bind.format` keeps literal text around its placeholder, `TextOverflow::ScaleToFit` keeps the narrowest retry, and
  `UiThemes::toggle` swaps only the built-in pair.
- Player look, volume, mute and reduced-motion choices now take effect. SA-script text no longer breaks mid-word,
  ordinal `NUMBER()` is no longer ignored, and UI-only textures now reach profile packs. (ADR-3464, ADR-3323, ADR-3359,
  ADR-3148)
- UI and localization audit fixes: glossary word matching no longer panics on multibyte text; translation memory
  prefers the requested locale's newest exact pair; TTML import accepts `<profile>` and export escapes `xml:lang`;
  WebVTT cue ids such as `NOTES` are kept; `untranslated` counts only missing source messages; a failed locale replace
  keeps the previous source; touch-target spacing measures hit boxes.
- UI: showing a tooltip no longer overwrites `Tooltip.text` with the shown hint, so its placeholders follow a later
  device switch; a fixed-size multi-line `TextInput` that starts with text word-wraps from its first frame and re-picks
  the wrap on a size change; idle dropdown and tab-bar syncs compare labels in place instead of cloning them.
  `read_webvtt` starts a new cue at a `-->` line once a block has its timing (separator lines holding spaces no longer
  merge two cues), and TTML writes a tab or lone CR as `&#9;`/`&#13;` so cue text round-trips. (ADR-5900)
- UI correctness: `ui.set_value` text writes and the accessibility set-value request go through
  `TextInput::set_external_text`, so they cut on grapheme clusters, sanitize newlines in single-line fields, refuse
  read-only fields and filter rejections, and clear the undo history; `ui.drag_drop` presses at the drawn widget in a
  camera viewport; MSDF composite glyphs apply `SCALED_COMPONENT_OFFSET`; captions stay hidden until the first cue
  starts; menu hover works in a camera viewport; `ui.set_value` refuses numbers outside the f32 range, and
  `ui.set_target` refuses `ppm`, `pivot` and `fade` values that break the f32 cast.
- Dragging a video seek slider seeks to the dragged time (two drags of 0.4 seeked to 0.4, not 0.8; new resource
  `UiVideoPendingSeeks`); `pop_screen` of an already despawned screen reveals the next screen with `ScreenChanged`; raw
  strings no longer break `loc!` key gathering; `ChildOf` cycles no longer spin hit testing or extract
  (`focus::MAX_ANCESTOR_STEPS`, 4096). Screen-reader and agent accessibility requests no longer reach a control under a
  dialog opened, a widget disabled or an input layer pushed since the last sync. (ADR-5567)
- `Text::line_height` is honored by layout (lines were always 1.2 × the font size; `regia_quick` `line_spacing` now
  spaces lines); `UiInputState::pointer_consumed` is written each frame; `UiVideoPlugin` no longer panics in an app
  without the UI interaction plugin. (ADR-5615, ADR-5550)
- Turning subtitles or sound captions off hides the line already on screen, and `conversation.ron` caption settings
  (`overlap`, `max_visible`, `min_display_ms`, `max_extend_ms`, `reading_cps`, `reading_cps_cjk`) reach the caption
  board. (ADR-5778, ADR-5783)
- Screen-reader requests answer against the current tree: a press on a background node under an open dialog was
  answered Ok. An errored `repeat` host no longer re-reads its collection every frame. `regia-light-high-contrast`
  keeps a focused list row's ring inside the row, and the editor theme panel refuses to save over every shipped theme.
  (ADR-5814, ADR-5664)
- A resize or format change drops the backdrop blur's reduced-size pairs of every downsample factor, and
  `ui.backdrop_stats` reads zeros after the last UI quad goes away (a closed frosted panel still read
  `quads: 1, blurred: 1`). (ADR-5420)
- UI audit fixes: the screen reader rebuilds after a removed state component or a despawned node; a refused two-way edit
  puts the widget back; a third caption in one frame shows; packaged Fluent bundles keep a select expression's closing
  `}` at column 0 (the locale failed to parse and the game ran without texts), and an ideographic space after the indent
  no longer panics; the accessibility audit counts `RoundedClip` as a clip and `GroupOpacity` as fading text; a tap on a
  resting scroll view no longer leaves it past its end; animated UI materials, video seek thumbs and world panels keep
  moving while other UI redraws, and `animate: false` materials hold; a refused dynamic image no longer leads to a fatal
  validation error on the next one; a seek slider whose seek never lands follows playback after 120 frames; `regia loc
  validate` checks ordinal selectors against the locale's ordinal categories; `regia loc gather` skips `loc!` lookalikes
  in raw strings. (ADR-5576, ADR-5577)
- Quick layer: cached text shapes are dropped after a glyph-atlas page recycle and after a `text_scale`, `min_font_px`,
  font locale or language change; `Draw::is_empty` counts a drawing in progress. New
  `QuickRenderer::glyph_atlas_wipes()`.

#### Telemetry

- `.regiatrace` per-second index offsets, early-flushed hitch GPU fields, map-name overflow, negative and empty frame
  stats, counter wrap and rate-key hashing are fixed; exports name the flag `forced`.
- Telemetry audit fixes: a stall frame over 5 s keeps its GPU-object creation counts in the window that held it;
  `.regiatrace` decoders reject a 10th varint byte with payload bits past `u64` (the chunk ends); `Recorder::mark`'s
  rate-limit table caps at 1024 names (least-recently-used eviction); a NaN or infinite trace window means no window;
  `summary.json` `shaders.pipelines` is the warm-time count again, beside the new `shaders.pipelines_total`. (ADR-5152)
- Pass-trace capture no longer copies the breadcrumb ring every frame (65601 allocations over 200 steady frames before,
  1 after, through the new `Breadcrumbs::for_each_fresh_pass`), and trace events encode into a stack buffer (2000
  allocations for 1000 identical `PassOpen` events before, 0 after).
- A `.regiatrace` file's header `adapter` names the render device's adapter (it was always empty). (ADR-5640)

### Core, licence and security

- Deep hierarchies despawn without stack overflow; small JSON values can no longer make the agent server allocate about
  16 times the message (ADR-2306)
- A torn or bit-flipped save loads the previous generation, and a corrupt `settings.ron` is no longer overwritten
  (ADR-3102)
- A physical camera's lens is kept on save (ADR-2323)
- `regia_ecs` loads that panicked (opaque keys without hash, NaN keys, deep postcard recursion, NaN key saves) are now
  errors; missing fields keep the container `Default`'s ignored state. (ADR-4951)
- ECS audit fixes: a missing reflected field keeps nested `#[reflect(ignore)]` state of its type's `Default`;
  canonical-order saves count one entity miss per emitted entity and break equal-key ties by value, so equal maps
  save equal files. The frame profiler no longer aborts when a span exits in a thread-local destructor, and releases
  exited threads' tables.
- Deterministic math: `det_exp_f64` and `det_powf_f64` underflow to subnormals instead of huge negatives (debris drag
  could explode), `det_cbrt` is accurate at extreme magnitudes, and `det_ln_f64` decodes subnormals.
- Reflected map ordering encodes value forms only for keys that tie, so canonical serialization of nested maps is
  linear again (the cost multiplied by the branching factor per nesting level); the canonical bytes are unchanged.

#### Cross-platform determinism

- Nav: crowd avoidance, oriented-box obstacles and the default 45-degree slope use pinned trig; ORCA neighbors sort by
  physical state; equal-height span merges keep the flatter normal; a floating-origin rebase can no longer interleave
  with a nav step. (ADR-4769)
- Terrain: dual-contour vertices, soft brush falloff and droplet erosion no longer follow platform libm; VT
  over-capacity allocation keeps the same pages everywhere; holes RON uses LF on Windows.
- Fluid: moving solids, physics coupling, CFL substeps and whitewater use software math; GPU kernels use bit-specified
  sin/cos and saturate the fixed-point scatter, so GPU results repeat on one device; across devices the CPU tolerance
  applies (ADR-4753, ADR-5598)
- Procgen: overlapping terrain edits and biome captures resolve by lowest guid; ecology thinning uses `detmath` (new
  `detmath::cbrt`); the `.pgout` input hash and plant skeleton hash are complete and order-free; point rotations
  normalize in scalar f32. (ADR-4775)
- World streaming: replays recover reads that miss their cap and apply recorded retries; banked fixed steps sample
  current poses; level-open waits only on `SimReady` cells; the world hash sees a rebase's delta. (ADR-4812)
- 2D and VFX: sprite animation uses an integer-nanosecond clock, sRGB tint conversion
  avoids `powf`; VFX ignores non-finite `dt`, curl noise works at negative coordinates, attractors give no NaN.
- `det_atan2` (and the `detmath` twin) follows IEEE 754 on signed zeros and infinite pairs and propagates NaN;
  `det_atan(±∞)` is ±π/2; `det::exp(±∞)` and `±1e30` no longer return NaN; `det_exp_f64` and `det_ln_f64` are correct in
  the subnormal range; the deterministic `exp_f32` no longer returns infinity on [88.38, 88.5). (ADR-5410, ADR-5415,
  ADR-5416, ADR-5447)
- Remaining platform-dependent sites move to software math: fluid MIC(0) preconditioner, solid SDF lengths, trapped-air
  speeds and validations (`math::sqrt_f32`/`sqrt_f64`), crash fire-site radii (`det_cbrt`), and the lag-compensation
  swing test (scalar `quat_dot`). (ADR-5414)
- Deterministic math: `det_exp_f64` underflows to zero and overflows to `+inf` at the exponent edges
  (`det_exp_f64(-720)` returned about `-6.6e303`), `det_ln_f64` rescales subnormals, and `det_powf_f64(±inf, y)` edges
  follow; the impact-site wreck rest pose uses `det_quat_from_axis_angle`, so its pinned bits hold on every target.
- `det_atan2` follows IEEE 754 on every zero and infinity corner, and `det_exp_f64` returns the finite value at `n =
  1024` (and `exp_f32` at `k = 128`) instead of infinity. Gamepad trigger ranges latch per pad, and a non-finite sample
  reads 0 without latching. (ADR-5495)

#### Security and legal

- Save listing reads headers only and refuses files over the 1 GiB cap; links planted at agent token, audit, temp WAV
  and state paths are refused; paks and DCC-feed peers can no longer force huge allocations before auth;
  `[::1].evil.example` is not loopback. (ADR-3285, ADR-3206)
- Generated third-party notices now include optional media codecs, the opt-in AV1 assembly build deps and the vendored
  lewton.
- Crash-uploader quarantine is a single atomic rename again, and reports on volumes that cannot prove file identity
  (FAT, exFAT) fail with `ReadError::IdentityUnproven`. (ADR-5410)
- SBOM ported-source `bom-ref`s no longer repeat, and the asset licence record covers `frec`, `rfdrec`, `gif`, `avif`,
  `fbx`, Ogg audio and extensionless binary files.
- Asset and agent input review: a 128 MiB Workshop pak could force an 8 GiB allocation at mount; a GLB of a few hundred
  bytes could make the importer collect about 24 GB (sparse accessor without a buffer view); glTF images had no edge or
  total limit (now 32768 texels and 4 GiB); glTF node graphs that are not a forest are refused; plant envelope, scene
  export and BVH retarget reads followed absolute or `..` buffer URIs; KTX2 DFD texel-block dimension 0xFF panicked
  (`regia_asset::hostile_ktx2_dfd` is public); `ColorLut::from_png_strip` trusted a hostile PNG header;
  `content.check_host` echoed the host's reply through a read-only token; the DCC feed touched a planted link before
  refusing it; `platform.power_state` `dir`, `demo.record_stop` and `configurator.open_product` paths are confined.
  (ADR-5869)
- Multiplayer and mods review (no sandbox escape or panic found; nine unbudgeted paths bounded): Lua component
  declaration options, texts and defaults are bounded, long `regia.state` keys no longer copy per child table, and
  `ModFiles::from_mods_vfs` checks the size cap before reading; one pak entry over the per-file limit is refused from
  the table of contents; a client's zero-byte streams cannot evict other peers' join and leave events;
  `SnapshotDecoder` refuses a delta past `MAX_ENTITIES`. (ADR-5870)
- The agent token was readable by other local accounts on Windows: `.regia/agent/token`, `endpoint.json`,
  `regia mcp --http`'s token, the DCC feed's token and settings and the safety audit log are created with a DACL that
  names only the current account, `SYSTEM` and `Administrators`, and an audit log an earlier version left readable is
  rewritten owner-only. `regia_net::private_file::{create_private, exposed_to_other_users}` are public. Unix is
  unchanged (mode 0600). (ADR-5489)

### Tests, docs and tooling

- Breadcrumb and trace methods are actually registered, and `frames`/`passes` trace streams record. Gate fixes cover
  tests written against the old cull default, the shader contract rows and the energy BRDF axis, plus a `regia_media`
  opus-only build and flaky warm-shutdown tests. (ADR-2307, ADR-4777, ADR-2681)
- `gi-compare` refuses mismatched or empty image planes (`[image_shape]`). Invalid `bake-lightmaps` settings are
  rejected before scene load without touching existing output. The sealed-box leak disagreement between harnesses is
  explained.
- `terrain_vt_crater` now waits 24 frames with temporal GI off and requires bit-exact frames and pages (it allowed 2 %
  of channels at 2 LSB); a merged behavior-tree asset was reverted to its base.
- Determinism pins were re-recorded where fixes moved bits on purpose: the abilities trace, `PIN_SLIP` and the vfx v6
  pin. Contract tests were added for character movement and queries.
- `net-match run` seed handling, stop-hook counters (`HostedLink::final_status`), match judging and server exit
  reporting; book corrections for Steam cloud saves, overlay and Deck presets. (ADR-3075)
- Pass harness fixes (Android uninstall, wake, immersive notice, unreachable phone; Deck script; `windows-pass` adapter
  step; copied-xtask launch), Windows-only and Metal-only test fixes and tolerances (ADR-3373, ADR-3954), Windows-native
  acceptance results, demo package verification, and lint fixes for newer toolchains (ADR-3388).
- Flaky timing tests are stabilized, missing sample assets skip with a note (`REGIA_REQUIRE_ASSETS=1` enforces them),
  and agent-guide error labels and examples are corrected. (ADR-2431, ADR-3918)
- Test assertions tightened in audio and telemetry, the `dlss-stub` feature row compiles again, and docs, coverage
  tables and format hashes were matched to code.
- Stale pins found when merges met: format versions, scene format names, template and test-device expectations, and
  `ragdoll_panel`. The race trace was re-pinned twice, each time by attributed experiment.
- Load-sensitive tests now use exact checkpoints and per-thread counters, with no budget raised: `lens_effects_alloc`
  (record budget 54), `force_fields_alloc`, the pipeline-worker shutdown, radiance-cache allocation and
  `actions_mutants`.
- RTX 5090 "Parent device is lost" failures were mitigated, not proven fixed, by keeping fewer devices (ADR-1584).
- Duplicate decision, COVERAGE and provenance rows are removed; `agent-examples`, `book-check`, `check-features
  content-signing`, `.ui.ron` v6 hash and the corpus fixtures are green again; the `matgraph-wind` fuzz hang guard is
  raised to 1800 s.
- Debug overflow panics in SpaceMouse parsing and rebind ordinals, `FuzzRng::chance(0)`, `track_spawn_order` hook
  install, `LoadError::NoClip`, gate-script parsing and lock hygiene, and many book examples were fixed
- CI/test fixes: `ci_workflows` no longer compiles a page under CI-ignored `docs/releases/**`, CI runs `duplicate-deps`,
  clang-cl wrapper flag order, Windows-only unused `mut` in a configurator test, showcase no-mist overlay strips
  multi-line `mist:` entries, `check-formats` corpus errors. (ADR-3301)
- Docs/provenance: 37 ADR-1661 URL gaps closed (1,805 URL occurrences), 350 duplicated provenance rows removed, ADR-3373
  row re-cited to permissive sources, agent-guide corrections (`registry.query` tag cap, `fluid.play stop`,
  `editor.bake_set`, `xr.capture_eye`). (ADR-1661, ADR-4581, ADR-4931)
- Book, specs and agent pages now match the code for `regia_cli` (`--quality`, templates, `procgen bake|verify`, items
  marked not built), `regia_editor` (`editor.budget` `hero`, `editor.new_project` `top_down`, autosave suffix, undo
  scope), material graphs (`normal` output, `normal_space`, format v2, compiled passes), `regia_nav` link docs and
  `regia_quick` (`rotate` direction, `fill_rule`, justification, the nine quick examples).

### Release-candidate 2 fix round

Fixes merged onto the first release candidate (`c512a612d`) for the second (ADR-7419); the release fragments in
`docs/releases/` have the details.

- **Document paths** (ADR-7388). Every editor and agent document path goes through one rule,
  `regia_agent::req_path::document_path_in`: the UI designer and UI animation open/save, conversation open/save, the
  mode2d map, rules and tile-map saves, the usd/ply/stl/psd/fbx/obj/bvh inspectors and `svg_report`, `net.sim_clients`
  `recording`, and the panels that used the ADR-1816 output rule. A path outside the project and the temp dir answers
  `path_outside_project`; symlinks out, UNC, drive and device forms are refused. The dev-build UI component reader and
  `data_panels::confined` no longer follow a symlink out of the project.
- **Editor** (ADR-7412). Importer reads refuse a FIFO instead of hanging; a
  hyphenated game package hot-reloads; the Cloth panel binds only an instance of the opened file; a UI-animation key
  drag is one undo step; no in-process PIE session starts beside separate processes; sequencer stills and cloth paint
  strokes respect their caps. Deleting, duplicating and respawning entities added on top of prefab instances undo
  correctly, a live prefab update keeps them, failed tile-map saves keep the level dirty, and `quit.request` no longer
  overwrites a crash-recovery autosave. Review fixes (ADR-9694): a prefab reload or save whose
  new template cannot spawn (it nests itself) fails and keeps the old template and every instance instead of
  despawning them; the quit autosave goes beside a pending recovery file (`<name>~quit.level.ron`) so the unsaved edits
  survive too; a root holding a base and its variant is rebuilt once; `editor.keymap_import`, `editor.keymap_export`
  and `editor.trace_timeline` follow the document path rule; live reloads no longer slow down with each reload.
- **Scene**. After a floating-origin rebase, a data-layer respawn, a streamed placed level and a
  `Transform`-less level root land in local space (the offset was missed or applied twice); every entity of a streamed
  placed level carries `PlacedMember`, so a full-world snapshot no longer copies its interior into the host and
  `snapshot_level` of it keeps its roots; a layer respawn inside a streamed placement goes back under its instance;
  the placement cap walks prefab-added children; an unparsable layer `defs` entry warns.
- **Agent and framework**. Cancels wake the local transport; `regia upgrade-project --write`
  keeps a format 2 save's entity id table; `SaveGame::save_raw` refuses a format 1 payload with entity ids; bird
  startle, cutscene input contexts, live sequence edits, caption pages, absolute-anchored radar layers and camera-driver
  duplicate warnings are fixed; `assets.texture_coverage` reads `.meta` sidecars through the capped reader.
- **Audio, media and assets**. A tiny ambience one-shot interval no longer hangs the game thread (`.ambience.ron` minima
  below 0.01 s are refused); an emitter with an empty cue no longer panics; a send-bus convolution with a NaN mix builds
  fully wet; a TOC dependency on a pak name mounted more than once binds to every such pak, so a patch mounts after each
  copy of its base (ADR-9705); `reposition` seeks the feed to the clamped time. Second audio pass: the mono downmix
  cannot overflow into NaN, `.hrir` files at an integer multiple of 48 kHz keep every tap (sinc resample, DC gain kept),
  a take still starts on a suspended output and a resume during a take is deferred to its end, virtual voices advance at
  the effective pitch, bus meters K-weight at the device rate, paused emitter voices stay paused across track rebuilds,
  air absorption follows `spatial_blend`, non-finite gains never reach the mixer, `Audio::played()` keeps a bounded
  tail, and the slow-frame streak resets during a mixdown.
- **UI text and input**. U+2028/U+2029 are line breaks in `TextInput` (spaces in a single-line
  field) and the caret map follows the normalized breaks; a mouse press and release in the same frame batch still
  clicks; focusing a read-only field keeps the IME and soft keyboard off; a text field's label and caret parts stay out
  of the accessibility tree, so its content is spoken once, as the value.
- **Rendering, VFX and fluids**. Forward decals render end to end; ribbons and
  trails build and retired emitter runs age while significance caps are on; the fluid heightfield cache no longer keys
  on a buffer address; precipitation atlas, cloud-shadow and sun-occlusion size checks cannot wrap or take NaN.
- **Networking and Steam** (ADR-7399, ADR-7349,
  ADR-7317). A car stops when its driver's peer leaves; Steam Input glyphs come from the real client; bots, join
  snapshots, known hosts, `.netdemo` exports, `steam.layout` params and the source limiter are hardened. In the
  `net_game` Steam build Shift+Tab no longer shows the scoreboard, the window layer frees the cursor while the Steam
  overlay is open (`CursorRelease`, `SteamSettings::release_cursor_on_overlay`), `REGIA_STEAM_OVERLAY_TRACE` records the
  overlay messages, and the HUD's first row shows the overlay state.
- **Physics** (ADR-7417). Queries pass through colliders with no entity instead of failing; characters
  climb walkable slopes at walking speed (the achieved-velocity clamp compared 3D motion with the horizontal ask);
  `VehicleSimWorld::sync_statics` reports a non-finite car (`VehicleError::StateNotFinite`); destruction detach
  velocities are finite and queued hits follow the floating origin; ragdoll stepping, impulse pads and active-ragdoll
  motor changes are fixed.
- **Terrain and streaming**. A clean cook indexes streamed levels from the current cook; a level
  opened during a low-memory trim starts at the trimmed budget; the threaded terrain brush keeps edits made during a
  drag; stroke logs, tile-cache cancels and HLOD/voxel evictions in flight are bounded or corrected; out-of-range
  scatter instances are reported.
- **Packaging and present**. Editing `[cook.chunks]` and related `Regia.toml` keys repacks;
  an interrupted pack is not taken as fresh; a damaged chunk pak is not staged; a present-mode or HDR change during
  frame generation applies.
- **Platform passes** (ADR-7344,
  ADR-7387, ADR-7386, ADR-7393). An idle editor answers agent calls at once (an agent request wakes its event loop).
  Sample data tables carry committed `.meta` sidecars (packaging no longer mints random ids in the source tree);
  `vfx_lab --deck-check` no longer counts its start-up frame; the dedicated server build compiles again
  (`ambient_bird_calls` is client-only); `cargo xtask check-unwraps` skips two test-only `regia_agent` files; several
  tests that raced or depended on the host (vehicle_lab A-B-A, net_game hosted session on macOS, smart-object
  dogfood, asset manifest index) are fixed.
- **Integration and gate fixes** (ADR-7419). The `regia_media` `av1`, `video` and `media-encode` feature builds compile
  their tests again; `regia_editor`'s crash-report tests no longer misfile an editor panic as `pie` beside a parallel
  PIE test; `cargo xtask notices` in a tree without `.git` skips the legacy `.ferrite/` cache; the
  `alloc_counter_global` worker test keeps its allocations in optimized builds; `procgen_roads`' lifecycle test accepts
  a loader restricted to lavapipe by `VK_LOADER_DRIVERS_SELECT`; the `frame_work_ratchet` table records the larger sky
  uniform (ADR-5944) and forward shader (forward decals, haze, probe specular).
- **Platform passes of rc2** (ADR-7483, ADR-7334, ADR-7484, ADR-7539, ADR-7541).
  Fourteen GPU test binaries whose parallel tests each opened a Vulkan device, and
  `studio_measure`, take turns on one shared device (`regia_test_utils::serial_gpu`): per-test devices livelocked the
  NVIDIA driver now and then and hung the GPU test pass; the Arc pass loads the ANV driver alone. On macOS
  the allocation tracer no longer counts its own first mutex locks or std's backtrace frames. The third_person sample's
  cook, run from a test binary, builds in its own target dir, so `cargo test --workspace` doctests no longer fail
  after the third_person tests. The Steam Deck, the Pixel 9 Pro and the N150 min-spec laptop pass every row of
  the candidate without an engine change.
- **Release-candidate 3 test reds and the rc2 Windows pass** (ADR-7570, ADR-7566).
  `net.sim_clients` reads `recording` through the typed helper, so a
  non-string `recording` answers `invalid_params`; `cargo xtask notices --check` in a tree without `.git` honors the
  tree's `.gitignore` files, so cook output and fetched test assets are not listed as assets; the `hwrt_gate` test no
  longer creates dozens of devices beside the gate's own (that churn livelocked the NVIDIA driver on an eight-GPU
  host). On Windows the editor's video panel refuses root- and drive-relative paths (`\x.webm`, `C:x.webm`) and
  device names, like its list's add; the agent socket shape check is host-independent; the `plates` test waits for a
  renderable frame; and six tests take Windows path spellings.

## Upgrade: host changes since v0.2.13

The engine version is 0.5.0, release line 0.5 (`regia --version` prints `regia 0.5.0`). A project stamped with the 0.1
or 0.2 line reports `upgrade_needed` in `regia engine info`: run `regia upgrade-project --write`, which migrates the
formats below and stamps the project with line 0.5. (ADR-7691)

### Formats, wire versions and migrators

- **Rendering:** `.matgraph.ron` v2 to v3 and `.matfunc.ron` v1 to v2 (version-only, `regia upgrade-project --write`
  rewrites them). Importer bumps (PNG/JPEG 1.2.0, `material_ron` 1.5.0, glTF 1.13.0) recook once, and `.material.ron`
  stays v7. (ADR-2318, ADR-3320, ADR-2732)
- **Rendering:** New workspace dependency `libm` 0.2.16 in `regia_render`. `naga` becomes a normal dependency of
  `regia_pt`, naga `msl-out` is on, and `regia_xess` is a new crate with `xess` features on `regia_render`, `regia` and
  `regia_dylib`. (ADR-2725, ADR-2644, ADR-4656)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `.flmap` v5 (BC6H/ASTC HDR texels, denoise header), v6 (source-mesh hashes), v7
  (pages); direction encodings 2/3 within v5. Older files load, `regia upgrade-project --write` rewrites them, and newer
  files do not load on older builds. (ADR-2314, ADR-2635, ADR-2656, ADR-3300)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `.fgiv` v5 (`BAKED_GI_VERSION` 5): v4 added the split section, v5 adds cropped split
  bricks (`to_bytes_v4` for hand-written v4). v4 files still load, and `regia upgrade-project --write` rewrites v1-v4
  at v5 (sections byte for byte). New `check-formats` and `upgrade-project` rows. (ADR-2618, ADR-3237)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** Scene format v11 (`LevelLook::lightmap`) and v13 (`LevelLook::captures`); older levels
  migrate. (ADR-2313, ADR-2860)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** New formats: `.bakeck` v1, `.fcap` v1, `ies-profile` v1, `.shot.ron` v1. Checkpoints
  written before `.flmap` v7 do not resume. (ADR-3095, ADR-2860, ADR-2592, ADR-2463, ADR-3300)
- **World:** `.fvox` v4 (`voxel_far_migration_sources()` = `[1, 2, 3]`, cook version 4); `.wstream` v2 (v1 migrates);
  `.terrain.layers.ron` v5; scene v12; nav cell v2 (v1 loads without links); `.vfx.ron` v7; `.tilemap.ron` and
  `.autotile.ron` v2; `.matgraph.ron` v3 and `.matfunc.ron` v2. Run `regia upgrade-project --write`; older engines
  refuse the new versions.
- **World:** New formats: `hlod-voxels` (`.wvoxels` v1, no migrator), `grass-density` v1, `nav-link-anchor` v1,
  `.fluidcache` v1, `vehicle` v1, `hull-set` (`FCHS` v1).
- **World:** Recorded hashes and pins move: fluid digests (re-baseline), `NavCrowd::digest` and nav cooks on other libms
  (up to 1 ulp), `droplet_erode`, volume-mesh vertices, `.pgout` input hashes (one stale read, then re-bake), Int-param
  hashes, sprite frame indices at step boundaries.
- **Gameplay systems:** `.ragdoll.ron` v4, `.vehicle.ron` v4 (`vehicle_migration_sources()` is `[1, 2, 3]`),
  `.destructible.ron` v2, `.motiondb.ron` v2 with cooked payload v2, and `.spatialquery.ron` v2. Older files load, and
  `regia upgrade-project --write` rewrites them (vehicle rewrites drop comments and leave a `.bak`).
- **Gameplay systems:** New v1 formats: `physmat`, `impactbank`, `destructionfx`, `trailer`, `vehicleaudio`,
  `motiondb-cooked`, `traversal-settings`, `traversal-ladder` and `traversal-jump-point`; `.utility.ron` v1 is frozen.
- **Gameplay systems:** The protocol hash changes: `DestructionPoses` becomes a `ToOwnerMessage`, and traversal adds a
  replicated component. Old and new peers refuse each other at the handshake.
- **Gameplay systems:** About 80 new agent methods across the `physics.*`, `ragdoll.*`, `ai.*`, `anim.*`, `audio.*`,
  `vfx.*`, `vehicle_ai.*`, `lua.*`, `gameplay.*` and `editor.*` families. `physics.vehicle_telemetry` rows carry new
  keys, and clients with closed method or key lists must allow them.
- **Networking and Steam:** `CRYPTO_VERSION` 2: older builds and this one refuse each other with
  `crypto_version_mismatch`. `NET_PROTOCOL_VERSION` 3 (minimum 1). `DestructionMessage/2` refuses older processes.
  (ADR-2416, ADR-3315, ADR-2305)
- **Networking and Steam:** `server.toml` is v3 (`[persistence]` from v2, `[bots]` in v3); v1 and v2 load, and `regia
  upgrade-project` migrates. (ADR-3354, ADR-3346, ADR-4854)
- **Networking and Steam:** `bans.ron` is `format_version: 2`: a v1 bare IPv6 entry becomes `/128`; engine-written block
  bans are `addr/64`. (ADR-4936)
- **Networking and Steam:** `config/net.ron` advances to v2 (`replication.entropy`, default `Off`) with a v1 migrator.
  (ADR-3315, ADR-4854)
- **Networking and Steam:** New formats: `known-hosts` v1, `content-trust` v1, `content-signature` v1, `server-players`
  v1, `.vehicle-snapshot.ron` v1. (ADR-2416, ADR-2710, ADR-3354, ADR-3080)
- **Platforms:** `.render_job.ron` is version 4 (`regia upgrade-project` re-saves v1-v3; older engines refuse v4). New
  formats at version 1: `viewer-package`, `.renv`, four product-configurator contract schemas,
  `docs/ci/size-budgets.toml`, `sbom.json`. (ADR-3369, ADR-2757)
- **Platforms:** Recook Android once (`regia cook --platform android --clean`) for terrain indexes; recook stand-in
  viewer packages and motion databases. (ADR-4914)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New formats: `agent-task` v1, `collision-set` v1, `cooked-sound` v1, `generator` v1,
  `import-rules` v1, `kind` v1, `provenance-policy` v1, and the review queue `queue.json` (version 1, now 2 with a
  load-old, write-new rule). (ADR-2621, ADR-2341, ADR-2788, ADR-2412, ADR-3321, ADR-2488, ADR-2428, ADR-2821)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** Bumped formats: `gpu-mesh` v2 (older runtimes refuse it, so recook with `regia cook
  --ship`) and `editor_layout.ron` v2 (older editors refuse it). `regia upgrade-project` migrates `.session.ron` v1→v2,
  `input_overrides.ron` v1→v2, and v0 `.table.ron` row lists. (ADR-2788, ADR-2771, ADR-2316)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** Material files stamped older than a block they hold now fail to load, and
  `format_version: 0` is refused. Old saves need a `SaveMigrations` hook for every step, and device-name save slots move
  to `<name>_.save`. (ADR-2394, ADR-2385)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `.seq.ron` v5 (v3 → v4 video sections, v4 → v5 spawnable bindings and voice
  Doppler; older files load through the migrators and `regia upgrade-project --write` rewrites them), `.camera.ron` v2
  (`migrate_camera`), `.music.ron` v2, `.render_job.ron` v3 and `.ui.ron` v10 (v6 → v10 steps only renumber; v9 and
  older files load unchanged, `regia upgrade-project --write` renumbers them); older engines refuse the new files.
  (ADR-2405, ADR-2862, ADR-2948, ADR-3197, ADR-3231, ADR-2517, ADR-3369, ADR-3148, ADR-2958)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** New frozen formats: `ecology` v1, `.fvox` `voxel-far` v1, `glossary` v1,
  `.uisettings.ron` v1, `CookedVideo` v1; run `cargo xtask check-formats --update`. (ADR-2456, ADR-2445, ADR-3359,
  ADR-2866, ADR-2410)
- **Final versions after integration:** Bumped formats, older files load or migrate through `regia upgrade-project
  --write`: `.matgraph.ron` v4, `.matfunc.ron` v3, scene format 13, `.ragdoll.ron` v4, `.motiondb.ron` v2,
  `.vehicle-snapshot.ron` v2, `.trailer.ron` v2, `.seq.ron` v5, `.fgiv` v5, `.ui.ron` v10, `.render_job.ron` v4,
  `config/net.ron` v2, `server.toml` v3, `bans.ron` v2, `.terrain.layers.ron` v5, camera v2, `editor_layout.ron` v2
  and voxel far v4.
- **Cross-cutting:** `post-volume` v3 (reads v0 to v2), new `prefab-variant` v1, `physical-camera` v1, `profile-capture`
  v1 and `.aircraft.ron` v1; `regia upgrade-project` rewrites embedded components
- **Cross-cutting:** `TAG_INPUT` frames carry per-command views, but `REGIA_NET_WIRE_VERSION` stays 2, so mixed builds
  can misread input
- **Cross-cutting:** Existing Windows projects add the `windows_subsystem` line to `launcher/src/main.rs`
- **Cross-cutting:** Recook lightmap tiers to get the seam fit and gutter correction (files still load); recook
  primitive-built streamed levels (index bytes change, not format); content that cooked to broken output now fails (huge
  cubic keys, zero-scale joints, out-of-range priority volumes); postcard data nested over 128 levels and NaN-keyed maps
  may fail or reorder once. (ADR-4995, ADR-4993, ADR-4951)
- **Cross-cutting:** Replays/lockstep recordings of slip-tire or AI-driven vehicles replay to slightly different bits;
  some fluid CPU digests and one GPU pin move. (ADR-5128, ADR-4963)
- **Gameplay systems:** `.bt.ron` is version 2 (`compare`, observer `abort`, `state_tree` nodes); version 1 loads, and
  `migrate_bt_ron` / `regia upgrade-project` rewrite it. The canonical writer keeps unchanged v1 node lines. (ADR-4543)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** New format `.ambience.ron` v1 (`check-formats` row `ambience`,
  `upgrade-project` entry, `docs/formats/ambience-ron.md`). `camera_body.json` and `bird.json` v1 are versioned host
  specs, not frozen content formats. (ADR-2721, ADR-1502)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** Recook `.ldtk` sources (merged tile layers stored the level offset twice; duplicate
  identifiers and external paths outside the folder now fail), `.material.ron` files whose overlay masks or normals are
  asset ids, and lightmap tiers (derived tiers keep `source_hashes`). FBX/OBJ (importer 1.2.0) and glTFs whose images
  match import rules recook once; LDtk IntGrid object ids change once; Aseprite slices that do not cover the canvas get
  frame-space values. (ADR-5570, ADR-5571)
- **Platforms:** Android packages carry `media_index.ron` for nested packaged media; an APK packaged before it does not
  extract nested media until it is packaged again.
- **World:** `AnimatedSprite`'s serialized clock field is `time_ns` (was `time`); old saves load with the animation
  phase restarted and the frame kept.
- **Gameplay systems:** `ChooserBinding`'s demo snapshot layout gains its evaluation cache (serde defaults; demo
  snapshots do not persist across builds, so no migrator). (ADR-5415)
- **Platforms:** Packages built with this `regia package` ship `media_index.ron` at the assets root; Android extracts
  packaged media to `<internal>/content/movies/...`. Older packages use the walk fallback (top-level media only).
- **Rendering:** `.decal.ron` v2 (`metallic`, the metallic channel, `shading`); v1 files load and `regia
  upgrade-project` rewrites them. New format `lens-effects` v1 (a scene component); saved scenes without it are
  unchanged and left-out fields read as off. (ADR-3255, ADR-3196)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** The glTF importer ends at 1.13.0, not 1.12.0 (ADR-2732 took 1.12.0), so glTF sources
  recook once more. New frozen format `prefab-instances` v1 (first version, no migration). `.ply`, `.stl`, `.psd`,
  `.usda`, `.usdc`, `.usd` and `.usdz` files under `assets/` now cook (they were unsupported-extension errors); a USD
  file with no mesh fails its cook (`usd_no_mesh`). (ADR-3016, ADR-3244, ADR-3235)
- **Gameplay systems:** Save format 2 (`SAVE_FORMAT_VERSION` 2): `SaveFileRaw` gains a trailing `entity_ids` table and
  older engines refuse the new saves. Format 1 saves without entity references load unchanged; their entity fields load
  as `None` or skip the component (`save_entity_ref_missing`). `regia upgrade-project --write` rewrites `.save` v1 as v2
  (keeping a `.bak`). Saved entities are written in a fixed order, so two saves of one world differ only in the
  timestamp. Code that decodes `SaveFileRaw` with postcard must use the header's version. (ADR-3239)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** New format `cells.json` (`lightmap-cells` v1), a bake cache listed in
  `check-formats`, `regia upgrade-project` (never migrated) and the fuzz registry. No `.flmap` version change: cell
  seams update direction texels in place, storing a changed block-compressed section back as RGBA8. (ADR-3318,
  ADR-4559)
- **Networking and Steam:** New format `.netdemo` v1, in `check-formats` and `regia upgrade-project` (listed, never
  rewritten: a file belongs to its protocol); fuzz target `netdemo`. No wire change. (ADR-3021)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New frozen format `texpack` v1 (`.texpack.ron`); `regia upgrade-project` lists it
  and `cargo xtask check-formats` hashes it. Existing textures cook to the same bytes and keys. (ADR-3090)
- **World:** Android terrain cooks change key (no format bump, no migrator): recook Android paks once with
  `regia cook --platform android`. (ADR-5294)
- **Gameplay systems:** A v1 `.trailer.ron` that carries v4 tire transient fields (`relaxation_length`,
  `pneumatic_trail`, `camber_stiffness`, `static_camber`, `combined_slip`) is refused, matching `.vehicle.ron`'s v3
  gate. No format version changed.
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** PO and XLIFF files exported before this release keep the old pre-fill
  markings, so their pre-fills still import unreviewed: export again. A WebVTT file exported with raw `&` or `<` still
  imports, and text that looks like a cue tag or character reference now imports as what it stands for. (ADR-5576,
  ADR-5577)
- **Platforms:** A `mesh.rvm` whose bounds span overflows `f32` fails `regia viewer verify` with `pack_bad_mesh`;
  re-cook it. (ADR-5591)

### New struct fields (struct literals break; add `..Default::default()`)

- **Rendering:** `regia_render`: `RenderProfile::{brdf_energy, shadow_occlusion, many_light_sampling, subsurface_kernel,
  cluster_residency}`, `FrameQuality::{many_light_sampling, subsurface_kernel, cluster_residency}`,
  `RenderSettings::{shadow_occlusion, visibility_default}`, `ExtractedFrame::shadow_occlusion`,
  `MorphStats::motion_instances`, `SssrStats::cost`, `TerrainBudget::foliage_subsurface`,
  `RenderThreadSettings::queued_frames`, `FrameGraphReport::dispatch`, `VendorFrameReport::render_preferred`,

- **Rendering:** `restir_di::ManyLightMode::Production`, `VendorRuntime::InitFailed`, `ForwardReason::SwayGraphSurface`
  (`ALL` has 8), `regia_matgraph::Node::Noise`, `regia_editor::shell::Panel::MaterialGraph`,
  `WaterBodyError::{CausticsTier, ShaftSteps}`, `gpu_bench::Suite::Features`. New enums `BrdfEnergy`,
  `VisibilityDefault`, `VisibilityDefaultState`, `StartupTrigger`, `SubsurfaceKernel`. (ADR-2652, ADR-2679, ADR-3916,
  ADR-2318, ADR-2432, ADR-2309, ADR-2632, ADR-3149, ADR-2987, ADR-3170)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** New enum variants: `DebugViewMode::{LightmapDensity, LightmapOnly, LightmapInvalid}`,
  `PtLight::SphereSpot`, `CookError::Ies`, `AccelError::RefitShape`. New exhaustive enums: `RadianceCacheTerm`,
  `TableAir`, `PrecipResolution`, `FogSunShadowRule`. (ADR-2656, ADR-2592, ADR-2310, ADR-4106, ADR-3294, ADR-3249,
  ADR-3122)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `BakedGiFootprint::edge` takes `(x, y, z, blend_distance)`. `Lightmap::irradiance_at`
  and related methods take stacked rows, and shaders that include `LIGHTMAP_WGSL` directly must append
  `lightmap_lookup_wgsl(false)`. `LocalCaptureDesc` / `LocalCaptureInfo` are no longer `Copy`, and `MAX_LOCAL_CAPTURES`
  is 32. (ADR-3998, ADR-3300, ADR-2860)
- **World:** `Node::Wind`; `NavLinkPhase::{Cancelled, Queued, TimedOut}`, `NavTileCacheError::GridGrowth`,
  `CrowdAgentState::{LinkQueued, LinkTimedOut}`, `NavLinkError::Io`, `NavLaneError::Io`; `WfcError::Cancelled`;
  `FluidError::BadSolid`, `GpuFluidError::TooManySolids`; `CookError::{Fluid, Collision}`; `SpawnMode::Event`,
  `ShapeDef::{MeshSurface, SkinnedMesh}`, `VelocityMode::Normal`, `RenderMode::Ribbon`; `Orientation::{Isometric,
  Staggered, Hexagonal}`, `Layout::WangCorner`, `Brush2d::Stamp`, `Mode2dError::{Stamp, NoStamp}`; `Panel::{Terrain,
  VfxEditor}`.
- **Gameplay systems:** `RagdollMode::Active`, `RagdollShape::Sole`, `RagdollFileError::BadActive`;
  `ToiFailure::DegenerateMotion`; `Generator::Custom` and `Test::Custom` (spatial query).
- **Gameplay systems:** `ImportError::Bvh`, `CookError::Animation`; the editor `Panel` enum gains `BehaviorTree`,
  `StateTreeEditor` and `MotionMatching`.
- **Gameplay systems:** `FeatureGroup::{FootContact, FootPhase}` (`COUNT` is 10), `MotionMatchError::BadContacts`,
  `VehicleError::{NoGearbox, InvalidGear}`, `hud::PauseAction::Settings` (sample), and the new set
  `regia_app::FixedUpdateSet`.
- **Networking and Steam:** `SessionError::KnownHosts`, `LinkEvent::HostKeyPinned`, `LinkError::KnownHosts`,
  `HostTransport::UdpAnyPort`, `WireOp::{Instance, Skip}`, `ContentError::{SignatureInvalid, SignatureMissing}`,
  `AssetsCommand::{Keygen, Sign}`, `LuaErrorCode::EntityCap`. `HostEvent` is `#[non_exhaustive]`.
- **Platforms:** `Format::AppImage`, `CookError::ColorLut`, xtask `Role::{Dll, Dxc, Launcher, Overlay}`; new enums
  `PacingHint`, `PacingCost`, `ScheduleExecutor`, `PowerSource`, `AnisotropyPath`, `Interface`.
- **Platforms:** `DlssStatus::runtime` can now read `ReportsUnsupported`, `NeedsNewerDriver` or `InitFailed` after a
  failed hook. (ADR-4937)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New variants: `GenError::Cancelled`, `PrefKind::Text`, `InputEdit` (three),
  `CookedMeshLoadError::TrailingBytes`, `PakError::UnsupportedFlags`, `ImageValidationError::ZeroSize`,
  `HeightfieldValidationError::ZeroSize`, `PlacedProblem::MissingInstanceGuid`, and the `regia assets pak-diff` CLI enum
  arm.
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `spawn_prefab` takes a final `root_guid: Guid`, and `instantiate_prefab` refuses a
  root without `EntityGuid`. `SuperResolution::evaluate` returns `DlssCommands` (use `dlss.submit(&queue,
  encoder.finish())`), `output_log_panel` returns `Option<LogLink>`, and `Player::draw_first` returns `Schedule`.
  (ADR-4806, ADR-2396, ADR-2696, ADR-2685)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `AiPlugin` installs `on_add`/`on_remove` hooks on `BehaviorTree`/`StateTree`, so hosts
  must register their own hooks before it. Glob imports of `regia_net::*` and `regia_procgen::*` may now clash on names
  such as `Seq` and `Direction`. (ADR-2396)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `Node::{Rail, Crane}`, `SequenceError::LoopTooDeep`, `SectionEdit::{TimeScale,
  Spatial, Repeat}`, `VideoEvent::{TextCue, TextCueEnd}`, render-out `Format::Webm` and `Sink::Encoded`,
  `CookError::{VoxelFar, Font, Video, UnsupportedPlatform}`, `ProjectPathError::{TrailingDotOrSpace, Remote}`,
  `UiError::Invalid`, `TextureRef` layer variants, `ScaleMode::IntegerScale`, `BindSource::Item`, editor
  `Panel::{AudioMixer, CueEditor, UiInspector, Video}`.
- **Cross-cutting:** `PostVolumeShape::ConvexHull`, `NetSet::Gameplay`, `StreamedOpen::AlwaysLoaded`,
  `TransportError::RemoteCleartext`, `LoadError::NoClip`
- **Cross-cutting:** `FRAME_STEPS` gains `shadow_occlusion` at index 0 and `shadows` reads it. (ADR-5178)
- **Release-candidate 2 fix round:** `regia_physics::vehicle::VehicleError::StateNotFinite` (ADR-7417).
- **Gameplay systems:** `DefKind::{Compare, StateTree}`, `BtCompileError::BadStateTree`,
  `StateTreeError::BadBehaviorTree`; new types `AbortMode` (`self` in RON and JSON), `BtCompareOp`, `BtValueKind`,
  `BtLiteral`. The `bt:` prefix in state-tree task paths is reserved. (ADR-4543)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia_editor::shell::Panel::{Cloth, Conversation}` (ids `cloth`, `conversation`)
  and `CookError::ImportRules(String)`. (ADR-2884, ADR-3298, ADR-5570)
- **Networking and Steam:** `session::SessionError::BanStoreSaveFailed` and `bans::BanError::NotSaved { id, error }`.
  (ADR-5881)
- **Rendering:** `RenderError::UploadTooLarge(String)`, `regia_asset::CookedMeshValidationError::NotTriangles { lod,
