  count }`; new enum `regia_render::MeshRefresh` (`Upload`, `Rewrite`, `Keep`). (ADR-5313)
- **Networking and Steam:** `SessionError::BanStoreSaveFailed(String)` (agent code `ban_store_save_failed`).
- **Core:** `regia_crash` `ReadError::IdentityUnproven`. (ADR-5410)
- **Networking and Steam:** `regia_steam::WorkshopEvent::{DownloadFailed, UnsubscribeFailed}`. (ADR-5124)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia_scene` `PlacedProblem::Spawn(String)` (`reason()` is `spawn_failed`).
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia_asset_pipeline::ImportError` gains `GltfExtension { code, extension, message
  }`, `Ply(PlyError)`, `Stl(StlError)`, `Psd(PsdError)` and `Usd(UsdError)`; `ImportError::code()` is new. (ADR-3016,
  ADR-3244, ADR-3235)
- **Rendering:** `ProbeSpecular::LobeHue` (ADR-2996); `regia_agent` render-out `Aov` and `AovFormat` gain variants and
  `AovPixels` a `material` field (ADR-3256).
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `Channel::Video`, `Section::Video`, `SectionEdit::{VideoTarget, VideoLoop,
  TimeScaleMode}`, `SequenceIssue::{SpawnFailed, VideoMissing, VideoFailed, VideoUnavailable}` (ADR-2948, ADR-2862,
  ADR-3197); `regia_editor::shell::Panel::Localization` (`BUILT_IN_PANELS` lists it; a saved layout gets it added)
  (ADR-3260).
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** xtask `plate_floors::Command::Attribute` (ADR-5798).
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia_editor::shell::Panel::{LookDev, UiAnim}`, `CookError::TexturePack`,
  `TerrainDataError::{AstcAlignment, AstcEncode}`; `TerrainDataError::AstcTilingUnsupported` is removed. (ADR-5896,
  ADR-3092, ADR-3090, ADR-5294)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `regia_ui::authoring::WidgetDef::{LobbyList, Scoreboard, Radar,
  MarkerLayer}`. (ADR-2958)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `DdsFormat::{Bc4Snorm, Bc5Snorm, Bc6hUf16, Bc6hSf16}` (ADR-3266);
  `dcc_feed_bake::BakeError::InvalidData(String)` (agent reason `invalid_value`);
  `scene_export::ExportError::NonFinite(String)` (`glb_non_finite`).
- **Networking and Steam:** `SteamCommand::Layout`. (ADR-1332)

### Changed signatures, behavior and defaults

- **Upscalers and asset packs (rc6):** new public constant `regia_render::upscaler::reason::DLSS_NEEDS_VULKAN`; TSR's
  two aux motion targets grow from 4 to 8 bytes per output pixel. Android projects with asset packs get new activity
  code on the next `regia package`. A sound of a fast-follow, on-demand or external chunk now travels only in that
  chunk's pak: load it through the asset server once the chunk is mounted, not as a loose base-package file. A large
  fast-follow pack on a metered network can show the store's Wi-Fi consent dialog at launch. Tools that match DLSS
  blocker lists on an NVIDIA DX12 device see the new `dlss_needs_vulkan` first (see Agent protocol under Changed).
  (ADR-7694, ADR-7695, ADR-7696)
- **Rendering:** `GpuCuller` defaults to the order-fixed pack, and an offline host that wants exposure adaptation must
  pass `set_exposure_dt` every frame. `extract_frame`'s light and camera queries now yield the entity. (ADR-4777)
- **Rendering:** `FrameRenderer::set_instance_set` can return false when the set does not fit, `DdgiSettings` replaces
  non-positive or non-finite fields with defaults, and `CloudSettings::map_resolution` clamps to 4096.
  `sun_shadow_pcf_only_for` takes `android: bool`, and `FrameRenderer::set_shadow_occlusion` is overridden every frame
  by `RenderSettings`. (ADR-2275, ADR-2482)
- **Rendering:** A host driving its own `ScatterField` calls `restart_lod` at job start, and a host running
  `server::start` itself inserts `server.progress`. `VfxGpuHandle::set_step_seconds` no longer needs 0 for a second
  render. (ADR-2672, ADR-2728)
- **Rendering:** `SurfaceInput` gains a trailing `facing: f32`, so graph pipeline keys with tangent-space normals change
  once. A graph with more than 16 static switches or 4096 lowered nodes fails to compile.
- **Rendering:** `regia_physics::vehicle::drive_vehicles` takes a rebase-holds parameter, and `RESOLVE_STORAGE_BUFFERS`
  is 11, so devices with exactly 11 or 12 storage buffers per stage get a lower visibility tier. Lightmap bakes refuse
  `bounces: 0`. (ADR-2435, ADR-4906)
- **Rendering:** `xtask::preflight::plan` takes `features: bool`, `preflight::Args` gains `no_features`, and a new Cargo
  feature needs a `MATRIX` row. Exact matches on `warm job panicked` must match the prefix, and frame-path counter pins
  now see the newly counted creates. (ADR-2322, ADR-2681, ADR-2303, ADR-2585)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** CLI bakes default to denoise, crease, stitch and auto density on, with denoise passes
  by density; `--max-pages` becomes 4 once the paged-bindings frame patch lands. `miss_cap` is 0, `DynamicGiTier::apply`
  writes the new DDGI fields, and aerial interiors are on. (ADR-2314, ADR-2404, ADR-4108, ADR-3300, ADR-2605, ADR-4757)
- **World:** `NavTileCache::grow_to` can return `nav_grid_growth` for west/south shifts;
  `TerrainSharedDraw::update_heights` with unchanged heights no longer bumps the generation; `MacroBake` accepts grids
  up to 16 x 16. (ADR-5007, ADR-2450, ADR-3367)
- **World:** Runtime hosts run `OnLoad` generators themselves; `FromShape`/`FromSpline` without a shape fails
  `procgen_bounds_missing`; unregistered `spawn.prefab` override paths fail to compile; baking a graph with overrides
  fails `procgen_bake_overrides`; `in.overlap` results can move to `outside`. (ADR-2421, ADR-3027)
- **World:** `apply_shake_requests` needs the `VfxView` resource; hosts drawing a `VfxScene` themselves pass
  `ribbon_vertices()` to `VfxGpu::write_trails`; `ui_interaction`'s query gained `Option<&UiTargetNode>`;
  `drive_vehicles` takes `Option<Res<PhysicsLayers>>`. (ADR-2406)
- **World:** `NodeKind` gains provided methods `writes_output` and `eval_owned`; `BuiltinNode::owned` is replaced by
  `BuiltinNode::in_place`; `procgen.cleared`/`procgen.detached` events drop `run_id`; `procgen.anchors` adds an `extra`
  key. (ADR-2724)
- **Gameplay systems:** New system parameters: `step_physics`, `check_joint_breaks`, `gather_cloth_wind`;
  `apply_cloth_reaction` takes a richer query. Apps that add these systems through the plugins need no change.
- **Gameplay systems:** Input: `evaluate_actions` runs in `FixedUpdate`, so `FixedUpdate` readers order
  `.after(InputSet::Evaluation)`. Use `just_pressed_this_frame()` in `Update`. Timers, tweens and coroutine second-waits
  also moved to `FixedUpdate`. (ADR-4768, ADR-4645)
- **Gameplay systems:** Startup panics if a tracked physics component is inserted, or its hooks taken, before the
  physics plugin builds. Old `break_force` tunings break about 4x sooner. Chance effects on guid-less targets refuse
  with `failed_chance`. (ADR-4755, ADR-4756)
- **Gameplay systems:** Behavior changes: `can_hear` passes across teams, the `AudioBusSettings` default is 1.0, and the
  Android `ClothBudget` default is 8,192. Sidecar-less level ids from absolute or backslash paths change once, and
  `Collision2d.a`/`.b` and overlap results come in collider-handle order.
- **Gameplay systems:** `MovementMode` custom ids at or above `0xFFFF_0000` are reserved for the engine. NaN or infinite
  glTF keys now fail `regia cook`, and project creation rejects application ids with Java reserved suffixes.
- **Networking and Steam:** `ServerReplication::ack_state` takes an `EntropyMode`; `WorkshopUpload::check` takes `&mut
  self`; `SealedHeader::counter` is the whole field (use `record_counter()`, `epoch()`); `destruction_net_server` takes
  `RelevanceInputs`.
- **Networking and Steam:** Defaulted additions compile unchanged: `HostGame` hooks, `SessionRules::owned_hand_off`,
  `AssetResolver::resolve_loaded` (a loading resolver should override it), `NetMessage::{VERSION, message_id}`,
  `SteamBackend` service methods.
- **Networking and Steam:** `NetSender` reads `NetAssets`: a system holding `ResMut<NetAssets>` and `NetSender`
  conflicts. (ADR-2665)
- **Networking and Steam:** A client that pinned a listen server refuses it after a key change; keep the key, use
  `allow_unpinned` for LAN, or `net.forget`. Players behind one IPv6 /64 share `max_peers_per_ip` (8) and bans.
  (ADR-3283)
- **Networking and Steam:** With `RulesPlugin` and `net`, `DefaultRules` spawns a character per remote player; a leaving
  peer's entities become server-owned. (ADR-2413)
- **Networking and Steam:** Replicated root positions snap to `position_step_m` (0.5 mm half-step by default; `None` for
  raw), and unregistered `Handle<CpuMesh>` and similar fields now send guids. (ADR-2665)
- **Networking and Steam:** A Lua mod reaching 4,096 entities gets `lua_entity_cap`; Steam Input set names change for
  non-ASCII contexts; terrain hashes change and terrains recook once. (ADR-2418, ADR-2440)
- **Networking and Steam:** Projects generated from `net-game` earlier keep their copies: copy the new client/server
  mains, `net_stats_line` and dev profile from a fresh project. (ADR-3075)
- **Platforms:** `frame_gen::desktop_gpu` takes `max_storage_buffers_per_shader_stage`; `BanStore::remove` returns
  `Result<bool, BanError>`; `load_level_lut`/`load_post_volume_luts` take `Option<ResMut<AssetServer>>`;
  `apply_ui_accessibility` takes `Option<Res<SystemTextScale>>`.
- **Platforms:** `regia_configurator::Resolution` (`statement: Option<String>`, `reasons: Vec<Refusal>`, `details`
  removed), `Refusal::reason` renamed `code`, `resolve::statement` removed; `PlannedMaterial::path_mm` replaced by
  `stone`.
- **Platforms:** `FORWARD_AXES` gains the `FORWARD_TRIM` axis. (ADR-3145)
- **Platforms:** Android: single-threaded schedules (opt out with `ScheduleExecutor::MultiThreaded`), Back is
  `BrowserBack`, large system font sizes enlarge game text, agent scratch output lives in app-private dirs. (ADR-4933,
  ADR-4932, ADR-4815)
- **Platforms:** Steam Deck: `RenderSettings::default()` is `Deck`; use
  `RenderSettings::from_profile(RenderProfilePreset::DesktopHigh)` to keep the old frame. (ADR-3289)
- **Platforms:** Windows: an existing `server.key` with a broad inherited DACL is refused at startup (delete it, mint a
  new one, redistribute the public key); editor config moves to `%APPDATA%\regia` with no migration; Intel GPUs no
  longer write `cache://pipelines/*.fpc`; a named shipping profile fails without exe icon and version info. (ADR-4828,
  ADR-4825, ADR-3288, ADR-2645)
- **Platforms:** Paths with `:` or Windows device names are refused on every host; `editor.content_chunks_state` refuses
  a `platform` outside `^ {1,32}$`; `server.toml` `net.identity_file` outside the server directory fails with exit 2.
  (ADR-1816)
- **Platforms:** A cooked `.cube` wins over a loose file of the same name; packages gain `sbom.json`; `android-pass
  --cycles` defaults to 50. (ADR-4830, ADR-3935, ADR-4904)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** Moved test files now run as `cargo test -p regia_ui --test suite <name>::`, and
  `xtask` gains `regia_platform`, `bevy_ecs`, `regia_net`, `regia_xr` and `regia_cli` dependencies. (ADR-2986)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Systems with extra parameters (only direct callers break): `evaluate_actions`
  (`InputAssists`, `LookSettings`), `audio_sync`, `tick_ui_materials`, `ui_scroll`, `apply_theme_text`,
  `ui_interaction`, `detect_ui_damage`. `MusicSystem::{play, tick, start_clock}` are no longer public. (ADR-2646,
  ADR-3464, ADR-2517, ADR-3025, ADR-2866)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Changed defaults: a game with no `AudioListener` hears from the camera; more
  than 4 (Android 2) decoding videos fail with `limit_exceeded`; fullscreen video with `layer >= 0` draws over the UI
  when `VideoOverlayPlugin` is added; `FirstPerson::bob > 0` now bobs; a mid-file WebM read error is
  `Failed`/`io_failed`, not `Ended`. (ADR-2517, ADR-2410, ADR-2486)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `NUMBER` and `DATETIME` are reserved Fluent function names; a `Bind.format`
  with `{` in literal text is now a placeholder; `.svg` under `assets/` moves into the pak; `assets/loudness.ron` as a
  raw file conflicts with packaging; `FileSaveStorage::read` returns `SaveError::Corrupt` over the cap. (ADR-2698,
  ADR-3350, ADR-4898, ADR-3285)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `FootstepPlugin` is in `regia::DefaultPlugins`; remove a host-added copy.
  (ADR-2442)
- **Cross-cutting:** Renamed or removed: `TouchPoint::started_at` becomes `age`. `MusicSystem::play`, `start_clock` and
  `tick` are private (ADR-2517). `PlayerCore::add_underruns` is removed. `import_fuzz::targets` returns
  `Vec<FuzzTarget>`. `SessionHub` must be built through its constructor. `meshlet_stream` requires the `client` feature.
- **Cross-cutting:** Defaults that change: `FOG_SHADOW_BIAS_TEXELS` is 0.5, `GpuCuller` uses the ordered pack, the
  Android `ClothBudget` is 8,192, Android schedules are single-threaded, and `regia package` validation is `Fail`. Zone
  exposure always holds without a step; supply `set_frame_dt` to adapt. `just_pressed()` is now per fixed step. (The
  zone-exposure method name differs between fragments: see the report.)
- **Cross-cutting:** Security behavior: agent clients must use `ws://127.0.0.1`. Windows servers refuse a `server.key`
  other users can read. Sealed clients refuse a changed host key. The Windows editor config moves to `%APPDATA%`. The
  Android Back key is `BrowserBack`.
- **Cross-cutting:** Determinism pins and digests move: physics rollback checksums, fluid digests, nav digests,
  abilities `BASE_TRACE`, the VFX V6 pin and replay hashes. Re-baseline any stored values.
- **Cross-cutting:** `NetCodec::to_bytes` returns `Result<Vec<u8>, WireError>`; `ProcGraphDocRoute::open` returns
  `Result<Option<ProcGraph>, ProcGraphRouteDeny>`; `locales_to_ship` returns `Result<_, MissingSourceLocale>`
- **Cross-cutting:** `poll_android_gamepads` takes `Local<AndroidPendingSamples>` and `Local<GamepadScanGate>`;
  `regia_media::av1::THREADS` is a `LazyLock<ThreadBudget>`; `HostGame::max_substeps` has a default body
- **Cross-cutting:** `material_key` values changed meaning; `SdfShape` and friends implement `PartialEq` (drop host
  impls); `Pooled` has an `on_remove` hook (conflicts with host hooks); `solid_impulses()` is empty after
  `set_moving_solids`; `OffscreenWarm::start_in_place` preserves the renderer on error. (ADR-4950, ADR-4963, ADR-4641
  proposed)
- **Cross-cutting:** xtask: `store_texts(windows_bytes, linux_bytes)`, `player_readme(...)` replaces `demo_readme`;
  store placeholder `{{INSTALL_MB}}` splits into `{{INSTALL_MB_WINDOWS}}` / `{{INSTALL_MB_LINUX}}`. (ADR-5002)
- **Cross-cutting (the post-v045b integrations):** the merges appended about 1,300 rows to
  `docs/api-snapshots/BREAKING.md`, each row named with its ADR; the per-item list there is authoritative.
  Named host-visible breaks not listed above: `regia_asset::ModsLimits::max_pak_entry_bytes` is removed —
  `max_file_bytes` (1 GiB default) is the one cap for a file and for one pak entry, so a struct literal that set it
  sets `max_file_bytes`. (ADR-5869, ADR-5870, ADR-5993)
- **Cross-cutting (post-v045b):** rendering internals changed shape — `DrawStats`, `ExtractedMesh`, `FrameMesh`,
  `GpuTimings`, `UploadStats`, `RenderError`, `LensEffects`, `AtmosphereLuts`, `gizmos::GizmoBatch`,
  `shader_reload::ShaderSwap`, `select_mesh_lods`, `extract_render_overrides`,
  `forward_permutations::FORWARD_SOURCES`, `lightmap::LightmapReceiverReport` and `DynamicCasterReport` (new
  `skinned_casters` field). (ADR-5418, ADR-5800, ADR-5898, ADR-5993)
- **Cross-cutting (post-v045b):** world and gameplay internals changed shape — `FftOcean`/`OceanCost` (foam-history,
  whitecap and tier-quality knobs), the scatter reports and stats (`ScatterReport`, `ScatterClassReport`,
  `ScatterFrameStats`, `ImpostorInstance`, `ImpostorProto`, `ImpostorSource`), `WaterCaustics`/`CausticStats` and
  `sync_water`/`sync_terrain_water`; `move_character`, `move_anchored_character`, `apply_force_fields` and
  `destruction::ActiveSection.colliders` (chunk-aligned handles); `GraphInstance`, `MontagePlayer::slot_layers`,
  `FinishedEval`, `MotionMatchFootLocks`, `run_motion_match_players`, `apply_animation`,
  `BlendSpace2D::weights_into`; BT/StateTree `BehaviorTreeDef`, `Blackboard`, `BtCompileError`, `DefKind`, `DefNode`,
  `BtOp`, `StateTreeError`, `StateTreeInbox`, `PerceptionStats`. (ADR-3146, ADR-4543)
- **Cross-cutting (post-v045b):** net and UI internals changed shape — `NetRelevanceView` (no longer `Copy`),
  `net_session_lifecycle` (takes `CleanExitInProgress`), `QuicListener::{certificate_der, local_addr}`,
  `QuicSocketControl::drop_next`, the voice `VoiceRelay`/`NetVoiceLink` methods, `WorkshopEvent`; UI
  `interaction::update_pointer`, `scrollbar::scrollbars_update`, `focus::ui_focus_navigation`,
  `soft_cursor::drive_soft_cursor`, `list::virtualise_lists`, `layout::mark_layout_dirty`, `tween::apply_ui_transforms`,
  `video::ui_video_controls`, `BackdropStats`, `UiHotPathCounts`, `loc_check::SelectorInfo`, `loc_po::ExportOutput`;
  `Audio::sync_emitter_frames` takes cues, `BusEffect` gains `Convolution` (exhaustive matches), `AudioReverbZone`
  gains IRs; `MeshValidationError` gains `HeightfieldTooLarge`, `CookedMeshValidationError`, `SplitBrick`,
  `TileMapAsset::tile_world_rect`, `SpritePassState`, `batch::PreparedFrame`, `extract_2d_frame`, `LoadedTileMap`.
  (ADR-2721, ADR-5699, ADR-5789)
- **Release-candidate 2 fix round:** `regia_framework::ambient_bird_calls` compiles only with `regia_framework/client`
  (it never compiled without it; `ground_shock` stays in every build). `SaveGame::save_raw` refuses a format 1 payload
  with entity ids, and `upgrade_save_bytes` leaves a payload older than format 1 for the game. `.ambience.ron` one-shot
  interval minima below 0.01 s are refused (`ambience_invalid`, `MIN_AMBIENCE_INTERVAL_SECS`). Characters on the
  rapier controller climb walkable slopes at walking speed; world queries pass through colliders with no entity.
  `regia_scene::layers::apply_member_presence` parks an `Absent` member with no template record. The first
  `regia package` per platform after updating cooks and packs once (new pack stamp, also written by `regia cook`).
  A `vfx_lab --deck-check` report no longer counts its start-up frame. A `TextInput` with no placeholder and no
  `Accessible` label no longer takes its content as its accessible name: it is reported as
  `a11y_unnamed_interactive`, so give it a placeholder or a label. A render-out take starts on a suspended audio output
  and leaves it suspended; `.hrir` files at another rate load at their measured level; a TOC dependency on a pak name
  mounted more than once orders the dependent after every such pak (ADR-9705). Every entity of a streamed placed level
  (not only its roots) carries `PlacedMember` of its instance: snapshot such a level by its own id (`snapshot_level`),
  since a full-world snapshot leaves its entities to it. New additive items:
  `regia_editor::autosave::{autosave_path_beside, autosave_unsaved_level_beside}`,
  `regia_platform::{CursorRelease, CursorCalls, OccludedAcquires, OCCLUDED_ACQUIRES_HIDE}`,
  `regia_steam::{SteamOverlayTrace, STEAM_OVERLAY_TRACE_ENV}`, `regia_agent::server::set_app_wake`,
  `regia_editor::mode2d::save_dirty_tilemap_and_mark_saved`, `UiAnimDoc::apply_all`,
  `FrameRenderer::{set_forward_decals, set_forward_decal_atlas, forward_decal_stats}`, `SolidField::refresh_heights`.
  (ADR-7388, ADR-7412, ADR-7413, ADR-7417, ADR-7419)
- **Platforms:** `regia::write_packaged_media_index` is gone; use `regia::PackagedMediaIndex` and `media_index.ron`.
  `spacemouse::report::DeviceDecoder::new` and `ReportLayout::known` take the product id (`0` when unknown;
  `DeviceDecoder::for_device` and `ReportLayout::known_with_buttons` are new), and `poll_android_gamepads` also takes a
  `Local<GamepadScanGate>`. (ADR-5881)
- **Gameplay systems:** `CollisionQuery::move_shape` takes `dt` and `carry_dt` (implementors add them);
  `advance_conversation_clock` (`Local<Option<u64>>`) and `refresh_physics_footstep_surfaces` take extra system
  parameters; `run_motion_match_players` skips `LayerParked` players; `GraphInstance::Layer::mask_joints` holds a cache
  key, not a joint count; `follow_goal` returns NaN for a non-finite target. New default methods:
  `VoicePlayer`/`RunnerEnv::caption_now_us`, `SaveStorage::read_previous_header` and
  `EntityIdMapper::entity_id_for_order` (override it when `entity_to_id` counts misses or assigns ids). (ADR-5881,
  ADR-5572)
- **Gameplay systems:** Prefab `i64` overrides past 2^53 keep the exact value, non-finite or inexact float overrides
  fail `override_type_mismatch`, and integer parameters with fractional bounds fail `param_type_mismatch`. Values move:
  the Linux x86_64 tire-transient digest, `det_exp_f64`/`det_powf_f64`/`det_cbrt`/`det_ln_f64` outside the tested
  grid, aarch64 `det_nlerp`, server-bot inputs, `road.pieces` on rolled splines; re-baseline stored values. (ADR-4773,
  ADR-5598)
- **World:** `instantiate_prefab` returns `SceneSpawnError::Invalid` for a nil root `EntityGuid`, and a nil placed-level
  id gets `missing_instance_guid`; a failed re-open of an active level leaves `LevelManager::state` at `Active`;
  `plant_export::host_load_mesh` refuses node cycles and partial triangles; `FluidSim::solid_impulses` is empty after a
  GPU sync. (ADR-5598)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `PcmFeed::channel` returns `Option<(PcmProducer, PcmConsumer)>`. Hosts that
  drive `VoiceChatSession` with the output tap call `acknowledge_output()` or `take_output()` after `played()`.
  `SoundAssets::add_packed` can return `AudioError::DecodeLimit` (stream sounds over 30 minutes),
  `ImpactBank::plan_break` returns `None` for non-finite magnitudes, 0 Hz WAVs fail to load, non-finite video volumes
  play at 1.0, and playlist entries after the first start at 0, not `phase_offset`. (ADR-5881, ADR-5589, ADR-5602)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `verify_checksums` adds `checksum_file_link` and `checksum_file_unreadable` and
  reports device names as `checksums_invalid`; `regia cook --lightmap-tiers` fails on an invalid `Regia.toml`; `regia
  loc cues export` with an unknown `--locale` writes nothing; Aseprite `.meta` values outside the atlas bounds fail
  `aseprite_structure`; non-portable MaterialX image paths warn `mtlx_texture_path`; `total_texels`/`page_texels`
  saturate; `regia assets pak-diff` no longer follows directory symlinks. `InspectorContext::record_lane_value` is new
  for custom Euler drawers. (ADR-5581, ADR-5570, ADR-5574)
- **Networking and Steam:** `#[derive(Replicate)]` refuses out-of-range `bits` literals in any spelling and clamps a
  const width, so a peer that still hashes the raw const disagrees on the protocol fingerprint. Registering an asset key
  on a second live handle moves the key to the newer handle (the older replicates its next key, or unset). A mock
  Workshop update with no valid files fails with `SubscribeFailed`.
- **Platforms:** Product viewer: `ProductRecord::canonical_picks` keeps conflicting spellings (`duplicate_value`);
  `Camera::clamped` resets or clamps out-of-range lenses; `Schema::compile` returns `SchemaError::Malformed` for
  non-schema `items`, `not`, `if`, `then`, `else`, `contains`, `additionalProperties` or `propertyNames`; `Solid::clip`
  adds no face for a non-cutting plane; `Library::look` returns `material_not_drawable` for substrate cycles;
  `bake_library` refuses non-increasing n/k spectra; an IOR below 1 draws as 1.5; generated gem meshes put length on
  +y; non-positive gem sizes fail `pack_bad_recipe`; positive `spin_deg` rolls counter-clockwise; `setPick` lists each
  adjusted group once. (ADR-5587)
- **Rendering:** `GizmoBatch::segments` is `Arc<[GizmoSegment]>` and `joints` is `Arc<[GizmoJoint]>` (use `Arc::from`);
  `select_mesh_lods` takes `Local<MeshLodSnaps>`; `render_override::OverrideReach` is the public `Local` of
  `extract_render_overrides`. A frame-gen luma buffer is indexed with `flow_luma_stride` (`FlowLevelPlan.luma_offset`
  counts padded rows). `LabFrame` is 768 bytes (trailing `clock`), `ShadowVp` 96, `VelocityFrame` 304, and bit 7 of
  `interior_half.w` marks a live clock. (ADR-5159, ADR-5160, ADR-5162, ADR-5206)
- **Rendering:** Hosts that edit `CpuMesh` bytes in place call `note_mesh_content_changed` (or edit through
  `with_unique_mesh_mut`), or the GPU copy stays stale. `regia_quick::Drawing` is not `Send`. LUT weights, weather
  coverage and cloud type share one blend or map per 1/256 step. `EmissiveLightReport.refits` rises only on a key-0
  fingerprint miss, and lab texture keys above 4,096 bytes change. `material_signature` values change for emissive
  maps; do not persist them. (ADR-5159, ADR-5208, ADR-5305, ADR-5313, ADR-5451)
- **Rendering:** `admit` returns `fits: false` when VRAM or RAM use is unmeasured (check `MemoryUse::vram_measured` and
  `MemoryUse::ram`); `.cube` files with an empty or inverted domain no longer load; a duplicate `LocalCapture` id on a
  second entity is refused.
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** A CPU probe trace with `geometry_only: true` returns zero harmonics. `PtSession::run`,
  `run_with` and `run_ranges` return before the device is idle. `LeakGeometry::triangle_count` counts a mesh once.
  (ADR-5154)
- **World:** `CellOutput.output` is `Arc<RunOutput>`. `ProcGenQueue` starts runs in `EntityGuid` order. Dense
  `NavPlugin` crowds use at most six ORCA neighbors and a crowd step without `NavRegionPlugin` rebuilds at most 16
  tiles; new `NavTileRevision`, `NavTileCache::take_partial`, `NavCrowd::sync_tile_revision`. `VfxProfile` event
  capacities return to 8192 (desktop High/Ultra) and 1024 (Mobile High). Separation is capped at
  `regia_fluid::SEPARATE_CELL_CAP`. (ADR-5143, ADR-5153, ADR-5313, ADR-5447)
- **Gameplay systems:** `ReplayEntity.kind` is `Arc<str>`. `CollisionQuery::move_shape` gains `dt` and `carry_dt`;
  `move_character` and `move_anchored_character` gain `carry_dt`. `physics_audio_occlusion` takes
  `ResMut<OcclusionRayStats>` plus three `Local` buffers (scheduling through `PhysicsAudioOcclusionPlugin` is
  unchanged). A host that adds `AircraftController` before `AircraftPlugin` builds panics at startup. `ShotPlayer`,
  `rotation_yaw`, `det_yaw_pitch_roll` at the poles and guid-keyed chooser draws change as described under Changed.
  A body spawned with a zero rotation starts at identity. New: `ChooserBinding::stream_id`,
  `regia_ecs::spawn_order::{SharedSpawnOrder, track_shared_spawn_order, try_track_shared_spawn_order}`,
  `Audio::voice_playing`, `Skeleton::write_globals`, `ClothSolver::reuse_snapshot`. (ADR-5135, ADR-5410, ADR-5411,
  ADR-5415, ADR-5416, ADR-5447)
- **Networking and Steam:** `NetVec` clones are shallow (copy on write); a transform changed after a lag-compensation
  query and before `LagCompRecord` in the same tick is not in history; a finished `NetVisualOffset` is removed. A
  `NetServer` never given a unix clock derives one from the wall clock, so its timed bans expire; replays supply the
  base with `NetSession::set_unix_base_s` or `SessionHub::set_unix_base_s`. `geom::blend` and `interp::nlerp` return
  the unnormalized blend on degenerate input. (ADR-4770, ADR-5297, ADR-5444)
- **Platforms:** Windows: a `server.key` or `known_hosts.ron` whose DACL grants any principal outside owner, `SYSTEM`
  and `Administrators` is refused at startup, and new files no longer inherit parent ACEs; a save over a destination
  held open without delete sharing fails after retries with the old document intact; the recents file moves to
  `%APPDATA%\regia\recent_projects.txt`. `DeviceDecoder::new(descriptor, product_id)` (pass 0 when unknown).
  `NullXr::submitted()` and `haptics()` hold at most 32 records. Android gamepads that never send input can take up to
  600 frames to appear. `regia_media::codec::DecodedPicture.planes` is `[Arc<[u8]>; 3]`. (ADR-5296, ADR-5301, ADR-5411,
  ADR-5414, ADR-5444)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia_cli::dcc_feed::ItemData::Skeleton`'s second field is `Vec<JointSource>`.
  `regia_agent::endpoint::write_owner_only_os` is new. (ADR-5291)
- **Core:** `det_atan2` zero-corner results follow IEEE (`atan2(±0, +0) = ±0`, `atan2(±0, −0) = ±π`) and NaN
  propagates; finite inputs are bit-unchanged. Recorded fluid digests from earlier pre-release pins no longer match.
  (ADR-5414, ADR-5416)
- **Rendering:** `GpuBatch::is_static` means every member is static-eligible, `DrawStats::static_batches` counts only
  all-static batches, `ShadowCaster::is_static` no longer changes shadow caching, and `StaticMoveWarnings::recent`
  holds one entry per moving entity. (ADR-5906)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `LightmapTierRule::default().padding` is `None` (was `Some(1)`) and is resolved from
  the tier's encoding (`LightmapTierRule::chart_padding`); new `LightmapTierRule::validate` and
  `MAX_TIER_CHART_PADDING`. Call `derive_lightmap_tier` to keep the bake's layout bit for bit, and recook tiers that are
  block-encoded without `padding` or use tightly packed imported UV2. (ADR-5621)
- **World:** `SwellInstant.terms` is private; use `SwellInstant::terms()`. Several `TileCache::request` calls for one
  slot before `tick` keep every view's tiles wanted. `GpuFluid::new` and `read_back` drop non-finite particles, and
  `regia_fluid` moving-solids digests recorded before this release no longer match. LDtk maps whose object ids
  collided get new ids on the next cook. `VfxPoolStats::events_overflow` counts one frame after the event fired, and
  `probe_steps`/`probe_effect` keep the GPU's events under overflow. (ADR-5219, ADR-5915, ADR-5734, ADR-5976)
- **World:** `lod_classes.ron` and `validation.ron` globs read `**/` as whole folders (tighten a glob that relied on
  cutting a folder name); after an over-budget despawn, lockstep announces the next unloads one step later.
  (ADR-5586, ADR-5940)
- **Gameplay systems:** `regia_framework::explosion::apply_explosions` takes
  `Query<(Option<&RigidBody>, Option<&ColliderHandle>)>`; `physics_wind::apply_wind_drag` takes
  `Query<(&RigidBodyHandle, &WindDrag)>` plus a `Local<Vec<(RigidBodyHandle, WindDrag)>>`;
  `physics_audio::refresh_physics_footstep_surfaces` takes a last `(Query<&ChildOf>, Query<&RigidBodyHandle>)`. Hosts
  that only add the plugins are unaffected. Bodies an earlier explosion pre-filter skipped are pushed again (use
  `Explosion::ignore`), and a silent impact now starts the body's cooldown. (ADR-5898, ADR-5899)
- **Gameplay systems:** `#[derive(BtTask)]` on a union is an error; a paused state tree keeps only the newest 64
  events; `MontagePlayback::new` resolves `start_offset` across section links. Lua: two handles for one entity are one
  table key, a component pass whose fresh charge plus hook exceeds `instruction_budget` disables the mod, and a
  non-finite `vec3`, non-finite bounds or a number/text key collision answer `lua_bad_value`; new `ModWorld` methods
  `set_component_prop` and `owned_count` have defaults. (ADR-5905, ADR-5733)
- **Networking and Steam:** `Lobbies::start_matchmaking` checks the create spec only when `create_if_none`;
  `AchievementsConfig::validate` refuses thresholds outside `i32`; `lobby()` may report a locked lobby as `Private`
  with `joinable: false`; `WorkshopItemStatus::error` also carries download and unsubscribe failures. New default
  methods on `LobbyBackend`, `SteamBackend` and `P2pMessages` need no host change. (ADR-5124, ADR-5217)
- **Platforms:** Windows: an `admin.token` or `server.key` whose DACL holds a callback, compound or unknown allow ACE is
  refused (replace it with plain ACEs); an `admin.token` granting only its owner, the current user, `SYSTEM` and
  `Administrators` now loads; `.regia/agent/token` and `endpoint.json` are refused to another local account; the
  Android signing file and content key also grant `SYSTEM` and `Administrators`. New public module
  `regia_net::private_file` (`create_private`, `exposed_to_other_users`). A `RUSTFLAGS` variable replaces the
  engine's stack link flags. (ADR-5880, ADR-5616, ADR-5617)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia_editor::code_reload::service_hot_reload_job` takes `Option<&HotReloadHost>`
  and is called every frame; `Client::set_read_timeout` works on Windows named pipes (`Some(Duration::ZERO)` is
  refused); new `regia_agent::local::{MAX_HANDSHAKE_BYTES, HANDSHAKE_ACCEPTED, HANDSHAKE_REFUSED}`,
  `endpoint::remove_socket_if_ours`, `regia_asset::project_ops::{asset_project_path, find_path_referencers}` and
  `regia_editor::content::find_path_referencers`. A moved asset's text references inside comments are not rewritten.
  A host that inserts `regia_agent::GpuDisabled` also gets it back from a product `render.out_job` and
  `ui.theme_preview`. (ADR-5879, ADR-5878, ADR-5877, ADR-5876, ADR-5180)
- **Regia Forest Demo:** `showcase::app::WarmTerrainBuild` is not `Clone`; `publish_detection` takes a
  `Local<PublishedDetection>`; `BootSplash::set_warm_queue(None)` now selects the inline pipeline (create a fresh
  `BootSplash` to keep the app's queue); `pipeline_warm::CANCELLED_AT_SHUTDOWN` is public. (ADR-5620)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** assets with a default volume, pitch or loudness gain play at that level on
  positional and emitter voices, and cues with an authored low-pass sound filtered on `play_cue_on` and emitter
  autoplay (clear `filter_cutoff_hz` and `filter_offset_octaves` for the old open sound).
  `UiHotPathCounts::layout_map_copies` is removed; a tween ending on the identity transform removes `UiTransform`
  (read it with `.copied().unwrap_or(UiTransform::IDENTITY)`). `VehicleAudioState` has no public constructor; read it
  from `VehicleAudioRuntime::vehicles`. `summary.json` `shaders.pipelines` is the warm-time count; the lifetime total
  is `shaders.pipelines_total`. (ADR-5926, ADR-5977, ADR-5900, ADR-5922, ADR-5152)
- **Core, licence and security:** `det_exp_f64` and `det_ln_f64` return different (correct) values only at the
  underflow and overflow edges and for subnormals; all other inputs keep their bits.
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia_editor::import_fuzz::targets()` returns `Vec<FuzzTarget>`. A model with
  `KHR_lights_punctual` lights brings them into the scene through the named hierarchy; pass `lights: false` for the old
  result. `GltfExtensionTable::unknown` no longer holds `KHR_materials_variants`, `KHR_lights_punctual`,
  `EXT_mesh_gpu_instancing` or `EXT_texture_webp` blocks. glTF files with signed 8- or 16-bit `COLOR_n` accessors fail
  to import: re-export them as unsigned normalized or float. `regia export glb`'s `mesh_missing` now means the cook
  lacked the mesh too. (ADR-3262, ADR-3016, ADR-5958)
- **World:** `WorldStreaming::pin` refuses a `radius_scale` outside `0.1..=8` with `StreamRuntimeError::BadIndex`
  (`stream_settings_invalid`) instead of clamping it, and `open_streamed_from` returns
  `StreamOpenError::Format(WorldFormatError::Corrupt(..))` for an index that fails validation. A sun that moves by less
  than half of a 1/1024 bin reuses the previous terrain bounce grid (ADR-5234). A destructible with more than 65,536
  bonds or a name or `fx_tag` over 256 bytes fails `validate`, `apply_events` refuses a batch over 65,536 events
  (`DestructionError::BadEvent`), and `ClothSolver::add_grid` / `add_rope` refuse more than `MAX_ASSET_PARTICLES`
  particles (`ClothError::Degenerate`); `MAX_DESTRUCTIBLE_BONDS`, `MAX_DESTRUCTIBLE_STRING` and `MAX_EVENTS_PER_CALL`
  are re-exported from `regia_physics::destruction`.
- **Rendering:** A host that creates its own Vulkan instance with `wgpu::Instance::new` does not keep the drivers
  loaded: create it with `regia_render::create_window_instance` or create a `RenderDevice` first (ADR-5794).
  `forward_permutations::FORWARD_SOURCES` is `[(&str, &str); 9]`, and `TranslucentLayer::forward_decal_feed` has a
  default body (ADR-3255). `BvhTriangle::a[3]` values in `(0, 1)` are masked coverage, zero stays opaque (ADR-4557). The
  `regia_pt` lightmap stitch takes the page height (`page_rows`), and `PtSession::run_ranges` can return
  `PtError::Scene("workgroup range does not fit in two dispatches")`.
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Hosts that run `ui_video_controls` outside `UiVideoPlugin` insert
  `UiVideoPendingSeeks`; `tick_ui_materials` takes `MaterialTick` when present. `PcmFeed::channel` returns an `Option`;
  `physics_audio::PairVoice` gains `sound` and is no longer `Copy`; `virtualise_lists` writes `ScrollDirty` instead of
  `LayoutDirty`; `UiHotPathCounts::layout_map_copies` is removed (ADR-5900). `SoundAssets::add_packed` can return
  `AudioError::DecodeLimit` (ADR-5471). `AnimatedSprite`'s reflected field is `time_ns`; saves with a float `time` still
  load.
- **Networking and Steam:** `BanError::NotSaved` is removed (drop the arm; ADR-5833).
  `regia_net::session::lifecycle::MAX_AUTH_STRIKES` starts the backoff instead of a lockout, and `AUTH_STRIKE_WINDOW_MS`
  counts from the last wrong try (ADR-5993).
- **Platforms:** `regia::write_packaged_media_index` and the `regia-media-index.txt` file are gone: packages carry
  `media_index.ron` (`regia::PACKAGED_MEDIA_INDEX`, `PackagedMediaIndex`, with length and FNV-1a stamps and pruning,
  behind the `media` feature), and packages written by older builds fall back to the directory walk.
- **Cross-cutting:** `regia_configurator::schema::MAX_EVAL_STEPS` is removed (use `MIN_EVAL_STEPS` and
  `EVAL_STEPS_PER_VALUE`): `Schema::validate` has one budget of 1,000,000 evaluations plus 256 per value of the
  document, and a document past it fails with a `maxSteps` violation (`contract_too_deep`), never a pass; the `maxWork`
  keyword is gone. `regia_input::spacemouse::DeviceDecoder::for_device`, `ReportLayout::known_with_buttons` and
  `known_button_count(vendor, product)` are new, and descriptor-less decoding counts buttons by vendor and product.
  `regia_framework::chooser::binding_stream` is public. (ADR-5922, ADR-5923)
- **Rendering:** `regia_pt::PtTexture.rgba` is `Arc<[u8]>` (was `Vec<u8>`): build it with `Arc::from(vec)` or
  `shared.pixels_arc()`. `render_out::aov::AovRequest::parse` returns `RpcError`. A cloth section's `MeshRenderer` mesh
  is no longer a fresh `Arc` per frame: frames share one cache key and alternate two arcs, so code that relied on `Arc`
  identity changing must call `note_mesh_content_changed`. (ADR-5535, ADR-3256)
- **Rendering:** An IES file with a `TILT=INCLUDE` table whose factor at 0° is not 1 now cooks a peak (and flux) scaled
  by that factor; a tilt table that is not ascending, or has a negative factor, is `IesError::Invalid`. WGSL
  `probe_spec_channel` takes a fourth parameter (pass `0.0` for the earlier result), and `DdgiUniform::ctrl.w` bit 7
  belongs to the probe specular mode. (ADR-3247, ADR-2996)
- **Rendering:** `terrain.get` `anisotropy_lod_offset` is measured: 0.25 on a Mali phone where it was 0; set
  `REGIA_TERRAIN_ANISO_LOD_OFFSET=0` or call `TerrainGpu::set_anisotropy_lod_offset(0.0)` for the old filtering.
  `seeing.temporal_chart` returns different ghosting numbers for the same request, and with `upscaler: "fsr3", scale:
  0.67` `checks.ghosting.met` is now false. (ADR-5797, ADR-5890)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `BakedGiVolume::join_split_set` takes each brick from a part that holds its first
  cell (before: the part that owned it) (ADR-3237). A host that read `level.get_baked_gi` `installed_by_level: false`
  as "a part is missing" must read `parts.failed` (ADR-3006). `LightmapLevelPlugin` registers `CellLoaded`,
  `CellUnloading` and `StreamedLightmapCells` and runs `sync_cell_lightmaps` (ADR-3318).
- **World:** `WaterQuery` reads `RenderSettings` when present; a host that drives `FrameRenderer` without
  `WaterPlugin` calls `WaterRenderer::set_camera_cuts(renderer.camera_cut_count())` each frame for foam restarts.
  `ImpostorProto` gains `season` and is 80 bytes (`extra[1]` is the bare card, negative for none),
  `ImpostorInstance::pad` is now `leaf_rank`, and `ImpostorSource` holds `parts: Vec<ImpostorPart>`. Re-run the
  stream cook so cooked levels keep their `LevelSettings`. (ADR-2843, ADR-3146, ADR-5608)
- **Gameplay systems:** Register `MapEntities` (`#[reflect(Component, MapEntities)]` plus an `impl MapEntities`) on
  every saved component that holds an `Entity`; without it the component is no longer saved
  (`save_entity_ref_unmapped`), and a reference past the table refuses the load (`SaveError::Corrupt`).
  `ScriptCtx::physics()` collides with a local trait that adds `physics`; systems ordered against `ScriptsSet::Late`
  now really order against `late_update`. A touched `VirtualStick` overrides device input for its action while the
  finger is down. A steady kinematic ragdoll's bodies are no longer marked `Changed<Transform>` every step. Default
  eye limits move by up to 0.2°. (ADR-3239, ADR-5613, ADR-5610, ADR-5669, ADR-5614)
- **Networking and Steam:** `NetNode::peers`, `has_peer`, `ServerReplication::holders`, `pending_acks` and `net.resend`
  name and count real peers only; peer ids from `0xFFFF_FF00` are reserved (`FIRST_VIRTUAL_PEER`);
  `NetInterpolationPlugin` stands down while `NetReplayClock` exists. A hand-written `ReplicateCodec` that sends an
  entity by another route should call `h.write_str("entity")` in `layout` (this changes its protocol hash). (ADR-3021)
- **Networking and Steam:** A Lua component declaration with more than 64 `options`, an option over 256 bytes or more
  than 64 KiB of names, options and defaults is refused (`lua_bad_value`); a Lua mod whose component hooks together
  exceed one instruction budget per frame is disabled (`lua_instruction_budget`). On a passworded server ten wrong
  passwords in a minute make that address block wait up to a minute. (ADR-5870)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** Rebuild dynamic extensions with `regia plugin build`: a library without
  `REGIA_EXTENSION_STAMP` answers `not_an_extension`. A different compiler or target is an `abi_mismatch` naming
  `rustc_hash` or `target_triple`. (ADR-2844)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** An editor session refuses every reload after its engine dylib is rebuilt, and also
  after any other cargo build into the same target dir lays down another `libregia_dylib-<hash>`: restart the editor.
  `regia package --profile shipping` reads `[profile.shipping]` only from the workspace `Cargo.toml`. (ADR-5627)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** A glTF that shares a node between parents no longer imports; a glTF image over 32768
  texels or over 4 GiB of images in all is an error. (ADR-5869)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `AudioSpatial::new` turns Doppler on (factor 1): set `doppler: 0.0` for the v4
  sound. A `reflect:<Component>.<field.path>` property track with no registered applier is now applied through
  reflection instead of skipped. A player with spawnables starts in `waiting` (pre-roll). `regia_framework::sequencer`
  re-exports the take recorder names, so glob imports may clash. `SoundAssets::add_packed` can return
  `AudioError::DecodeLimit`. (ADR-3197, ADR-2637, ADR-2948, ADR-5471)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** A tween that ends on the identity transform removes the entity's
  `UiTransform`: read it with `.copied().unwrap_or(UiTransform::IDENTITY)`; `ui.tween` describe reports `transform:
  null` for such a node. (ADR-5900)
- **Platforms:** A process that sets `REGIA_JSON_LOG`, `REGIA_PLAY_INPUT` or `REGIA_SMOKE_SECONDS`, or passes
  `--play-input`, now plays a recording or exits after the smoke limit; unset, behavior is unchanged. New
  `regia_framework::startup_playback` and `regia_core::log::{init_with_layer, ExtraLogLayer, JSON_LOG_ENV}`. (ADR-3968)
- **Rendering:** `DirectionalLightUniform::_pad_dir` carries the sun specular disc radius as `f32` bits: write 0 or use
  the constructors. `EmitterGpu::events.y` carries the vortex `radial`, and effects with a non-zero `radial` now move
  differently. (ADR-3190, ADR-5629)
- **World:** `water_body::plugin::sync_water`'s last parameter is a tuple with the new `DrawnOceansReport`, and
  `WaterPlugin` adds `cache_drawn_water` to `PreUpdate`. A floater out of the water has no `ExternalForce` (treat
  `None` as zero). Android terrain slots must be square powers of two of at least 256 texels, and hosts that loaded a
  terrain input's generic cooked copy by path load the tiles instead. (ADR-3251, ADR-5817, ADR-5294)
- **World:** A `.montagevar.ron` with extra fields fails with `VariantError::Ron`; a `.road.ron` piece `side` must be
  `Left` or `Right`; `filter.compare` on a vector needs a component suffix (`position:y`); `|placed` counters have no
  spacer rows. (ADR-5633, ADR-5645, ADR-5644)
- **Gameplay systems:** Hosts reading a paused tree's `StateTreeInbox` see its events until it resumes;
  `RetargetReport::mapped` is ordered by rest correction (look roles up by `role`); `ComponentDecl::props` is in name
  order (use `ComponentDecl::prop(name)`); `AbilityStats::warnings["unknown_attribute"]` counts once per application;
  `CurveRef` derives `Reflect`; a text `.chooser.ron` NaN fails with `chooser_bad_number`. (ADR-5779, ADR-5816,
  ADR-5517, ADR-5639, ADR-5638)
- **Networking and Steam:** A client value in a `#[replicate(skip)]` field is kept through updates (reset it yourself);
  `ReplicateCodec::keep_local` and `HostGame::set_net_rates` are provided methods. Under the dedicated host the
  `server.toml` rates win over `config/net.ron`, clients get `HitShapes`, and the console `status` prints more than
  one line. `Lobbies::start_matchmaking` with `create_if_none` can return `MembersInvalid`, `TooManyKeys`, `KeyInvalid`
  or `ValueTooLong`. `VoiceChatTransport` hosts see extra `VoiceControl::MuteHint` messages. A bound predicted spawn
  gets no `NetVisualOffset` at bind. On a server with a streamed level open, profiles other than `sent_to_all`,
  `owner_only` and `follow_parent` gain `custom_filter: Some("stream_cells")`. (ADR-5648, ADR-5650, ADR-5656,
  ADR-5642, ADR-5649, ADR-4011)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `Audio::sync_emitters` also takes the trailing `cues` argument, and a
  `config/audio.ron` now wins over an inserted `AudioSettings::buses`. `CaptionBoard::advance` drops filtered rows
  already on screen. A game that repairs a broken `repeat` source with `bypass_change_detection` must mark the resource
  changed. A menu item with an empty `shortcut` shows its registered chord. `AnimatedSprite` saves gain `time_sub`
  (older saves load with 0). (ADR-5699, ADR-5778, ADR-5814, ADR-5665, ADR-5795)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `AssetSet::format_version` reads 2 after loading a v1 file; a `.functest.ron` whose
  `Inject` fails stops at that step; chunk reference error lines start with their reason code; Java keywords and
  Windows device names are `BadName`; `GameRules::type_path` is a provided method. New public items:
  `regia_agent::local`, `Client::connect_local`, `endpoint::{write_socket, read_socket, remove_socket, SOCKET_FILE}`,
  `regia_asset::project_ops`, `regia_agent::hot_reload::{HotReloadHook, HotReloadHandler, HotReloadJobPending}`,
  `TimerHub::infos`, `FogVolume::interior`. `Client::set_read_timeout` on a Windows named-pipe connection returns
  `ClientError::Connection`. (ADR-5632, ADR-5637, ADR-5636, ADR-5643, ADR-5708, ADR-5700, ADR-5707, ADR-3257)
- **Platforms:** Android packages always hold `media_index.ron`; a media file with `:` or `\` in its path is staged but
  not extracted on Android (rename it); `regia::is_safe_packaged_media_path` is new. (ADR-5795)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `DynamicGiTier::High.settings() != DdgiSettings::default()`, and a fresh
  `FrameRenderer::ddgi_settings()` returns the High tier's settings: build High from `DynamicGiTier::High.settings()`
  (or `apply`). High and Ultra carry `dims: [20, 12, 20]` (19 200 probes). `DdgiSettings::normal_bias` and `view_bias`
  steer only the probes' visibility test on every layout, `visibility_blend` selects only the cascade blend, and
  `front_weights` no longer includes the edge term (set `edge_visibility` too to keep that look).
  `DynamicGiTier::matches` is false for settings copied from the previous tiers until the new fields are set. Hosts
  that size `sdf_pool_bytes` to an exact mesh set need about 7-36 % more. `DdgiReadback::irradiance` axis texels with
  `x + y` odd hold the irradiance at the surface behind the probe, `DdgiReadback::rays` carry the hit normal in the six
  lowest mantissa bits, and a host shader reading packed tiles through `ddgi_packed_irr_load` must skip the corner
  texel (0, 0). Every frame with dynamic GI changes. (ADR-5001, ADR-5290, ADR-5338, ADR-5457, ADR-5506, ADR-5716)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** recook lightmap tiers (every derived tier's texels change). A repacked tier's UV2
  differs from the bake's, so a host that built receivers from the bake must rebuild them from the tier's `.flmap`
  (`regia_render::lightmap::receiver_mesh` does). New `derive_lightmap_tier_padded`, `DEFAULT_TIER_CHART_PADDING`,
  `BLOCK_TIER_CHART_PADDING`; `derive_lightmap_tier` keeps the bake's layout. Re-bake lightmaps to drop the denoiser
  leak; older `.flmap` files load unchanged. (ADR-5125, ADR-7590)
- **Platforms:** a `DisplayPacer` made by hand has no wake-ahead until `set_wake_ahead` is called; `PlatformPlugin`
  inserts the `FrameWakeAhead` and `FramePerfHint` resources. On Android `seeing.memory_report` `reserved_bytes` reads
  lower (smaller allocator blocks). `regia package`'s `sbom.json` lists only the package platform's crates.
  (ADR-5461, ADR-5423)
- **Rendering:** the frame uniform's `texture_lod.w` can be negative (the sampler path with that many mips of offset);
  1 still selects the footprint path. `regia_render::backend_policy::clears_integer_targets_exactly` is new. For exact
  skinned shadows in all four cascades set `DynamicCasterShadows::On { cascades: 4, .. }`. (ADR-5459, ADR-5807,
  ADR-5418)
- **World:** `VfxScene::spawn` can return `None` for a loaded name when a per-system significance cap refuses it; a
  host that sets no caps runs the same schedule as before. (ADR-3275)
- **Networking and Steam:** `SteamBackend::restart_app_if_necessary(&self, AppId) -> bool` has a default body
  (`false`); a host backend wrapping the real client that wants the shipping restart must override it. `net_game`
  gains features `steam` and `steamworks`. (ADR-5375)
- **Audio:** `db_to_gain` returns 0 for NaN and clamps at +120 dB; `distance_gain_faded` returns 0 for a NaN
  `max_distance` and plays the curve without the fade for an infinite one; `Audio::played()` keeps the most recent 4096
  entries; `Audio::resume_output` returns `false` during a render-out mixdown and applies the resume at `end_mixdown`;
  `AmbiencePlayer::step` makes at most 256 calls per one-shot per step.
- **Core:** `cargo xtask notices --check` sees optional dependencies: a crate that adds an optional dependency must
  regenerate the notices. (ADR-5556)
- **Gameplay systems:** `PhysicsPlugin` tracks `ForceField` spawn order: adding it panics at build when a `ForceField`
  already exists or another owner took its component hooks, so add `PhysicsPlugin` before spawning force fields. A
  character on moving kinematic ground keeps its place instead of riding a second carry after a contact, and
  trajectories recorded on such ground change in their last bits (on a rising platform it now rests at its skin width,
  1.7 cm lower at 1 m/s and 60 Hz). (ADR-5583, ADR-5972)
- **Gameplay systems:** A `FixedUpdate` system sees a multi-step frame's mouse motion on the last step; determinism pins
  and recorded snapshot hashes that cover traversal moves or entity sets and maps must be re-pinned;
  `Changed<BehaviorTreeStatus>` no longer means "the tree ran"; a rig that skips an evaluation also skips spring and
  gaze writes; `AnimationClip::sample_curves` no longer empties `out` first (a longer buffer is truncated, surviving
  slots overwritten); montage cursor times and root motion can differ in the last bits; a game that relied on
  `lua.restart` keeping a failed run's entities finds only that run's spawns. (ADR-5599, ADR-5573, ADR-5100, ADR-5606)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `PcmFeed::channel` returns `Option<(PcmProducer, PcmConsumer)>` and refuses
  channel counts outside 1..=8; `VoiceChatSettings::validate` (and `from_ron`) refuses `redundancy: true` with
  `frames_per_packet: 2`.
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `generated_mesh_export::generated_mesh_glb` returns `Result<Vec<u8>, GlbWriteError>`
  (`GlbWriteError` is re-exported from the crate root); `GpuMeshError::Malformed`'s text for a non-triangle LOD changed
  (match the variant); new stream-cook warning code `stream_priority_volume_invalid`. A game's own WGSL checked by
  `cargo xtask check-shaders` or `shader_hlsl::wgsl_dx12_problems` can now fail the FXC assertion rule, and
  `cloud_shadow_lookup` treats a switch of exactly 0.5 or NaN as off. (ADR-5971)
- **Rendering:** The grass shader's `GrassParams` uniform grew from 224 to 256 bytes (hosts that copy
  `grass_common.wgsl` take the new struct); `ShockShakePlugin` also installs `BlastGustGrassPlugin`; `AIR_SPEED_M_S` and
  `SPEED_OF_SOUND_M_S` alias `regia_vfx::crash::SHOCK_FRONT_SPEED`. Hosts with `CloudSettings::shadow_map` on get darker
  air and, on Desktop High and Ultra, less probe bounce under clouds; flat fog with rain under a closed cover is 0.12 of
  its color. `LookMaterial::params` never returns a negative attenuation, and `CirrusSheet::lerp(a, b, 1.0)` returns `b`
  exactly. (ADR-1497, ADR-5944)
- **Networking and Steam:** Use `is_locally_controlled_owned(role, owned, owner)` on the authority
  (`is_locally_controlled` is unchanged); servers see `client_quit` instead of `suspended` for a desktop clean exit; a
  second ban of one target refreshes its row; `regia_platform::CleanExitInProgress` is exported in every build;
  `SteamBackend::input_controller_type` has a default body; tests that set `MockSteam::set_input_activation_fails(true)`
  read no action values until they clear it. (ADR-5580, ADR-5789, ADR-1332)
- **Platforms:** On a Deck `ActiveGlyphs::kind` is `SteamDeck` before any pad connects (`steam.deck`, `steam.glyphs` and
  `steam.layout` report `deck`); `PromptControl::canonical` maps `Axis(DPadY)` to `Axis(DPadX)`; `read_power_supply_dir`
  returns `unknown` when the only supplies are `scope=Device` or `present=0`; a host-built `DisplayPacer` gets the grid
  release and grid follow by default (`set_grid_release(false)`, `set_grid_follow(false)` for the old behavior); a game
  that re-enabled `back_closes_primary` and expected an earlier Back to close the window must handle it itself;
  `Residency::stats` totals are cumulative across suspends. (ADR-1332, ADR-5946, ADR-5600)
- **World:** `TileMapAsset::tile_world_rect` returns `([f64; 2], [f64; 2])`; convert with `as f32` after the map's
  transform. `LevelTerrain::validate` rejects `height_scale_m` above 1e6 m. Multi-tag procgen query dependencies re-bake
  once, and `StaticSnapshot::content_hash` values change (nothing persists them). (ADR-4634)

### Agent protocol

- **Rendering:** New agent methods: `render.brdf_energy`/`set_brdf_energy`,
  `render.forward_studio`/`set_forward_studio`, `render.frame_work`, `render.ssr_stats`/`set_ssr_cost`,
  `render.get_visbuffer_residency`/`set_visbuffer_residency`, `render.vis_lod_hysteresis`, `seeing.temporal_chart`,
  `seeing.frame_aov`, `app.startup_work`, `memory.idle_ledger`, `telemetry.screenshot_mark`, `assets.texture_sets`,
  `water.set_caustics`/`caustics_stats`, and `editor.matgraph_*`. `AgentPlugin` now installs the breadcrumb and
  telemetry methods and `StartupWorkPlugin`. (ADR-2632, ADR-2308, ADR-2711, ADR-2557, ADR-3118, ADR-4631, ADR-2638,
  ADR-2951, ADR-2987, ADR-2307, ADR-3320, ADR-2432, ADR-2318, ADR-2490)
- **Rendering:** Additive reply fields that strict clients must accept: `play.hitch_report` `creates`/`creates_dropped`,
  `render.out_progress` `warming`/`app_busy`/`busy_ms`, `render.get_upscalers` `reactive_mask`/`unavailable_code`,
  `render.get_capabilities` `upscalers`, `render.graph` `dispatch` and the extra `prepass_occlusion` step,
  `render.get_visbuffer` masked/sway/LOD fields, `render.permutations { shader_costs, platform }`, and
  `material.compile` limits and codes. New error codes include `brdf_energy_bad_mode`, `forward_studio_bad_mode`,
  `bench_unknown_feature`, `permutation_budget_exceeded`, `material_graph_layout_invalid`, `xess_needs_vulkan` and
  `dlss_package_invalid`. (ADR-2303, ADR-2582, ADR-3101, ADR-2679, ADR-4905, ADR-2664, ADR-4901, ADR-4656, ADR-4758)
- **Rendering:** New CLI: `regia assets texture-sets`, `regia render --reference --preset/--caustics/--denoiser`
  (`--spp`/`--bounces` optional), `regia bench --on/--preset`, `cargo xtask check-shaders --matrix`, `cargo xtask
  check-features`, and `cargo xtask gpu-bench --suite features`. New environment variables: `REGIA_BRDF_ENERGY`,
  `REGIA_FORWARD_STUDIO`, `REGIA_SSSR_COST_BOUND`, `REGIA_PACKAGE_DLSS`, `REGIA_XESS_SMOKE_*` and
  `REGIA_DLSS_CHECK_STUB`. (ADR-3320, ADR-2433, ADR-2309, ADR-2644, ADR-2322)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** New methods: `render.aerial_interiors`, `render.set_aerial_interiors`,
  `render.get_contact_calibration`, `render.set_contact_calibration`, `render.fog_shadow`,
  `render.lightmap_encode_report`, `render.set_baked_gi_brick_probe`, `render.restir_gi`, `render.set_restir_gi`,
  `render.set_many_light_sampling`, `render.many_light_stats`, `render.set_light_profile`, `render.save_local_captures`,
  `render.load_local_captures`, `render.get_capture_bake`, `render.set_reference_overlay`, `seeing.capture_plate`,
  `terrain.trace_proxy`, `terrain.set_trace_proxy`.
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** New result keys (for example `seeing.compare` `psnr_db`, bake JSON
  `density_scaled`/`pages`/`timings`/`checkpoint`, and `density.warnings` code `lightmap_atlas_scaled`) and new error
  codes for bake checkpoints, lightmap tiers, levels, `.fcap` and the radiance cache.
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** New CLI: `regia bake-captures [--check]`, `regia cook --lightmap-tiers [--json]`, and
  `regia bake-lightmaps` flags `--checkpoint`, `--resume`, `--max-pages`, `--encoding`, `--denoise`, `--crease`,
  `--stitch`, `--auto-density`, `--seam-budget` and `--write-level`.
- **World:** New feature `fluid` on `regia` and `regia_dylib` (launcher and dylib must agree), `gpu` and `physics` on
  `regia_fluid`.
- **World:** New agent methods in `fluid.*`, `nav.*`, `procgen.*`, `stream.*`, `terrain.*`, `vfx.*`, `editor.vfx_*`,
  `editor.procgen_*`, `ui.*`, `material.sway`, `world.hlod_proxy`, `render.voxel_far_stats`, `physics.*`, and seven new
  `procgen.*` event kinds; tools that match `agent.schema` exhaustively see them.
- **World:** New CLI: `regia procgen bake`, `regia procgen verify`, `regia tiled-autotile`; `terrain_lab
  --foliage-wind`, `showcase --foliage-wind`.
- **Networking and Steam:** New agent methods listed under Added; `session.state` adds `players`, `net.messages` adds
  `version`/`stable_id`, `net.conditioner_set` returns `conditions`, `editor.capture_viewport`/`editor.hierarchy` take
  `client`, `lua.list` adds cap fields, `net.crypto` adds `rekeys`/`pre_proof_shed`.
- **Networking and Steam:** New CLI: `regia assets keygen|sign`, `regia assets chunks --max-size`, `regia server` split
  crates with `--relay`, `regia package --server`, server flags `--shared-port`, `--bots N`, `--join CODE`; xtask
  `net-match`, `coverage`, `templates-check`, `steam-demo-upload`.
- **Networking and Steam:** New features: `net-quic` (quinn 0.11.12), `content-signing`, `regia_ai/net`.
- **Platforms:** New methods: `executor.get/set`, `platform.power_state`, `renderer.minspec_explain`,
  `xr.walk_start/walk_set`, `viewer.*`, `viewer_app.*`, `looks.*`, `configurator.*`. New fields: `pacing.*` additions,
  `render.get_profile.runtime_default`, `terrain.get.anisotropy_path`, `play.hitch_report.blocking_poll_sites`,
  `ui.a11y_platform_*.android`, `platform.paths` `pipeline_cache.state: driver_skipped`.
- **Platforms:** `configurator.resolve` answers reason objects (`reasons[].code`); the touch `a11y_target_size` unit is
  dp; match `xr_no_loader` by code, not message. New CLI: `regia clean`, `regia viewer`, `regia render --job`, `cargo
  xtask binary-size`/`web-player`.
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New methods (additive): `agent.job_status`, `agent.job_cancel`, `agent.run_tests`,
  `agent.open_in_editor`, `review.*`, `agent.golden_test`, `agent.bless_golden`, `editor.*` baseline, diagnostics,
  viewport, `editor.inspect_selection`, `kinds.*`, `project.describe`, `import.report`, `import.materialx_report`,
  `assets.import_rules`, `assets.collision_report`, `assets.pak_diff` (permission `tools`), `cook.cache_stats`,
  `asset.export_glb`, `gen.*`, `procgen.wfc_solve`.
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** Breaking: wrong-typed optional params and `5.0` for an integer are refused. Clients
  must handle `{ job, state: "running" }` from `gen.*` and `golden_path_denied`, and `seeing.reference_trace` may return
  `too_many_pending`. (ADR-2622, ADR-2821, ADR-2396)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** Breaking: `editor.hot_reload` is removed: call `agent.hot_reload`
  (build permission, MCP tool `hot_reload`), a job whose result reports the reload, or poll `editor.code_status`.
  (ADR-5993)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New methods (the post-v045b integrations): `agent.build_game`, `agent.hot_reload`,
  the `abilities.*` family, net replay (`net.replay_open/close/play/seek/state`, `net.replay_record_start/stop`,
  `net.replay_ring_export`), ambience (`audio.ambience_list/play/stop`, `audio.bus_effect_set/bus_effects`,
  `audio.convolution_stats`, `audio.time_stretch_probe`), `camera_body.*`, `cutscene.*`, `bird.*`, `sequence.record_*`,
  `sequencer.video_sections`, `render.set_sized_light_ltc`/`sized_light_ltc` and the forest-flight `terrain.*` methods,
  plus the game-widget `ui.*` methods listed under Added. The authoritative method table is
  `docs/api-snapshots/agent-protocol.txt`. (ADR-5993, ADR-2958)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `editor.set_property`/`set_transform` no longer require `entity` when `entities` is
  given (new reason codes). `scene.query_entities` and `scene.get_entity` results gain `kind`, `role` and
  `unclassified`. (ADR-3948, ADR-2488)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `Permission::Review` and `Panel::{AgentChanges, Preferences, WorldSettings}` are new
  enum variants, so exhaustive matches break. `gen.run`/`gen.verify` need `build` in `.regia/agent.toml`. (ADR-2346,
  ADR-2321, ADR-2696, ADR-2412)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Permission changes: `audio.loopgen_render`, `render.exposure_log`,
  `pacing.trace`, `ai.bt_live`, `voice_chat.stats` move to `play`; `profiler.set_capture`, `procgen.set_debug`,
  `project.describe` move to `edit_scene`. Grant the new class in tool scopes or `.regia/agent.toml`. (ADR-3285)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Response changes: `camera.rail_state`/`crane_state` return `"bound": false`
  for an unbound named driver; `input.assists`/`input.haptics` gain fields; `ui.view_models` reports `list` for `Vec`
  fields; `.regiatrace` exports read `forced`; empty `FrameStats` percentiles are absent; `regia mcp --http` closes
  silent connections after 10 s. (ADR-3231, ADR-3464, ADR-2489, ADR-3285)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Many new methods under `demo.*`, `camera.*`, `sequencer.*`, `audio.*`,
  `media.*`, `editor.video_*`, `editor.ui_designer_*`, `ui.*`, `loc.*`, `seeing.flash_check`, `assets.voxel_far`,
  `assets.font_subset`, `biome.validate_ecology`; the generated manual lists them.
- **Cross-cutting:** Changed codes and results: `procgen_bounds_missing`, `cell_corrupt`, `stream_settings_invalid`,
  `xr_no_loader`, `missing_instance_guid`. Results gain `reactive_mask`, the residency budgets, `specular` and save
  `copy`. `configurator.contract_check` refuses boolean schemas.
- **Cross-cutting:** Error code and permission changes: five editor methods need `edit_scene`, `editor.prefab_variant`
  `removed_children` is a count, thermal strings are lowercase, and `request_too_large` caps requests (ADR-3285)
- **Cross-cutting:** Value changes: `loop.set` bounds, `anim.montage_control` `montage_not_playing`, `chooser.set_cell`
  without `value` errors, `steam.workshop_unsubscribe` of an unknown item errors, and save, cloth and DCC-feed results
  gain fields
- **Cross-cutting:** Default changes: `EntryProfile::shed_count` is 10 and `PIECE_MAX_SHARE` is 0.6;
  `FuzzRng::chance(0)` is `false` (ADR-1517)
- **Cross-cutting:** Additive: `play.hitch_report` `work`, `quality.get_detected` `host`, `ai.perception_hysteresis`,
  validator code `dialogue_duplicate_id`; `render.graph` `shadows.reads` adds `shadow_occlusion#0`; `transients` counts
  may rise by one. (ADR-4947, ADR-5131, ADR-4586, ADR-5178, ADR-4945)
- **Cross-cutting:** Breaking: `render.set_upscaler` `before`/`after` carry the vendor frame and reactive-mask sources
  and vendor picks need `RuntimeRenderer` (`F0003`); `types.describe` schema shapes; oversized cloth ints report
  `cloth_bad_param`; stricter finite-`f32` params on biome, GI debug, overlap, weather, propagation, tiles;
  `nav.cook_triangle_areas` rejects invalid volumes; five editor methods move to `edit_scene`, `stream.open` failure
  reasons split, `loop.set` bounds, lowercase thermal tokens.
- **Release-candidate 2 fix round:** the routed editor document methods (UI designer and UI animation open/save,
  conversation open/save, mode2d map/rules/tile-map save, the importer inspectors, `svg_report`, `net.sim_clients`
  `recording`) answer `path_outside_project` (F0002, with `path_reason`) for a path outside the project and the temp
  dir (ADR-7388); `seeing.memory_report` has a `device_allocated_bytes` field; a character command stick axis of
  `-128` answers `character_input_range`; `stream.ready` with a very large radius answers `ready: false`; an idle
  editor answers at once (ADR-7393). `editor.keymap_import`, `editor.keymap_export` and `editor.trace_timeline` follow
  the same path rule, `editor.keymap_export` writes only a name ending in `keymap.ron` (`bad_path`), and
  `editor.keymap_import` reads at most 1 MiB; `instances_updated` from `editor.save_prefab` and
  `editor.set_variant_override` counts level-authored roots rebuilt (a root holding a base and its variant counts
  once); `editor.save_prefab` can fail after writing the file when the instances cannot spawn from it, and
  `editor.set_variant_override` can answer `instance_update_failed` (F0002) (ADR-9694).
- **Platforms:** `pacing.get` reports `drains`; `pacing.simulate` with `cost: work` reports late start, so numbers for
  overrunning runs change. (ADR-5564, ADR-4913)
- **Gameplay systems:** New methods `editor.cloth_panel_state`, `editor.cloth_panel_edit`, `editor.cloth_paint_stroke`
  and `editor.conversation_open|graph|edit|save|preview_step`; `physics.cloth_inspect` adds `scene_wind` and
  `global_wind`. `ai.bt_edit` `add_node`/`set_node` accept `abort`, `compare`, `value_kind`, `literal`, `literal_hi`,
  `rhs_key` (and `clear_literal`, `clear_literal_hi`), `ai.bt_get` returns them, and new issue codes include
  `type_mismatch`, `bad_compare`, `abort_not_allowed`, `abort_without_key` and `bad_state_tree`. (ADR-2884, ADR-3298,
  ADR-4543)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `types.describe` omits `skip_serializing` struct fields and describes glam values as
  their serde arrays (`Quat` 4 numbers, `Affine3A` 12, `Vec2`/`Vec4` arrays). Stricter params: `physics.field_set`
  `mask`/`priority` and `physics.field_probe` `layer` ranges, whole-number `audio.vehicle_audio_set` counts, f32-range
  `render.set_clouds` values and `terrain.set_grass` `clear.at`, finite `editor.set_property` values (`INVALID_PARAMS`,
  reason `value_mismatch`), `assets.pak_diff` `chunk_size` up to 64 MiB (`pak_diff_invalid_chunk_size`),
  `loc.check_fit` paths (`path_outside_project`), `xr.drive input` (`xr_invalid_value`), `viewer.view_simulate`
  (`view_bad_event`, `view_too_many_events`), `procgen.wfc_solve` limits (`wfc_bad_params`). (ADR-5597)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New codes and fields: `editor.placed_edit` `placed_level_not_open`,
  `editor.diagnostics`/`editor.trace_record` `bad_param`, `audio.impacts_set` `impacts_no_bank`,
  `render.get_visbuffer_residency` `stats.ram_evictions`, `editor.lightmap_status` `checkpoint` for level bakes. The
  `voice_chat.stats`, `net.messages` and `world.hlod_proxy` result descriptions now name every returned field, and
  contracts list codes their handlers already returned (`ui.scroll`, `ui.theme_mode`, `ui.a11y_tree`, `ui.nav_move`,
  `ui.nav_lint`, `ui.shortcut_trigger`, `abilities.grant` `grant_limit`); regenerate clients typed from the old
  descriptions. (ADR-5574)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `editor.step`, `editor.matgraph_edit` (`pos`) and `editor.import` (`sources`)
  validate stricter and answer `invalid_params`; `editor.budget` may report a higher `materials` value for models that
  mix own and overridden materials. (ADR-5413)
- **Rendering:** `render.memory_budget` admission reports `fits: false` with a reason when a side is unmeasured;
  `render.get_dynamic_gi` reports the corrected `rays_per_frame`.
- **Networking and Steam:** Ban host operations report a failed `bans.ron` save as `ban_store_save_failed` (it read
  `ban_invalid`) and still kick the peer.
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** the local transport's server answers each handshake with one frame
  (`{"authenticated":true}` or `{"authenticated":false}`) that a client reads before its first call (`Client` does).
  `editor.move_asset` answers `{ from, to, id_referencers }` and refuses absolute, drive, UNC, `..`, symlinked and
  goldens destinations; `editor.edit_asset` on the open level answers `asset_level_open`; match `editor.rename_asset`
  errors by `data.reason`. `agent.hot_reload` lists `hot_reload_no_report`. A placed level that fails to spawn answers
  `spawn_failed`. `render.graph` reports `shadow_occlusion` in `dispatch.inactive` on CPU-culled frames.
  `regia upgrade-project --json` `build_scripts` has a `server/build.rs` row for net-game projects. (ADR-5878,
  ADR-5876, ADR-5877, ADR-5879, ADR-5626, ADR-5617)
- **Gameplay systems:** `audio.impacts` pair entries gain `sound`, and `played` counts started voices only;
  `audio.impacts_set { seed }` without a bank answers `impacts_no_bank`; `physics.field_set` positions are world space
  for child entities, and out-of-range `layer`, `mask`, `priority`, empty curves and negative ranges answer
  `invalid_value`; `audio.set_emitter` with both `sound` and `cue` answers `INVALID_PARAMS`; `plant.export` refuses
  device names with an extension (`plant_bad_name`). (ADR-5899, ADR-5926)
- **Rendering:** `seeing.reference_trace`/`seeing.compare` `scene_info` gains `brdf_energy`, `material_clock`,
  `origin_offset` and `foliage_wind` (additive).
- **Rendering:** New `render.get_ssgi_gather` and `render.set_ssgi_gather` (`ssgi_gather_bad_step`).
  `render.set_lens_effects` takes `starburst` and `after_dof`, and `render.lens_effects` reports them with
  `starburst_active`; `vfx.decal_budget` gains `shading` (`forward_switch`, each decal's path, counts per path,
  `forward_max`, `forward_tiles`); `render.hwrt` reports `sun_shadows.frame.masked_casters`; with no composed panels
  `xr.capture_eye` reports `image_source: "eye_targets"` instead of `"display"`. (ADR-3964, ADR-3196, ADR-3255,
  ADR-4557)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New read-only methods `asset.gltf_extensions` (errors `gltf_ext_params`,
  `gltf_ext_unsupported`, `gltf_meshopt_invalid`, `gltf_quantization_invalid`, `gltf_instancing_invalid`,
  `gltf_unreadable`, `path_outside_project`), `editor.ply_inspect`, `editor.stl_inspect`, `editor.psd_inspect` and
  `editor.usd_inspect` (codes in `data.usd_error`). `editor.step` refuses `frames` outside `1..=10000` instead of
  clamping; `viewer.view_simulate` refuses more than 1,000,000 frames before running; `physics.field_set` answers reply
  reason `invalid_value` for an out-of-range `mask` or `priority`; `loc.check_fit` answers `path_outside_project`
  (`F0002`) for a `document` outside the allowed roots. (ADR-3016, ADR-3244, ADR-3235, ADR-5923)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `ui.set_value` on a `TextInput` can leave a read-only or filter-rejected field
  unchanged, and on a number widget can answer `ui_value_out_of_range`; `ui.drag_drop` can answer `ui_node_off_window`.
  `ui.world_markers` and `ui.target_bracket` refuse an unknown key or a wrong-typed value under every action, `query`
  included (`invalid_params`: `F0002` with `data.param`, or `bad_param` / `bad_color`). (ADR-5957)
- **Cross-cutting:** Wrong-typed parameters now answer `invalid_params` instead of being ignored: `ui.choice_state`
  `widget`, `ui.choice_pick` `input`, the anchor `world` of `captions.set_dialogue_style`, `net.replay_record_start`
  `level`, `net.replay_record_stop` `target`, `audio.set_emitter` `sound`/`cue`, `bird.*` `name`, `ambient_life`
  `position`, a take binding's `camera`, `stream.cell_relevance` `peer`, a plate's `time_of_day.day_of_year`, and
  `render.probe_specular_lobe` `direction` / `mode` without `cube`. `terrain.save_holes`, `terrain.save_paint`,
  `terrain.export_heights`, `terrain.save_scatter_paint` and `terrain.save_grass_density` are mutating: a read-only
  session is refused them and they count against the mutating rate limit. (ADR-2622, ADR-5960, ADR-5869)
- **Cross-cutting:** Result and parameter schema changes strict clients must accept: `audio.impacts` pairs report
  `sound`, `pacing.get` reports `drains`, `sequencer.rename_binding` reports `videos`; `assets.cooked_pack`,
  `procgen.anchors`, `replay.step`, `save.quick_save`, `ui.a11y_platform_tree`, `vfx.spawn_decal`,
  `audio.list_emitters`, `level.get_baked_gi_stream`, `memory.idle_ledger` and `memory.report` (`owners`,
  `largest_owner`), `render.get_probe_specular` and `render.probe_specular_lobe` changed their results; `executor.set`
  is mutating with tighter `executor` limits, and `world.pick` tightened `max_distance`. Type schemas publish every glam
  vector, quaternion, matrix and affine as a fixed-length array. (ADR-3335, ADR-5564, ADR-5924)
- **Rendering:** New methods `render.area_light_fallback`, `render.set_area_light_fallback`, `render.ambient_life`,
  `render.set_ambient_life`, `render.set_ambient_birds`. New reply fields: `render.get_capabilities`
  `material_anisotropy_lod_offset`; `memory.report` `owners`, `memory.idle_ledger` `owners`/`largest_owner`;
  `render.get_probe_specular` `lobe_hue`/`hue_floor`; `water.ocean_cost` `history_bytes`/`history_dispatches`;
  `water.state` foam and `queries_follow_tier` fields. `render.out_start`/`render.out_job` `aovs` take six more names;
  new codes `unknown_aov`, `aov_format_unsupported`, `area_light_fallback_bad_enabled`, `ambient_unknown_season`,
  `ambient_unknown_preset`. (ADR-3247, ADR-3024, ADR-5670, ADR-3351, ADR-2996, ADR-2843, ADR-3256)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `render.get_lightmaps` adds `level.cells`, `level.cell_stack`, `stale_receivers` and
  `receivers.stale_fallback`; `level.get_baked_gi` adds `memory`, `parts` and `host_layers`;
  `level.get_baked_gi_stream` tiles add `failed_parts`; codes `lightmap_cells_*` and `lightmap_receiver_stale`.
  (ADR-3318, ADR-3006, ADR-3237)
- **World:** New method `procgen.fingerprint` (tool `procgen_fingerprint`); `terrain.set_scatter` takes `class_caster`
  and `class_season`, and `terrain.scatter` reports them. (ADR-4593, ADR-3146)
- **Gameplay systems:** New method `save.inspect { slot }`. `kinds.list` may return more kinds and notes;
  `project.describe` lists a running `PreUpdate` under `map.unread_schedules`. (ADR-3239, ADR-5609)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New methods `editor.pie_processes` and `editor.loc_table`, `loc_set`, `loc_gather`,
  `loc_usages`, `loc_pseudo`; `editor.pie_net_config` takes `separate_processes`/`windowed`, `editor.pie_net_run` and
  `editor.play` take `separate_processes`, and `editor.pie_net_clients` can give the reason `separate_processes`.
  `editor.seq_section_edit` gains ops `binding_kind`, `video_target`, `video_loop` and `time_scale_mode`, and op
  `spatial` takes `doppler`/`occlusion`. New codes `pie_process_*`, `loc_panel_*`, `take_*`. (ADR-2682, ADR-3260,
  ADR-2948, ADR-2862, ADR-3197, ADR-2637)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** A `configurator.*` `package_dir` longer than the platform's path limit answers
  `F0003` on every host when a folder on the way is absent (macOS answered `F0002`). `platform.power_state` with a
  `dir` outside the allowed roots is `F0002`, and `terrain.save_holes`, `save_paint`, `export_heights`,
  `save_scatter_paint` and `save_grass_density` are `mutating` in `agent.schema`. (ADR-5550, ADR-5869)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `sequence.player` gains action `skip`, setting `restore_on_stop` and state
  `prerolling`, `markers_skipped`, `spawnables`; `sequencer.spatial_voices` takes `all` and reports Doppler, occlusion
  and stretch fields; `sequencer.rename_binding` reports `videos`. (ADR-2948, ADR-3197, ADR-2862)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New methods: `editor.edit_asset`, `editor.reimport`, `editor.move_asset`,
  `editor.delete_asset`, `editor.set_import_settings`, `editor.rename_asset`, `editor.lookdev_trace|status|cancel`,
  `editor.ui_anim_*`, `editor.static_move_warnings`, `scene.spawn_prefab`, `level.open`, `level.unload`, `rules.get`,
  `timers.list`, `tweens.list`, `stream.cell_relevance`, `assets.terrain_tiles`, `assets.texture_coverage`,
  `assets.texpack_dry_run`, `render.sun_specular_disc`, `render.set_sun_specular_disc`. `agent.build_game` and
  `agent.hot_reload` add the `F0002` reasons `build_game_bad_name`, `build_game_busy`, `build_game_no_manifest`,
  `build_game_spawn_failed`, `hot_reload_busy`, `hot_reload_no_game` and the `hot` job kind. (ADR-5706, ADR-5700,
  ADR-5896, ADR-3092, ADR-5704, ADR-4011, ADR-5294, ADR-3090, ADR-3190, ADR-5707)
- **Cross-cutting:** Additive params and result fields: `ui.click` `modifiers`; `procgen.tiles` `warnings`;
  `ui.a11y_tree` `sync_cost`; six `stream.stats` keys; `water.caustics_stats.passes` shaft fields,
  `water.state.last_frame.ocean_refused`, `water.ocean_cost` `refused`/`error`; `assets.cooked_pack` per-pak `kinds`
  and submesh counts; `replay.step` `clamped`, `events_total`, `events_truncated`; `save.quick_save` `copy`;
  `seeing.compare` `width`, `height`, `aov`, `mime` and a `mode` param; optional `context` on `camera.add_additive`,
  `camera.push_overlay`, `camera.release_layer`; `terrain.stroke` `from_height`, `to_height`, `seed`, `wavelength`;
  codes `lagcomp_dev_only`, `net_dev_only` (shipping builds), `pak_diff_report`, `restart_required` on `plugin.reload`.
  (ADR-5665, ADR-5646, ADR-5379, ADR-3265, ADR-3251, ADR-5517, ADR-5628, ADR-5783)
- **Cross-cutting:** Behavior: read-only profiles get `F0012` from `editor.procgen_pin` and the editing forms of
  `editor.procgen_starters`, `editor.procgen_reload` and `editor.procgen_capture` (grant `edit_scene`);
  `voice_chat.mute` with a `{ sender }` target and `block: true` is invalid params (block an `{ account }`);
  `level.place_level` honors `transform.rotation`; `level.layers { layer }` without `ScenePlugin` is `F0002`
  `layer_unknown`; an MCP client attached to an editor sees more tools; `configurator.open_product` needs an array for
  inline `documents.kits`; `plates`, `procgen.wfc_solve`, `render.save_local_captures`, `render.permutations`,
