  `ai.statetree_validate` and `ai.statetree_edit` refuse a wrong-typed optional parameter; `render.precipitation`
  `drawn.splashes` is 0 without an occlusion view (read `wanted.splashes` for the asked count). (ADR-5646, ADR-5642,
  ADR-5779, ADR-5628, ADR-5802, ADR-2622, ADR-5823)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `render.get_dynamic_gi` tier rows gain `front_weights` (true on every tier) and
  `edge_visibility` (true on `low` and `medium`); `high`/`ultra` report `probes: 19200`, `reach_m` 548.7,
  `seed_updates` 4, `visibility_blend` true and `recent_weight` 3; `finest_above_m`/`finest_below_m` read 4/3 m on
  `low` and `medium` and 4/3.5 m on `high` and `ultra`. (ADR-5001, ADR-5290, ADR-5566, ADR-5716, ADR-5909)
- **Rendering:** additive keys `play.hitch_report` `gpu_passes.nodes` and `gpu_passes.stamps_outside_nodes`,
  `render.foliage_wind` `last_frame.skinned_casters`, `render.draw_integrity` and `render.set_skinned_bounds`
  `skinned.refused`, `terrain.get` `anisotropy_lod_offset`; `render.get_occlusion` `unbounded` no longer counts swaying
  casters whose material declares its offset. (ADR-5386, ADR-5418, ADR-5459, ADR-5389)
- **Platforms:** `pacing.set` takes `wake_ahead_ms` and `perf_hint`; `pacing.get` reports `wake_ahead_ms`,
  `wake_ahead_polled`, `wake_ahead_predicted` and `perf_hint`; `pacing.trace` rows gain `tick_delivered_ms` and
  `woke_ms`. (ADR-5461)
- **UI and tools:** `ui.backdrop_stats` replies gain four keys (ADR-5420); `editor.viewport_stats`
  `pane.textures.last_size` reports physical pixels on a HiDPI window.
- **World and gameplay:** new methods `vfx.significance`, `vfx.significance_set` (ADR-3275), `physics.track_step` and
  `physics.track_state` (error `track_bad_input`, ADR-4629); `vfx.spawn` refused by a per-system cap answers `F0002`
  `refused_by_cap` instead of `F0003`.
- **World:** `vfx.crash` takes `gust` and `gust_sites` and returns `wall_reach`, `wall_rings`, `gust`, `gust_sites`,
  `camera_distance`, `camera_arrival_in` and `camera_wind`; `vfx.crashes` adds `gust_puffs`, `gust_sites` and
  `camera_wind`; `vfx.crash_profile` adds `wall_reach`, `wall_lag`, `wall_leads_front` and `wall_height`; new
  `vfx.blast_gust`. (ADR-1497)
- **Platforms:** `pacing.set` takes `release`, `grid_follow`, `start_offset_ms` and `drain_run`; `pacing.get` adds
  `steady_start` and `grid_moves`; `pacing.trace` rows add `release`, `sleep_late_ms`, `work_run_delay_ms`,
  `wait_run_delay_ms`, `preemptions` and `wake_cpu`, and the answer `origin_ns`. New read method `steam.layout`.
  (ADR-5946, ADR-1332)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** New `ui.choice_state`, `ui.choice_pick`, `captions.anchor_state` and
  `captions.set_dialogue_style`. (ADR-3234)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New editor-host methods `editor.crash_reports` and `editor.crash_report_read`
  (`report_not_found`, `invalid_report_path`, `crash_reports_unavailable`) (ADR-3931). New error code
  `configurator_bad_options`; `viewer.render_still` answers `viewer_bad_value` for a `contact_shadow` outside 0..1;
  `viewer.view_simulate` answers `view_too_many_events` past 1,000,000 frames; `assets.pak_diff` lists
  `pak_diff_report`; `dcc_feed.bake` documents `invalid_value`; `profiler.capture` can answer `path_outside_project` or
  `golden_path_denied` (`F0002`, with `data.path_reason`); wrong-typed navigation `id`, `user_id` or `enabled` is
  `F0002` with `data.param`/`data.expected`; `agent.schema` exports union kinds as type arrays and `any` with no `type`;
  numbers that overflow finite `f32` and stamp extents that overflow signed 64-bit get the invalid-parameter errors;
  hosts that attach `TextSystem::shape` results by hand run `shape_text` before `ui.text_shape`. (ADR-5895, ADR-5600)
- **Rendering:** `render.get_dynamic_gi` and the `render.set_dynamic_gi` states report `normal_bias` and the reference
  fixture's `thin_wall_leak_fraction` (0.0); `render.get_clouds` reports `shadow_map.air { shadowed, steps, haze_steps,
  overcast }`. (ADR-3947, ADR-5944)
- **Gameplay systems:** `physics.bike_step` takes a `front_ray` or `rear_ray` past `f32` range as a miss instead of
  `bike_bad_input`.

### CLI, features and dependencies

- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New commands: `regia mcp --http|--headless`, `regia golden`, `regia editor`, `regia
  gen`, `regia describe`, `regia preflight`, `regia export glb`, `regia assets import-rules check`, `regia assets
  pak-diff`, `regia cook cache stats|prune`, and the shared-cache flags.
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** Behavior changes: unknown `regia doctor --platform` exits 2, bad `regia preview` paths
  exit 1, and a bad `[cook.shared_cache]` or `[kinds] strictness` fails the cook. Images or `.bin` files referenced by a
  `.glb` no longer cook standalone, and `DdgiSettings::update_divisor` caps at 8192. (ADR-2051, ADR-4390, ADR-2612,
  ADR-2488, ADR-2394, ADR-2385, ADR-2395)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** New commands: `regia media encode`, `regia mcp token`, `regia assets
  font-subset`, `regia loc status|check-fit|cues`, `regia loc check --glyphs`, `regia bench --small`, and xtask
  `demo-package`, `demo-notices`, `sbom`, `duplicate-deps`, `flash-check`, `check-ecology`, `showcase-table`,
  `api-snapshot --rotate`. Building `showcase` without default features gives the agent-free demo build. (ADR-2827,
  ADR-3111, ADR-2760, ADR-3301, ADR-3804, ADR-2456, ADR-2444, ADR-2527)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** New dependencies: `vorbis_rs` 0.5.6 and `resvg`/`usvg` 0.45.1 (pipeline only),
  `icu_segmenter` 2.3.0 (~420 KiB per release binary), `rav1e`/`unsafe-libopus` behind `media-encode`. Packagers
  regenerate and ship the updated third-party notices. (ADR-3230, ADR-3350, ADR-3323, ADR-3369)
- **Cross-cutting:** rmcp 3.5 → 3.9 (adds `sha2` 0.11.1). `libm` 0.2.16 becomes a direct render dependency, `symphonia`
  0.6.1 a workspace dependency, and `windows-sys` gains two features. New features: `regia_agent/media-encode` (WebM
  render-out) and `xess`.
- **Cross-cutting:** New xtask commands/flags listed under Added; Steam owner sessions pass `--ignored` and set
  `REGIA_STEAM_RUN_COMMIT`; Windows hosts need `icacls` and `USERNAME` for literal signing secrets and `regia assets
  keygen`; custom runners presenting a `BootSplash` must start the warm queue; dev-deps `tracing-subscriber`
  (`regia_net`) and `regia_nav` (`stream_world`). (ADR-4896, ADR-5126, ADR-5131)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia loc cues export --json` reports `unresolved_keys`; `regia loc cues import
  --json` no longer replaces a different track (pass `--out` or remove it first); a project's `config/import_rules.ron`
  rules using `**/` should be rechecked with `regia assets import-rules check`. `cargo xtask book-check` and `cargo
  xtask agent-coverage` fail on broken heading anchors and on method parameters missing from the book. (ADR-5581,
  ADR-5586, ADR-5866)
- **Platforms:** `regia doctor` without `--platform` treats Android problems as warnings; `regia new --template` accepts
  hyphen spellings; `regia package` writes `media_index.ron`.
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `regia upgrade-project --write` plans `server/build.rs` for net-game projects (status
  `add`; it appends `[build-dependencies]` to `server/Cargo.toml` with a `.bak`); `regia cook --stats --platform
  android` prints a new first line; `regia test --level` folds `.` and `..`; `cargo xtask sbom --profile game` lists
  only the shipped feature set (pass `--features` or `--all-features` for more). (ADR-5617, ADR-5799, ADR-5903)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New `regia import <file.usda|.usdc|.usd|.usdz> [--out GLB] [--scale F] [--json]` and
  `cargo xtask walkthrough`. New pipeline-only dependency `openusd` =0.8.0 (with `logos`, `zip` 8 and a second `strum`;
  `check-layers` refuses it in a runtime crate, and `deny.toml` ignores four `zip` 8 advisories in archive-extract APIs
  that openusd never calls until it moves to `zip` 9). The `image` crate gains its `webp` feature (one new crate,
  `image-webp`). `regia_render` adds `RenderDevice::wait_after_submit_on_this_thread` behind `test-hooks` only.
  (ADR-3235, ADR-3016, ADR-5718)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `regia bake-lightmaps --cells`, `--cell-ring N`, `--cells-force`. (ADR-3318)
- **Rendering:** New environment variable `REGIA_MATERIAL_ANISO_LOD_OFFSET`. (ADR-5670)
- **Platforms:** `--play-input PATH`, `REGIA_PLAY_INPUT`, `REGIA_SMOKE_SECONDS`, `REGIA_JSON_LOG`; `regia clean --json`;
  xtask `package-smoke`, `plate-floors attribute` and `release-notes`. (ADR-3968, ADR-5627, ADR-5798, ADR-2767)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New `regia_editor` binary `regia-pie-fixture` (a test fixture: packaging scripts that
  copy every editor binary should skip it); `windows-sys` gains `Win32_System_JobObjects`. `regia_agent` and
  `regia_editor` build `regia_framework` with `media` (`regia_media` is new for `regia_editor`); to play video sections
  build with `regia/media` (and `media-codecs` for AV1, Opus and Vorbis). (ADR-2682, ADR-2862)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** New commands and flags: `regia export usda`, `regia procgen bake --prune`,
  `regia new --template empty`, the dedicated server's `--upgrade-config`, `cargo xtask stream-bench --soak`.
  `regia procgen verify` `extra:` lines end with `(orphan_bake; ...)`. (ADR-4604, ADR-5644, ADR-5643, ADR-5650,
  ADR-3265)
- **Tools, editor and pipeline:** `cargo xtask check-layers` fails a runtime crate or sample that declares `regia_pt` or
  turns on `regia_agent/reference-trace` or `regia_agent/denoise`: move that to a desktop tool crate. `regia_editor`
  depends on `regia_pt` (desktop only), and `regia_agent` uses the workspace `windows-sys` on Windows
  (`Win32_System_Pipes`). The `net-game` template's `game/Cargo.toml` turns on `regia/ui`. (ADR-5630, ADR-5896,
  ADR-5708, ADR-2958)
- **Lightmaps:** `[lightmaps.tiers.<name>] padding` (1..=16) in `Regia.toml`; a project that replaces
  `[lightmaps.tiers.ultra_low]` gets padding 1 unless it sets `padding = 2`. `regia cook --lightmap-tiers --json`
  reports `derive.repacked_charts` and `derive.chart_padding`. (ADR-5125)
- **Platforms:** `REGIA_TERRAIN_ANISO_LOD_OFFSET=<mips>` (ADR-5459); the `lightmap_device` sample's shipping profiles
  need `LIGHTMAP_DEVICE_KEYSTORE` and its password and alias variables (ADR-5382). xtask (repository tool):
  `dist --notices FILE`, `--no-default-features`, `--features LIST`; `demo-package` writes `licenses/EULA.md`,
  `licenses/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` and `WINDOWS-CHECK.md`; `android-pass` prints a `forwards:` line; `windows-pass`
  `summary.json` test entries gain `skip_reason` (schema stays 1). (ADR-5419, ADR-5578, ADR-5423, ADR-5806)
- **Regia Forest Demo:** `showcase::demo::agent` and `showcase::demo::quality::publish_detection` exist only with the
  `agent` feature (the default). Re-run `cargo xtask demo-package` to carry the fixed binary, README, `DECK-CHECK.md`
  and `WINDOWS-CHECK.md` into the next candidate. (ADR-5419, ADR-5452, ADR-5578)
- **Platforms:** `regia steam layout [--project] [--family] [--context] [--glyphs DIR] [--allow-keyboard-only ACTION]...
  [--check]` (`--check` fails on an error); `regia package --steam` warns about actions a controller cannot reach;
  `vfx_lab --deck-check --preset NAME [--panel lcd|oled]`, `--deck-presets` and `--deck-validate DIR`; `deck_check.json`
  records `preset`, `panel`, `panel_detected` and `knobs`. (ADR-1332)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** `cargo xtask doc-examples [--update] [--lower "<why>"]` (`docs/ci/doc-examples.toml`,
  `format_version = 1`, a preflight step) and `cargo xtask docs-links` (`docs/ci/docs-links.toml`).
- **Cross-cutting:** `regia_framework` now depends on `regia_terrain`. (ADR-1497)

## Platform evidence

Only runs that the release fragments or the evidence ledger cite are listed here. A platform or device that is not
named was not run for that feature. Linux runs use lavapipe (software Vulkan) unless a card is named.

Candidates named below: rc1 `c512a612d`, rc2 `b890ff25e` (final head `7c61f9da9`), rc4 `b98b6e9f4`, rc5
`9d6aee15c`, and rc6, the code of this release: rc5 plus the TSR half-scale, vendor-upscaler and Android asset-pack
finishing work (ADR-7694, ADR-7695, ADR-7696). Earlier rows name their commit or date. Ledger rows are those of
`docs/release/v0.5.0-evidence-ledger.md` (2026-11-14 snapshot); perf-log rounds are in `docs/perf-log.md`.

- **Merge gate on rc5 (2026-11-14).** Every test passes: lavapipe 33600 passed / 0 failed, RTX 5090 12068 / 0,
  doctests 247 / 0, and the documentation step passes. Tests marked `#[ignore]` (card measurements, goldens not yet
  blessed, `--ignored` end-to-end runs) did not run in that gate (ledger snapshot header).
- **Android, Pixel 9 Pro (Tensor G4, Mali-G715, Android 17, 60 Hz).** The only Android device in the test farm since
  2026-11-06. `cargo xtask android-pass` on the arena sample's shipping library (50 lifecycle cycles, 10 rotations, a
  10-minute soak, 3 cold and 3 warm starts, then the crash-record check):
  - rc4: 3 of 3 PASS, `presented_p99` 1.011 / 1.009 / 1.008, 9 of 9 budget rows in each run, Auto pacing 8 of 8
    windows within 1.05 x. rc4 to rc5 changes no Android code path (ledger A13).
  - rc2: 3 of 3 PASS on `b890ff25e` with 27 of 27 budget rows, and 3 of 3 PASS on `7c61f9da9` with 26 of 27: one
    Mobile High window read `presented_p99` 1.054 x (limit 1.05) while the frame work stayed under the refresh. An
    interleaved A/B of the two heads (12 windows each, medians 1.0096 x and 1.0097 x, none over 1.05) and the next run
    (1.008 x) show it as a one-off. PSS 625.7-659.1 MB (budget 700), lifecycle PSS growth -0.69 to +2.27 % (limit
    5 %), soak thermal status 0, crash record PASS (perf-log, Android pass of 2026-11-13).
  - rc1: 3 of 3 PASS, 27 of 27 budget rows, `presented_p99` 1.010 in each run, PSS 658 MB (perf-log, Android pass of
    2026-11-12).
  - The first start after install still compiles every pipeline: 4.3 to 5.9 s to the first engine frame, over 5 s
    in 5 of 6 rc2 runs. Later cold starts load the pipeline cache saved after the first frame (ADR-5857) in 2.0-2.2 s,
    so the budgeted median of three starts passes.
  - Paced frames start on the vsync grid: five full passes read `presented_p99` 1.007-1.009 (perf-log, A13 vsync-grid
    round of 2026-11-11, ADR-5946). Smaller GPU allocator blocks bring the arena's Mobile High PSS from 717-722 MB to
    598-631 MB (ADR-5423). The lifecycle drill's one-time PSS step (ADR-3372) still fails it now and then: one of five
    rounds in the vsync-grid work (+7.17 %) and one of three after ADR-5423 (+6.21 %); none of the nine rc1 and rc2
    runs.
  - The same phone runs the four Android templates with wait/expect/screenshot and autosave (ledger B04), the lightmap
    profile switch (ledger L05), tilemap, autotile and terrain paks (ledger C05, B16), sequence camera and audio data
    (ledger B07, C03), the retargeting `cross_arch` pins (ledger B15) and TalkBack sessions with 0 audit findings over
    122 nodes, including a live density change (ledger C12).
  - Asset packs through the store's local testing (bundletool) on this phone: install-time chunks at the first frame,
    fast-follow delivered with no request, on-demand fetch, cancel at half way, fetch after cancel, force-stop and Home
    mid-download, release and refetch; the shipping build passes the owner check's logcat script 7 of 7 (ledger N13).
  - Limits: no low-end, Adreno or other Mali device (the Pixel 6 class, Mali-G78 reference phone) ran any v0.5 row
    (ledger A13, C12). The arena's landscape swapchain is rotated by the compositor (no pre-rotation), so every frame
    is composited on the GPU (ADR-5946). The 2.5 ms wake-ahead and the `start_offset_ms` findings come from this phone
    alone (ADR-5461, ADR-5946).
- **Android, earlier devices (v0.2.15 candidate, perf-log Android pass of 2026-11-06, ADR-3210).** Pixel 6 (Mali-G78),

## Known issues

Source: the v0.5.0 evidence ledger (`docs/release/v0.5.0-evidence-ledger.md`, snapshot 2026-11-14, rc5 candidate
`9d6aee15c`, with the rc7 update on the release commit `59ff4b61d` for the RTX 3080 Ti DLSS and XeSS rows (ADR-7718,
ADR-7760) and the post-tag update of 2026-11-15 (ADR-7816), ADR-4935; ledger row IDs in brackets), the fragments' own
Known issues, and, for the last subsection, the v0.5.1 fix fragments. A row marked *owner* waits for an owner decision
or session, not for code.

### Lightmaps and GI

- `regia bake-lightmaps` does not read an authored `TEXCOORD_1` (UV2): an authored layout is Rust-API only, and the CLI
  always unwraps (spec 11 § 11). Receiver specular is a follow-on. The native Windows lightmap set ran on the Intel N150
  laptop (35/35 on DX12 without DXC and 35/35 on Vulkan); no NVIDIA or AMD Windows DX12 lightmap run is recorded. [L01,
  L04]
- The owner's look verdict on the lightmap gallery and a production-scene bake budget are open (*owner*); the only timed
  bake is the density-16 gallery (70.956 s). [L07]
- Dynamic GI on the Low (and Medium) tier keeps the 2 m probe layout for v0.5.0 (owner decision 2026-11-11, ADR-5993):
  Desktop High meets every acceptance bar in all five scenes, but Low misses the 0.08 mean-FLIP bar in the color room
  (0.158), the door room (0.136) and the shaft (0.102); the trench and the tunnel pass. The cause is the 2 m lattice
  (ADR-5938); the denser candidate costs 45.7 MB more probe memory and 62 ms probe-update frames on the Steam Deck, so
  it moves to v0.5.x. The owner's GI look round is open (*owner*). The radiance-cache golden has never run:
  `radiance_cache_golden` is ignored in the rc5 gate on lavapipe and the RTX 5090 because
  `goldens/radiance_cache_on.png` does not exist (lead bless). [A01, A02]

### Rendering

- FSR 3.1 at 0.67 render scale ghosts at 0.029 against spec 27's 0.02 target (`temporal_chart`), and misses the
  thin-geometry, disocclusion-smear and stability targets; at 0.5 it misses all five quality targets. The target does
  not move (ADR-5993). [A05]
- Half-scale TSR (0.5) meets every temporal chart target with one bar of margin (thin orbit 0.905 against 0.90, 57 of 63
  lattice bars; the mean over run lengths 104-136 is 0.879), and the TSR pass costs more than before: +0.05 ms at
  1920x1080 and +0.17 ms at 3840x2160 output on an RTX 5090 (TSR 0.5: 0.210 and 0.751 ms). TSR stays optional and no
  preset uses it; Desktop High stays native with TAAU, and promoting 0.5 to a preset is a later decision. On lavapipe
  and an RTX 5090 that is the result above. On Metal (rc6 `ed43351dc`) `tsr_050_orbit_thin_section_2_3` reads 0.889 (56
  of 63 bars) and fails. The new resolve has not run on Windows or a phone (ADR-4934, ADR-7694). [A05]
- The render graph is the minimal slice that the owner approved on 11-09. `render_into` records every built-in pass in
  the graph's node order, and two frame-local targets come from the view's transient pool. The step products stay locals
  of `render_into`, not fields of the frame context. Three items move to v0.5.x: the persistent HDR scratch images, the
  renderer buffers whose ownership is not audited (lights, clusters, draw lists, ReSTIR reservoirs), and transient
  aliasing (ADR-4945). Per-pass timestamps in the hitch report name their graph node. [M02, M03, M06]
- The dense visibility-buffer path stays off in every preset (22 % faster on an RTX 5090 and 28 % on an RTX 5080 at 4M
  triangles); its switch thresholds come from those two NVIDIA cards, and an AMD bench is still owed (ADR-3149). [A04]
- With the Khronos validation layer on, the two hardware ray-query modules (`hwrt_shadow_ray_query.wgsl`,
  `sun_trace_ray_query.wgsl`), the path tracer (`pt.wgsl`) and the lightmap baker (`lightmap_bake.wgsl`) report
  `VUID-StandaloneSpirv-None-10684`: naga 30 creates the variable inside its own ray-query helper, and the BVH stacks
  are Function arrays (ADR-7587, v0.5.1 line; ADR pending in v0.5.0). The baked GI and product viewer reports are
  listed under Fixed after v0.5.0. [M04]
- DLSS has no DX12 path: in a DLSS build with the runtime present the engine opens Vulkan only on Windows (DX12 adapters
  cannot be selected); with DLSS off, DX12 uses the built-in upscalers. XeSS passes every row on the Intel N150 (206 of
  206 smoke runs with execute pacing). On the RTX 3080 Ti the contract, lib, renderer and conformance rows pass. The
  hardware smoke fails its zeroed-heap assertion: this card has no host-visible device-local memory type usable by
  optimal-tiled images (only the 214 MiB BAR heap, plus buffers and linear images), so XeSS uses its own temporary
  allocations (ADR-7718). A diagnostic build that printed that assertion instead of failing it passed 200 of 200 runs
  with exact repeats and the canonical values. XeSS on AMD Windows is not run, and `regia package` has no XeSS staging.
  DLSS and XeSS stay opt-in and off by default. Shipping the vendor SDKs (notices, attribution, and the three XeSS
  provenance rows) is an owner decision (ADR-7695). [A06, A07]
- Hardware ray-traced sun shadows on Ultra: the GTX 1080 and the Intel N150 refuse ray queries as designed and take the
  fallbacks. ReSTIR GI is not built for Windows, and its GTX 1080 fallback is not run (that card was offline in every
  recent sweep). Spatiotemporal blue noise on the shipped code is measured on lavapipe, three RTX 5090s, an RX 7900 XTX and
  the Steam Deck; the RTX 5080, the Intel N150 and the GTX 1080 were not re-measured on it (ADR-5457, ADR-5960). [A08,
  A11]
- Layered thin film, sheen and rough transmission, post-process volumes and LUTs, lens flare and dirt, and the water
  tiers wait for owner look verdicts (*owner*); lens flare and dirt stay opt-in until then. None of these ran natively
  on Windows beyond clippy, and several plates are lavapipe only. Strand hair is not started (v0.5.x). [A14, B12, C04,
  C07, C08, C09, C11]
- The reference path tracer's cloud layer is regression-checked only; the ADR-1834 tau 2-10 gap remains, and `regia_pt`
  was not run natively on Windows. [A12]
- The UI backdrop blur's pooled targets return to baseline across 50 open/close cycles (ADR-5420), but the pool is held
  until the renderer is dropped or the frame size changes: a pause menu closed long ago still holds 1.1 to 3 times one
  frame. There is no idle or memory-pressure trim. [C10]
- On an Intel Arc Pro B65 (not a gate card), after a material changes its sampler with the temporal resolve on, 1824
  values of the resolved frame are one 8-bit level off (none with the resolve off); the same one-level difference shows
  on Metal. No tolerance was added (ADR-7483); whether the resolve should converge exactly after a material change is
  open (ADR-3954).

### Gameplay

- On the default build, `nav_world_lab`'s physics sections (ragdoll, traffic) differ between operating systems, and
  `crowd_lab`'s crowd digest differs between x86_64 and aarch64 (glam's SSE2 and NEON paths, ADR-5176). With the
  `cross-platform-determinism` feature every sample trace is byte-identical on Linux, Windows, Android and macOS
  (ADR-5128). Even then, parry3d's BVH optimizer (`log2`) and rapier's joint limit helper (`sin_cos`) call the platform
  libm, and `aero`, `vehicle_bike`, `vehicle_trailer` readouts, `pd` and `vehicle_feedback` still call it with no sample
  exercising them. [B01, B02, B05, B06]
- Crowd retargeting measured 0.203/0.694 ms against 2/6 ms on the MacBook Air M5 (500 agents, 60 joints); the timing
  test was not run on an otherwise idle machine, `cross_arch` is not run on Windows or macOS, and the owner's artist
  look on `crowd_lab` and the CesiumMan retarget (foot slide 0.0022-0.0023 m/s) is open (*owner*). [B15]
- Sequence audio has no audible-playback check, and the sequencer's render-out has no card run. [B07, C03]

### Networking and Steam

- The owner's two-account Steam session passed on 2026-11-12: lobby, sockets, rejoin, content check, the cloud-save
  round trip and the `net_game` leg (`docs/evidence/steam/2026-11-12/`). Open: the owner's hand retest of the Steam
  overlay after the hold-to-show fix (ADR-7349) on Vulkan and DX12 (*owner*); the real-Steam `net_game` leg ran with the
  network conditioner off, so a conditioned Steam leg is proven on the mock only; the session ran on an earlier commit,
  and the release gate builds `regia_steam` without `--features steamworks`; native Windows clippy with `--features
  steamworks` is not run. [B03, N05, N08]
- The v0.5 network scope is client, listen and dedicated servers over raw UDP and Steam sockets, lobbies and the content
  handshake (owner answer Q12). The packaged dedicated-server run (`cargo test -p regia_cli --test server_package_e2e --
  --ignored`) passed 4 of 4 on an earlier candidate and was not rerun on the release commit. [B19, N07]
- On the Intel N150 Windows laptop the windowed debug client makes 55 of 107 corrections against a bar of 20 or fewer,
  and the Windows windowed client fails the real UDP match there; whether that is a v0.5.0 bar or a release-client one
  is an owner decision (*owner*). [N02, N04]

### Tools and pipeline

- Licensee distribution: the licensee EULA (*owner*), a clean-machine proof and native Windows clippy of `regia_cli`
  are open. [B18]
- Android asset packs pass the store's local testing on the phone, but store delivery itself is unverified until the
  owner's Play internal-testing check (`docs/release/play-internal-testing-asset-packs.md`; *owner*): the store's own
  fast-follow download after install, network errors and the Wi-Fi consent dialog. A request does not survive the
  process (after a kill mid-download a chunk reads `NotInstalled` until the game requests it again), and the cook does
  not check the store's pack size limits. [N13, B18]
- The First Person, Third Person and Top Down templates add no sun, so their levels are lit by the sky alone: the
  ground looks flat and blue with no shadows (most visible in Top Down, where the view is all ground). Add a
  `DirectionalLight` in `level::setup_level` (the Net Game template shows one); a fix is planned for v0.5.1. [B04]
- A verbatim local path (`\\?\C:\...`, what `canonicalize` returns on Windows) counts as a remote form outside the
  project root (ADR-3206, ADR-7566): list agent `output_roots` and `replay_roots` as `C:\...`.

### Platforms

- Android: the Arena sample holds 60 fps on the Pixel 9 Pro. With the vsync-grid frame release (ADR-5461, ADR-5946) five
  full passes on 2026-11-11 read presented p99 1.007, 1.007, 1.009, 1.007 and 1.009 against 1.05; the presented-p99 row
  passed in each, and one of the five failed its lifecycle PSS step at +7.17 % (ADR-3372). The release-candidate passes
  read 1.007-1.011 (rc4 1.011 / 1.009 / 1.008, rc5 1.011 / 1.009 / 1.007, rc6 1.010 / 1.010 / 1.008), except one rc2
  Mobile High window at 1.054 that the next run (1.008) did not repeat. The first start after install compiles every
  pipeline (4.3 to 5.9 s to the first frame); later cold starts load the saved pipeline cache (about 2.0 to 2.2 s,
  ADR-5857). No reference-class (Pixel 6), low-end, Adreno or Mali device was available for any Android row. [A13]
- Accessibility: TalkBack reads each control, a live Android display-size change applies (ADR-5379), and the
  screen-reader sync costs a mean of 0.27-0.58 ms per frame in a release build. Open: the owner's screen-reader
  checklist (TalkBack gestures, Orca, Narrator/NVDA, VoiceOver) (*owner*); the p99 headroom is thin and no low-end phone
  was tested. [C12]
- Windows: native Vulkan and DX12 presentation, quits and the feedback suite pass on the Intel N150 laptop, and on an
  RTX 3080 Ti `cargo xtask windows-pass` passed on rc1 and again on rc6, whose code is this release's, including the
  native crash path (an access violation writes a bundle and a minidump). Not run: AMD on Windows and the native
  crash path on the N150. Every engine executable (regia.exe, the editor, the samples, the tools) and every
  template project asks Windows for an 8 MiB main-thread stack instead of the 1 MiB default (ADR-5181, ADR-5617). The
  release editor's first frame can take 6-9 s on the RTX 3080 Ti for a new executable (about 46 pipelines cold in the
  driver's cache; ADR-7393). [X03]
- Performance: an RTX 5080 table at 2560x1440 (Ultra p50 8.9-10.5 ms, p99 11.9-21.7 ms) and a Steam Deck row (p50
  16.3-24.2 ms) are recorded, and an earlier GTX 1080 round put the build 1.6 % slower than v0.2.17 at High 1440p p50.
  Not done: an RTX 5090 table on an idle machine and an N150 showcase-table row. The rc6 Linux GPU sweep (the code of
  this release) ran the player row on an RTX 5090, an Intel Arc Pro B65 and an RX 7900 XTX; its RTX 5080 row did not
  run (host down), the GTX 1080 did not answer, and that round is not in this commit's perf-log. [X02]
- macOS is testing only, with no signing, notarization or Apple shipping target. Editor UI captures and windowed frame
  times need an unlocked console (ADR-7335), and the 13-shot editor capture is not repeated after the latest fixes.
  The rc6 Metal workspace run (macOS 26.6) fails two visibility-buffer close tests, `close::residency_pressure` and
  `close::without_the_texture_table`: the test rock is cooked at test time and the cook differs by architecture, and
  Metal passes all five close tests when given the x86_64 cook. Desktop High and Ultra read p50 33.38 ms on battery in
  that pass; a wall-power rerun is open. [X04]
- XR covers the no-loader contract and the simulator only: OpenXR is not built, `--xr openxr|auto` runs on the desktop,
  and real headsets are not supported (*owner* scope). [X05]

### Regia Forest Demo

- The demo archives verified before the tag (Linux tar and Windows zip, panic=abort, glibc 2.30) were built from an
  earlier commit and predate the windowed `--exit-after-frames` fix (ADR-5452) and `WINDOWS-CHECK.md`. The shipped
  archives were re-packaged from the release commit `59ff4b61d` on 2026-11-15 and pass the package check and the Steam
  Deck (Desktop Mode over SSH) and Intel N150 rows (ADR-7784; sizes and hashes under Release artifacts). The package
  carries the draft EULA and placeholder Steam ids until the owner's texts and ids arrive (D05). The Windows demo is
  cross-built with cargo-xwin, not with native MSVC. Under full cloud cover the sun keeps hard shadows, and the ground
  renders black about half an hour after sunset; the demo panel avoids both (ADR-5419). The trailer's overcast shot
  shows a sky the player can no longer pick (*owner*: keep or re-shoot). [D01, D02]
- Cold first launch: no pipeline is built outside the warm-up queue (`pipelines_outside` 0 at Low and Ultra), but on a
  PC whose graphics driver has not seen the build's shaders the first launch takes 79 s to the forest on the Intel N150
  laptop (second launch 4.2 s); the loading screen keeps presenting (ADR-5578). The forest at Low runs at 14-17 fps
  there. [D03]
- Install passes on the RTX 5080, RTX 5090, lavapipe, the N150 laptop and a Steam Deck OLED in Desktop Mode over SSH.
  The demo does not hold 90 fps on the Deck; the Deck preset caps at 40, which holds. With the Deck's screen blanked the
  demo waits about a second per frame until the screen is on. Open (*owner*): Game Mode and sleep/resume (steps in
  `book/user-guide/steam-deck.md`), a physical pad and keyboard, and the clean-Windows Visual C++ runtime check. [D04]
- The EULA (still the draft; `docs/demo/EULA.md` does not exist yet), the sample-content license
  (`LicenseRef-Regia-Sample` until it is named), the app type, store art approval, the content-survey answer and the

- Starter content is an inventory only; packaging is not authorized until the owner approves the list and licences.
  [X06]

### Known limits by area

#### Rendering

- On the visibility-buffer path, masked persistent sets cast no shadow, alpha-to-coverage is absent, and hashed-alpha
  frames keep masked meshes forward.
- Vendor upscalers get a fixed 16.667 ms frame time; frame generation moves particles with the surface behind them
  because VFX layers write no reactive mask.
- Chromatic-aberration lens effects keep a second full-size HDR copy, and the default VFX layer holds about 128 MiB of
  pooled targets at 1440p.
- The shadow occlusion cull stays off in every preset (ADR-2482): it costs one indirect command per caster and made a
  synthetic scene's shadow passes 4.2 to 4.8 times dearer on an RTX 5090; it gains where casters are hidden (masked
  hidden shadows 4.08 to 0.77 ms on an RTX 5080, ADR-5389). Thin film on 500 materials costs 5 to 8 ms of CPU.
- Skinned casters bound by the linear-blend palette box; compute-skinned units stay unbounded and redraw whole cascades
  (ADR-5418).
- `transmissionTexture` is not read, and Mobile lite tiers shade sheen, skin, eye, hair, clearcoat and anisotropy as
  standard materials.
- Default LOD screen sizes give LOD2 and LOD3 the same threshold, so LOD3 is never selected with five or more levels.
- Quarter-resolution SSGI is a process-wide switch, and a held native or converged temporal view can reuse its old
  output after the switch: call `FrameRenderer::camera_cut` when switching. The traced-shadow any-hit binds no mask
  texture, so a cutout that exists only in a texture casts a dappled field of per-draw coverage, not leaf shapes.
- Forward decals: terrain receives them only through the projected pass, only the default receiver layers are seen,
  Android keeps the projected pass, and the projected pass brightens a normal-mapped decal (x1.69 on the spec 27 decal
  row).
- Cloud shadows: beyond about 8.5 km air and ground keep their sun under any cover; the cloud layer's own aerial
  perspective, froxel fog without `fog_coupling`, VFX particles and baked GI volumes are not dimmed; probes and image
  light take the layer's mean cover, so bounce under a broken layer is too even.
- Inside an `InteriorVolume` that holds the eye, the forward interior shade reads the legacy probe grid, not a baked GI
  volume, so a tunnel with both shows a flat floor. The renderer does not sample the tiled terrain texture slots on any
  platform; terrain look comes from the material arrays and the heightfield.
- On the RX 7900 XTX (RADV) the visibility buffer's frames differ from the forward path's beyond the tests' bounds with
  the per-device anisotropic LOD offset (0.25); `REGIA_MATERIAL_ANISO_LOD_OFFSET=0` restores them. The offset is not
  measured on AMD's own drivers or DX12, which keep 0. On a Mali phone at a Mobile tier the lite forward path filters a
  quarter mip sharper than the other draws of the same frame.
- Frame generation is proven headless only (display pacing and added latency are not measured) and costs about 6.6 ms of
  GPU time per generated frame on an RTX 5090 at 720p (11.1 ms at 2160p). Many-light sampling uploads at most 512
  lights. Registered host passes are not GPU-timed.
- The emissive-map signature hashes every byte of each emitting material's map every frame (about 0.11 ms per MiB at
  opt-level 1), so a 16 MiB map exceeds the 1 ms emissive budget.
- With extreme content two visibility-buffer scratch buffers can outgrow `max_storage_buffer_binding_size` (about 523K
  instance chunks with 8 or more shadow jobs, or 33.5M LOD candidates, on a 128 MiB device). The DDGI brick pool keeps a
  rewritten mesh's old brick, and a wind-only frame clock zeroes a material's own time.
- There is no post-volume override for the lens starburst, and `ProbeSpecular::Lobe` (the default) keeps a red-to-yellow
  nadir spot until the owner's look call (*owner*).

#### Lightmaps, GI and lighting

- An additive level's lightmap needs the primary level to name one; the primary's intensity applies to all, and the
  combined atlas is RGB9E5.
- Lightmap specular is one field per texel, so mirror floors need a reflection capture; captures sit at the box center
  and feed no diffuse.
- Bake `--checkpoint` does not combine with `--incremental`, and checkpoints are rewritten whole (about 600 MiB for
  4096^2, once a minute).
- Dynamic GI reads sun bounce short in interiors lit through openings, and small openings flicker. A probe field whose
  traced scene stays unchanged stops updating after 512 updates of a finest probe (4096 frames on High, Medium and
  Ultra, 8192 on Low; ADR-5001).
- The radiance cache stays off in every preset, and ReSTIR DI has no production hookup.
- `RectLight` and `TubeLight` emit nothing on Desktop Low and Mobile tiers; IES profiles skip Mobile forward, terrain
  and fog (the frame never binds the profile atlas to the terrain pass), at most 7 per frame.
- Terrain holes, grass, far impostors and skinned, morphed or vertex-animated meshes cast no hardware-traced shadow.
- Fog reads one sun cascade, calibrated fog is not the default, and only four interior volumes are read at a time.
- Per-cell lightmaps stack into one atlas of at most 8192 rows (a cell past it keeps the probe light,
  `lightmap_cell_stack_full`); a neighbor's edit does not re-bake a cell, so light near the border can lag until
  `--cells-force`; cells of an additive streamed level are not stacked.
- Imported UV2 charts whose boxes overlap still move together in the tier repack (ADR-5621). Mobile Low uploads no
  lightmap directions, so a smooth metal receiver's baked reflection differs from the full shader by up to 3.8 code
  values. On a phone most of a lightmap's memory is CPU-side (HIGH package: +60.6 MiB native heap for a 10.6 MB upload).
- A baked GI layout past four parts of 128 MiB still fails with `bake_volume_too_large`.
- Dynamic GI: an instance set over 16,384 instances is not traced by the probes, emissive sets light them only at ray
  hits, probes read a material's `roughness` and `metallic` factors rather than its maps, and a probe close behind a
  large single-sided panel counts as inside geometry. The Low tier's probe update takes 4.4 ms at 800p on the Steam
  Deck.

#### World

- The GPU fluid path lacks emitters, reseeding and position correction and computes no body impulses; viscosity, surface
  tension and GPU two-way coupling are deferred.
- Fluid floats bodies slightly high on coarse grids, light bodies can oscillate, and only box, sphere and capsule
  colliders couple.
- The streaming VRAM bound and residency wait are off by default.
- Procgen bakes ship as loose files, `[procgen] bake_tiers` is not read from `Regia.toml`, and baking a graph with
  prefab overrides fails.
- A headless procgen bake has no physics or terrain plugin, so ray casts get no hits; road pieces are not cut back at
  junctions.
- Nav jump-link arcs are fixed at take-off, links and anchors have no editor tools, and tight road bends fold.
- Voxel-far and HLOD proxies get no UV transform, and voxel proxies draw only the finest level and feed no GI.
- Alpha-tested textures keep their coverage in every mip only with the opt-in `.meta` `alpha_cutoff` (ADR-3090; HLOD
  proxy atlases with holes cook with a 0.5 cutoff). Textures without it, and `compress: Some(false)` textures (mips
  built at load), still lose coverage.
- The engine frame does not bind its sun cascade to underwater light shafts; a host binds one with
  `WaterRenderer::set_sun_shadow`.
- Mesh particles still draw the built-in cube, ribbons are CPU-simulated without curl noise or depth collision, and
  disabled VFX modules are not saved.
- The FFT ocean stays opt-in; procgen point seeds repeat past about 2.2 km, and terrain picking loses precision past
  2^20 m from the origin.
- Physics: a static glTF prop that mixes `collision_mesh_*` with other proxies gets no collider; `overlap` and
  `overlap_aabb` report a tiled terrain once per tile collider, and an in-place tile rebuild emits `CollisionEnded` and
  `CollisionStarted` for resting pairs; `thermal_erode` accepts a non-finite `amount` or `talus` and writes NaN heights;
  an in-place edit of a fluid heightfield is not seen until `SolidField::refresh_heights`; the GPU fluid path does not
  slide particles out of static colliders that cross a domain wall.
- Navigation avoidance ignores `NavAgent.height`, and a nav region rebuild (`apply_nav_regions`) does not apply a
  pending floating-origin shift first. With two streamed levels open, a queued teleport move stays queued when its level
  closes. Biome grass multipliers do not reach the GPU grass.
- VFX depth-collision slide lifts along world +Y; significance-reduced instances scale rate emitters only, and the tier
  caps are design values not measured on devices.
- Scatter seasons: impostors baked before a season was set have no bare card, and a leafless far LOD made only of a
  masked billboard draws nothing, so a winter broadleaf far field thins out; set the season before `bake_impostors`.
  Ambient swarms, butterflies and motes live in boxes around the eye, so a camera flying high carries them along.

#### Gameplay systems

- Active-ragdoll landing needs 24 or more solver iterations and stepping needs 32; stepping still misses acceptance, and
  `RagdollProfile::humanoid` cannot balance.
- Vehicle chassis roll and load transfer are about a tenth of the geometry, `Damper` tires have no slip curve, and a
  held brake creeps on slopes.
- Tire transients have no longitudinal relaxation or lagged camber thrust, there is no force-feedback wheel driver, and
  trailer wheels cannot steer.
- The aircraft model has no downwash, ground effect, propeller wash, unsteady stall or fuel burn, and there is no
  rotorcraft.
- Swept CCD skips rounded shapes, half-spaces, polylines and voxels, sweeps at most 256 triangles per pair, and pushes
  only the swept body.
- `display.resolution`, `display.vsync` and `SpawnRequest.prefab` are not applied, and split-screen rects are not
  written to cameras.
- Very large cloth sheets can still shear up to 1.32x.
- AI sight cones are horizontal and sight pairs are not spatially culled; `AiDead` does not stop behavior trees ticking.
- Lua allows one script component per entity, play-in-editor does not load project mods, and an empty or nil-holding
  list comes back as a map.
- `MontagePlayer::apply_stage` applies only the latest fixed substep's root delta, so hosts that drive the player on
  their own schedule under-count root motion. `VehicleSnapshot`'s digest leaves out four tire fields; skid-steer tracks
  are planar (no suspension, terrain or collision response); a zero-area structural bond without an explicit `max_load`
  breaks under any load, and `damage_scale: 0` does not protect against overload; a 2D game with floating-origin
  streaming can see one step of doubled or undone shift.
- Determinism: shots, vehicle AI, scripts and cloth use the soft `track_spawn_order`; `apply_force_fields` orders bodies
  by entity bits; `net_vehicle` yaw paths, 2D physics write-back, `aerosol.rs`, `display_radiance`, `tonemap_agx` and
  painted and clump scatter placement call the platform libm; VFX filled-sphere child offsets can differ by up to 2 ulp
  between GPUs.
- Input: two `Hold` bindings on one action share the `hold_fired` latch, so the second does not fire while the first is
  down; time the fixed-step substep cap discards stays as a lag between `Time::elapsed()` and the fixed clock. Steam
  Deck back grips and trackpads have no `GamepadButton` (Steam Input actions only), and no default Steam Input
  controller configuration is generated.
- Saves: an `Entity` inside a value reflected as one opaque value is written raw, references held by saveable resources
  are not remapped, and `LevelSettings` does not round-trip through reflected postcard outside the cook.
- Hot reload: script slots are not in the reload snapshot, so a game script keeps running the old code after a reload;
  any other cargo build into the editor's target directory refuses every later reload until the editor restarts; the Lua
  mod watch misses a same-size rewrite within the file system's modification-time granularity.

#### Networking and Steam

- Signed manifests stop forgery, not rollback: an older signed manifest the game still accepts is adopted, and DLC entry
  files fetched from a host are unsigned.
- An adopted remote manifest is not persisted across restarts, and `install` chunks are not patched in place.
- Trust on first use does not protect the first connection to a host, and a shared `known_hosts` directory is unsafe on
  multi-account machines.
- There is no path challenge on rebinding, so a reader of client traffic can redirect sealed server traffic for up to a
  second.
- The pre-proof flood shed is global, so legitimate requests wait during a flood, and relay `Bind` needs no source
  proof.
- Lag compensation trusts the shooter's view tick up to `max_rewind_ms`, does not rewind IK targets, and cannot snapshot
  inertialized transitions or active montages. With the 250 ms default and the usual 100 ms interpolation delay, shots
  are clamped from about a 150 ms round trip; `book/agents/net-lag-compensation.md` says how to raise it (ADR-5993).
- Pushed bodies are not predicted on the pushing client, and vehicle prediction holds static colliders only, so
  moving-body bumps arrive as corrections.
- A client below 60 simulated ticks per second resyncs forward about once a second, and `NetSession` clients that stall
  over 10 s time out.
- Server persistence encodes and fsyncs on the tick thread, and components with `Entity` fields are not safe to mark
  `Saveable`.
- `mods://` lookup is case-sensitive with no load-order file, `Vfs` readers do not see it, and mounting a large Workshop
  item stalls one frame.
- Lobby identity is not wired automatically: a game must call `Lobbies::set_identity(game, &manifest)`, or every build
  without one lists and joins every other's lobbies. A Lua mod's manifest `id` is not tied to its mount id, so a
  Workshop item can claim another mod's id, load first and receive its saved state and owned entities.
- Net replay: `net.replay_open` cannot set `ReplayOptions::level_restore`, an explicit `stop_recording` on a stalled
  disk blocks its caller, one file holds one level, and a client cannot record its own stream. Voice chat: a re-ranking
  that drops a speaker sends no stopped edge, and up to 200 ms at the start of a new speaker in a full set can be
  dropped. A client rider on a kinematic body with ramps or stairs can still be corrected. The game port's ban refusal
  follows the system clock, so a forward clock step can end it early.

#### Platforms

- Packaged Android games do not ship `Regia.toml` boot movie settings; set `BootMovies` in code.
- The runtime does not read a packaged per-platform `render_profile` yet, and the Android pak stores the heightfield
  twice.
- On Intel N150-class laptops Desktop Low misses 30 fps while Auto quality still picks it.
- DX12 without DXC builds its pipelines through FXC, which is slow (about 20 minutes for one lightmap test binary and
  about 9 minutes for one Desktop High renderer on the N150, 27 s on Vulkan); the FXC stack-overflow crash is fixed (see
  Fixed). On the Intel N150 (driver 101.7088) the Windows SDK's DXC cannot create four pipelines (lab blend, terrain
  shared color, DDGI relocate and DDGI blend visibility; 0x80070057) that FXC and Vulkan build; the shipping DX12
  compiler is an open decision. (ADR-1583, ADR-1624, ADR-5971)
- On Android, servers end through the panic path only.
- Android: the 2.5 ms wake-ahead was measured on the Pixel 9 Pro (a phone with slower wake-ups may need a longer
  `wake_ahead_ms`); the 50-cycle lifecycle check still sometimes shows a one-time +34-48 MB PSS step that then stays
  flat (ADR-3372); turning TalkBack on mid-game shows Android's permission prompt and pauses the game; ASTC terrain tile
  bytes can differ between cook hosts of different ISAs.
- Windows: native crash capture stays off unless `REGIA_NATIVE_CRASH=1`; a generator cancel does not stop processes the
  command left running after it exited (a process that calls `setsid` escapes the cancel on every platform); `regia_pt`
  atomic EXR, PNG and JSON writes do not retry a sharing violation; the agent folder `.regia/agent/` keeps its inherited
  ACL (its files are private), and an `audit.1.jsonl` rotated by an earlier version keeps its ACL until it rotates out.
- The engine has no watchdog for a driver that hangs inside `vkCreateDevice`.

#### Tools, editor and pipeline

- `regia engine package` follows tracked symlinks out of the checkout, and relative `--dist`/`--out` paths go verbatim
  into steamcmd VDFs.
- Pak and release signing is deferred, and the `regia engine verify` manifest is not signed.
- Plain `http://` shared cook caches have no encryption or authentication, `https://` trusts web PKI roots only, and the
  live cooker skips the cache.
- The MCP HTTP transport has no server-to-client event stream, so notifications are dropped, and editor extension
  methods are not MCP tools.
- An agent with `build` permission or a shell can still overwrite golden images; the sandbox and git diff are the
  backstop.
- Prefab Apply changes the loaded template for the session only and refuses nested prefabs.
- MaterialX imports one material per file with at most 7 images and 15 parameters; specular IOR, sheen, thin film and
  refraction are dropped with a warning.
- Import and export: glTF `KHR_texture_transform` over float UVs reaches only the side table (the file draws with
  untransformed UVs), `KHR_meshopt_compression` is not read, and the cooked export loses skins, morph targets, a second
  UV set and the hierarchy; USD skips UsdSkel (skinned meshes come in rigid), `UsdTransform2d`, point instancers and
  implicit shapes, passes light intensities through unconverted and reads a time-sampled attribute's earliest sample;
  PLY point clouds, PSB, CMYK, indexed and 32-bit PSD, layer effects and STL colors are not imported; `regia export
  usda` writes geometry only and copies `TEXCOORD_0` without flipping `t`; Aseprite group opacity multiplies into each
  child; DDS volume textures are refused, and the material loader reads 2D textures only.
- CLI: `regia package --platform linux` does not package the net-game template's `client/` and `server/` layout (build
  `cargo build --release -p <name>_client` instead); `regia doctor` without `--platform` checks the Android tools and
  fails `jdk_missing`/`gradle_missing` on a desktop-only machine (run `regia doctor --platform linux`); `regia package
  --profile shipping` ends in a single-threaded fat-LTO link (18 to 30 minutes on a build machine) and reads
  `[profile.shipping]` only from the workspace `Cargo.toml`; `android-pass` finds `adb` only through `ANDROID_HOME`,
  `ANDROID_SDK_ROOT` or `PATH`.
- Editor: opening an editor document (`editor.conversation_open` and the others) discards unsaved edits, and
  `level.convert` does not refuse a level with unsaved editor edits; `scene.spawn` entities are not written by
  `editor.save_level` (use `editor.spawn`); every Localization panel edit is an undo entry and marks the level unsaved;
  the conversation preview steps without waiting for line durations; on a HiDPI or small window the viewport renders far
  more pixels than it shows, and the `editor` preset has no render-scale setting.
- Play-in-editor: separate server and client processes ignore the network conditioner settings, and the editor does not
  build their binaries (run `regia server --project <dir> --clients 1` once); multi-client PIE server worlds do not
  install the Static move guard.
- Sequencer: only the top sequence's spawnables spawn, `.variant.ron` paths are not loaded (use `guid:`), the editor
  preview reports `seq_video_unavailable` for video sections, and a take recorded in PIE is lost if PIE stops before
  Stop.
- Content safety: the working directory is an allowed root for agent request paths (a game started from the home folder
  lets `mods.mount`/`mods.read` read text files under it); the viewer pack's kit GLB reader and the editor's glTF
  hierarchy and mesh loaders use the unchecked `gltf` entry points; the runtime mounts an installed chunk pak without
  comparing it with its manifest entry; a moved asset's path inside a longer string is not rewritten.
- A Play asset-pack retry that sees the old transfer's stale `DOWNLOADING` and then its `CANCELED` echo fails with `the
  store cancelled pack`, and a real cancel before the retry's first store row is dropped as the echo.

#### Regia Forest Demo

- Low, Deck and phone tiers draw a fixed sky, and a benchmark run under vsync measures the capped rate.
- No ambience or footstep recordings ship, and footsteps read neither painted terrain layers nor physics materials.
- With star flux off (the default) most stars vanish at 3840x2160, and stars show through dense night clouds.
- The walk bob has no footstep events and stacks with the handheld camera bob when both are on.

#### Audio, media, UI and accessibility

- There is no runtime flash limiter, and the flash check maps black to zero and can miss a flash when `cell_px` exceeds
  1.
- Motion settings do not scale FOV kicks on rig nodes, shots or the sequencer, and the color-vision filter misses UI and
  post-off views.
- The UI accessibility lint reads no pixels and misses layer-only focus indicators, and light themes' hover button text
  measures 3.96:1.
- On Android an accessibility-off race in `accesskit_android` can still abort, window focus is not forwarded, and
  `REGIA_ACCESSKIT=0` keeps the adapter off.
- Loudness normalization is off by default in a packaged game, and music voices do not survive a suspend.
- A transcoded `.wav` holds Ogg bytes under its name, there is no Opus or per-bus quality, and the mixer dock does not
  write `config/audio.ron`.
- Material video slots keep a CPU copy, players past the pool are refused rather than queued, and AV1 encode is slow and
  8-bit 4:2:0 SDR only.
- CFF, collection, COLR and unusual-cmap fonts ship whole, bundled engine fonts are not subset, and there is no
  per-locale font setting.
- Locale formatting has no currency names, compact notation, non-Gregorian calendars or IANA zones, and Lao, Khmer and
  Myanmar draw tofu with bundled fonts.
- Recycled virtual-list rows carry unbound state, backdrop blur inside a group layer is skipped, and world-space panels
  have no mips.
- Audio: overlapping IR reverb zones do not blend two IRs, time-stretched voices are outside the voice limit and
  ducking, `config/audio.ron` is read only as a loose file next to the project (not from a pak or APK) and overrides a
  host's `AudioSettings::buses`, binaural rendering and sound propagation do not apply to sequence voices, and a docked
  bulkhead between two pressurized interiors on an airless world scores as silence.
- UI: `font_runs` treats any character after a ZWJ as an emoji continuation; `layout::resolved_rtl` has no ancestor cap,
  so a parent cycle can stall layout; the UI extract cull drops text shadows and glyphs that reach past a node's rect;
  two automation clicks in consecutive frames merge into one; the choice widget draws no nodes and focus-on-open does
  not move the focus ring; captions ignore the theme `subtitle` tokens and the high-contrast setting; a widget
  re-enabled after the screen reader's last sync is refused for one more frame.

### Fixed after v0.5.0

These bugs are in v0.5.0; the v0.5.1 line fixes them.

- Render extraction ignores `Visibility`: a hidden mesh, or a mesh under a hidden parent, still draws, so the
  sequencer's `visible` track and `regia_net`'s hide-until-shown do not hide meshes in a game, and the `third_person`
  sample's Fox rig and sword stay visible while the player is downed (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7200, ADR-7588 on the v0.5.1
  line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- With the Khronos validation layer on, `vkCreateShaderModule` reports `VUID-StandaloneSpirv-None-10684` for the baked
  GI screen and point lookups, the product viewer's family shaders contact shadow, looks and their gem and frosted
  material hooks (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7587 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Switching a data layer off at runtime hides a procgen generator's instance sets but leaves its prefab instances
  visible and its colliders in physics (invisible collision); switching it back on leaves the prefabs hidden and the
  colliders parked, so players walk through generated trees. Tile cells keep the state they were applied with, and
  generated output ignores its generator's data layer (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7556, ADR-7595 on the v0.5.1 line,
  ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Procgen: a nested `spline.modules` repeat (`repeat(repeat(post, 1000000), 1000000)`) or a large `pattern_from` pattern
  tries to build every term before any limit fires; biome child rules allocate `parents × count.hi` candidates before
  the first limit check, and a `(0, u32::MAX)` count overflows; `points.project` onto a few far-apart boxes can allocate
  about 400 MB per run or per tile cell (fixed in v0.5.1)
- Procgen: instance colliders built before the editor loaded the meshes stay unit boxes until a manual re-run; road
  decals published inside a called graph (`graph.call`, `graph.loop`, a biome graph) are dropped; a baked streaming
  generator that found no bake keeps reporting `procgen_bake_missing` after a level change, or after the bake file
  appears in the same directory; two tiled cells that export one point-set name with differently typed attributes
  silently drop the second cell's points (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7595 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Navigation: NaN or negative agent parameters are accepted (a NaN `max_climb` joins cells across any height, so paths
  climb cliffs, and a NaN `radius` turns erosion off), as is a `max_slope` of pi/2 or more; `set_off_mesh_links` keeps
  invalid links and cooks them into cell blobs its own loader rejects; `NavLaneGraph::build` compares every lane start
  with every lane end, which is slow near the 65,536-lane cap (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7558 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending
  in v0.5.0)
- Navigation crowds: when a streamed tile with off-mesh links loads or unloads, link reservations keep the old index, so
  a second agent can start across an exclusive link that is still occupied and events name the wrong link; an infinite
  authored link duration parks an agent for about 4.9 hours at 60 Hz; an origin shift or grid growth applies a waiting
  tile patch to the new numbering; a portal to a missing polygon panics the crowd; a huge `max_steps_per_advance` with
  one long frame runs millions of steps (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7558 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Behavior trees: a decorator with `abort` set does not hold its branch out while its key is false (after a `self` or
  `both` abort the branch starts again on the next tick, and a key that is false on the first tick does not stop it); a
  tree nested deeper than 256 levels overflows the stack on its first tick or abort; a `condition` with an empty key
  always fails and is flagged only by `ai.bt_validate` (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7559, ADR-7596 on the v0.5.1 line,
  ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- AI: utility hot reload matches by file name, so editing `assets/bosses/guard.utility.ron` also reloads
  `guard.utility.ron`; an async `RouteAll` query with a candidate-relative `to` and no `scan_limit` resolves every
  anchor in one tick outside its budget (set `scan_limit` to avoid it); a `NaN` lattice spacing emits one item and a
  `NaN` ring radius emits NaN positions; the smart-object slot index stays stale when a slot's ancestor loses its
  `GlobalTransform` (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7559, ADR-7596 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Animation: socket-targeted IK (`TwoBoneIK`, `LookAt`) on a rig away from the origin aims off by the rig's world
  position (a rig at (100, 0, 0) aims its limb 100 m off), and a socket entity parented to its rig is placed twice by
  transform propagation (fixed in v0.5.1)
- Animation: a hand-built skeleton whose parent list is short or names a later joint panics in the IK solvers, body aim,
  eye gaze, the ragdoll pose blend and the motion-matching warps (cooked loads already refuse such skeletons; call
  `Skeleton::validate` on hand-built ones). A negative `UpdateMode::Lod { far_rate }` freezes the animator; a synced
  blend space whose dominant clip has zero length fires notifies from every sample; the montage `Slot` check runs only
  for the first graph and slot list an animator sees (fixed in v0.5.1)
- Abilities: a `WhileTagged` start that fails its check writes no `AbilityLifecycle::Refused`; chance-stream rows of
  despawned targets are never pruned, so a streaming world keeps one row per short-lived target in memory and in the
  save, and each roll replays its stream from the saved position, so rolls get dearer over a long game (fixed in v0.5.1;
  ADR-7573 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Saves and placed levels: loading a save despawns the subtrees of `Saveable` entities, non-saveable children included
  (an emitter, a camera rig, a pooled attachment); `placed::check_placement` does not count prefab template expansion
  against the 100,000-entity placement cap (the per-instantiation work bound still applies); a data-layer member
  respawned under a `Transform`-less group after a floating-origin rebase comes back at its file position (fixed in
  v0.5.1; ADR-7601 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Lua: `LuaMods::restore_state` revives two mods that declare the same component name, so both `on_update` hooks tick
  the same entities and share their values; a scratch `lua.run` (no `mod`) leaves its spawns in the world until the
  entity cap refuses later scratch spawns; `lua.globals` lists a key twice when a table holds both `1` and `'1'` (fixed
  in v0.5.1)
- Net vehicles and bots: an ordinary seat exit runs `release_vehicle`, logging `net.vehicle_owner_left` and resetting
  input on every exit; a bot whose player index the `PlayerManager` forgot (a restored or reloaded world) never gets the
  game rules' `on_player_left` (fixed in v0.5.1)
- Lobbies: when Steam refuses the second or third publish write of `Lobbies::launch`, the lobby keeps advertising the
  torn-down session as `launching` and joinable; a refused engine-key or joinable write on create still reports
  `Created(Ok)`; after `Steam::shutdown()` and a re-`initialize()`, `Lobbies` keeps polling the dead backend and
  matchmaking stalls (fixed in v0.5.1)
- `NetSession::resume` on a session opened with `connect_on` (a Steam lobby or loopback socket) dials plain UDP at the
  synthetic address and times out silently; reconnect such sessions with `resume_on`. A failed in-game travel reports
  the new level in `status` and `/health` while the old one runs, and a save-on-leave layer re-saves every tick (fixed
  in v0.5.1)
- The `net_game` sample's Shift (scoreboard) stays held when the Steam overlay opens without a focus loss (fixed in
  v0.5.1)
- Audio: a resume whose output fails to re-open (a Bluetooth headset still reconnecting) leaves audio off; the
  microphone goes silent after a headset switch; a WAV header with a huge sample rate can abort a cook or
  `audio.loudness` (about 109 GB of windows at a 4 GHz rate); a reverb impulse response resampled to the mix rate has
  the wrong gain (a 48 kHz IR on a 96 kHz mix is 6.02 dB too loud, a 96 kHz IR on a 48 kHz mix 6 dB too quiet; same-rate
  IRs are correct) (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7602 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Video: a looping file that declares a duration under 1 ms floods the main thread with `Looped` events (about 17
  million per 60 Hz tick); a player that comes back into view resyncs with a keyframe seek, leaving an audio offset of
  up to one keyframe interval; clips under about 8 fps lose audio; a PCM track without an Audio element plays silent
  (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7563 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- VFX: a NaN or infinite value in many `.vfx.ron` fields loads (a NaN `gravity` gives NaN positions; a NaN `count_scale`
  silently kills the emitter); an event link whose worst-case child count reaches 2^24 packs to zero and never fires,
  and larger counts spawn the wrong number; `VfxGpu::push_spawn_jobs` does not bounds-check a host's `emitter_slot`
  (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7560 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Packaging: `regia cook --quality fast` followed by `regia package` ships the fast cook's uncompressed paks; a version
  bump in a legacy `Ferrite.toml` does not repack, so the package keeps the old content build id; chunk-assigned assets
  without a cooked file ship in no pak with only a warning; a cooked cache without `asset_manifest.bin` mints a build id
  from empty bytes (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7612 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- Streaming: `stream.pin` with a `wait` over an area the grid cannot list (past 2^22 cells) stores the pin, and
  `play.wait_until pending_waits == 0` runs into its own timeout; `stream.teleport` to a target outside the grid's range
  does the same (fixed in v0.5.1; ADR-7612 on the v0.5.1 line, ADR pending in v0.5.0)
- `regia upgrade-project` walks `build` and `dist`: it rewrites staged or shipped frozen-format files in place and drops
  `.bak` files into a shipped bundle (regenerate those trees with `regia package`). `regia test` fails a suite that runs
  longer than 60 s ("did not finish within 1200 polls"), and reports "0 tests ran" for a refusal whose message merely
  contains `no_tests` (fixed in v0.5.1)

## Golden changes

- Re-blessed on the lavapipe reference after the owner's 2026-11-09 look approval: `lightmap_quad_box`,
  `lightmap_forward_quad_box` and `lightmap_forward_quad_box_lite_low`. Reason: the denoiser's chart-border taps
  (ADR-4108) change every bake, and receiver specular (ADR-4762) changes lightmapped receivers. The two forward
  references were first added with the v0.3.0 docs (ADR-3211), and the golden test now fails when a reference is
  missing.
- Frames these changes can move, with no golden re-blessed for them:
  - Frames with entry-streak shed pieces (showcase shot 3, about frames 480 to 960; ADR-1517).
  - Sky, sun and glass-crack frames after the software `libm` change (ADR-4777).
  - Normal-mapped double-sided graph materials seen from behind, and the sky looking straight down from under 1.3 m
    (ADR-4950).
  - Terrain frames after the gap-sun azimuth and detail-normal clamp fixes and the UNORM fade encoding.
- Re-blessed on the lavapipe reference after the owner's 2026-11-12 GI look approval (ADR-9690): `regia_quick`
  `quick_3d.png` (the metallic sphere's reflection and the contact light follow the High-tier GI), and the
  `studio_frame_pin` sunless and sunless-image sphere pins (the sphere catches the floor's bounce light).
- Re-blessed on the lavapipe reference after the owner's 2026-11-13 sign-off (ADR-7552), at the v0.5.0 candidate and
  byte-identical to the signed-off frames: the four `lookdev` `default_look_golden` shots (small shifts on the props,
  the hull underside and the cockpit interior from the GI merges), and the
  `the_studio_authority_frames_keep_their_pixels` sphere pins, [167, 122, 130] and [159, 81, 80] (ADR-5716 (7)).
- No other golden image was added or re-blessed. Opt-in features leave default frames unchanged, as their fragments
  state (contact calibration, calibrated fog, fibrous cirrus, post-volume LUTs, the dense visibility switch, TSR and
  BRDF modes).
- Threshold, not a re-bless: `atmosphere_golden` compares on a hardware GPU at the ADR-270 sanity floor; Metal-only
  tolerances were added (ADR-3373, ADR-3954); a new `amd_rdna3` plate floor (ADR-3957).
- Non-image pins re-recorded on purpose, each by attributed experiment:
  - The `race` determinism trace and the abilities `BASE_TRACE` (`0xee07_db8d_9b1e_615a`).
  - The VFX v6 pin (`0x6d37_3f31_5bb2_da27`), `PIN_SLIP` and the corpus fixtures of the bumped formats.
  - Still to re-record: `frame_work_ratchet` (DesktopHigh/Ultra `upload_bytes`, 864 against 544 B; ledger B08).
- Non-image pin re-recorded: the Linux x86_64 tire-transient digest moves from `0x7b6a_597b_8cb2_230d` to
  `0x79ff_136f_98e0_d47c` after slip tires moved to the `det_*` kernels. (ADR-4773)
- Threshold, not a re-bless: the GPU fluid spinning-paddle pin is asserted on the lavapipe reference device only;
  other devices are held to the CPU tolerance. (ADR-5598)
- Non-image pins re-recorded on purpose: every `regia_fluid` acceptance, moving-solids and coupling digest (separation
  cap, software sqrt sweep and complete digest; the 30-step foam-count trajectory is unchanged) (ADR-5313, ADR-5414);
  `SEED77_JOBS_BITS_HASH` `0x2a42_8090_3834_baa1` to `0x8a0d_331b_8d57_8ba0` (the test path uses `det_sin_cos`);
  guid-keyed chooser draw sequences (ADR-5415).
- New pins: mask and normal FNV-1a hashes for all 12 built-in glass stamps (`laminate_set()` and
  `pressure_pane_set()`).
- Not re-blessed and may move, to review on the lavapipe reference: `quick_strokes.png` (plain strokes skip lyon,
  ADR-5305), frames with blended draws or frame-clock graphs (sort by bounds center, larger uniforms; ADR-5160), and
  deep parallax or mesh-occluder scenes (the POM step and occluder fixes).
- Re-blessed on the lavapipe reference (ADR-5910): the five `planet_frames` goldens `planet_airless_noon`,
  `planet_earth_noon`, `planet_mars_noon`, `clouds_behind_peak` and `clouds_noon`. Reason: the dynamic GI probes trace
  the scene's 12 km ground quad since ADR-5716, so the tower's shaded faces take the sunlit ground's one bounce where
  the sky map's lower half lit them, and are darker (airless 0.245 to 0.062, Earth 0.239 to 0.147, Mars 0.179 to 0.128).
  Nothing else in the frames moves.
- Re-blessed after the owner's Q3 approval on 2026-11-11: `lightmap_forward_quad_box_lite_low` (the old image was the
  frame before ADR-4930's energy correction). (ADR-5993)
- Rule, not a re-bless: `lookdev` `default_look_golden` compares frame 64 of a fresh renderer with the goldens
  (`LookOptions::settled_golden()`) and fails if frame 72 differs from frame 64; the SSIM bar stays 0.98. (ADR-5990)
- Non-image pins re-recorded on purpose: the `default_graphs_unchanged` shader-text hash of `hull_panel` and
  `docs/formats/matgraph.example.ron` (`0x5196_3dbb_2067_f543`, parallax step count); the fluid coupling digests
  `0xe924_b082_81f2_2163` / `0xbd65_9117_36a9_e789` (ADR-9230); the Linux x86_64 `vehicle_tire_transients` pin
  `0xdbbe45dea13227b8` (ADR-7298); the `abilities_equivalence` trace, now folded by spawn index (ADR-5960).
- Re-blessed after the dynamic GI changes: the six `planet_frames` goldens whose first frame gained probe light on
  Desktop High's layout (ADR-5457), plus `planet_mars_noon` and `planet_airless_noon`, which that test had never reached
  before; then `clouds_behind_peak`, `planet_earth_noon`, `planet_mars_noon`, `planet_airless_noon` and `clouds_noon`
  again because the probes now trace the scene's 12 km ground quad (ADR-5716, ADR-5910).
- Non-image pin: `crowd_lab --test cross_arch` (feature `cross-platform-determinism`) is re-pinned to the crowd digests
  after the two-bone IK blends with `det::quat_slerp` (ADR-5410); the nav digest is unchanged.

## Release artifacts

Tag `v0.5.0` (annotated) on commit `59ff4b61d267a4473006581aa4ef08aecaf8c095`, 2026-11-15. The demo archives and the
release binaries were built from that commit on 2026-11-15 (ADR-7784, `docs/evidence/v05-release-artifacts.md`); their
`COMMIT` file and the `git` field of `demo-package.json` name it. `cargo xtask demo-package --verify` re-hashes the
archives against `CHECKSUMS.b3` (the BLAKE3 check). The package uses placeholder Steam ids until the owner's ids arrive;
re-packaging with the real ids may change the archive hashes below (D05).
